Údaje a zabezpečení and Bankovní účty then say they load once connected, `adminGuard` sends to Můj přehled with `needs-connection`, the interceptor asks for a refresh before any call that carries the token (not before a call it sends without one, `auth-interceptor.ts` below), and the connection coming back asks for one) and `sessionAcceptedAt` (when the server last accepted the session, sealed into the copy by `DonorCopySync`); `register` opens no session, `confirmEmail(token)`, `completeSecondFactor(challenge, code)`, `changePassword`, `confirmSecondFactor(code)`, `disableSecondFactor` and `signInWithPasskey()` (options → the device → the answer) do, `setupSecondFactor(confirmation)` only returns the enrolment, `removePassword(confirmation)` adopts the new session, `confirmWith(password | null)` turns the password, or for an account without one the device's sign-in prompt over `/api/donors/auth/passkey/options`, into the `Confirmation` the credential changes send, `requestEmailChange` changes nothing until the link is opened; access token in memory; one refresh shared between callers, and between the tabs of the browser (plan v3, 2.8): the refresh runs under the Web Lock `nfnn-refresh` (`session-tabs.ts`: `TAB_LOCKS` is `navigator.locks`, null where the browser has none, which then refreshes without it), a restore gives up at once when `Connectivity` says the app is offline, before it asks, or when it goes offline while the restore waits (`wentOffline`); a restore whose refresh has no answer after 1 s (`CONNECTION_CHECK_AFTER_MS`) also asks `Connectivity.tryAgain()`, so a network that hangs ends it within about 6 s and the visitor waits signed out with the hint kept (`AuthStore.waitsSignedOut()` true until a session is adopted or forgotten, so what follows the status can tell this wait from a session known to be gone), and the next restore asks again; its request is cut off after 15 s (`REFRESH_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`, the `XMLHttpRequest` aborted before the lock is released, so no other tab refreshes with the cookie a late answer would still replace) and a tab gives up waiting for the lock after 20 s (`REFRESH_LOCK_WAIT_MS`, longer than the request); the tab that refreshed announces the new session on the `BroadcastChannel` `nfnn-session` (`SESSION_CHANNEL`, a `session-refreshed` message of `version` 1 with the `AuthResponse`, checked by `isSessionRefreshed`, which also asks for the user's id and roles and the expiry, so a tab of another build after a deploy changes nothing with a message it would misread; posting never fails the refresh; the channel closed with the store), every other tab adopts it unless it holds the session of another donor; a refresh, once started, runs to its answer or its timeout even when its caller is gone (`share({ resetOnRefCountZero: false })`), because an aborted answer would be a lost one; `logout()` runs under the same lock (or, after 20 s of waiting, without it), its request cut off after 15 s like a refresh's so a stalled sign-out never holds the other tabs' refresh, then forgets the session and posts `signed-out`, on which every other tab forgets its own; a sign-out the server did not answer (no connection, the timeout, a 5xx, 408 or 429; a 4xx counts as heard) still forgets the session on the device, but the refresh cookie stays valid on the server, so the store keeps `nfnn.sign-out-pending` (`nfnn.sign-out-pending-app` in the installed app) in `localStorage`, the signed-out notice adds "Server se o odhlášení zatím nedozvěděl…" (close the browser on a borrowed computer), the sign-out is sent again at every start and whenever `Connectivity` comes back online, and while the mark is set `restore()` refreshes nothing even when the session flag is present (so a flag planted in the devtools resumes no session); only an answer clears it, and so does a new sign-in, whose cookie replaces the old one (security audit #915, FRONTEND-1), and a tab that waited for the lock and meanwhile got one sends no refresh of its own (one that did not still presents the cookie the first tab's answer set, so the server sees a plain rotation); only a refusal (a 4xx other than 408 and 429) ends the session in the tab and drops the flag, and nothing else the browser keeps; a refresh without an answer (no connection, the timeout, the lock wait) keeps the session and the flag, and a `restore()` that got none leaves the visitor anonymous until the next `restore()`, which tries again at once after a quick failure and only after 10 s (`SLOW_RESTORE_PAUSE_MS`) after a timeout or a lock wait that gave up, so the guard of the first page does not keep a blank page waiting a second time; `restore()` on start calls `refresh` only when the `localStorage` flag `nfnn.donor-session` says a session may exist; session kinds (plan v3 2.10): `session-jar.ts` gives `SESSION_JAR`, the browser's jar (`nfnn-refresh`, `nfnn-session`, `nfnn.donor-session`) or, when the app runs installed (`runsInstalled`: `display-mode: standalone` or `navigator.standalone`), the app's (`nfnn-refresh-app`, `nfnn-session-app`, `nfnn.donor-session-app`), so the installed app and a tab of the same browser keep their own lock, channel and flag, and `auth-api.ts` sends `Nfnn-Session: APP` on the app's refresh and sign-out, so the backend reads and clears the app's own cookie; `login`, `completeSecondFactor` and `signInWithPasskey` take the kind the dialog chose (`BROWSER`, `REMEMBERED` or `PUBLIC`; the app always sends `APP`, an e-mail confirmation `BROWSER`), and the store keeps the answer's `session()`; a `PUBLIC` session keeps its flag in `sessionStorage` instead, so nothing of it outlives the browser, and `storesNothing()` tells the device copy (2.14) to keep nothing on the device; because `sessionStorage` belongs to one tab, a tab opened from a public-computer session starts signed out and offers the sign-in again, which is accepted for a borrowed computer), `auth-interceptor.ts` (Bearer on `/api/**` except the auth endpoints, a request whose `HttpContext` sets `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN` (the public receipt verification, the payment details and the setup status) and a `GET` of an answer that is the same for everyone (`answersTheSameForEveryone`, the anchored list of `core/updates/service-worker-bypass.ts`: the home content, news, projects, photos, payment details and bank logos; #893, because the resource server checks any Bearer before `permitAll`, so an expired token or an ended session made the public content, the service worker's background refresh and `keptAnswerRefreshInterceptor` answer 401 and the kept copy was never updated), which go out without the header and are never refreshed; parses an `application/problem+json` Blob body into the `HttpErrorResponse` first; on 401 `auth/expired-token` (and, since 2.9, `auth/session-ended`, a token of a device signed out elsewhere, whose refresh then fails and forgets the session, a sign-out for the device copy, 2.14) refreshes once and retries), `donor-guard.ts` (waits for the restore, else goes home and opens the sign-in dialog with `returnTo` set to the asked address), `login-dialog.service.ts` (`open(mode = 'login', { returnTo, afterSignIn, email })`, `LoginDialogMode` `login` | `register` | `reset`, read once by the dialog as its initial tab, `email` prefilling that tab's address (the first setup passes the administrator's); `keepRegistrationDraft`/`registrationDraft`/`dropRegistrationDraft` hold the registration fields (never the password) of a dialog closed half way; the dialog calls `signedIn()` after a sign-in (with or without the second factor), which closes it and then opens `returnTo` when given, else stays on the page and calls `afterSignIn` (Jakub 2026-10-23: a sign-in from the home page stays there, the header then offers "Můj přehled"; only a sign-in asked for by a protected page returns to it). `returnTo` comes from the guards, from the session-loss handlers of the overview and of every administration page (`router.url` before going home) and from the password-reset page (`/darce/prehled`)), `passkey-authenticator.ts` (`PasskeyAuthenticator.supported()`, `create(options)` and `get(options)` over `navigator.credentials` with base64url both ways; a refusal becomes `PasskeyCeremonyError` with `cancelled` (`NotAllowedError`, `AbortError`), `already-registered` (`InvalidStateError`), `unsupported` or `failed`; specs replace it with `testing/FakePasskeyAuthenticator`), `device-name.ts` (`deviceName(document)`: iPhone, iPad, Android, Mac, Windows, Chromebook or Linux from the user agent, the default name of a new key), `core/navigation/external-navigation.ts` (`ExternalNavigation.assign(url)` leaves the app for another site; specs replace it), `api-problem.ts` (`describeProblem(failure, generic)`: title, detail and field messages in one line, or `generic`; `problemOf` also reads the second-factor `challenge`), `testing/` fixtures (`sampleDonor`, `secondFactorRequired`, `secondFactorEnrolment`).
- Bounds on client work (`.claude/rules/reliability.md`): the public content asked for ahead: an article or a story at most once per address while its answer is kept (10 min, at most 50 answers) or on its way, the first answers of the tabs once per start 1.5 s after the first page, never offline or saving data; an article waits at most 300 ms for its answer before it opens; the pairing dialog in Platby waits at most 300 ms for its first search; an answer asked ahead and shown within 10 s is not asked for again behind the page; a loading skeleton shows after `--delay-loading` and holds the loaded content back at most `--duration-loading-min` from the moment it showed, never offline; the launch screen stands at most 10 s after the first navigation starts (`LAUNCH_SCREEN_LONGEST_MS`), so a page whose chunk never arrives cannot hide the app, its notices and the offline pill for good; the bank check polls for at most 90 s, read again on the live `bank-import` hint, else every 2 s without a stream and 15 s with one; the payment panel's watch for an arriving gift (`gift-arrival/`) asks the bank only after an intent to pay, 30, 60, 120, 240 and 480 s after it and at once on a return to the tab, at least 30 s apart, at most 12 times and for 10 minutes after the last intent or return, never in a hidden tab or offline, and ended when its page is left; the add-account watch makes at most 20 checks 30 s apart, paused while the tab is hidden; the administration's sleep check (`AdministrationAccess`) runs every 15 s only while the administration is unlocked and the page visible, reads two clocks and asks nothing of the network; the backup job is polled 20 × 3 s; the import card's run of older bank payments 40 × 3 s; the restart wait 100 × 3 s and the restore wait 300 × 3 s; the key card's wait for a new signing key after a withdrawal 100 × 3 s; the device status question is asked again when the connection returns (`DeviceStatusRetry`, 2.21) only after an unanswered one, one round per return from offline, one request per device secret held; the administration copy (2.20a, 2.20b) is refreshed by a read at most once a minute, after a change at most once in 30 s per tab, never for five minutes after a 429 of its sync (shared by the device's tabs), and a mismatch with the manifest's summary is reported at most once per copy contents and once an hour. The live stream (`core/live/`, 2.16) runs only in a visible tab with a listening page and is reopened after 1 s, doubling to 60 s with jitter, never after a 401 or 403 until the session is renewed (a new sign-in or the next token refresh). The boot watchdog (slice 2.4) waits 15 s, re-arms in 15 s steps up to 60 s while the app's code has arrived, then shows its panel; the app reloads by itself at most once per 60 s per reason (`ReloadGuard` in `sessionStorage`: `boot`, `update`, `unrecoverable`, `chunk-load`), never without the storage; a new version reloads by itself only at a free moment and never offline (`AppUpdates`: within `START_UP_WINDOW_MS`, 10 s, of the start, at a navigation, or at once for a new API generation, once per generation and build in a tab, or an unrecoverable worker), and `AppUpdates` waits at most `UPDATE_CHECK_TIMEOUT_MS` (10 s) for the worker's check; the place kept for a reload is read once and only within 60 s, and putting the page back waits at most 5 s. The service worker serves a kept public content answer at once and fetches it again behind it (5 s for one it never kept), gives the network 1 s before it serves the kept payment details (only on a network failure or that timeout, never on a 5xx) and keeps at most 100 public answers for 30 days, the payment details for a day and 300 photos for 365 days. A page transition covers the page for at most 5 s while the next page loads (`PAGE_COVER_LONGEST_MS`) and moves for at most 2 s (`PAGE_TRANSITION_LONGEST_MS`), then the live page shows whatever its copies still do; the page a swipe previews waits at most 300 ms to be stable (`PREVIEW_DRAWING_LONGEST_MS`), each of the preview's two navigations at most 1 s (`PREVIEW_STEP_LONGEST_MS`), and a settling swipe waits for its preview, so at most that long. The connectivity probe gives up after 5 s, and a session restore waits for no network the app knows is gone. A session gets one silent refresh, then a clean sign-in. A receipt drawing that fails is reported to `/api/operations/client-errors` (1.20); the error reports also name a start the watchdog had to rescue (`boot` / `start-up`), a new version that failed to install (`sw-install`) and a running version the server no longer has (`sw-unrecoverable`, both `service-worker`).

## Shared UI (`shared/ui/`)

- `form-field/`: `<app-form-field label [hint] [messages]>` around one control marked `appFormControl` (needs `id` and `ngModel`); renders `<label for>`, the hint and the message for the first error key (or the error's own text when `messages` has no entry for it, as the server's `{ server: message }`), sets `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby` once touched or submitted (touched during a pointer press only from `pointerup` or `pointercancel` on, so a message that appears on the blur cannot push the submit button away before the click; a message already shown stays during a press; a required field the visitor only passed through without typing says nothing until the form is sent: its own blur marks it untouched again, so the message cannot move the form while the next field is tapped (on iOS the press ends before the focus moves, so the press guard cannot help there), while a field the app marks touched itself, as `placeServerErrors` or a refused publish date do, shows it; with the on-screen keyboard open (`SoftKeyboard.isOpen()`), Enter in a field with `enterkeyhint="next"` moves the focus to the next text field of the form instead of sending it, unless the whole form is already valid, as after the password manager filled it in; with a hardware keyboard Enter sends the form as on any web page (#359, #384)); `form[appFocusFirstInvalid]` focuses the first invalid control on submit; `server-errors.ts` `placeServerErrors(form, errors, controlNames)` puts the field errors of a 400 problem on their controls (touched, cleared by the next edit) and hands back those without a field. Control look is the global `.form-control` in `src/styles/_form-controls.scss`; components only space fields.
- `password-reveal/`: eye button "Zobrazit heslo" (`aria-pressed`, `aria-controls`) switching the input between `password` and `text`; every password field uses it.
- `account-username/`: `<app-account-username [email]>` is a hidden, read-only `input type="email" autocomplete="username"` (`tabindex="-1"`, `aria-hidden`) holding the account's e-mail. Every form that asks for the current password without an e-mail field (the account card's e-mail change, password change, password removal and second factor on and off, the passkeys panel's new key) puts it before its first field, as Chrome and Safari ask of such forms: a password manager then knows the account the password belongs to and fills no other field (Safari ignores `autocomplete="off"` and took "Název klíče" for the user name, Jakub 2026-10-31).
- `disclosure/`: `appDisclosure` on every panel that opens and closes through `motion.expand` (T7, Motion): with `[closed]` it sets `hidden` and `inert` together, so a closing panel is out of the Tab order and the screen reader's reach from its first moment while its height still closes, and opening drops both at once, so its content takes the focus in the first frame; on content an `@if` removes it sets `inert` when Angular destroys the view, while the element stays for its leave (the account card's four panels, "Jak přiřazujeme platby?", "Jak nastavit trvalý příkaz", the device, passkey and bank account confirmations and "Starší"; review R29b).
- `live-notice/`: `<app-live-notice [notice]>` takes a `Notice` (`{ kind: 'status' | 'alert', text }` or `null`) and keeps a `role="status"` and a `role="alert"` region rendered, showing the text as `.ui-notice--status` or `--alert` in the matching one, so a screen reader announces it; every admin page's action line uses it. The line fades in (`motion.appear($rises: false)` on the inner `<p>`, opacity alone through its own keyframes, so no distance is overridden for what it holds; no room is reserved, because the line is created with its text and a reserve on the empty region would leave a gap under every page's intro, so what follows still moves down and each page's group decides its reserve, P28), while both regions stay in the DOM, so VoiceOver does not announce a region again; a shown line whose text changes keeps its element and does not fade again, and the line never takes the focus (#1217). `look="note"` shows the same text as a `.ui-group-note--status` or `--alert` line under a group of rows instead of a block (the donor's Upozornění card), `look="footer"` as a `.ui-card__footer--status` or `--alert` footer line of a card (the alerts page).
- `copy-button/`: `<app-copy-button [value] what="číslo účtu">`: "Kopírovat" plus the hidden `what` (an optional `label` replaces both with a visible label of its own, as "Zkopírovat zprávu pro technika"); shows "Zkopírováno" for 2 s and says it once in a `role="status"` line (an optional `copiedStatus` gives that line a sentence of its own); the button's accessible name never changes: the label and an `aria-hidden` "Zkopírováno" (`content: attr(data-label) / ''`) stand in one grid cell, so the pill keeps the width of the longer wording, a longer label of its own too (the pill never grows past its row: at the largest text the reserve may wrap instead), and the two cross-fade through `motion.state(opacity)`; a visible sentence when the browser refuses (`failedStatus` for one of its own), under the button aligned to the right (`align="start"`: to the left). Its look is the global `.ui-pill-button` (`--done` while it shows "Zkopírováno"), which `share-button` shares, or with `look="tinted"` the small tinted `.ui-button--small` for a row whose other actions are small tinted buttons (the attention list of `/sprava`, #1560, and the backup key's row beside „Stáhnout jako soubor“ on `/sprava/zalohy` and `/prvni-nastaveni`), which swaps its faces the same way (`copy-button--copied`) without the pill's green outline; the pill changes colour through `motion.state(background-color, border-color, color)` and reaches a finger on a touch screen by itself.
- `share-button/`: `<app-share-button [pageTitle] [url]>` (v3 2.26a): where the browser has the Web Share API and `navigator.canShare({ title, url })` does not refuse (or is missing), one "Sdílet" pill with a share icon that calls `navigator.share({ title, url })`, ignoring presses while the share sheet is open (`aria-disabled`); the visitor closing the sheet (`AbortError`) says nothing, any other refusal shows the `role="alert"` line "Sdílení se nepodařilo. Odkaz na stránku si můžete zkopírovat." with the copy button beside it, both kept until a share succeeds (so trying again and closing the sheet keeps the offer); each refusal renders the alert as a new element (an `@for` keyed by the count of refusals), so a screen reader hears a second refusal too, even one the browser answers at once. One `<app-copy-button>` serves both the fallback and the offer after a refusal. Without the API, or when `canShare` refuses, only `<app-copy-button>` "Kopírovat odkaz", whose status line says "Odkaz je zkopírovaný, můžete ho vložit do zprávy." and, when the browser refuses to copy, "Zkopírovat se nepodařilo. Odkaz najdete v adresním řádku prohlížeče." (the link is not written on the page), aligned to the left. Nothing loads from elsewhere and the backend is not asked. In a list of articles (v3 2.26) `[subject]` names the article: the Sdílet button and the copy button carry it as visually hidden text after their label ("Sdílet Zahrada pro děti"; the copy button's `labelDetail`), so every button of a feed has a name of its own, and `tone="dark"` (host class `is-on-dark`, also on `copy-button` as `tone`) puts its status and refusal lines in the on-image colour over a photo. Texts under `ui.share.*`; spec `share-button.spec.ts`.
- `skeleton/` (#1216): `loadingView(state, isLoading, showsAtOnce)` in a page's class holds its load state for the template: `state()` is the page's own state, except that once a skeleton has shown it keeps the loading value until the skeleton has stood `--duration-loading-min` (0.4 s) from the moment it showed, and only before what follows it: the content or a failure alike waits out that minimum and then appears (`appears()`), so a failure after a shown skeleton comes in as content does instead of cutting it short (E2 applies the minimum whenever the skeleton really showed, #1655); only a state the page names in the optional third argument `showsAtOnce` shows at once, never held and without the appearance (today only the payment panel's failure, which draws over its own held skeleton, #1373); `skeleton()` turns true only after `--delay-loading` (0.3 s) of loading, through a timer that reads both tokens from the theme (`LOADING_TIMINGS`; never `animation-delay`, which the reduced-motion rule zeroes, and without the tokens there is no skeleton), cancelled when the page is left; `busy()` is true from the start of a load until the content shows, for `aria-busy` on a card already shown around an inset skeleton; `appears()` is true for content that follows a shown skeleton, which takes `.ui-skeleton-reveal` (one opacity-only `motion.appear` for the block), and turns false `--duration-slow` after it showed, so content drawn again later (a list that becomes a table when an iPad turns) does not fade again and never when the data came within the delay. Offline the loading ends at once, without the hold. `<app-skeleton [rows] [inset] [heading]>` draws the `ui-skeleton` block `aria-hidden="true"`: a `.ui-card` with a title bar and `rows` lines, or only the lines inside a card already shown (`inset`), or, given the `heading` of a card known before its data, that card with its real title over inset lines, the look of a card already shown around an inset skeleton, so every card of the administration loads as its title over skeleton rows (#1557: Zálohy's four cards, Aktuality and Příběhy pass their titles; Platby and the Dary a dárci card of `/sprava` take inset lines under their shown title instead of a plain „Načítáme…“ sentence; a card whose title comes with its data keeps the bar). The title is an `h2.ui-card__title` like the card's own, which a screen reader reaches (the VoiceOver headings rotor lists the cards while they load), so only the rows are `aria-hidden` then and the host is not (#1902); the input is `heading`, never `title`, because a static `title` on the element would also be written as its HTML `title` attribute, where `portal-no-break-spaces` refuses a no-break space, and the skeleton's title carries the same no-break spaces as the card title so both wrap alike at large text (#1908: „Klíč k&nbsp;zálohám“, the card's own id `@@admin.backups.key.title`); `.ui-card--padded` drops the inset padding; a page draws one per main card its content shows (Zálohy four, Stav serveru four inside its `.cards`), so nothing grows at the swap (Q17); it fades in over `--duration-base` (`motion.state(opacity)` from `@starting-style`, a short fade under reduced motion), only its outer block: a titled card fades as `.ui-skeleton-card` and its inset rows inside it do not fade a second time (#1902). The page keeps its loading sentence as the `role="status"` line, visually hidden, so a reader hears today's text once and nothing after loading. Used today on Příběhy and Řazení příběhů, Aktuality, Logy (both cards), Stav serveru, Soubory logu, Úrovně logování, Odeslaný přehled, Zálohy, Historie záloh, Akce správců, Platby and the Dary a dárci card of `/sprava`, each failure block there taking `.ui-skeleton-reveal` from `appears()` too; a card whose skeleton showed its real title takes `.ui-skeleton-reveal-under-title` instead (on the card, or on a component whose root is the card), so only what is under its title fades in and the title, already shown by the skeleton, stays still (Zálohy, Aktuality, Příběhy); never over data from the device copy or over content already shown.
- `load-failure/` (#1590, #1628, #1655): one failure pattern for data a page could not load. `.ui-notice--action` (`_notice.scss`, the block of the Aktuality and Pomáháme lists: a flex row that wraps, `--space-5`/`--space-6` padding, `--radius-card`, a 1 px transparent border, the text first and its action on the right inside the block, a `ui-cta` in it at its full 52 px) carries it; `.ui-load-state--error` of the administration's failures not moved yet takes the same block in the danger colours (inset in a card as a block with its margin), its `role="alert"` on the message alone, never on the block with its button. Below `phone` width the danger block (`.ui-notice--alert.ui-notice--action`) pads by `--space-4` instead of `--space-5`/`--space-6`, so the sentence keeps the width of the phone (#1902). A ghost CTA in it draws its border in `--color-danger-control-border`, the page border in the default light look and `--color-danger-border` in the dark appearance, at least 3:1 against `--color-danger-bg` wherever `check-themes.mjs` measures it (every appearance but the default light look, as for the field border). With more contrast in the dark appearance it takes `--color-border-field` instead, as the control theme does under more contrast, so the edge rises with the rest (6.81:1). The administration's `[compact]` `ui-button` in the same block takes the token only in the dark appearance (#1924), where its fill `--color-accent-soft` sits at 1.22:1 on `--color-danger-bg`; in the light appearance it keeps its transparent border, so the default look stays as it was. The danger block (`.ui-notice--alert.ui-notice--action`, `.ui-load-state--error`) draws its border in `--color-danger-border`, so its edge shows in the dark appearance, where `--color-danger-bg` is close to the page, and in forced colours. `<app-load-failure [retrying] [compact] (retry)>` draws it with the message as the only `role="alert"` (a `p.ui-notice`, so the button is not read as part of the alert) and „Zkusit znovu“ beside it (`ui-cta ui-cta--ghost`, or the administration's `ui-button` with `compact`), with `appNeedsConnection`, `[appBusy]` and `app-busy-label` „Načítáme…“ under its own `BusyAnnouncement` (the button keeps its name „Zkusit znovu“ with `aria-busy="true"`, and a visually hidden `role="status"` in the block says „Načítáme…“ once after `--delay-loading`): a retry keeps the failure and its focused, busy button until the load settles (the page keeps its `error` state and a `retrying` signal while it asks again, so no skeleton or loading line replaces the block and the focus is never lost), and the button stays busy at least `--delay-loading` plus `--duration-loading-min` from the press, so a refusal that comes at once still shows the retry was tried. Every press first keeps the focus on the pressed button (`PageFocus.keepOnPressed`, with `preventScroll`), because iOS WebKit gives a tapped button no focus and hands it to the nearest focusable ancestor, `main#obsah`, so VoiceOver would read the landmark instead of „Načítáme…“ and `keepNear` would find the focus taken and leave it there after the load. It runs from a capture `click` listener on the component's host, not from the button's `(click)`, because `BusyButton` and `NeedsConnection` swallow a press while busy or offline in their own capture listeners on the button, which run after the host's, so a second press during „Načítáme…“ or a press offline keeps the focus on the button too. Each new failure renders the alert again (`@for` over a failure count, as `share-button` re-renders its refusal), so a screen reader reads it again while the focus stays on the same button. When content replaces the block, `PageFocus.keepNear` moves the focus, only if nothing else took it (the focus on `body`, or on the sheet's own `<dialog>`), and with `preventScroll`, to the nearest place that stays shown, walking the ancestors of the block outwards: at each one first its `h1[tabindex="-1"]`, then the ancestor itself when it takes focus (`tabindex="-1"`, such as the passkeys region, so the focus stays beside the reloaded list in the account sheet too), and only when the walk reaches the sheet's `<dialog>` its title `h2[tabindex="-1"]` (`#account-section-title`) or the dialog; the heading comes before its ancestor because `main#obsah` takes focus itself and holds the page `h1`. The retry label wraps under the text instead of overflowing the block at the largest text. Used by the portal's gifts failure (Můj přehled, Moje dary, Potvrzení: `DonorDonations.retry()`), Bankovní účty, the passkeys list of Údaje a zabezpečení (whose row then says „Nepodařilo se načíst“ instead of „Načítáme…“ for good, through the panel's `countUnknown`), Zálohy, Platby, and with `compact` and a `retrying` signal of their own Akce správců, Historie záloh, the import, backups and Dary po měsících cards of `/sprava` (`importRetrying`, `backupsRetrying`, `overviewRetrying`), both cards of Logy (`healthRetrying`, `filesRetrying`), Soubory logu, Úrovně logování, Stav serveru, Aktuality, Příběhy, Řazení příběhů, Odeslaný přehled, Správci, Upozornění, Fotka v sekci O nás, Fotka v pruhu Přispět, the search of Dárci (a retry sends the same query again without the „Hledáme dárce…“ line), the account and gifts failures of the donor card (`accountRetrying`, `donationsRetrying`), Od minulé návštěvy of `/sprava` (which keeps the failure shown while the visit is checked again), Historie vydání, Výpis vláken, Varování a chyby dnes, the news and story editors, Nastavení and Hromadná potvrzení (`loadRetrying`, beside the run's own retry), and the article detail (`/aktuality/:slug`, `/projekty/:slug`), which shows it under an `h1` of its own („Aktualita se nenačetla“, „Příběh se nenačetl“) (#1902; a failure inset in a card takes `.ui-load-state--inset` on the component, whose padding stands for the old block's margin). Still on `.ui-load-state--error`: the log reader, Lidé fondu, Měsíční e-mail, Bankovní loga and the variable symbol claims (moved after their open PRs), the donor card's bank accounts, whose retry reads the copy first and moves the focus to the accounts card with its own status line, the settings' wait after a restart, whose retry starts the restart watch with its own spinner, and the blocks without a retry („Takový dárce není.“ with its link back, the donor card's missing gifts record (`@@admin.donorDetail.donations.missing`), the alerts' missing key). A failure sentence says only what failed („Aktualitu se nepodařilo načíst.“), never „Zkuste to znovu.“ above „Zkusit znovu“ (#1800). The floating notice (`.ui-notice--floating`) is at most `--measure-prose` wide, centred at the bottom (#1599), `--space-4` above the bottom safe area where no tab bar stands under it (the installed app on a device with a home indicator, #1902), and the room the page keeps for it under its content and its focused elements (`.has-floating-notice`: `padding-bottom` on `body` and `scroll-padding-bottom`) adds the same safe area, so the notice never covers the end of the page or its last link. Alignment (#1602): a card keeps one alignment, so the empty Moje dary and the sign-in and registration sheet (`.sub`, `.consent`, `.recovery-offer`) start their text at the leading edge with the fields and boxes around them.
- `busy-button/` (#1218): `button[appBusy]="running"` switches a button off while its request runs with `aria-disabled="true"` instead of `disabled`, so the focus stays on it; while busy it ignores click, tap, Enter, Space and the submission of its form (a submit button). With `appNeedsConnection` on the same button the two add up: busy or offline gives `aria-disabled="true"`, written only by `NeedsConnection` (`alsoUnavailableWhile`, which `BusyButton` hands its state), so neither clears what the other still needs; the offline hint stays `NeedsConnection`'s alone. It goes only on buttons, never on fields; the handler itself still refuses a second run while busy; the button looks like `:disabled` (`.ui-button`, `GrayText` under `forced-colors`), moves into it through `motion.state(opacity)`, has no pressed state (`pressed.when-pressed`) and no hover (`motion.hover` skips `[aria-disabled='true']`), keeps its size, and says why with its existing text ("Ukládáme…", or the "Hledáme…" line it names with `aria-describedby`). Today on "Uložit a restartovat" in Nastavení, "Uložit nastavení" in Přehled e-mailem and "Hledat" in a log file; the other `[disabled]` buttons move page by page.
- `busy-label/` (#1250, R65, R66): `<app-busy-label [busy]>` around a `[when-idle]` and a `[when-busy]` label inside a button: both stand in one grid cell, centred; the busy label shows only after `--delay-loading` (read from the theme; at once without the token) and the resting one returns at once; the coming label fades in by `motion.state(opacity)` (Motion) and the leaving one is gone at once; the busy label is always `aria-hidden`, so the accessible name stays the resting label (explicit exception from D1/#1250, Label in Name), and the label sets `aria-busy="true"` on its button while `busy`; a card that provides `BusyAnnouncement` shows the shown busy label's text, visually hidden, first in its `role="status"` line until the result replaces it. The label wraps between words within the button at enlarged text, and where the row has no room for the longer label the unseen one gives way (it may break anywhere and takes no height), so the button stays within its row. With `BusyAnnouncement` today on the busy buttons of `/darce/udaje` (Přihlášená zařízení, Účet, Přístupové klíče, Připojené aplikace), each card with its status line; the administration uses the fallback; without a `BusyAnnouncement` the busy label is the accessible name after the delay and no `aria-busy` is set (R69); `busy-label/testing/accessible-text.ts` reads a control's text without its `aria-hidden` parts, as its accessible name.
- `qr-code/`: `<app-qr-code [matrix] [variant] label>` draws a QR code from a module matrix (`qr-matrix.ts`: `QrMatrix`, `isDark`, `alignmentCentres`), the browser's own from `qr-encoder.ts` for every QR code (since 1.4 the backend sends none), as inline SVG with a 4-module quiet zone, `role="img"` named by `label`. `branded` (default, the payment look chosen by Jakub on 2026-10-23): round dots in `--color-qr-module`, rounded finder squares and alignment marks in `--color-qr-mark`, and over the centre a blue disc with the logo's star (`--color-logo`, `--color-logo-fill`; the matrix must be level H); when the matrix changes it morphs in a wave from the star outwards (kept dots pulse, gone ones shrink, new ones grow in the mark colour and settle to the module colour, ~2.6 s, Web Animations API, nothing under `prefers-reduced-motion`). `plain`: black squares, used for the authenticator link. `pngDataUrl(sidePx)` paints a copy with the token colours and returns a PNG made on a canvas. `qr-encoder.ts` is the browser's own QR encoder (no library), bit for bit the symbol ZXing 3.5.4 drew on the backend with the ISO-8859-1 hint (the backend checked it until 1.10 retired ZXing; the shared cases keep what it drew): `encodeText(text, level)` picks the mode as ZXing does (only digits numeric, the 45 alphanumeric characters alphanumeric, anything else ISO-8859-1 bytes behind the ECI 1 header; a character outside ISO-8859-1 is refused instead of written as `?`), takes the smallest version that holds the text at the level asked for (none above 40: refused), and keeps the first of the eight masks with the lowest ZXing penalty (rule 3 counts a finder-like run only when four light modules inside the symbol lie on one side; the edge is not light); `encodeSegments(segments, level)` takes segments built by hand (`byteSegment`, `numericSegment`, `alphanumericSegment`, `eciSegment`), for the verification link of a receipt (its prefix in bytes, its payload in digits, the level chosen by the receipt). It returns the matrix with its version, level and mask; about 3 ms for an authenticator link (version 9), about 40 ms at version 40. A text over 7089 characters, more than any QR code holds, is refused before any work. Besides the payment and authenticator cases it runs `shared-cases/qr/encoder.json`, 112 symbols ZXing encoded at every level, at versions 1 to 40 and in every mode, which the backend runs against ZXing too.
- `sheet-head/`: `<app-sheet-head [title] [titleId] (backPressed) (closePressed)>`, the head of a page shown in a tall sheet (an account section and, on phones, the administration, #989): a sticky grid of a round Zpět, the title in the middle and a round Zavřít, in the look of the sheet's × (`--sheet-close-size`, `--color-sheet-close`, a `ButtonText` ring in forced colours, as the sheet's × has), `--sheet-close-inset` from the top, its leading button beside `--window-controls-inset-inline`, its own rounded top corners on `--color-sheet-grouped`; the title stays on one line like an iOS navigation title, cut with an ellipsis, at `--text-sheet-head` (the sheet's body size capped by the viewport's width), so a long donor name never grows the head; with `titleId` the title is that `h2` (the account section, focused on open), without it an `aria-hidden` line beside a page that keeps its own `h1`; the host takes `--sheet-head-top`, where it sticks (the account sheet sets minus the tall panel's top padding, the administration the sheet's top edge).
- `side-nav/`: `<app-side-nav [groups] label [navId] [panel]>` renders an area's two-column layout: a sticky panel left (`--side-nav-width` 240 px, below the top safe area; from `nav` the whole layout takes the header's measure, at most `--content-max` and from `wide` `--content-max-wide`, with the header's `--space-8` at the start (not its `--window-controls-inset-inline`) and at the end `--space-8` less the `--space-6` every page keeps inside (`.ui-page`, the donor area's `.container`, which has no width cap of its own since its paragraphs carry `--measure-prose`, #1221: its cards span the column the layout caps, 872 px at 1280 to 1599 px, an iPad Pro on its side included, and up to 1 112 px from `wide`), so the content ends on the avatar's right edge as the logo starts on the left one, and the items' text starts on the logo's left edge, the active item's `--color-side-nav-active` background reaching left by the link's padding; under `forced-colors` the active item keeps a `Highlight` start border) with a `nav` (`aria-label` = `label`) of `SideNavGroup`s (`{ id, label | null, items: SideNavItem[] }`; a titled group's `ul` is `aria-labelledby` its title, an untitled one has no label) of `SideNavItem` links (`{ path, label, exact?, badge? }`, `routerLinkActive` with `aria-current="page"`, prefix match unless `exact`; a `badge` `{ count, description }` with a count above 0 adds an accent pill with the count (`aria-hidden`) and the `description` as visually hidden text, so the area passes `groups` as a signal-computed value when the count changes), and the projected content (`<ng-content />`, a `router-outlet`) right. With `[panel]="false"` (an area shell passes `!CompactLayout.isCompact()`) only the projected content stays, in the same `main#obsah`, so a page is not made again when the width crosses `nav`; the panel holds only links and no menu button (since 2.24 phones reach the sections from the account sheet in the donor area and from the overview's rows in the administration).
- `radio-group/`: `[appRadioGroup]` on a group of `role="radio"` buttons makes it a `radiogroup` that answers the arrow keys, Home and End by focusing and clicking the next choice; the buttons bind `[tabindex]` 0 for the chosen one and -1 for the rest (sign-in dialog: donor type and sign-in method; password reset: method).
- `markdown/`: `parseMarkdown(text)` (`markdown-parser.ts`) turns a news or project body into blocks (paragraph with line breaks, heading level 2 or 3, list, quote) of inline nodes (text, bold, italic, link, break), the subset in `docs/api-contract.md` ("`content` — public CMS"); a link keeps only an `http(s)`, `mailto:` or site-relative address, everything else, HTML included, stays text. `<app-markdown [text]>` renders the tree with its own elements through a recursive `ng-template` and `{{ }}` bindings (no `innerHTML`), in the prose look (70 ch, serif headings); a plain click on a link to a page of the site (site-relative or the site's own origin, not under `/api/` and not a file with an extension) opens it through the router, so it adds no browser entry (own history, #431), and every other link, or one clicked with a modifier key, is followed by the browser. `<app-markdown-toolbar [target]>` above a textarea: the `role="group"` "Formátování textu" with Tučně, Kurzíva, Nadpis, Odrážky, Číslování and Odkaz, each wrapping the selection or prefixing its lines (a placeholder word selected when nothing was), then focusing the textarea and dispatching `input` so `ngModel` follows; the field's hint says how to format and that the editor's "Náhled" shows the look (the body-only preview under the textarea went with the editors' whole preview, Jakub 2026-10-24). Texts under `ui.markdown.*`; specs beside each.
- `file-button/`: `<app-file-button inputId [accept] [describedBy] [disabled] [look] (chosen)>` is a file choice: a visually hidden `input type=file` and its label with the projected text, `<label class="ui-button">` for `look="button"` (the default) or `<label class="ui-list__row ui-list__row--action">` for `look="row"` (the input draws no ring of its own: keyboard focus on it draws the site's ring on the label, outside a button and inside a row, `Highlight` in forced colours, so the 1 px input never leaves a fragment of a ring clipped by its card, #1581; `e2e/file-field-focus.spec.ts`); `chosen` emits the `File` and the input is reset, so the same file can be chosen again (buttons: the photo in the news and project editors, the O nás photo, the photo of the Přispět band, a person's portrait and signature scan in Lidé fondu; a row: the receipt file on `/overit` and `/sprava/overit`).
- `pdf-button-face/` and `pdf-progress/` (design P1, #823): the button and the line of a receipt PDF. `<app-pdf-button-face [state] downloadLabel preparingLabel openLabel>` goes inside the page's own button, which keeps its look and its `aria-busy` and `aria-disabled` (no longer dimmed while busy): one layer per state, all four in the same grid cell, each its icon (hidden from screen readers: download, an indeterminate ring in `currentColor` with a 25 % track that turns through `motion.spin` while it is the current layer, a share icon for `ready` that grows in through `motion.done`, an exclamation in `--color-danger` for `failed`, `currentColor` on a primary button and under `forced-colors`; sized `1.15em`, so it grows with the text) and its label ("Zkusit znovu" for `failed`) centred as a pair with the gap `--space-1-5`; the current layer (`pdf-button__layer--current`) shows and the others are `aria-hidden` and `visibility: hidden`, their labels empty boxes drawing their text from `data-label`, and the layers cross-fade in `--duration-base` (`motion.state(opacity, visibility)`), so the button keeps the width of its longest label, no icon moves within its layer, and its text and accessible name hold only the shown one; the face stands on the top of its line (`vertical-align: top`), so a button that holds it is as tall as a block as in a flex row (the overview's yearly receipt and the one of Moje dary, #1603). `<app-pdf-progress [progress] [compact] [reservesCancel] (cancelled)>` (its host carries `pdf-progress--idle` while it shows neither text nor "Zrušit"; `reservesCancel` keeps the line as tall as "Zrušit" also while it is empty, so a row that holds it never grows when "Zrušit" appears) shows a `PdfProgressState` (`running` with its phase text, or `ready`, `done`, `cancelled` with the whole sentence and a `brief`) in an always rendered, one line high `role="status"` line: a phase only once it has lasted `PHASE_SHOWN_AFTER_MS` (300 ms), an end at once, as a check and the brief drawn from `data-brief` (hidden from screen readers, so the status says the whole sentence, kept visually hidden), the success colour for `ready` and `done`; "Zrušit" from 8 s on (`PDF_CANCEL_OFFERED_AFTER_MS`) beside it, outside the status.
- `download-progress/` (the bulk yearly receipts only): `<app-download-progress [progress] [compact] (cancelled)>` shows a `DownloadProgressState` (`running` with `step` of `steps`, its text and an optional `detail`, `ready`, `done`, `cancelled`) in an always rendered `role="status"` line (its own typography: `--text-sm`, `--text-xs` when `compact` as in the payments table, neutral while running, ready and cancelled, the success colour once done, `overflow-wrap: anywhere`; the caller sets the alignment on the host), a 12 rem `<progress>` (at most the width it gets, named by the text, with the `detail` as its `aria-valuetext` and shown beside it, hidden from the accessibility tree, so a detail that changes often, such as the page, is not announced) while running and "Zrušit" once it has run for 8 s (`CANCEL_OFFERED_AFTER_MS`); it has no button of its own for `ready`, because the button that started the download opens the ready file.
- `diagnostics-download/`: `<app-diagnostics-download source>` (`account-page` or `receipt-pdf`) says to attach the diagnostic file to a message about trouble with the site, that it holds only the state of the site on this device and goes nowhere by itself, links `mailto:info@nfnn.cz?subject=Diagnostický soubor` (the subject only; under a receipt PDF failure, `source="receipt-pdf"` (`underReceipt`), the lead is "K e‑mailu nám prosím přiložte diagnostický soubor. …" without the link and the saved and ready-to-share lines leave out the address, because the failure line right above names it), in a `PUBLIC` session asks to delete the file after sending it, and one button (works offline; `aria-disabled` while busy, so it keeps the focus) prepares the file through `Diagnostics.prepare` ("Připravujeme soubor…") and saves it (`save`, then a `role="status"` line "Soubor <name> je ve stažených souborech. Přiložte ho prosím k e‑mailu na info@nfnn.cz.") or, where `needsSecondTap` says so (an iPhone or iPad that can share the file, as `PdfDelivery` decides for the receipt), becomes "Sdílet diagnostický soubor" and opens the share sheet at the second tap (a closed sheet keeps it ready, a failed share saves it); a failure is an alert that asks to write without it.
- `focal-point/` (slice 2.23b): `FocalPoint` (`{x, y}` in whole percent), `PHOTO_SHAPES` (each shape the site crops a content photo into, with its ratio and the position it used before a point existed: `newsCard`, `projectCard`, `articleTop`, and since 2.26 `newsFeatured` (12:5), `newsFeaturedMedium` (3:2) and `newsFeaturedTall` (4:5) for the leading news of Aktuality as wide, middle and upright as the card stands on a computer, a tablet and a phone, `newsCardSide` (1:1) for an older news card with its photo beside the text `storyTile` for a story of the year and `storySplit` (3:2) for the only story of a year on a computer; 2.23a adds the wide band and the square), `objectPosition(point, shape)` and the directive `img[appFocalPoint][focalShape]`, which binds the image's `object-position`; the news card, the project card and the article top use it, so a photo without a point keeps its earlier crop.
- `quote-mark/` (#1618): `<app-quote-mark>`, the one drawn quotation mark of the site (an `aria-hidden` SVG filled with `currentColor`, `--space-12` wide), used by the founders' note of O nás and the quote card of Přispět; the page sets its colour and space.
- `avatar/`: `<app-avatar [name] [src] [size] [srcset] [sizes] [loading]>` is the one place that draws a person's round picture: the portrait (`alt=""`, `width`/`height` = `size`, cover-cropped to a circle) or, without one, the initials of the first two words of `name` in capitals (Jakub 2026-10-31: "ZZ" for Zdeňka Znamenáčková); the whole host is `aria-hidden`, because the name always stands beside it. The parent's class sets its width, height and the `font-size` token of the initials; the theme gives the circle, the light surface behind it and the serif. Used by the home "Kdo fond vede" block and `/sprava/lide-fondu` (the list and the portrait preview).
- `dialog/`: `<app-dialog labelledBy [wide] [tall] [grouped] [presentation] [pushesHistory] [dismissible] [depth] [showsClose] (dismissed) (steppedBack)>` (`[depth]` above 0 keeps that many steps of the dialog's own in the history through `DialogHistory.showStep`, so Back, the app's edge swipe and `history.back()` first emit `steppedBack` and leave the dialog open, and a page that keeps its depth after `steppedBack` gets its step pushed again; `[showsClose]="false"` hides the × while a step is shown) (`[dismissible]="false"` while a step runs: Esc is refused, × is disabled, the backdrop and Back do nothing, and a close the browser forces anyway opens it again) on a native `<dialog>` opened with `showModal()` (focus kept inside, page inert, focus to the `autofocus` control; opened by a finger or a mouse, its title takes the focus (the element `labelledBy` names, given `tabindex="-1"`, no ring, `preventScroll`; the dialog itself when the title is not inside it), whatever control asks for `autofocus`, because a ring after a tap is a bug (Jakub 2026-11-06, design plan J3), Safari on the iPhone would otherwise ring the × after every tap and, focusing it while the sheet still slides in, scroll the dialog so that the sheet stopped short of the screen's bottom edge, and opened by the keyboard the browser focuses the `autofocus` control or its first control as before (`core/input/input-modality.ts`, `InputModality.lastPressByPointer()`, listening from the app initializer `inputModalityAtStart`); when the focus moves from a control inside it onto the `<dialog>` itself, it goes back to that control (`focus({ preventScroll: true })`), because Safari on the iPhone gives an activated `<button>` no focus and, at the press, moves it onto the dialog (`tabindex="-1"`), so VoiceOver left the pressed „Přihlásit se“ for the dialog's first control, the × (#1504, seen on the simulator); it does not when the focus comes from outside the dialog (opening it), when the dialog focuses itself (its title missing, or leaving), when the control is gone, disabled or inert, or when the press began outside that control (a tap on the dialog's empty part blurs a field, as everywhere), a press that turned into a gesture being forgotten (`pointercancel`, the sheet moving under the finger, or the edge swipe holding it; never at `pointerup`, which on iOS comes before the emulated `mousedown`); a sheet's dialog clips instead of hiding its overflow, so nothing can scroll it; at `bp.down(nav)` every sheet's × is a round button of `--sheet-close-size` (the size of the header's Zpět) on `--color-sheet-close`, `--sheet-close-inset` from the top and the right edge, with an X icon in place of the × glyph, and a `[tall]` sheet (the account sheet, after the Music app's account sheet on iOS, Jakub 2026-11-06) fills the screen up to `--sheet-top-gap` under the top safe area whatever its content and starts its content on the row of the ×; a `[grouped]` dialog lays its panel on `--color-sheet-grouped`, so its `.ui-list--grouped` lists stand as inset cards with `--sheet-group-gap` between them (the account sheet, after the Music app's account sheet on iOS in the style of NFNN, Jakub 2026-11-06: the logo as its heading, named Účet, on the row of the round ×, only on a phone: from `nav` the header right above the dialog already shows the logo, so the `h2` "Účet" is visually hidden and alone names the dialog and its `nav`, and the card of the donor stands one `--sheet-group-gap` under the row of the round controls (`CompactLayout`, desktop team 2026-11-16); a card of the donor (portrait or initials, name, Údaje a zabezpečení) that leads to Údaje a zabezpečení; the other sections as one card without icons, the variable symbol as a note under it, Správa for an administrator, Přidat na plochu as a blue action and Odhlásit se as a centred red row, each its own card; the foundation's line under them as quiet `--color-sheet-secondary` text; every measure a `--sheet-*` token taken from the Music app: 40 px round ×, 18 px side inset, 47 px rows in the body size, 24 px cards 32 px apart, no visible grabber on a `[tall]` sheet); Esc, × and backdrop emit `dismissed`; focus returns to the opener on destroy; full-screen up to 480 px). `presentation` (`dialog-presentation.ts`, plan v3 2.22) is `auto` (the default), `centered`, `sheet` or `full`, written to `data-presentation`: `auto` takes `DIALOG_PRESENTATION`, an injection token that an area shell may provide and that is `centered` (today's dialog) unless one does, so no dialog changes its look until the app shells of 2.23 and 2.24 provide `sheet`; at `bp.down(nav)` a sheet is anchored to the bottom (`--sheet-max-width`, at most the screen height less `--space-12`, its own scroll, top corners `--radius-sheet`, `--shadow-sheet`, the bottom padding above the safe area, a grabber with `aria-hidden` across the top of the sheet (`--space-8` high, the visible bar its `::before`), which a primary pointer drags down (2.24, `sheet-drag.ts`, `SheetDrag`: the panel follows the pointer through the CSS `translate` property, and closes like × once it moved at least the larger of 72 px and a quarter of its height, or on a flick of 0.5 px/ms after 24 px, otherwise it springs back over `--duration-sheet` on `--ease-sheet`; a released sheet starts at the finger's speed, whether it goes or springs back (`SheetDrag.settleEasing`, the curve `easingLeavingAt` of the back swipe, set on the panel as its `transition-timing-function` until that `translate` transition ends; its duration is the theme's `--duration-sheet` on `:root`, never the panel's own transition, which the dragging class still turns off at that moment, and its way is what is left to the window's bottom edge when it goes, so the centred form sheet's longer way counts, or the way it was pulled when it springs back; a finger at rest or moving the other way keeps `--ease-sheet`); a finger still moving up as it lets go has changed its mind, and the sheet springs back however far it went (#382)); a finger also pulls the sheet by its content as on iOS (#382, below `nav` only, touch events because Safari cancels pointer events once it scrolls): `canPullSheetFrom` lets a touch pull only when the panel and every element between it and the touched one are scrolled to their top, no text is selected, and it did not start on the focused text field, a `textarea`, `select`, slider, `canvas`, `contenteditable` or `app-zoomable`, which keep their own gestures, and `pullIntent` decides after 10 px: mostly down (more than 1.2 times sideways) pulls the sheet, anything else (up, sideways, or a finger that rested 300 ms first) is left to the content for the rest of that touch, a second finger springs the sheet back, and a pulling touch calls `preventDefault`, so the content neither scrolls nor bounces and the keyboard directive below sees no scroll (an open keyboard stays until the sheet closes, the experts' choice for #382, so a sheet that springs back keeps its field and caret); the backdrop's veil (its `background-color`) and its blur fade with the share of the sheet pulled away (`--dialog-pull`, also set from `[pulledAway]` by the zoom viewer); below `nav` the blur sits on the sheet's `::backdrop` and fades by that layer's opacity, since the radius of `backdrop-filter` never animates; every close of a sheet below `nav` (a release far enough or a flick, ×, Esc, the backdrop, Back, the home-screen app's swipe, `history.back()`) slides it out as a native iOS sheet does: the dialog takes `dialog--leaving`, the panel goes from where it is (where the finger left it, or its resting place) to `translate: 0 var(--dialog-sheet-away)` (`100%`, below the screen's edge) over `--duration-sheet` with `--ease-sheet` while the veil and blur fade out with it, the panel is inert meanwhile (so a second press, a backdrop tap or a new pull does nothing) and the dialog itself holds the focus (no ring), and only when its leaving transitions (the panel's `translate`, the dialog's `opacity`) have finished (`getAnimations({ subtree: true })` read after the render that starts them, since the dragging class turns transitions off until then; at the latest the longest duration plus 0.3 s, so a transition the browser never runs, as in a hidden tab, cannot hold the dialog) it emits `dismissed`, so the page removes it, the history entry goes back as before and the focus returns to the opener on destroy (while the modal is open the page under it is inert and cannot take the focus); Esc is kept from hiding the dialog at once (`cancel` prevented while it may be), and a close the browser forces during the slide ends it at once; Back during the slide adds no entry; a page that keeps it open gets it back in place, and after a Back asks for its entry again; every other dialog, of every type and at every width, leaves by the motion system's Dialog (`motion.present`, below: the dialog fades out over `--duration-base` with `--ease-in` and a floating panel shrinks back to `--motion-scale-enter`), with the same inert panel, focus on the dialog, safety end, Back and Esc, and under `prefers-reduced-motion` every dialog closes at once (#1391, #1401); only a sheet's leaving takes `--dialog-pull` to 1, so the photo viewer's dark ground fades with the dialog, and the viewer's own swipe down closes it by itself without passing through the dialog's close; a dialog that is not dismissible, and one whose page sets `[unsavedInput]` (an opt-in for input that took effort and is kept nowhere else; no dialog sets it yet, since the registration keeps its draft and the other sheets hold a code or a tick), only stretches under the finger with growing resistance (`rubberBand`, at most 96 px) and springs back, as iOS does under `isModalInPresentation`; a release of the second kind that would have closed it opens a nested sheet `role="alertdialog"` "Zahodit rozepsané údaje?" with "Pokračovat v psaní" (focused, also Esc, Back and its own pull) and "Zahodit", which closes the sheet; ×, Esc, the backdrop and Back close without asking, as on iOS, where only the gesture is guarded; the × stays the way that needs no gesture, WCAG 2.5.7), the side padding at least the side safe area, and the action row `.ui-dialog__actions` (`_dialog.scss`) sticky at the bottom of the sheet above the bottom safe area, so the buttons of a long sheet stay in reach, the × kept, WCAG 2.5.7) and `full` fills the screen; above `nav` both are the centered dialog. From `tablet` to `nav` (an iPad upright) a sheet is a form sheet instead (#1246): centred, every corner `--radius-sheet`, at most `--dialog-max-width-wide` wide and never closer than `--space-5` to either edge (`min(var(--dialog-max-width-wide), 100% - 2 * var(--space-5))`, which holds at 200 % text), as high as its content up to the screen height less `--space-12` with its own scroll, while a `[tall]` sheet (the sign-in, the account sheet) keeps the fixed height `--sheet-form-height` (iOS's form sheet, 620 pt) within that, so pushing a section of the account sheet in and out never changes its height (P20); without a grabber's bar or its `grab` cursor (neither does a `[tall]` sheet's hidden grabber offer one) and with its bottom padding and sticky action row clear of the bottom safe area, since it does not lie on the screen's edge; arriving from below the screen's edge (`--dialog-sheet-away: calc(50dvh + 50%)`); below `tablet` it stays anchored to the bottom and the sign-in stays `[tall]` on a phone. Above `phone` a floating panel is at most the window's height less the dialog's padding (`max-block-size` from `dvh`) and scrolls inside, its bottom padding included, so a long account sheet in a low window keeps Odhlásit se in reach (#1247); while more of it lies below, its bottom edge shows a shade `--scroll-edge-fade` high (`--dialog-scroll-shade`, `--scroll-shade-amount` of `--color-text`), drawn as two background layers in the panel's own ground (`--dialog-panel-ground`, the grouped ground for a `[grouped]` panel): a cover that scrolls with the content (`local`) and the shade fixed to the edge (`scroll`), so the cover hides the shade once the end is in sight, with no script. The dialog widths `--dialog-max-width` and `--dialog-max-width-wide` are in `rem`, so a dialog grows with the text size and its lines keep a readable length. A panel has a transparent 1 px outline, which forced colours paint, so its edge stays visible without its shadow. A dialog that fills a phone (the centered one up to `phone`, `full` up to `nav`) keeps the safe areas (#1249): its panel's top padding and the ×'s `top` add `--safe-area-top`, its bottom padding the bottom inset and its side padding at least the side insets, the × at least the right inset. The donor area presents its dialogs as sheets (`DIALOG_PRESENTATION` `sheet` on the route of `DonorArea`, as the administration's shell does, #1248). While the on-screen keyboard is open (`core/layout/soft-keyboard.ts`; `keyboard-avoidance.ts`, the directive `KeyboardAvoidance` on the `<dialog>`, #359) the dialog keeps the part of the screen the keyboard covers as its bottom padding (`--keyboard-covered-height`, `_dialog.scss`; the full-screen phone dialog fills it with the surface colour, a sheet sits above the keyboard), so its whole content can be scrolled above the keyboard, and the focused field is scrolled the least way into the visible part (16 px clear of its edges), together with its form's submit button when both fit, on focus and whenever the visible part changes; a one-finger drag that scrolls the dialog with the keyboard open (one that starts on a text field, uses two fingers or comes after the finger let go does not) moves the focus to the panel (`tabindex="-1"` until the focus leaves it), which closes the keyboard as a native form sheet does (#395) and keeps the focus inside the dialog. Every dialog keeps a history entry (2.22): on open it calls `DialogHistory.open` with a listener of its own, Back over that entry emits `dismissed` (a sheet after its slide), and when the page keeps the dialog open (a running restore, erasure, pairing or withdrawal refuses to close) it asks for the entry again after the next render, so the following Back still lands on the dialog and not on the previous page; on destroy it gives its own entries back with `close(listener)`, leaving those of a dialog opened over it. `[pushesHistory]="false"` is set by the two dialogs whose history is kept elsewhere: the sign-in dialog, whose `LoginDialogService` adds entries for its steps and hands its entry over to the page it was opened for, and the unsaved-changes dialog, which exists only while a navigation waits on its guard (an entry pushed then would race the router restoring the address when the visitor stays). The panel ends with a visually hidden `role="status"` region, rendered with the dialog and empty, which shows `DialogAnnouncer.message()` (`dialog-announcer.ts`, root): while a modal dialog is open the page behind it is inert and out of the accessibility tree, so a message from behind it (the chunk-load bar) is announced only from inside the open dialog; `announce(text)` returns the function that withdraws it, which leaves a later announcement standing. `dialog-history.ts` (`DialogHistory`, root service) gives every dialog a step back in the site's own history (`core/navigation/own-history.ts`; the browser's own Back leaves the site since #431): `open(listener)` pushes one entry for the same address (state key `nfnnDialogDepth`), deferred until a running navigation ends (`router.currentNavigation()` is null), so a guard-opened dialog adds it on the page it redirected to; `showStep(depth, listener?)` adds or takes back entries for further steps of that dialog (the sign-in dialog names its own, so a dialog over it keeps its entry on top); Back over its own entry calls the listener's `stepBack()` or `close()`, and a jump past the page closes the dialog and leaves the navigation to the router; `close(listener?)` takes that dialog's entries back with `history.go(-n)` (without a listener, the top dialog's), and Back tells the listeners only once the stack is settled, so a listener that closes itself on Back adds no entry; an entry that carries the depth when no dialog owns one (a reload with a dialog open, Forward onto the entry of a closed dialog) is never left as a dead Back press: `app.config.ts` starts `DialogHistory` before the first page opens (`dialogHistoryAtStart`), which takes such an entry back unseen by the router, and a dialog opened on such an entry adopts it instead of adding another; it keeps a stack of open dialogs (2.24), each with its own entries: a dialog opened over another adds its entry on top, Back closes or steps back only the top one, a lower dialog that closes by itself takes back only its own entries and leaves the one above open, a jump back past several closes them top first, and a dialog that closes while another opens in the same moment (the pairing dialog giving way to "Mezitím se to změnilo") leaves exactly the new one's entry; `LoginDialogService` passes its own listener, so the sign-in dialog leaves another dialog's entry alone; a navigation a page starts itself while a dialog is open (`sendToSignIn` after a 401) leaves the dialog's entry under the new page, one extra Back press, until such a navigation hands the entry over like the sign-in dialog does; the fold at start strips the mark from the entry before it goes back, so the router's first navigation never copies it to the page below; if that traversal never arrived, the next Back would be taken as the fold's; the tests press Back from the page (`history.back()`), so the home-screen app's swipe over the entry a refused close asks for again is on Jakub's device checklist; `handOver(url)` replaces the top entry with the page a guard-opened dialog was opened for, which the router then replaces again (the router replaces an entry whose address is already the target), so Back from that page returns to the page under the dialog; `release()` forgets the entries without traversing (a link that opens another page in their place with `replaceUrl`), and any imperative navigation forgets them too, so closing later never leaves the page. Its popstate listener sits on `window` in the capture phase and is registered when the module loads, before Angular's `Location` registers its own: Chrome runs listeners on `window` in registration order, and the dialog's own entries must never reach the router, whose same-address `NavigationSkipped` would make `public-shell` move the focus to the page heading. `testing/fake-dialog-history.ts` records the calls for specs. When the appearance changes while a dialog is open (its own button in the account sheet or the device's setting, both through `Appearance.dark()`), the dialog steps out of view for two frames (`dialog--repainting`, `visibility: hidden`) and gives the focus back to the control that had it (#1299): while a modal `<dialog>` is shown, Safari keeps the status bar in the page's background colour of the moment it opened, and so does an app added to the home screen before the site asked for `black-translucent`, so the strip under the clock stayed in the old appearance until the sheet closed; nothing else made WebKit read the colour again (the `color-scheme` and `theme-color` metas, the body's background, a scroll, a view transition, an extra invisible modal, opacity down to 0.05 or one hidden frame, measured in the iOS 27.2 simulator), and going from dark to light the page shows without the dimming for one frame.
- `needs-connection/` (plan v3 2.7): `[appNeedsConnection]` on a control that needs the server (every submit button, every button without a `type`, every `ui-button--danger`/`--danger-soft` button and every button whose `(click)` calls a method of its component that subscribes, `firstValueFrom` or `lastValueFrom`, directly or through other methods of the component, checked by `scripts/needs-connection.mjs` in `npm run lint`; its exceptions in `WORKS_WITHOUT_CONNECTION`, a whole template or `template#handler`, each with its reason and reported once it names no control (a `template#handler` entry also clears a danger button whose click calls only listed handlers): the receipt code reader of `/overit`, which checks offline, the sheet's "Zkusit znovu", which is the probe, sign-out from the account sheet, which forgets the session on the device without the server, and the two panel switches of the first setup, and the dialog that deletes the data on this device, which is local and signs out the same way): while `Connectivity.offline()` it sets `aria-disabled="true"` (keeping a value it had), the class `needs-connection-offline`, and a hint `span.ui-needs-connection` "Potřebuje připojení" after the control, named in its `aria-describedby`; the control stays focusable and its click (capture phase) and the submit of its form (capture phase at the document) are swallowed, so nothing is sent and the text typed stays.
- `zoom-viewer/` (plan v3 2.23d, Jakub 2026-10-31): `<app-zoomable [content]>` wraps a photo or a QR code in a button named "Zvětšit fotku: <description>" ("Zvětšit fotku" without one) or "Zvětšit: <QR label>" (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `cursor: zoom-in`, no other visible change) that opens `<app-zoom-viewer [content] (closed)>`: an `<app-dialog presentation="viewer">` (full screen at every width on `--color-surface-inverse`, the × and the tools in `--color-on-inverse`, safe areas kept) holding the content as `ZoomContent` (`zoom-content.ts`: `{ kind: 'photo', src, alt, width?, height? }`, the largest stored width, uncropped, or `{ kind: 'qr', matrix, label }`, drawn again by `app-qr-code`) fitted to the stage (a size container, `touch-action: none`). The viewer zooms itself, because the page cannot (Accessibility, `frontend.md`): two fingers pinch around their middle and pan, a double tap or double click zooms to 250 % at the point and back, the wheel (and a trackpad pinch, which Chrome sends as a wheel with Ctrl) zooms under the pointer without scrolling the page, `+`/`=`, `-`, `0` and the arrows act on the focused stage (`role="group"`, named by the hidden title "Zvětšená fotka" or "Zvětšený QR kód" and described by a hidden hint), and the buttons Oddálit, the level ("150 %", which returns to 100 %) and Přiblížit, each step announced through `DialogAnnouncer` ("Přiblíženo na 150 %"). At 100 % one finger swipes it away as in Photos (#382): the content follows the finger (sideways too), the dark background fades through the dialog's `[pulledAway]`, and a release that `SheetDrag` reads as closing closes the viewer as × does, while a short or upward swipe eases back; zoomed in, the finger pans instead, a second finger turns the swipe into a pinch, and a mouse never swipes. From 100 % to 400 %; dragging stops at the content's edge (`zoom-geometry.ts`: `zoomAround`, `panBy`, `clampView`, `zoomStep`, the content's own layout size, so a transform never feeds back). Today it wraps the photo of a news or project page (`content-article`), the About photo (`about`) and the QR code of Přispět (`payment-panel`); the QR codes inside dialogs (the 1 Kč add-account payment, the second-factor recovery) are not wrapped, since a dialog over a dialog waits on `DialogHistory`'s stack (2.18 / 2.24).

## Layout

Scrollbar gutter (`styles.scss`): from `nav` up, `html` reserves the scrollbar's strip (`scrollbar-gutter: stable`), so a page that does not scroll keeps the fixed header and the content on the same axis as one that does; the landing stage, which never scrolls, reserves none, so the hero runs to the window's edge, and neither does the launch screen, whose icon stays in the middle of the window as the launch image draws it. From `nav` up `html` carries the page's ground itself (`--color-surface`, `--color-surface-grouped` on the grouped pages, `--color-surface-inverse` while the contact section is in view, `--color-image-shade` on the landing), so the strip shows it, and while a modal dialog is open `html` takes the opaque colour of that ground under `--color-backdrop`, changing through `motion.state(background-color)` as the backdrop fades in (no transition under reduced motion) (`--color-backdrop-tint` mixed at `--backdrop-strength`, the scrollbar's track and thumb too; a gradient would not reach the strip, which paints only the canvas colour), so no bright strip stays beside the backdrop.

`chunk-load-notice/`: rendered by `app.ts` after the `router-outlet` while `ChunkLoadRecovery.failure()` is set, with the text "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít. Web byl mezitím možná aktualizován nebo vypadlo připojení. Zkuste ji načíst znovu." When the first page failed (`initial`; the router has activated nothing, so no shell is on screen), a page of its own: `main#obsah.ui-panel-page` with a `.ui-panel`, the `h1` "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít" (focused; the tab title becomes "Stránku se nepodařilo otevřít | Nová naděje – nadační fond" until the router sets the next page's title), the rest of the text as the lead, the button "Načíst znovu" and the link "Přejít na úvod" (`href="/"`, a full load). After a later navigation, a bar over the page (`.chunk-load-bar.ui-notice--alert.ui-notice--floating`, fixed at the bottom, at most `--measure-prose` wide and centred) whose button "Načíst znovu" takes the focus and is `aria-describedby` the message, so the message is announced once, with the focus move; the bar is no `role="alert"`, because an alert would announce the same text again and a screen reader that cuts speech short on the focus move could drop it; when the focus cannot reach the button (an open modal `dialog` makes the page behind it inert and leaves it out of the accessibility tree, so no region of the bar would be announced either), the bar hands the message, read from its own rendered text so the one i18n id `layout.chunk-load.message` serves both, to `DialogAnnouncer`, whose region inside the open dialog announces it, and withdraws it when the bar goes; the notice itself renders no status region, so a page's own status region stays the only one; while it shows, `:root` carries `has-floating-notice` and `--floating-notice-height` (measured, kept by a `ResizeObserver`; the themes set it to `0rem`), which add that room below the page (`body`'s `padding-bottom` on every page but the landing page, whose photo keeps its own room) and to `scroll-padding-bottom`, and a `focusin` listener scrolls a control the bar would hide above it in the next animation frame, after a left field has added its error line (Chromium does not scroll an element that is already in the viewport behind a fixed bar), leaving controls in an open `dialog` alone, so the bar never covers the end of the page or the focus (WCAG 2.4.11). That room-keeping lives in `shared/ui/floating-notice/floating-notice-room.ts` (`keepRoomForFloatingNotice`, a list of the bars on screen per document that keeps room for the latest, so when one bar hands over to another the page keeps its room until the last bar goes), shared with `signed-out-notice/`: rendered by `public-shell/` after the tab bar (where signing out lands; outside the donor and administration shells, whose pages keep their own status regions), an always rendered `role="status"` region that, while `core/auth/signed-out-notice.ts` (`SignedOutNotice`, root; `show(reason)`, `signed-out`, `idle` or `device-data-deleted`) is shown, holds a `.ui-notice--status.ui-notice--floating` bar "Odhlásili jste se." (for `idle`, the hour without use on "Cizí počítač": "Po hodině bez používání jsme vás z bezpečnosti odhlásili.", because the visitor did not sign out themselves, #1001) with "Zavřít" (focus to `main#obsah`; a quiet button in the notice's own colour, as tall as `--touch-target-size`, #1733; under the tab bar the bar stands on the tab bar's edges, `--tab-bar-gap` beside the safe area); the account sheet's "Odhlásit se" shows it once the navigation to `/` has finished, and the next navigation, a new sign-in or "Zavřít" takes it away.

`offline-status/` (plan v3 2.7): rendered by `app.ts` after the chunk-load notice. An always rendered polite region (`aria-live="polite"`, no `role="status"`, through `DialogAnnouncer` while a dialog is open) says "Jste offline. Zobrazujeme údaje z 12:30.", "Server fondu teď neodpovídá." and "Připojení je zpět."; while `Connectivity.offline()` a pill button fixed at the bottom centre ("Offline · údaje z 12:30", or "Offline", in the warning colours, above the floating notice through `.has-floating-notice`) keeps room for itself (`offline-pill-room.ts`: `html.is-offline` and its measured height in `--offline-pill-height`, `0rem` in the theme, so `scroll-padding-bottom`, the bottom padding of the page and the sticky `.ui-action-bar`, raised above it, keep content and the focused control clear of it) and opens a sheet (`app-dialog presentation="sheet"`): "Jste offline" with "Bez připojení můžete jen prohlížet. The landing page takes no such bottom padding, because its hero is exactly one screen and padding made it scroll and showed a dark band under the photo in the installed app (Jakub 2026-11-10): the hero's text keeps the room instead (`--floating-room` in `hero.scss`, the pill's or the floating notice's height and `--space-3` each), so the pill and the notice stack between the text and the tab bar over the photo; `e2e/first-screen.spec.ts` checks it offline on the phone screens. Zkontrolujte Wi-Fi nebo mobilní data." or "Server neodpovídá" with "Připojení k internetu máte, ale server fondu teď neodpovídá. Zkoušíme to znovu sami.", the age of the data ("Zobrazujeme údaje z …, nemusí být aktuální.") with what waits ("Odeslat zprávu, přihlásit se nebo cokoli změnit půjde, až se připojíte.", or "… až server zase odpoví.") and "Zkusit znovu" (`Connectivity.tryAgain()`; while it tries `aria-disabled`, so the focus stays, "Zkoušíme se připojit…" or "Zkoušíme spojení se serverem…", then in its status line "Pořád se nedaří připojit.
