Za chvíli to zkusíme znovu sami." or "Server pořád neodpovídá. …" in the warning colour, or "Připojení je zpět." with a "Zavřít" button in its place that takes the focus); while the sheet is open only its own status line speaks, while another dialog is open the announcement goes through `DialogAnnouncer`; texts under `layout.offline.*`. A dialog whose opener is gone when it closes (here the pill) returns the focus to `main#obsah`.

App shell of plan v3 2.23 (the decisions of 2.1, variant E V1): every public page and the donor area share one header, every public page has, at every width, one floating tab bar (the landing page, the four tabs with their news and story pages, and for now the other public pages, Jakub 2026-11-11), the donor area and the administration have none at any width, as the tab bar of Music leaves the account (Jakub 2026-11-11; the Zpět button, or the logo on a wide screen, leads out to the landing page and its bar), and the donor area keeps its side panel on wide screens. `app-header/` (`app-header`): a `header` (the `banner`, `tabindex="-1"` without an outline) laid over the top of the page (`position: absolute`, the safe area added at the top, and its row aligned with `.ui-container`: `--space-8` at the sides, `--space-5` up to `tablet`, or the side safe area when larger), so it scrolls away with the page and has no bar of its own behind it: on the left the logo to `/` (`/images/logo-on-dark.svg` over a photo or a dark head, `/images/logo.svg` over a light one, chosen by the route's `head`; 115 px wide, from `wide` `--header-logo-width-wide`, 140 px, desktop team P30), on the right, signed out, "Přihlásit se" (a secondary pill in the glass of the back button and the tab bar, so the filled blue stays the page's own call to action, design plan 3.1; opens the sign-in; after it the focus moves to the button "Účet" that took its place; the logo may shrink and "Přihlásit se" keeps one line in `--text-header-action`, `--text-sm` capped by the viewport, so at 200 % text on a 320 px phone and at the largest iPhone text the row still fits and the header keeps its height) and, signed in, a round button named "Účet" (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`) that shows the portrait of an administrator linked to a person of the foundation (`account-sheet/account-portrait.ts`, `AccountPortrait`: `PeopleApi.mine()` asked once per signed-in administrator, kept by the donor's id, so the portrait of one account never shows for the next one signed in on the same tab, `?w=480`, the smallest variant; an error keeps the initials), otherwise the initials of the name (`core/auth/initials.ts`: the first letters of the first and the last word, read after a visually hidden "Účet", so the button is named "Účet JN" with the letters it shows and voice control finds it by them, WCAG 2.5.3, desktop team 2026-11-16; with a portrait or the icon it is named "Účet") or a person icon without a name, and opens the account sheet; a count the account holds for the donor (`core/layout/account-badges.ts`, `AccountBadges`: the donor area puts there the bank accounts with a question about an older symbol, the count the side panel shows, and clears it when the area closes) stands at the button's corner, `aria-hidden`, and its description ("1 otázka čeká na odpověď") joins the button's name, except on the page that holds it, and the sheet's row of that section shows the same count. A page deeper than a tab root shows the fixed round back button "Zpět" (`aria-label`, the glass of the tab bar) before the logo, at the left edge of the content column; on wide screens the donor area leaves it out (`backOnWide: false`), because its side panel leads around. Wherever the back button shows, it stands alone without the logo (Jakub 2026-11-05 for the donor area and the administration, 2026-11-06 for every page, #431: the two clashed side by side), and the account button or "Přihlásit se" keeps to the right; the logo shows only on a page without the back button (a tab root, the landing page, and the donor area on a wide screen, where the side panel leads around). Nothing is drawn over the top of the page when it scrolls (Jakub 2026-11-16, #634): no band, no blur and no fade under the status bar or the back button, so the content runs on under the clock as in a native iOS app; iOS itself sets the clock and the icons of the status bar light over a dark page and dark over a light one, and Safari in a tab draws its own soft edge under its status bar. The skip link waiting above the top casts its shadow only once it is focused and shown, because in the light appearance that shadow reached under the status bar as a faint 4 px line at 62 pt (found by the mobile team, 2026-11-16). `e2e/top-of-screen.spec.ts` checks in both appearances that the top of a scrolled tab root and of a scrolled article looks the same with and without the header, its controls aside, and that of an opened page with and without the skip link. Until 2026-11-16 the header's `::before` and `::after` (`scroll-edge.ts`) laid a band in the colour of the ground with a blurred fade there, which showed as a strip of its own under the clock. Below `nav`, on a page with the back button, once the page `h1` (`main#obsah h1`) has scrolled above the header row, its text stands centred at the top, between the back button and the account button, on one line with an ellipsis, `--text-lg` semibold in `--font-sans`, fading in over `--duration-fast` (none under `prefers-reduced-motion`, the global rule), in `--color-text`, and `aria-hidden`, since the `h1` still names the page (`app-header/large-title.ts`, `followLargeTitle`, checked in the next frame after a scroll, a resize and each navigation; Jakub 2026-11-06, design plan J5, compatible with "no top bar"); the account button is fixed there too (`app-header--pinned-account`), so it stays beside the back button instead of scrolling away. The logo keeps at least 96 px. The first section of every public page starts `--page-top` (the top safe area, `--header-height` and `--space-4`) or more below the top (`.ui-intro`, `.ui-panel-page`), so its title keeps clear of the logo. The global `scroll-padding-top` adds the top safe area, so a focused control lands below the header row and the back button (#352). `account-sheet/` (`app-account-sheet`, inside `<app-dialog>`, `presentation` `sheet` while `CompactLayout.isCompact()`, a centred dialog otherwise): the head with the portrait or the initials, the `h2` with the donor's name ("Účet" without one) and "Variabilní symbol …", a `nav` of `ui-list` rows (Můj přehled, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, Bankovní účty, Údaje a zabezpečení, and for an administrator "Správa" with the badge "jen správci"), "Přidat na plochu" while `AppInstall.way()` offers a way (Jakub 2026-10-31 13:38: it stays in the sheet whatever "Teď ne" on the install card decided; with the browser's prompt the row closes the sheet and asks the browser, on an iPhone or in Safari on a Mac it unfolds the install card's instruction, `aria-expanded`) and "Odhlásit se" (`ui-button--danger-soft`, "Odhlašujeme…" while it runs; then `/` and the signed-out notice), and under it, because the site has no footer (Jakub 2026-10-31 14:59 for phones, 2026-11-12 everywhere), `info@nfnn.cz`, "Kontakt" (`/o-nas#kontakt`), the registry line with the IČO and "Zásady ochrany osobních údajů" (the i18n ids `layout.footer.registry` and `layout.footer.privacy` of the former footer, kept as ids never change). The row of the section open carries `aria-current="page"`. A row or link hands the sheet's history entry over to the page it opens (`DialogHistory.handOver`, which answers whether the sheet owned an entry) and navigates with `replaceUrl` and the next app depth, so Back from that page returns where the sheet was opened; without an owned entry it navigates as a new entry, and to the address already open it only closes. The header closes the sheet when the session ends. `tab-bar/` (`app-tab-bar`): the `nav` "Hlavní sekce" with four links in a grid, O nás `/o-nas`, Aktuality `/aktuality`, Pomáháme `/projekty`, Přispět `/prispet` (`core/navigation/app-tabs.ts`, `APP_TABS`, `tabOf(url)` matches a tab's path and everything under it), each an inline SVG icon over its label, a filled glyph as iOS draws its tab bar (`fill="currentColor"`, no stroke; Jakub 2026-11-11), drawn for the site, never taken from SF Symbols: O nás the logo's star filled (`FOUNDATION_STAR` of `shared/ui/foundation-star/foundation-star.ts`, the one drawing the administration's section rows use too, in a square view box), Aktuality a page with three cut-out lines, Pomáháme a heart, Přispět a gift whose ribbon is the gap between its halves, all on a 24 grid at `--tab-icon-size` (`rem`, so it grows with the text); the current tab carries `aria-current="page"` at its root and `"true"` deeper in it, the pill (`--color-tab-pill`, its rim `--color-tab-pill-rim`: none in `nova-nadeje`, so the pill fills the bar up to `--tab-pill-inset` as an iOS tab bar does, and a tap just inside the bar's edge still lands on a tab: each tab's `::before` reaches over the pill inset, half the gap to its neighbours and, at the ends, the bar's side padding, so the inset is only drawn and the whole bar above a tab stays its target, #1252), and a bold label, so it is told apart by the pill and the weight, not by colour alone; hidden (`visibility: hidden`, out of the accessibility tree, its room kept) while the on-screen keyboard is open (`SoftKeyboard.isOpen()`, #359), as the keyboard covers a native tab bar; fixed at the bottom, centred, at most `--tab-bar-max-width` wide, the larger of `--tab-bar-gap-browser` and the safe area less `--tab-bar-home-indicator-overlap` above the bottom in a browser (#1253, the same formula as the app, not a sum) and, in an installed app (`display-mode: standalone`), the larger of `--tab-bar-gap` and the safe area less `--tab-bar-home-indicator-overlap`; its columns are as wide as their labels need (a label breaks only between words), and the four tabs always stand in one row (Jakub 2026-11-06): the labels take `--text-tab-label`, the theme's `--text-2xs` until four labels no longer fit the bar's width, and below that a size that follows the bar's width in `cqi` (the bar is the size container `tab-bar`), so at 200 % text on a phone the labels stay smaller than the rest of the text instead of breaking a word or the row; `tab-bar-room.ts` (`keepRoomForTabBar`) writes the bar's measured height into `--tab-bar-rendered-height` on the root (a `ResizeObserver`; the latest bar shown wins, the theme's `--tab-bar-height` when none), which `--tab-bar-clearance` is computed from; the page keeps `--tab-bar-clearance` free under its content at every width (`.with-tab-bar` except on the landing page and the hero; `e2e/tab-bar-room.spec.ts`, which also proves that the donor area and the administration show no bar and end their pages on screen; a `.ui-panel-page` takes it into its own dark padding and gives it back with a negative margin, so no white band shows under it) and in `scroll-padding-bottom` (`styles.scss`, `html:has(app-tab-bar)`), and a floating notice and `.ui-action-bar` sit above the bar (`_notice.scss`, `_action-bar.scss`: `bottom: var(--tab-bar-clearance)` wherever the bar shows). The donor area and the administration render no bar (`donor-area`, `admin-shell`), so their columns keep `--space-12` under their content, their `.ui-action-bar` stands at the bottom of the screen and the full-screen news and project editors need no flag of their own; a public route with `tabBar: false` (`pripojeni`, `prvni-nastaveni`) hides it in the public shell. A tab's link is the address it was last left at (`core/navigation/tab-memory.ts`, `TabMemory`: the last URL seen in each tab and the current tab, followed on every `NavigationEnd`), so a tab opens where the visitor left it, and the current tab pressed on a page deeper in it returns to its root, as an iOS tab bar pops its stack (`BackTarget.toTabRoot`: one step back in the history when the entry behind is that root, otherwise the root replaces the page, so Back never returns to the page left, #394); a plain click on the tab at its root scrolls to the top (smoothly, unless `prefers-reduced-motion`), modifier clicks open the link as a browser does. A finger can also slide along the bar, as on the tab bar of iOS 26, where a long press turns the selection into a lens that follows the finger across the tabs (`tab-bar/finger-across-tabs.ts`, `followFingerAcrossTabs`, started after the first render): the bar is `touch-action: none`, so the page never scrolls under the finger and the back swipe leaves it alone; a passive `pointerdown` of a touch or pen pointer on the bar (never a mouse) starts listening on the window to `pointermove`, `pointerup` and `pointercancel` of that pointer (the browser captures a touch pointer to its target by itself); a move of at most 10 px is still a tap, the link's click; beyond it the bar measures the tabs once and, in one `requestAnimationFrame` per frame, moves its pill (`.tab-bar-pill`, `aria-hidden`, under the list, the tab's size, through the `translate` property only, enlarged by `--tab-pill-drag-scale`) with the finger along the row of the tab under it, clamped to the row, while the current tab's own pill and colour give way and the tab under the finger (`tabUnderFinger`: the nearest tab to the finger held inside the bar, so beyond either end it is the end tab, also in the two-row layout at large text) takes `--color-tab-active`; letting go opens that tab where it was left (`TabMemory.urlFor`, through the router, so no browser entry is added), and the pill stands on it until the navigation ends (a refused navigation returns it); letting go over the tab already open, more than 24 px above the bar (the finger slid off it upwards) or on `pointercancel` opens nothing; a click arriving within 400 ms of a drag is swallowed. Under `prefers-reduced-motion` the pill jumps from tab centre to tab centre without the enlargement; under `forced-colors` it is a `Highlight` outline and the tab under the finger turns `Highlight`. The links, names and roles stay as they are, so keyboard and screen readers use the tabs as before, and no haptic tap is pretended (the web has none on iOS). `e2e/tab-bar-drag.spec.ts` drags in Chromium with real touch input (CDP), where it also proves the page does not scroll, and in WebKit with touch `PointerEvent`s. On a computer (from `nav`, P28 of the desktop team, 2026-11-16) the pill always stands on the shown tab instead of the tab's own background: `restPillOn` (`layout/tab-bar/pill-at-rest.ts`) sizes it to the tab and moves it by `translate` after every render that changes the shown tab and when the bar resizes, the first placement and the one after the landing page (no current tab, no pill) at once (the pill's style is settled with a `getComputedStyle` read before the glide is turned on, so the browser never sees the new position and the transition together), every later one gliding (`motion.move(translate, slow, spring)`; a drag takes the transition off while the finger leads); a tab under a fine pointer tints with `--color-tab-hover` (`motion.hover`; half the pill's strength, with its `-on-dark` twin, so the hover stays apart from the pill beside it), the labels take `--text-tab-label-wide` (`--text-xs` until four labels no longer fit the bar, then the same width formula `--tab-label-fit` as `--text-tab-label` on a phone, so at large text on an iPad on its side the bar keeps one row and its height, #1335); every label stays on one line (`white-space: nowrap`, no ellipsis: the size makes it fit, and the device check `every tab label on one line inside the tab bar` proves it in every pose, together with the current tab's label lying wholly inside its pill, the resting pill from `nav` up or the tab itself below, within 1 px); at every width each label reserves the width of its bold form (a hidden bold copy, `content: attr(data-label) / ''`, out of the accessibility tree, #1252), so a label keeps its width when it turns bold and the labels do not shift while the pill glides; `e2e/tab-bar-pill.spec.ts` proves where the pill ends. `core/navigation/page-transitions.ts` (`PageTransitions`, started by `pageTransitionsAtStart`; Jakub 2026-11-12, the design of the iOS UX designer and the iOS app developer): on a phone layout (`CompactLayout`) a page with Zpět slides in from the right over the page it was opened from, and leaves to the right over the page Zpět, a step back in the site's history or the swipe of the home-screen app returns to, which comes back from `--page-parallax` under a dim (`--color-page-dim`) that clears, as an iOS navigation stack pushes and pops; `page-transition-kind.ts` (`pageTransitionFor`) decides: `pop` for a `popstate` navigation or one `BackTarget` starts in place of a step back (`info: BACK_NAVIGATION`) from a page with a `parent`, `push` for any other navigation to a page with a `parent` within the same tab (or outside the tabs, as the account), nothing for the first page, a tab switch, a page without `parent`, a `replaceUrl`, a change of only the query or fragment, a dialog open or still sliding away (`dialog--leaving`), a wide layout or `prefers-reduced-motion`. Nothing that moves is live: when the guards let the navigation go (`GuardsCheckEnd`), a still copy of the page left (`still-copy.ts`: `cloneNode` of the shell host marked `page-layer`, without ids outside drawings, `name`, `autofocus`, `for`, `form`, frames, media and dialogs, so it neither duplicates an id nor joins a radio group, and a page under an open sheet is copied without the sheet) covers the screen in an overlay (`aria-hidden`, `inert`, appended to `body`, `styles/_page-transition.scss`) at the page's scroll position, kept in its own scroller inside a box that moves, so its fixed and sticky parts sit where they did; the router then renders the next page beneath it, and after the router's `Scroll` (its scroll restored) and two frames a copy of that page joins the overlay and both copies move by CSS animations of `transform` and `opacity` only (their end is awaited after a style read, so WebKit lists them) (`--duration-page`, `--ease-page`, the leading edge's `--shadow-page-edge` and, in the home-screen app, its leading corners rounded by `--radius-page-card` as the iPhone's screen is, Jakub 2026-11-12 after the iOS 27 simulator), after which the overlay goes and the live page is there, its `h1` focused by the shell as before. A navigation that brings no `Scroll` is revealed after a quarter of `PAGE_TRANSITION_LONGEST_MS`, and that wait reveals only the transition it was set for: once a `Scroll` had already finished it, the wait used to land on a swipe started in the meantime and remove its preview from under the finger (`page-transitions.spec.ts`; `e2e/back-swipe.spec.ts` failed 18 in 100 runs on six workers, 2026-11-12). The tab bar is decided from the copies, not from the route data (the donor area and the administration may drop it): when the page being left holds an `app-tab-bar` and so does the page arriving (or nothing of it is known yet, as under a swipe with no copy kept), the copies hide theirs (`--under-tab-bar`) and the overlay stays under the live tab bar, which does not move; when only one holds it, it rides with that page, as `hidesBottomBarWhenPushed` in iOS. The bar stays put as in iOS: the copies hide their round back button (`.back-button`) and account circle (`.account-button`), and a bar layer above both pages (`page-transition__bar`, never transformed) holds a copy of each page's header at that page's scroll offset, showing only those two; per item, when both pages show it alike (`background-color`, `color`, `border-color`, `backdrop-filter`, `opacity`, text, picture and place) one stands still, otherwise the leaving one fades out and the arriving one fades in in place, and a leaving item the arriving page lacks (Zpět on the way to a root, where the logo comes in) is gone by half the way; the item itself fades, never the layer, so its glass keeps blurring, by the finger's progress in a swipe and by `--duration-page` otherwise. Copies are taken once and placed as they are: a kept copy is laid under the finger without being cloned again, and none is kept when another tab opens, as Zpět never leads back across tabs. Global `html:has(…)` and `body:has(…)` rules look only inside `app-root`, so a copy in the overlay never restyles the live page. Animations inside a copy end at once, a cancelled, failed or skipped navigation removes the overlay, and `REMOVE_STYLES_ON_COMPONENT_DESTROY` is off (`keptPageStyles` in `app.config.ts`), so a closed page's component styles stay for its copy. While a transition runs, its copies hold the same classes and texts as the live page, so an e2e locator by class or text at phone size scopes to `page.getByRole('main')` (the overlay is `aria-hidden`). `core/navigation/back-target.ts` (`BackTarget`, started at app start with `TabMemory` by `navigationAtStart` in `app.config.ts`): counts the app's own history depth in the history state (`nfnnAppDepth`, through `Location`, so it survives a reload: the first navigation reads it back; popstate takes the restored entry's depth, `replaceUrl` and `skipLocationChange` keep it, every other navigation adds one); each entry also keeps the path of the entry behind it (`nfnnAppBehind`); `back(parent)` goes back in the history while the app has an entry of its own behind and that entry is in the same tab (or either page is outside the tabs, as the donor area), and otherwise opens the route's `parent` with `replaceUrl`, so a page opened from a link outside never leaves the site on Zpět and a deep page a tab reopened never returns into another tab; `destination(parent)` names that choice before it is made (`{ kind: 'step', depth, path }` with the depth and path of the entry behind, or `{ kind: 'parent', path }`), and `back` acts on it, so whatever shows where Zpět leads (the page under the back gesture) shows the page it opens; `returningByButton` tells the shell that the history step came from Zpět, so the public shell focuses the new page's `h1` as after any app navigation (a browser Back keeps the browser's focus). `core/navigation/edge-swipe-back.ts` (`EdgeSwipeBack`, started by `edgeSwipeBackAtStart` in `app.config.ts`): only in the app opened from the home screen of an iPhone or iPad (`navigator.standalone` with a touch screen), a swipe to the right from anywhere on the screen, as in iOS 26, is one step back as in a native app (issue #431, Jakub 2026-11-06). Safari has nothing of the site to go back to there (the site keeps its own history), so the system offers no back swipe and no touch is cancelled: passive `touchstart`, `touchmove`, `touchend` and `touchcancel` on the document follow one touch by its identifier, and scrolling, taps and the edge work natively. Each touch is read by `core/navigation/back-swipe.ts` (`BackSwipe`, a plain class, Jakub 2026-11-12): one finger takes the page once it leaves the 10 px of a tap moving right and more across than down (1.5 : 1), and from then on only its sideways position counts (`offset`, never left of where it landed nor past the width); a finger that moves up or down first, moves left, or rests more than 500 ms before it moves (a long press that selects text) is no swipe. On release (`release`, a `SwipeSettle`) it projects where the page would come to rest, its offset plus the finger's last speed for 500 ms (a speed older than 100 ms counts as rest), and goes back when that is past half the screen's width, the page moved at least 40 px and the finger was not heading back left at 0.3 px/ms or faster; otherwise the page slides back. The settle runs from the offset to the width or to 0 in the remaining distance at the finger's speed, at least as fast as a whole width in 350 ms and within 150–350 ms, on a `cubic-bezier` whose start slope keeps the finger's speed. A touch that starts on a field (`input`, `select`, `textarea`, `contenteditable`), on an element with computed `touch-action: none` (the zoom viewer's stage, the sheet's grabber, the tab bar) or inside a box that scrolls sideways and has more to show (`overflow-x` `auto` or `scroll`, wider content: the year stories, the log lines) is left to it. Once the finger takes the page, on a phone layout without reduced motion, no open dialog and a page with a `parent`, `PageTransitions.startSwipe(BackTarget.destination(parent))` lays the page under the finger: a still copy of the live page moves with the finger (`follow`, `translateX` of the offset) over the page Zpět leads to, which moves from `--page-parallax` to its place under a dim that clears as the finger goes across; that page is the still copy `PageTransitions` kept when the visitor left it by any navigation that goes one step deeper, also one opened from a sheet such as the account panel or one with no slide (one per depth of the site's own history, at most three, `KEPT_PAGES`, in memory only, dropped from the depth left onwards and not laid under the finger when the screen's width has changed since), or, when none is kept (the app opened straight on the page, a reload, or Zpět opens the `parent` in place of the page, perhaps in another shell), the page itself drawn under cover (Jakub 2026-11-12: "I want an exact clone of iOS"): the plain ground lies there for the moment it takes, then `core/navigation/navigation-preview.ts` (`NavigationPreview.visit`) opens that page with `skipLocationChange` and `info: PREVIEW_OUT` while the overlay hides the screen (each of its two steps given at most `PREVIEW_STEP_LONGEST_MS`, 1 s), waits for the app to be stable (at most `PREVIEW_DRAWING_LONGEST_MS`, 300 ms) and two frames, copies it, opens the page under the finger again the same way (`PREVIEW_BACK`) and puts back its scroll and focus, and the copy fades in under the finger (`--duration-fast`). The live page is not drawn again: `previewing-reuse.ts` (`PreviewingReuse`, the router's `RouteReuseStrategy` in front of `KeptTabPages`) detaches every level of it on the way out and attaches them again on the way back, and keeps every level of the page drawn for the preview, so a swipe that completes shows that very page without asking for its data again, while one called off, or any other navigation, lets it go (`destroyDetachedRouteHandle`). What follows navigations takes no step for the preview's own navigations, told apart by their `info` (`NavigationPreview.drives()`), never by a time window, so a real navigation that starts meanwhile (a sign-out, a swipe completing) is followed as usual and ends the preview without its way back: `PageTransitions`, `PageFocus`, `TabMemory`, the kept tab pages' shown and left hooks, `ShownAnswers` (which also records no answer while a preview runs), `AppUpdates` and the chunk-load recovery of a failed navigation; a swipe that completes waits for its preview to be over before it steps back, and one called off while it runs lets the previewed page go once it is over; `BackTarget` keeps its depth, as `skipLocationChange` never counts. A page with unsaved input is never previewed (`DirtyForms.any()`), and a preview that cannot open its page leaves the plain ground. On release the copies settle by the `SwipeSettle` through Web Animations (`settle`), and only after a settle that completes does the step back below follow; a settle back, a `touchcancel` or a `popstate` during the swipe slides the page back and nothing else happens; a page with unsaved input (`DirtyForms.any()`) slides back first and the step back then asks as Zpět does. After a completed settle the overlay stays, the destination's copy in place, while the router opens the page beneath it (no second slide), and goes after the router's `Scroll` and two frames, or after 5 s at most. A step back is then what Zpět does: with a `dialog` open, a step back in the history (`DialogHistory` closes the top dialog or its last step; the sign-in dialog's steps are kept by `LoginDialogService`), except while a navigation waits (`Router.currentNavigation()`: the unsaved-changes dialog of a guard), when the last open dialog gets a `cancel` and closes unless it refuses, as with Esc; without a dialog `BackTarget.back(parent)` with the route's `parent` (so it never leaves the tab's stack and the shell focuses the new page's `h1`), and nothing on a tab root. A `popstate` or `pagehide` between the touch's start and end means the app already went back, so no second step follows. In a Safari tab, Chrome on iOS, Android and on a computer it is off: the swipe and the browser's Back belong to the browser there and leave the site. Playwright's WebKit reports `maxTouchPoints` 0 and cannot perform the system gesture, so the swipe and Safari's behaviour are checked on the iOS simulator and the device (#431). `core/navigation/route-chrome.ts` (`RouteChrome.current`) reads the deepest route's data: `parent` (inherited; the article and project pages name their list, the privacy policy `/o-nas`, `/darce` and `/overit` `/`, so the verification page opened from Potvrzení has Zpět back to it), `head` (`photo` for `/`, `light` for every tab of the public site and every page under it (`/o-nas`, `/aktuality`, `/projekty`, `/prispet`, their articles and stories, the privacy policy and the 404) and for `/darce` and `/sprava`, `dark` by default for the standalone pages), `tabBar` (false for `/pripojeni`, `/prvni-nastaveni` and the administration's news and project editors), `landing` (`/`, whose route runs its guard on every navigation, so an in-app link to an old `/#…` address redirects too), `backOnWide` (false for `/darce` and `/sprava`). `index.html` sets `viewport-fit=cover`, so the safe areas are the page's to keep (the header, the tab bar and `.ui-container`'s side padding take `env(safe-area-inset-*)`, the top through `--safe-area-top`, above). An app opened from the home screen of an iPad in a window smaller than the screen (iPadOS 26 and later, "Windowed Apps", Stage Manager, a tiled window) has the system's window controls floating over its top-leading corner, and WebKit tells the page nothing about them (measured on iPadOS 27.2: every `env(safe-area-inset-*)` but the bottom is 0 in a window, and there is no `titlebar-area-*` or `navigator.windowControlsOverlay`), so `core/layout/window-controls.ts` (`WindowControls.start()` from the app initializer `windowControlsAtStart`) marks the root `has-window-controls` while `navigator.standalone` is `true` on a touch screen that is no iPhone (a Mac's Dock app has no touch, a Safari window keeps the controls in its own toolbar) and the window (`outerWidth` × `outerHeight`) is smaller than the screen, following `resize`; a maximized window shows no controls over the page (its top lies under the status bar) and has the screen's area, in either orientation. `styles.scss` then sets `--window-controls-inset-inline` and `-block` (`0px` in the theme) to the theme's `--window-controls-room-inline` and `-block` (74 px and 52 px in `nova-nadeje`: the collapsed controls end about 62 px from the window's leading edge and 41 px from its top), and, as iPadOS moves a navigation bar's leading items past the controls rather than below them, the header's leading padding, the back button and the skip link keep at least the inline room, and the donor area's sticky side panel keeps its first row below the block room; the controls expanded by a tap are a passing overlay and get no room, and nothing else needs it (a full-screen dialog starts its content 56 px down and closes at the top-right, a sheet leaves room above it). The site has no footer, at any width, in the browser, the home-screen app of an iPhone or iPad and the Dock app of a Mac (Jakub 2026-11-12, replacing the footer of 2026-10-31): the tab bar holds the sections, the foundation's name, IČO, seat and registry entry stand in "Údaje pro účetní" at the end of O nás with the e-mail beside the contact heading, and the privacy policy is linked from the note under that card, from Přispět, from the registration and contact form consents and from the account sheet. So `public-shell/` gives `main` the room for the tab bar (`with-tab-bar`, `--tab-bar-clearance`) on every page but the landing page, and no page has a `contentinfo` landmark. `public-shell/` asks `SetupRedirect.checkOnce()` (`features/operations/setup/setup-redirect.ts`) once per page load: while `GET /api/operations/setup` says `required`, it navigates to `/prvni-nastaveni`; a failure is ignored, so a running site never depends on it. Both shells start with the skip link "Přeskočit na obsah" (global `.skip-link`, fixed top-left, shown on keyboard focus) to `main#obsah`; while the tab bar shows, `public-shell/` follows it with a second one, "Přeskočit na navigaci" (`layout.skipToNavigation`, the same `.skip-link` in the same place, so the two never show at once), which moves the focus to the first tab without changing the address (`TabBar.focusFirstTab`); the donor shell has no tab bar and so no second link, and neither link shows in the still copy of a page transition: in `public-shell/` the `main` around the pages, in `donor-shell/` (`/darce`, `/sprava`) the content column: in the donor area that of `side-nav` at every width (one `side-nav` whose `panel` input drops the panel on phones, so the routed page stays the same component when the window crosses `nav`), in the administration likewise (the panel only above `nav`, 2.24). `public-shell/` also follows navigation for keyboard and screen-reader users: after a route change (not the first load, not back/forward, not while a dialog is open) it focuses the page `h1` unless the page already moved the focus into `main`, and after a link to a section (`/o-nas#kontakt`, also when clicked again) it focuses that section; both get `tabindex="-1"` and no ring. For an administrator with open faults (`OpenAlerts.alerts()`, the faults the app icon's badge counts) the first group above the sections is one row, "Nefunguje: {area}" with "od {Prague time}" or "Poruchy: N" with the areas, an amber count and the chevron, leading through `follow` to the fault's page (`core/push/alert-areas.ts`, `faultRoute`: the bank import `/sprava`, the backups `/sprava/zalohy`, the mail `/sprava/nastaveni`, several `/sprava/upozorneni`), and while faults are open the account button of `app-header` shows their count alone, filled with `--color-badge-warning` (`.account-badge--fault`), on every page and in place of the other counts of `AccountBadges`, named "Účet 1 porucha" (`faultDescription`: "1 porucha", "3 poruchy", "5 poruch"), so it matches the icon (Jakub 2026-11-14, variant A of #641: the app stays where it was, the account shows the way; the UX audit of #814 found the outlined blue count too quiet beside the red icon badge).

Switching tabs (Jakub 2026-11-05: switching between the tabs blinked, as if each page loaded only after the tap): a tab opens at once, with its last content, like a tab of a native app; and since switching still blinked the photos (Jakub 2026-11-12: the page of a tab was built anew on every switch, so Safari drew its new `<img>` elements only after decoding them, one or more frames empty, and the router scrolled the new page from the left tab's offset to the top with the page's smooth scrolling), a tab now keeps its page and its place. Five pieces do it. `core/navigation/tab-preloading.ts` (`TabPagesPreloading`, `withPreloading` in `app.config.ts`) loads ahead, once the first page has opened, exactly the routes whose `data` carries `LOADED_AHEAD`: the public shell, the content routes, the pages of the four tabs and the page of an article or a story (Jakub 2026-11-12, smoothness: its chunk was fetched on the tap) (a failure is swallowed, and the page then loads when it is opened, through the chunk-load recovery as before); every other page stays lazy, and the route names alone decide nothing, since the administration has an `aktuality` of its own. `core/navigation/kept-public-answers.ts` (`KeptPublicAnswers`, `keptPublicAnswersInterceptor`, the first interceptor of `appHttpClient`) keeps in memory the last answer of each GET of the public content (`/api/content/home`, the news and project lists by their query, a news or project page), at most 50 (`KEPT_ANSWERS_LIMIT`, the oldest forgotten first): a page that asks for one it already had within 10 minutes (`KEPT_ANSWER_MAX_AGE_MS`, by `performance.now()`) gets a copy at once (`structuredClone`, so a page that changes its answer changes no other's) and the server is asked again in the background, once per address at a time, so the next visit shows what the server answers now; an older answer, an address never asked for and a failed answer go to the server as before (the first visit of a tab still shows its loading line once), a background answer that fails keeps the kept one and a 404 forgets it, and any write under `/api/admin/content/` forgets them all, so an administrator sees a change at once. A copy served from memory is recorded in `ShownAnswers` (`core/connectivity/`, slice 2.7) with the `Date` of the answer it keeps, since it never reaches `connectivityInterceptor`, so the offline pill tells the true age of what a returning tab shows, and the background answer, once it arrives, records its own `Date` over it. A request for an address already on its way waits for that one request (`share()`, forgotten when it ends, so a failure lets the next asking go to the server again). It keeps only paths of the service worker's same-for-everyone list (`service-worker-bypass.ts`, pinned by its spec), nothing a donor or an administrator alone receives, and nothing outlives the tab. Getting the content ready ahead (Jakub 2026-11-12, smoothness: the first visit of Aktuality and Pomáháme showed their loading line and the photos drew late, and an article opened with "Načítáme stránku…" though its list had just shown it): `features/content/content-ahead.ts` (`ContentAhead`, `contentAheadAtStart` in `app.config.ts`) asks for an article or a story as soon as a finger touches its link or the keyboard reaches it (`pointerdown`, `focusin` on the document), or once the pointer has rested on it for `HOVER_INTENT_MS` (80 ms, so a pointer passing over a list asks for nothing), only for a same-origin link to `/aktuality/<slug>` or `/projekty/<slug>`, and, `CONTENT_AHEAD_AFTER_MS` (1.5 s) after the first page is drawn when that page is the home page or a tab (never from a page of its own such as `/overit`, the password reset or a connection), the first answers of the tabs once (`/api/content/home`, the first ten news, the projects and the news of the stories), and none of it offline or when the device asks to save data (`navigator.connection.saveData`); these requests carry `ASKED_AHEAD` (`askedAhead()`), so an answer already kept or on its way is not asked for again, and an answer asked ahead stays fresh for 10 s (`AHEAD_ANSWER_FRESH_MS`): a page that shows it within them does not ask the server again in the background, so an article opened afresh, which its resolver asks for ahead, is read once, and the reads whose page asks for a refresh after a kept copy (#608: the home content, the first news page and an article or story) ask for it ahead too, so the answer kept for the page is the fresh one and a page that joins a read still on its way gets both; the sizes they share with the pages are in `content-page-sizes.ts`. An article or a story then opens once its first answer is there or after `ARTICLE_WAIT_MS` (300 ms) at the most, and at once offline (`detail/article-ready.ts`, `articleReady`, the routes' `resolve`; a slower answer goes on for the page, which shows its loading line until it arrives; a read asked ahead or by the resolver is no longer aborted when the article is left, it ends as #608 bounds it), so a page fetched ahead opens complete, without its loading line. `core/navigation/kept-tab-pages.ts` (`KeptTabPages`, the router's `RouteReuseStrategy` in `app.config.ts`) keeps a tab's pages while the visitor is elsewhere, as a native tab keeps its navigation stack: the root of each tab (exactly the paths of `APP_TABS`, by the whole path, so `/sprava/aktuality` is never kept) whenever it is left, and the page a tab was left from (an article under Aktuality or Pomáháme) when the navigation leads out of that tab (the router's current navigation names where it goes), so returning shows that same page again, its photos already decoded and its form drafts and paging as they were, and Zpět from an article finds the kept list already drawn, so `PageFocus` focuses the link to the article left rather than the list's `h1`; going back from an article to its list lets the article go, and coming back to a tab elsewhere than the page it was left from lets that page go. At most a root and a last page per tab, eight pages, each replacing its earlier copy, and `PublicShell` destroys them all (`destroyDetachedRouteHandle`) when it is destroyed itself, on the way into `/darce`, `/sprava` or the app entry, so no page outlives its shell. The kept pages belong to the donor they were shown to (`KEPT_PAGES_OWNER`, which `app.config.ts` reads from `AuthStore`): the first strategy call of a navigation (the router calls the strategy only after the guards, so a navigation a guard turns away never uses the check up) compares it with the donor seen at the last navigation that reached the strategy, and when someone signed out or another donor signed in, every kept page is destroyed and the page left by that navigation is not kept, whatever order the sign-out and its navigation come in, so the next person on a shared computer never sees a name, an e-mail or a draft of the contact form of the one before. An unknown address under a tab (the `**` route) is never kept. Each kept page carries the scroll offset it was left at, read in `shouldDetach`, because the router takes the page out of the document before it calls `store` and the browser has then already clamped the offset. A kept page is not destroyed while away, so whatever it registered stays registered: a contact form with a draft on O nás keeps the draft while the visitor switches tabs, but a form out of the document does not count as half-typed (`TrackedForm`), so a new version still takes over when the visitor leaves the page with the draft, as it did when the page was destroyed, and a hidden draft holds back no edge swipe or administration lock elsewhere. `whenShownAgain` and `whenLeft` (the same file) tell a page, on the `NavigationEnd` that shows it again or leaves it hidden, what happened: O nás, Aktuality, Pomáháme and an article then ask for their content again quietly, without their loading line, unless a load of theirs is already running (a return to another page of the list starts one), keep what they show when the answer fails and take the answer when it arrives (Aktuality asks for the stories of the year again only when they never arrived); an article names the browser tab after itself (or as missing) again in a microtask, since the router names it after the route right after `NavigationEnd`, and drops a load still running when it is left, so an answer that arrives while another tab is shown neither renames the browser tab nor leads anywhere (it is asked for again on return); the not-found block lifts its `noindex` when its page is left and sets it again on return; and the payment panel ends its watch for an arriving gift (`GiftArrivalWatch.pageLeft`) when its page is left, as it ended when the page was destroyed, so no bank check or live stream runs from a page nobody sees. `TabMemory` (`core/navigation/tab-memory.ts`) puts a kept page back at its offset whenever a navigation shows it (the tab bar, a link, Zpět), with `behavior: 'instant'`, at once on `NavigationEnd` and again after the next render (only the latest placement counts), and once more in a microtask after the router's own `Scroll` of that navigation, whose scroll to the top would otherwise move the page, except when the address names a section (`/o-nas#kontakt`), which the router's anchor scrolling then reaches; only the tab bar returns to the page a tab was left from, while a link to a tab's root ("Kde pomáháme →") opens the root and lets that page go; `TabMemory.open`, which the tab bar calls for a tap and for a finger let go on another tab, navigates with `scroll: 'manual'` and opens a tab whose page is not kept at its top, at once. Every other navigation starts its page at the top in its first frame too, a new page as well as a change of its query only ("Starší" on Aktuality, a search or filter of the administration) (Jakub 2026-11-12, smoothness: the router sends its `Scroll` a frame after `NavigationEnd`, so a new page first showed at the offset of the page left and then slid to its top through the smooth scrolling the root had then for up to half a second): `TabMemory` puts it there with `behavior: 'instant'` on `NavigationEnd`, unless the navigation returns to a kept page, names a section, comes from the browser's history (`popstate`, a step of the site's own history included, where the router puts back the remembered offset; when it remembers none, as after a reload, `TabMemory` puts the page at its top instantly on the router's `Scroll` instead of letting it slide) or asks for `scroll: 'manual'` (the tab bar, the year filter of Pomáháme). `StatsCountUp` (`home/sections/stats-bar/`) lets the figures on O nás count up only the first time they come into view in a tab; afterwards they stand at their values, so a return to O nás does not replay the animation. `e2e/tab-switching.spec.ts` (also in the WebKit project `iphone`) checks that a tab seen before shows its content while its new request is still held, that the first switch to each tab after O nás loads no script, and, sampling every frame of a switch, that on a return the photos drawn before are drawn in each frame and none is asked for again, a scrolled tab opens at its offset and another at its top, an article left for another tab is the same on return, and Zpět from it shows the list at its offset, each without a frame in between.

List rows of plan v3 2.25: `CompactLayout.listsAsRows()` (`core/layout/compact-layout.ts`) is true below `nav` and also wherever the window is narrower than `nav` measured in the root text size (`innerWidth <= 899 × root font size / 16`, so an iPad on its side at 200 % text, 1194 px wide with a 32 px root, counts as narrow), following the media query, `resize` and a `ResizeObserver` on a hidden `1rem` probe, so enlarging the text switches at once; at the default text size on a wide screen it is false and every table stays as it was, pixel for pixel. Where it is true the lists render as `ui-list` rows instead of tables (one layout chosen by `@if`). A row (`src/styles/ui/_list.scss`) stands its lead at the first line of its text and its value and chevron in the middle of its height, also when its title wraps or the text is enlarged (one rule for the portal and the administration, #1549, taken from the bank logos of #1535): the row centres its grid tracks as a block (`align-content: center`), and `__lead` starts with the title (`align-self: start`), is `--list-lead-size` tall and moves by half the difference to one line of the row's text (`margin-block: calc((1lh - var(--list-lead-size)) / 2)`), so its tile, icon, avatar or logo, centred in it, stands on the middle of the title's first line whatever its size (a bank logo list sets `--list-lead-size` to its 40 px and the account number's size on the lead). A list of sections (`ui-list--centred`, the section rows of `/sprava` and `/sprava/vice`) keeps the iOS list row instead: its lead stands in the middle of the row's height like the chevron, a row of two lines too, and its count stays beside the title until the list is at most `pair` wide. The lead grows with the text up to a share of its list: in the nova-nadeje theme `--list-lead-size` is `max(var(--space-7), min(1.75rem, 8.75cqi))`, so it is never smaller than its 28 px, grows as a root size and stops at 8.75 % of the list's width (on a phone 320 pt wide at 200 % text it stays 28 px, and the title keeps the width it needs to break only between words; the control theme stops at `min(2.1875rem, 11cqi)`); the icon of a `__glyph` (`--list-glyph-size`, 5/7 of the lead in the nova-nadeje theme, 20 px at the default size, and 24/35 in the control theme; every icon drawn into it, the health and level icons of the logs included, one size, #1852) and the mark of a `__tile` (`--list-tile-icon-size`, 9/14 of the lead in the nova-nadeje theme and 4/7 in the control theme) follow it whatever width the SVG declares (#1562, #1780), and the sign `ui-status__icon` is `--status-icon-size` (3.5 rem in the nova-nadeje theme, 4 rem in the control theme) around its mark (#1792); the tile beside the verdict of `/overit` starts with its heading, and its mark is `--text-list-tile-mark` (the tile mark size) at a line height of one, so a mark drawn as text stays inside the tile at the largest text. Only a row with a `__state` and no `__meta` lets its title span both grid rows, so a row with neither has one grid row and no hidden second one (Jakub 2026-11-06). Inside a card the row decides by its own list's width (`list-card`), not the window's: while the list is at most `pair` wide in root units (200 % text and the largest text on a phone), every row with a `__value` puts the value under the title and the meta, from the title's start, and a `__state` goes on a line of its own under it; a row with a switch keeps it beside the text. A list whose values are wide (an amount, a date or a small button such as „Zkusit znovu“: Platby, the attention rows of `/sprava`, the other devices of Upozornění and a sent report, #1817) carries `ui-list--wide-values`, which makes it a `list-card` container of its own and moves the value under the text already while the list is at most `narrow` wide, so the text takes the row's width beside the lead, with a `__state` following the value on its line; a count (`ui-list__count`) stays by the arrow there. Every other list keeps a short value („42“, „za 2 ms“, „1 000 Kč“) beside its title until `pair`. A value under the title takes the regular weight and the payment amount its body size, so it never reads as a second title line, and the row's gap (`--space-1`) sets it apart from the meta above. The sign-out button of a device row (`devices-card`) sits in a `device-sign-out-slot`, drawn open and still whenever its list is drawn or refreshed (Údaje opening, „Starší (N)“ unfolding, a device added), because a list drawn or refreshed never animates; it folds away through `motion.row-leave` on `device-sign-out-slot--closing` when the inline confirmation opens, and unfolds through `motion.expand-on-open` only on the row whose „Zrušit“ was pressed (`device-sign-out-slot--returning`, cleared by the next question or by „Starší (N)“). The folded slot stays in the row, `inert` and `aria-hidden`, for as long as the question is open, so the value column keeps the button's width and the meta beside it never wraps to another number of lines: a re-wrap changes the row's height by a whole line in one frame, which neither folding the column later nor folding its width could smooth, since a line break is a step. The button sits in a wrapper without padding, so the fold reaches zero instead of stopping at the button's padding, and with less motion the folded slot closes its row at once. So the row's height and the question under it move only through the fold, at 390 px with the default text and at 200 % alike (`e2e/donor-motion.spec.ts`, by the jump rule of `e2e/segmented.ts`); „Zrušit“ returns the focus to the slot's button once it is no longer `inert`, without scrolling, and while the question folds (`devices-confirm--closing`, which exists only for its leave) `styles.scss` turns off scroll anchoring for the page (`overflow-anchor: none` on `html` and `body`), because the browser otherwise picks an anchor inside or below the changing row and scrolls the page with the slot's unfolding and the question's folding (#1903: in iOS WebKit at the largest text, the question in the middle of the screen moved the page by +49 and −72 px); at 375 px with a 53 px root text the page keeps its place in every frame. A `ui-person-row` (`_person-list.scss`) centres its avatar or logo on the lines beside it (name, e-mail and badges; code and state), never on the notes or the failure under them (`align-items: center`, the decision of #1660 that #1549 keeps), and draws its divider only between people, never after the last one (#1574). A `ui-choice` stands its radio on the middle of the title line (`grid-row: 1`), its title and detail in the second column. The signature preview of `/sprava/nastaveni` starts its portrait with the text beside it, as the receipt draws it. The status line of `/sprava/darci` is one `role="status"` region with one paragraph whose text and look follow the search (`statusLine()`), so a search changes its text once and VoiceOver reads it once (#1684), and each row is one link whose list item has no name of its own. The lists: the administration's payments (`/sprava/platby`) as rows grouped by month under a `h3.ui-list__group-title` ("Říjen 2026") that sticks under the header's back button (`--list-group-title-top`, the header's height in the administration), each row a `button.payment-row` (`aria-haspopup="dialog"`) with the sender (or "Bez jména odesílatele"), the date, "VS …" or "bez VS" and, when paired, "dárce …" (or "dárce bez záznamu"), the amount and at most one state badge (vráceno, jen ručně, Ověření účtu); a waiting payment's row opens the pairing dialog, a paired one's opens the sheet "Přiřazená platba" (`presentation="sheet"`) with the payment, the sender, the symbol and message, the donor with a link to the card, the method and tags, and "Zrušit přiřazení" with its inline confirmation (or the line that an issued receipt blocks it); the sheet closes after an unpairing, a refused one keeps it open with the reason inside it, its donor link hands the sheet's history entry over to the card (`DialogHistory.handOver`), so one Back returns to the payments, and a live change waits while it is open; the filter stacks wherever the list is rows; the donors (`/sprava/darci`) as link rows to the card with the name, "VS …" · e-mail (or "vymazán") · IČO and only the tags that say something (no "běžný účet"); the history (`/sprava/historie`) as rows with the action, the time, the administrator (or server, or "správce bez účtu") and the application, then the server's sentence, the donor card link and the collapsed technical details (with the administrator's e-mail); the donor card (`/sprava/darci/:id`) as the summary over a `.ui-card` of plain `.ui-list` rows of its parts (#849, Jakub 2026-11-14: Údaje, Přihlášení, Bankovní účty "1 účet / n účty / n účtů", Dary "n darů za … Kč", Potvrzení "1 vydané potvrzení / n vydaná / n vydaných potvrzení", Výmaz údajů; a part whose data failed says "Nepodařilo se načíst, otevřete a zkuste znovu", one still loading names no count), each part a page of its own at `/sprava/darci/:id/<part>` (`udaje`, `prihlaseni`, `bankovni-ucty`, `dary`, `potvrzeni`, `vymaz`; an unknown part opens the card) with only that card under the name, the header's back button leading to the card (the route's `parent` is `/sprava/darci/:id`, and `RouteChrome` fills in a parent's `:name` segments from the route's parameters) and, where the header shows no back button, the link "← <name>", its tables stacked as cards (`.ui-table--stacked`), and while its data loads or after it failed the part shows the wait or the failure with a retry under its own title; the Dary part shows the gifts as rows under a heading per year ("2026 · 2 dary · 800 Kč", refunds left out of the sum) with the date, the purpose and pairing, the sender account and symbol, the amount, the refund pill and the receipt number (or "Potvrzení zatím žádné"); on a wide screen the same address shows the whole card and focuses that part; and the donor's gifts (Moje dary and the overview) as rows per year with the date, the purpose and pairing tag, the amount, the refund pill and the receipt download under them. The `ui-list` row is a grid of named areas (`lead title value chevron` over `lead meta value chevron`, one line when a row has no meta), `ui-list__state` sits under the value, `ui-list__extra` spans the row under the title, and rows inside a `ui-card` take the card's padding.

## content

- The pages of the tabs (2.23) are built from `home/sections/`: `landing/` (`/`, `Landing`) shows only `hero` (full-bleed photo under the header, darkened at its top by `--hero-top-scrim` over the 160 px shade, so the white logo stays legible over the light wall, at least `100svh` tall and taller only when its text needs it, so the page fits the visible screen without scrolling in Safari, whose `100vh` is its larger viewport with the toolbar retracted, and in the installed app; copy, the CTA "Přispět" to `/prispet` whenever `ShortViewport.isShort()` is false, across the text column below `phone` and the compact button from there up (design plan J4, Jakub, replacing his decision of 2026-11-05 that phones and tablets show none), and "Kde pomáháme" to `/projekty` only while `CompactLayout.isCompact()` is false too, because below `nav` the tab bar right under it offers Pomáháme; while "Kde pomáháme" shows, the figures of O nás (`stats-bar`, the same component and texts) stand on the buttons' row, right-aligned beside them, in their `on-image` look (`--color-on-image` with `--shadow-text-on-image`, no hairline above, and with `[ownEntrance]="false"`, so they stand at their values at once and enter with the rest of the copy instead of fading in and counting up on their own), so the row grows no taller and the copy above keeps its place clear of the face (`e2e/first-screen.spec.ts` measures the figures too); the figures stand on a ground of their own, the tab bar's dark glass (`ui-glass ui-glass--on-dark`, `--radius-card`), because their row lies over the child's arm and dress (#1608; keeping them off the child altogether needs the hero's original, #1664), four in a row or two by two below the container width `figures`, never three and one (#1605); a short window (a phone on its side) shows neither; the eyebrow and the lead are the full `--color-on-image`, and from `wide` up the copy takes `--content-max-wide` as the header does, so it starts on the logo's edge (#1604); above `nav` the headline breaks after "Nová" (`width: min-content`) on the logo's axis and the overlay darkens the left part of the photo with `--color-image-shade`, fading out by 45 % of the width, before the face, so the copy keeps clear of the child's face and chin and holds WCAG AA contrast against the brightest pixel behind it from a MacBook to a 2560 px monitor (`e2e/first-screen.spec.ts`); the hero's lead is at most `31.25rem` wide (500 px at 16 px); while the hero is crowded (its copy with the figures would not fit the screen, or its lead would lie over the child's face, as at 200 % text on a 1280 × 900 screen, #1607, so the figures give way; crowded by the face, it stays so until the hero's width, the screen's height or the text size changes), its width ends before the child's face (43 % of the photo's laid-out width), and only narrows while the hero keeps its width; above 200 % (twice 16 px in a browser, 17 px in the home-screen app) the hero's lead narrows before the face only down to `18em`, with 24 px of hysteresis; where the column would be narrower it stays wide and may lie over the face, never hidden or shortened (`frontend.md`, Short screens); while the hero is crowded, its left shade holds `--color-image-shade` at 85 % across the lead and fades out over `--hero-shade-fade`: beside the face it ends at the face's edge, over the face it ends after the lead, and once that end reaches the face's right edge it covers the whole photo evenly to the right edge, so no pale strip of the photo shows beside it (#1897); it switches at once with the crowded state and never animates; below `phone` the eyebrow "nadační fond", which repeats the logo right above it, is only visually hidden, so the text and the button keep clear of the face and the page still fits one screen at 200 % text; the copy keeps the header's room above it, the safe-area insets beside it and room for the tab bar under it; where the hero's buttons would reach into the tab bar's strip (from the top of `--tab-bar-clearance`, safe area included), they move wholly below the window's edge: their top edge on the edge of the steady `--full-screen-height`, decided in the same measurement as the figures, instantly, back only with 24 px to spare; a height change from Safari's toolbar or scrolling does not change it; up to the height `hero-lead` (680 px, an iPhone SE) the lead is only visually hidden and the screen reader still reads it, so the copy stays under the child's face (Jakub on #371), and up to `short` (500 px, a phone on its side) the headline takes `--text-hero-short`, capped by the width and the height, so it stays in the left part of the photo beside the face; no footer; the root and the body behind it are `--color-image-shade` (`styles.scss`, `html:has(app-landing), html:has(app-landing) body`, the same rule as the 404 page's dark canvas on phones), because Safari paints the strip under its floating toolbar with the body's background, so that strip continues the photo's dark bottom instead of white; the `html` rule also gives the root `overscroll-behavior: none`, so pulling past the top or bottom no longer drags the photo away from the page, as the rubber band of Safari and the installed app on an iPhone did), `about-page/` (`/o-nas`, "O nás | …", `AboutPage`) asks `GET /api/content/home` once (`HomeApi`) and shows `about` (with its `h1` "Vznik a poslání") with the photo and the people from that answer, `stats-bar` and the contact form, which ends the page, `donate-page/` (`/prispet`, "Přispět | …", `DonatePage`) shows `donate-cta` (its `h1` "Přispějte na pomůcky pro děti") with the signed-in donor's name and symbol from `AuthStore`; news are the Aktuality tab (`news-page/`, below) and projects the Pomáháme tab (`projects-page/`, below); O nás ends with `<app-donate-band>` and the contact form, whose dark band reaches under the tab bar (`:host-context(main.with-tab-bar) .contact` takes the bar's clearance into its own bottom padding through a negative margin, as `.ui-panel-page` does) and, while any part of it is on the screen, marks the root `contact-in-view` (an `IntersectionObserver` on `#kontakt`, dropped with the form), which gives `body` the band's colour (`styles.scss`, `html.contact-in-view body`, beside the landing canvas rule), because Safari paints the strip under its floating toolbar and the overscroll at the end of a page with the body's background; meanwhile `app-root` takes `--color-surface` (`html.contact-in-view app-root`), so the sections above Kontakt, which have no background of their own, stay in the page's colour instead of showing the dark body through (#991, `e2e/page-end.spec.ts`). The sections: `stats-bar` (years counted from the registry entry of 28 October 2005 on the visitor's day; the figures enter row by row through `appReveal` (two to a row, the second row one `--duration-stagger` later; on the screen from the start nothing hides them), and each figure that is a count starts at 70 % of its value, never at zero, once its entrance has ended (the directive's `revealed`) and counts up to it in `--duration-count` on `--ease-out`, driven by a Web Animation without keyframes whose eased progress each frame reads, so it follows the page's animation clock; "100%" and the year 2005 never count; a screen reader reads the final figures all along from a visually hidden copy, printing sets every figure at its value, and with `prefers-reduced-motion`, without `IntersectionObserver` or without `Element.animate` they stand at their values at once (desktop team P13)), `about` (slice 2.23a, the decisions of 2.1 of 2026-10-31: an intro grid (`.ui-intro`) of the head (the `h1` "Vznik a poslání" and the lead on the work with FOD), the photo and the rest; the photo an administrator set at `/sprava/o-nas` (input `photo`, `about.photo` of the home content) is a square cut around its focal point (`focalShape="square"`, 480/960/1 440 px `srcset`, `alt` from the API or empty) inside `<app-zoomable>`, the stock photo without one; the FOD badge sits over its lower edge, flat in the accent fill with no glow, (full width under it up to 768 px) and the caption under it; then the paragraph on the founding years and `<app-stats-bar class="figures">`, the figures inline: serif numbers in the text colour with muted labels under a hairline, no blue band (Jakub 2026-10-31 01:01); the founding paragraph and the link "Kde pomáháme" of the mockup were left out; under it the size container `about-founders`: with a founders' note a `section.quote` with a visually hidden `h2` "Slovo zakladatelů", the drawn quotation mark `app-quote-mark` and the note as a `blockquote` of paragraphs, left-aligned on phones, and the row of signers (`ul.signers`: each the signature as it is, the portrait `app-avatar` of 48 px from the signature's `portrait`, the name and the `role` of the home answer's signature, two columns, one below the container width `pair`; each signer keeps the height of its own content, top-aligned, so the portraits share their top and the names their baseline whatever a neighbour's role or signature holds, #1609); without a note the featured people as cards under the `h2` "Kdo fond vede", and without people from `/sprava/lide-fondu` the built-in founders with their portraits (48 px, 108 px from the 320 px variant above `nav`); then the board line and its footnote in small text; specs `about.spec.ts`), `donate-cta` (`app-payment-panel` with `layout="split"`, into which the section projects the title and lead (`.ui-intro`, `beforeAmount`), the line before paying, the photo (`afterPayStart`), the `h3` "Zaplaťte" (`beforeBankDetails`) and "Co bude dál" (`afterPayment`)), in one order for every width (Jakub 2026-10-24, the audit's option B1); above 768 px and taller than `short` (500 px) it stands in two columns (2026-11-16, so the amount, the account number, the IBAN and the QR show together on the first screen of a computer, at 1280 × 800 too; from `nav` to `laptop` the payment column takes 3 parts to 2, from `wide` the page takes `--content-max-wide`, and from `nav` up Zaplaťte and the title are trimmed to their capitals (`text-box`) so they start level): the title, the lead, the amount, the line before paying and the photo on the left, "Zaplaťte" level with the title on the right with one card holding the bank details and, under them, the QR beside "Naskenujte a přispějte", then the receipt note, which takes the bank box's inner padding, then "Co bude dál" (the decorative photo with its quote only there, so a phone on its side keeps the phone's one column, the dark quote card at the photo's lower right, over the dress and clear of the child's face, opening with the same drawn `app-quote-mark` as the founders' note of O nás in `--color-accent`, #1618): (1) heading "Přispějte na pomůcky pro děti" and one sentence why; (2) "Zvolte částku": preset or custom amount (500 Kč preselected; "Jiná částka" opens an empty field, so the QR says "Nejprve zadejte částku" until an amount is typed; whole crowns with spaces, hint after leaving the field); (3) `.before-pay`, the line before paying: signed out "Chcete potvrzení o daru pro daňové přiznání? Přihlaste se nebo si založte účet ještě před platbou: …", then "Potvrzení vystavíme i na firmu: při registraci zvolte Firma a vyplňte IČO." (`.company-hint`), with "Založit účet" and "Přihlásit se" (they open the dialog; after a sign-in from here the page stays and the focus moves to the "Bankovní spojení" label, now with the donor's symbol), signed in "Platíte jako <name>, variabilní symbol <VS>." (input `donorName` from `AuthStore.donor().name`, shown alone); (4) `h3` "Zaplaťte": the bank details (account, amount, the signed-in donor's variable symbol, IBAN, each with a copy button; stacked bank rows let `Kopírovat` overhang its row by `--space-1-5`; the IBAN row never wraps its button under the value and the IBAN breaks between its halves, then only between groups of four), for a signed-out visitor right under them an amber callout "Potvrzení o daru k platbě bez přihlášení vystavíme, až si založíte účet a ověříte v něm bankovní účet, ze kterého jste platili." whose "založíte účet" opens the registration like "Založit účet" (testers of 2026-10-30 paid by QR without reading the line before paying), then the QR that `spayd()` and `encodeText` (level H) draw in the browser from `PaymentDetailsStore`, by `app-qr-code` at 200 px (nothing is asked after the details: a preset redraws at once; typed digits and "Jiná částka" dim the QR at once (`.qr-image-stale`, `aria-busy`, "Uložit QR kód" disabled) and redraw it 400 ms after the last key, plan D30, so the morph runs once for the finished amount and a new one interrupts a running one; a visually hidden hint on the field says so; an amount that is not valid shows "Nejprve zadejte částku" only after those 400 ms; when the details fail or `spayd()` refuses, the QR's alert with "Zkusit znovu" loads the details again; after the visitor changes the amount a visually hidden `role="status"` line says "QR platba na 1 000 Kč je připravená." once, after 1.2 s without a change, and only when it names another amount than the last one) with its text and "Uložit QR kód" (kept in place and disabled while the QR waits to be redrawn; saves a 720 px PNG made in the browser, `qr-platba-nova-nadeje.png`, "Připravujeme obrázek…" while it runs, then the always rendered `role="status"` line `.qr-saved` names the file and says it is in the downloaded files, until the amount changes); up to 480 px the QR sits above its text; the DOM order is the visual order, so Tab reaches the copy buttons before the QR; (5) `h3` "Co bude dál": signed in, the gift shows in the overview within an hour of the bank booking it (another bank's transfer usually arrives by the next working day) and the yearly receipt is downloaded in the donor account after the year ends; signed out, how an earlier gift paid without signing in is paired once the donor sends one with the symbol from the same account, and that the yearly receipt comes with a donor account; both end with the sentence that the gift can be deducted from the tax base under the income tax act; under the list the grey note "S platbou nám banka předá vaše jméno, číslo účtu a zprávu pro příjemce. Jak s nimi naložíme, popisují zásady ochrany osobních údajů." (`home.donate.privacy`, `.ui-footnote`, the link to `/zasady-ochrany-osobnich-udaju#udaje`; Jakub 2026-11-12: the page where a gift's personal data come from links the policy).
- `detail/` (`content-detail`, both `/aktuality/:slug` and `/projekty/:slug`): loads the article or project by its slug (`NewsApi.publishedBySlug`, `ProjectsApi.publishedBySlug`; when it answers with another slug, a former one, the page navigates to the current address with `replaceUrl`, so the canonical link names it); "Načítáme stránku…" (`role="status"`), a failure as a `ui-notice--alert` with `role="alert"` "Aktualitu se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to znovu." / "Příběh se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to znovu." with "Zkusit znovu", a 404 (`content/news-not-found`, `content/project-not-found`) renders `not-found` at the same address with `[missing]` set to the kind ("Tahle aktualita už na webu není." / "Tenhle příběh už na webu není.", "Všechny aktuality" / "Kde pomáháme" and "Zpět na úvodní stránku") and the tab title "Stránka nenalezena | …"; when loaded, the intro on the page ground with, on a phone only (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`), "← Všechny aktuality" / "← Kde pomáháme" (`/aktuality`, `/projekty`) in the accent colour (from `nav` up the header's back arrow is the one way back), the line `.ui-intro__meta` with the tag as the news card's pill (`.meta-tag`, `--color-accent-soft` ground, `--color-accent` text) and the date or year beside it in `--color-text-subtle` (`.meta-when`), as on the card (#1629), the focused `h1`, the perex as lead, then the photo (`?w=960`, `srcset` 480/960/1440, `alt=""`) and the body rendered by `<app-markdown>` (plain text becomes paragraphs at empty lines, with single line breaks kept), the photo's `alt` from `coverImage.alt` (empty when absent), under a signed news article its signer (`.signer`: `<app-avatar>` of 64 px from the portrait's `?w=480` copy or the initials, the name and the role), then `<app-share-button>` with the title and the canonical address (`canonicalAddress()` of `core/seo/canonical-link.ts`, so `https://nfnn.cz/aktuality/<slug>` or `/projekty/<slug>` whichever host serves the page; not in the preview), and the line "Chcete pomoct dalším dětem? Přispějte na jejich pomůcky." over "Přispět" (`/prispet`) with "Zpět na aktuality" / "Kde všude pomáháme" on a phone and "Všechny aktuality" / "Kde pomáháme" from `nav` up (both to the list; the texts of `content.notFound.news.list` and `content.donateBand.projects`); the header's "Zpět" leads to the list (`parent`). After the article, outside it, `content-detail` projects an `<aside>` named by its `h2` (`--text-3xl` at `--leading-heading`, as the list's "Starší aktuality", #1626) into the article's side slot (not in the preview): "Další aktuality" with up to three newest other news cards (`NewsApi.published(0, 4)`) in the list's grid (`minmax(min(100%, 26rem), 1fr)` columns, from `nav` up a third of the row but at least 20 rem, as the list's, so at 1280 px three cards as wide as the list's; `--space-7` apart, each card its own height; one column beside the article from `wide` up) or "Další příběhy z roku <year>" with up to three other story tiles of the same year, each stretched to its row as in the list (`PublishedStoriesStore`, its kept list when the Pomáháme tab loaded it); nothing when there are none or the list fails. Below `wide` it closes the page under the article; from `wide` the page takes `--content-max-wide` like the header, stays left-aligned and the aside is a side column whose top meets the photo's top edge: the columns are the text measure `minmax(0, 40rem)` and `minmax(0, 24rem)` packed to the start with a `--space-20` gap, so the column stands `--space-20` after the text while the page has room and otherwise shrinks with the text up to the header's right edge, one rule without a step (the article is a subgrid of both columns and of the header and body rows, the header spanning both, the body the first; `e2e/content-pages.spec.ts` measures it at 1600 and 1920 px). The loaded page is `detail/content-article` (`app-content-article [page] [kind] [preview]`, the `ContentPage` built by `newsPage()` / `projectPage()` in `detail/content-page.ts`, which also holds `ContentKind`), so the administration's preview shows the same page: with `preview` the title is an `h3` instead of the focused `h1` and the intro drops the room for the fixed header; `content-detail` focuses the heading through `focusHeading()`. Texts under `content.detail.*`; specs `content-detail.spec.ts` (Markdown and the alt included) and `content-article.spec.ts`.
- `news-page/` (`NewsPage`, `/aktuality`, v3 2.26 "Novinky", after Jakub's mockups of 2026-10-31): the intro (`.ui-intro`) with the focused `h1` "Co je u nás nového" and a lead, then a page of `PAGE_SIZE` = 10 from `NewsApi.published` (`?strana=`, 1-based, anything else is page 1; a newer page change cancels the answer still on its way). Page 1 waits for the news and the stories together, so nothing moves under the visitor once it shows: the projects (`ProjectsApi.published`, pages of 50 one after another, at most `MAX_PROJECT_PAGES` = 20, each project once by id) load once per visit to the page and are kept for a return to page 1 and for a retry; their failure, or no answer within `STORIES_WAIT_MS` = 4 s, only leaves the stories out. On page 1 the newest news leads as `featured-news/` (`app-featured-news [item]`): up to 768 px its photo across the card under a shade, from there up the card split like the donate band into the dark text side and the photo beside it in its own cell of at most 600 × 400 CSS px with the card's radius, so the cover is never upscaled on a Retina screen (`?w=960` with the 480/960/1 440 px `srcset` the server keeps, `sizes` 600 px from 769 px, `focalShape` `newsFeatured`, `fetchpriority="high"`; without a photo the dark card, from 769 px only as tall as its text), the tag and date, the title as an `h2` (in a card wider than `tablet`, the container `featured`, at most three lines, so the actions stay in a 1280 × 800 window with the longest title, #1621; the container is in rem, so enlarged text, an iPad at AX5 too, shows the whole title; the clamped title takes its own `--leading-clamped-heading` (1.25, while the other headings keep `--leading-heading`) and clips `--line-clamp-ink-room` (0.0093 em in both themes) below the top of its fourth line, given back by a negative margin; the clip is `padding-block-end: max(0em, room)` with `max-block-size: calc(3lh + room)`, so a negative room moves the edge up instead of being dropped as an invalid padding. At the leading of 1.2 the descenders of the third line ended about 1 px above the ring of a capital Ů on the fourth, and Chromium (snapped to device pixels) and WebKit (fractional, up to 1 px lower) placed them differently, so no single room fit every title size; at 1.25, measured on 4× screenshots from 860 to 1280 px, the descenders end 0 to 1.75 px above the fourth line and the ring starts 0.75 to 3.25 px below it, and 0.0093 em leaves at least 0.43 px of each in both engines (0.44 px in the control theme's font). `e2e/news-desktop.spec.ts` compares a 4× screenshot of the clip edge as rendered with one where the clip is lifted and the fourth line is transparent, at 1280 and 1100 px; the taller title gives the signer row `--space-4` above it instead of `--space-6` in that card (the text stays centred beside the taller photo, so the actions rise by half of that), so they still fit the 1280 × 800 window), a perex of three lines (two up to 480 px and in that wide card, so the signer row and the actions under it still fit that window), the signer (`app-avatar` of 40 px from `?w=480`, the name and, from 481 px, the role) and the actions, which wrap to the right under the signer when the text is large (from 769 px, with a photo or without, they follow the signer at the text's leading edge, #1622, #1885): `<app-share-button tone="dark" [subject]>` and the link "Číst celé" (its accessible name adds the title; it covers the whole card and shows only a round arrow up to 480 px).
