Then `year-stories/` (`app-year-stories [stories]`, `storiesOfNewestYear()`): "Příběhy z roku N" (each strip names its section by a heading id of its own) with the projects of the newest year in the site's order (each tile a 72 px photo `?w=480`, `focalShape` `storyTile`, or a heart, beside it the title linking to the project and under the title the tag, from 769 px on one line cut with an ellipsis where the strip is wider than `laptop` (the strip's container `stories`, in rem: about 71 rem at 1280 px at the default text, so at 1024 px and at enlarged text, an iPad at AX5 too, the tag wraps whole; not the tile's own width, whose padding and border are in px, so a threshold in rem there would turn over with the root size), both from the tile's top and the title never hyphenated, so the titles of a row line up (#1623); tiles at least 16 rem wide, so at 200 % text a tile keeps its words): up to 768 px a strip of 78 % tiles that scrolls sideways under the finger, above it a grid of every tile in rows, at most four in a row and fewer where four 16 rem tiles do not fit, with no sideways scrolling and the link "Všechny 3 příběhy" / "Všech 8 příběhů" (ICU plural, "Zobrazit v Pomáháme" for one) to `/projekty?rok=N`, which the Pomáháme page of 2.23a reads. Then "Starší aktuality" (on a later page only the visually hidden "Aktuality na straně N", with no leading news and no strip) as `app-news-card`s in an auto-filling grid (from `nav` `minmax(20rem, 1fr)` and at most three columns, so a card stays narrow enough to stand portrait through its container query; the DOM order is the visual order; each card is as tall as its own content, never stretched to its row, so a card without a photo keeps its button under its text, #1625) and the `nav` "Stránky aktualit" with "Novější", "Strana N z M" and "Starší" as the shared pager (`_pager`). The always rendered `role="status"` line (`tabindex="-1"`, collapsed to no height when it says nothing) says "Načítáme aktuality…" (only after `--delay-loading`, timed in TypeScript from the theme token, so `prefers-reduced-motion`, which zeroes every CSS animation delay, never shows it earlier; the empty line is there from the start, the timer stops when the answer arrives or the page is left, and the empty and failure states are never held back; when the text did show, that timer lets the leading news with the stories, or a later page's cards, arrive with `motion.appear`) and, after a retry that loads news, "Aktuality jsou načtené.". Nothing published is a `ui-notice` card in place of the leading news at its natural height, "Zatím tu žádná aktualita není." with "Zpět na úvodní stránku" or "Tato strana neexistuje." with "Na první stranu"; a failure is the same card as a `ui-notice--alert` with `role="alert"`, "Aktuality se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to znovu." or, while `Connectivity.isOffline()`, "Bez připojení se aktuality nenačetly. Až budete online, zkuste to znovu.", each with "Zkusit znovu", which moves the focus to the status line at once and, once loaded, to the first card's link (the status line when nothing is published, which then says the empty card's sentence to screen readers only) or, when it fails again, back to the button (desktop polish P21, P22). The news card (`news-card/`) shows under its perex the signer (`app-avatar` of 40 px from `?w=480`) and, when linked, Sdílet with the article as its `subject`, at the card's trailing edge whether it stands beside the signer or wraps under it (#1620); the tag and the date share a row that wraps at every width; from 769 px, in a card wider than `pager` (the container `news-card`, in rem, above the narrowest card of 20 rem at the default text, so at enlarged text, an iPad at AX5 too, the tag wraps whole instead of losing its letters), the tag keeps one line, cut with an ellipsis, but takes at least 6 em of its text or its whole text, so a date that would leave it less moves under it, and the date never gives its width up to it, so it stays on one line wherever it fits (#1619); below 769 px and in a narrower card the tag wraps whole; from a card 480 px wide (the container `news-card`) its photo stands beside the text (`newsCardSide`); a card wider than `cards`, like one up to `phone`, and the leading news from `tablet` press with `--pressed-scale-surface`. The page ends with the closing Přispět band of the mockups (`<app-donate-band [wide]>`, "O další pomoci napíšeme i díky vám", both links, the band photo from `HomeApi`, 2.23a); until the page first settles, stories included, the content keeps at least `--full-screen-height` (`content--unsettled`), so the band starts below the first window and moves in when scrolled to, and the page under the T12 page fade covers the window. From `nav` the gaps do not add up: `--space-20` between the intro, the leading news, the stories, the older news and the band, `--space-12` above the pager; from `wide` the intro and the content take `--content-max-wide`, lined up with the header. Motion (P31): nothing in the first window moves in; the leading news and the stories, which `.lead` wraps in `motion.appear`, carry no `appReveal`; it sits on "Starší aktuality", the older news (`.grid-item`: by their column in the row, read from the grid's resolved `grid-template-columns` through a `ResizeObserver`; in one column no step, otherwise the column plus one, at most three steps; #1340), the pager and the band. The story tiles lie on `--color-story-tile` (the light `--color-surface-alt`, in the dark `--color-surface-elevated`, so they are no darker than the page), and the strip reaches `--space-1` past its own padding on each side so its tiles line up with the heading. News titles (`.featured-title`, `.news-title`, the stories' heading) take `--numeric-titles`, as the story titles do (#1343). Texts under `content.news.*`; spec `news-page.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/content-pages.spec.ts` (the leading news, the stories, paging by ten, sharing and copying from the feed, the offline failure and the retry), the axe cases (with a signer and the failure) and the device step `news-feed`.
- `projects-page/` (slice 2.23a, `/projekty`, `ProjectsPage`, the Pomáháme tab with `data: { ...LIGHT_HEAD, ...LOADED_AHEAD }`): `PublishedStoriesStore` (root) reads every published project (`ProjectsApi.published` by 50, at most 20 pages) and the look-back years of the news (`NewsApi.published(0, 50)`, each article's `lookBackYear`; a failure there only leaves the links out) once and keeps them, so a return by the back button renders at once and `PageFocus` can focus the link to the story left. The `.ui-intro` with the `h1` "Komu jsme pomohli" and a lead, then the summary: a tile "N příběhů zveřejněných od roku Y" and a tile per category with at least one project (counts, not links; without any, the total tile alone in one column), every tile alike, its number above its caption in the text colour (#1634); the year chips, only with two years or more, (`nav` "Roky", links with `aria-current`) are filters of `?rok=N` and `?rok=vse`: a chip navigates with `scroll: 'manual'` (a modified click opens its link as usual) and the always rendered status line says "Rok 2024: 2 příběhy" or "Všechny roky: …", visible only while the stories load and otherwise visually hidden (`.stories-state--heard`, which an empty line keeps too, so the live region never leaves the accessibility tree), so choosing a year never moves the chips under the pointer (#1630); without the parameter the page opens on the newest year with a published project (Jakub 2026-10-31 15:20). Each year is a `section` with an `h2` of the year and its count and, when an article looks back on it, "Ohlédnutí za rokem Y" to `/aktuality/<slug>`; "Vše" shows each year as a sideways strip of small `app-story-tile`s ending with the link "Všechny 3 příběhy z roku Y" / "Všech 5 příběhů z roku Y" ("Celý rok Y" for one; `?rok=Y`); `StoryStrip` (`story-strip.ts`, `appStoryStrip` on the strip) follows whether it overflows and, on a computer with a fine pointer that hovers (`(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`, so not on a touch tablet) when it does, two outline buttons "Předchozí příběhy" and "Další příběhy" under its end (`aria-controls` the strip, `aria-disabled` at either end, so a button that reaches the end keeps the keyboard's focus) bring the first cut tile to the start or the last one to the end (smooth, instant under reduced motion), and a tile whose link takes the keyboard's focus (`:focus-visible`, never a press of the mouse, which would move the tile from under the pointer) while cut is brought whole to the start; every move is `scrollTo` on the strip itself, never `scrollIntoView`, so the page never scrolls with it; the start is a snap position, since the strip's proximity snapping would undo a scroll to its end (#1631); a year as a grid whose first tile is large (a square cut around the focal point over its photo, with the perex) and the rest small two per row, a last odd one full width (size container `stories`: four columns, two below `tablet`, one below `pair`, so 200 % text gets one column), followed, when there are more years, by the link "Všechny roky" (`?rok=vse`, named "Všechny roky: všechny příběhy" with the part after the colon visually hidden, as the status line says "Všechny roky: 12 příběhů"). On a computer (`CompactLayout` not compact, desktop team P6–P7) a year leaves no empty column: one story is a split card (`size="split"`, a 3:2 photo beside the title and perex, focal shape `storySplit`), two are both large in one row, three put the large tile beside the two small ones in one row, four are four small tiles in one row (a large one would leave a cell empty, #1635), in DOM order; small tiles also show the perex clamped to two lines and keep their photo at its 360:196 cut, so the photos of a row have one height, and a small tile stretched to its row gives the extra height to its text, with the category at the bottom (#1632); the large tile's photo fills it without setting its height, which its row and its `min-height` decide. The tag is the same white pill with accent text on every tile, and the category has no dot (#1633). Over `nav` the page keeps one rhythm (P4): `--space-20` between the intro, the years and the Přispět band, `--space-12` inside a section; from `wide` its head and body take `--content-max-wide` (P3). A tile is the whole card as one stretched link to `/projekty/<slug>` with the tag, the title, the category and, without a photo, a heart, on `--color-surface-elevated` (white in light, lifted in dark; the large tile with a `--color-media-rim` rim, a 1 px border on its own `::after` layer above the photo, since an outline was covered by the photo, P23). On a fine pointer a tile lifts by `--motion-lift` through `motion.hover` on its host, the same lift when its link has `:focus-visible`, and its hover shadow fades in as the host's `::after` opacity; a large or split tile presses with `--pressed-scale-surface` (P31). The year chips change colour through `motion.state`. Loading is the status line; a failure is one `ui-notice` card (`role="alert"`) "Příběhy se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to znovu." with "Zkusit znovu", or offline (status 0, as Aktuality) "Bez připojení se příběhy nenačetly. Až budete online, zkuste to znovu."; after a retry the status line names the year shown and the focus goes to the `h1`, or the alert comes back with the focus on "Zkusit znovu"; "Zatím tu žádný příběh není." is the same card in place of the stories, at its natural height (P21, P22). Each year's head, its grid rows (`[appReveal]` ordered by row: the large tile and the four small ones beside it take two rows, then four per row; on a phone one per row), its strip, the link to every year and the closing band move in (`appReveal`, P31). It ends with `<app-donate-band [showsProjects]="false" [wide]="true">`, as wide as the page from `wide`, rendered once the stories have first answered, so it never stands in the first window while they load. Specs `projects-page.spec.ts`, `published-stories.spec.ts`.
- `donate-band/` (slice 2.23a, `app-donate-band [heading] [lead] [photo] [showsProjects] [wide]`): the closing Přispět band of O nás and Pomáháme (and Aktuality since 2.26); with `[wide]` (Pomáháme and O nás, P3) its container takes `--content-max-wide` from `wide` like the page above it, without a transition, and keeps its text measure: the eyebrow "Přispět", an `h2`, the lead, "Přispět" to `/prispet` and, unless switched off, "Kde pomáháme" to `/projekty`; the photo set at `/sprava/pruh-prispet` (`donateBand.photo` of the home content, `?w=` 480/960/1 440, cut around its focal point, `alt` from the API or empty) or the stock `donate` photo. From `tablet` up the photo takes the right 60 % of the band, 50 % in the `[wide]` band from `wide` (`--band-photo-share`; `sizes` 720 px, so the 1 440 px source stays sharp on a 2× screen), the left part is the band's solid `--color-surface-inverse` and the text's gradient blends into the photo. The band's own box is the size container `donate-band`: narrower than `laptop` (68.75 rem, so at 900 px and, at 200 % text, on every screen up to 2 200 px, where the text's measure would cover the photo's focal point, #1613) the photo stands above the text, at 2 : 1 above `tablet` and at 3 : 2 up to it, and fades into the solid text part at its lower edge. Its edge carries `--color-media-rim` (transparent in light) as a 1 px border on its own `::after` layer above the photo (P23). It is not zoomable: it is the backdrop of a call to action, and its text and buttons lie over it. Below `nav` it takes `--shadow-media-sm`, and the pages before it add no bottom space of their own, so its padding is the one space above it and towards the tab bar.
- `news-card/` and `home/sections/projects/project-card` (`app-news-card`, `app-project-card`): a published article and a project as a card; both take `[linked]`, true by default, and false shows the title without its link for the administration's preview (specs `news-card.spec.ts`, `project-card.spec.ts`). The projects list `list/` (`content-list`) that the Pomáháme tab replaced is gone since 2.23a.
- `privacy-policy/` ("Zásady ochrany osobních údajů"; "Jaké e‑maily posíláme" lists the e-mails a donor gets (the templates in `backend/src/main/resources/templates/email/donors/`: confirmation and one reminder, password reset, e-mail change, security notices, an address used by another registration or account, erasure), says receipts, newsletters and per-gift e-mails are never sent and that these e-mails cannot be turned off; "Jak dlouho údaje uchováváme" says an unconfirmed registration is deleted with all its data 30 days after it was made (#455); "Vaše práva" says what an erasure deletes and what stays: the issued receipts unchanged on the server with the name and address (and a company's identification number) on them, the payment records with the bank statement's account number and payer name, and the record of the administrators' actions (A6; nothing about devices until Phase 2 brings copies onto them); under the intro a `nav` "Obsah" links every part as a numbered list, and each part's `h2` carries the same number in the markup (`.part-number` in the accent of the list's markers, then a no-break space and the title under its own i18n id, so the heading's accessible name reads "1. Správce osobních údajů"; a CSS counter would reach Chromium's accessibility tree only as ". ", #1641), whose `section`s carry the ids `spravce`, `udaje`, `emaily`, `uchovavani`, `prijemci`, `cookies`, `prava`, `kontakt`, `ucinnost`; from the page width `nav` in the root text size (a size container on the page) the same list stands in a column of its own right of the text, on the header's right axis until the gap to the text would pass `--space-20` and then `--space-20` after the text (one `clamp()` of the column gap, so no jump at a breakpoint, desktop team W3), and stays in view while the page scrolls (sticky under the header, as tall as the space above the tab bar and scrolling itself beyond it), and an `IntersectionObserver` over the sections marks the entry of the section in the reading band (15–40 % of the window from its top) with `aria-current="location"` (the earlier one when two are in it; kept across the gap between two sections, and handed to the section before when the reader scrolls back above it); on a phone, or with text too large for two columns, the list stays under the intro; "Evidence darů a potvrzení o daru" also says that `/overit` checks the signature in the browser and sends only the SHA-256 digest of the signed data for the status, and that sharing a verification link shares the receipt data, S-L7; "Hlášení chyb z prohlížeče" says what an error report holds and never holds, that it carries no account or IP address but could be matched by its time to other server records, which happens only when looking for a fault, that it is kept at most 14 days in the application log and in the container log until that is deleted by volume, and the limits name the 30 reports per 15 minutes and IPv6 counted by its /64), `not-found/` ("Chyba 404" as `.ui-intro__meta` above "Tuhle stránku jsme nenašli", links home and to the contact form, and under them a quiet line of underlined text links (not pills, each reaching 44 pt on touch) to "Všechny aktuality" and "Kde pomáháme" that leaves out the variant's own first link; the block is at least `--full-screen-height` minus `--tab-bar-clearance` tall (`min-height`, never `height`) and centred in it vertically with `align-content: safe center`, left-aligned, starting at the top when it does not fit; up to the height `short` (a phone on its side) the lead stands beside the title over two rows and the actions and the quiet line take a full row under both, in the DOM order; up to the height `hero-lead` (680 px, an iPhone SE in Safari or as an app) its band starts closer under the header and its buttons sit closer, so both show above the tab bar at load (#358); with the input `missing` (`news` / `project`) it says the article or project is gone and leads to its list first; `NOT_FOUND_TITLE` is the tab title, `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` while shown, because nginx answers an unknown slug of a detail route with 200 and cannot know it is missing; it stands on the page ground under a light header (`head: 'light'`), as every page under a tab does, so the canvas needs no colour of its own).

## contact

`contact-api.ts` (`POST /api/contact/messages`); `contact-form/` with honeypot, 429 and generic errors (both end with a `mailto:` link to info@nfnn.cz), a request that reached no server (status 0) "Bez připojení se zprávu nepodařilo odeslat. Zpráva vám zůstala; až se připojíte, odešlete ji znovu." (2.7; the send button carries `appNeedsConnection`), "Odesíláme…" while sending, no example placeholders in the name and e-mail fields, the e-mail hint "Odpovíme vám na zadaný e-mail." (the site names no reply time, and the sender gets no confirmation e-mail on purpose), a client check of the backend's address shape (`pattern`, a dot in the domain); a signed-in donor finds the e-mail, and for a person also the name, filled in from `AuthStore.donor()` (only empty fields, also when the session arrives later and again after "Odeslat další zprávu"), editable; a 400 with `errors[]` puts each message at its field through `placeServerErrors` (the first refused field takes the focus) (the e-mail field shows its own "Zadejte e-mail ve tvaru jan@example.com." for any server refusal) instead of the general failure; the thank-you ("Zpráva je odeslaná", "Odpovíme vám e-mailem na <address>.") fills an always rendered `role="status"` region and its heading takes the focus, appears (`motion.appear`, its ✓ through `motion.done`) while the card keeps the height the form had (`heldHeight`, measured at the switch, so nothing below jumps; the form leaves at once, without `motion.leave`, as it does not share a grid cell with the thank-you), and a refusal `form-error` appears through `motion.appear`; "Odeslat další zprávu" brings back an empty form (prefilled again for a signed-in donor) with the focus in the name field; and a line under the button that the data serves only to handle the message, linking the policy (legitimate interest, no consent wording; a `.ui-footnote` starting at the form's edge like an iOS group footer, not centred, #1610). Beside the heading only the e-mail stays; the seat ("Sídlo", no longer "Adresa") and the IČO moved into `accounting-details/` (`app-accounting-details`, Jakub 30. 10. 2026), a `ui-card` placed under the e-mail in the left column on wide screens and after the form below 768 px (grid areas); the section reads eyebrow "Kontakt", `h2` "Napište nám" (which names the form through `aria-labelledby`; the form card has no heading of its own) and "Máte dotaz nebo se chcete zapojit?", and the card comes before the form in the DOM unless `CompactLayout.isCompact()`, so the reading and Tab order follows the screen: heading, details, form on a computer and heading, form, details below `nav` (`e2e/contact-form.spec.ts`): "Údaje pro účetní" as a `dl.ui-data-list` with Název, IČO, Sídlo, Zápis (the registry entry), Číslo účtu and IBAN (from `PaymentDetailsStore` once it holds the payment details, the IBAN through `formatIban`; until the account is known the account row says "Najdete ho v sekci Přispět" linking `/#prispet` and the IBAN row is left out), a `copy-button` for the IČO, the seat, the account and the IBAN, and the line "Potvrzení o daru na firmu získáte, když si při registraci zvolíte Firma a vyplníte IČO."; under the card, in a `.contact-about` wrapper that takes its grid area, the grey note "Jak nakládáme s osobními údaji, popisují zásady ochrany osobních údajů." (`home.contact.legal`, `.ui-footnote--on-inverse`, aligned with the card's padding, Jakub 2026-11-12: like an iOS group footer, the site's privacy link at every width now that the footer is gone, also after a message was sent, when the form's own line is gone; at every width it is hidden while the form with its own privacy line is shown, so the sentence is never there twice, on a computer too since #1610); each row puts its label beside the value while the card is wide enough and above it otherwise (flex-wrap, no breakpoint), and a copy button sits right of its value or, in a narrow card, under it. Texts under `home.contact.accounting.*`, spec `accounting-details.spec.ts`.

## donations (services and helpers)

- `payment-details-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/payment-details` without the access token (`PaymentDetails`: `account` `{ number, iban, recipientName }`, `currency`, `messages` `{ donation, accountVerification }`, `accountVerificationAmountMinor`; `recipientName` is the SPAYD text for `RN`, never shown). The answer passes a shape check and the account check (`paymentDetailsOf`: the IBAN must be the IBAN of the account number, `czech-account.ts`), so a malformed 200 or an account that disagrees with its IBAN becomes a failed load instead of breaking a panel or showing that account. `payment-details-store.ts` (`PaymentDetailsStore`, root): `load()` asks once per page load (again after a failure), `state` is `loading`, `ready` with the details or `failed`; every answer is kept with its `Date` in `localStorage` under `nfnn.payment-details` (`KEPT_PAYMENT_DETAILS`, `{ details, writtenAt }`), and a load that reached no server (`isUnreachable`: status 0, or a gateway status without a problem body) shows the kept details instead (read back through `paymentDetailsOf`, so a changed or broken entry is refused), while an answer of the server that fails the check or is a problem shows none, so a changed account never brings back the old one, with `age` (`{ writtenAt, kept }`) telling the panel how old they are (plan v3 2.7); the Přispět panel, the standing order guide and the add-account dialog share it, so a session asks once. Once the held details are five minutes old (the answer's `max-age`), the next `load()` or the page becoming visible again (`visibilitychange`) asks again with the request header `Cache-Control: no-cache` (the XHR backend ignores Angular's `cache` option, which is set too), keeping the details meanwhile and on a failure, and replaces them only when they changed (an unchanged answer is a 304 from the ETag); `reload()`, behind every "Zkusit znovu", shows the wait and asks the same way. `iban-format.ts` `formatIban` writes an IBAN in groups of four for the panel and the contact section (the panel shows it through `ibanHalves` as two halves of whole groups that do not break inside, so a narrow column breaks it only in its middle, "CZ26 2010 0000" over "0055 4433 2217", never leaving one group alone, while the copy button copies the IBAN as one value), whose "Údaje pro účetní" (`contact/accounting-details`) shows the foundation's account from `PaymentDetailsStore` once the details are held, without asking for them itself.
- `czech-banks.ts`: `bankNameOf(account)` → the short name of the bank of a Czech account (`Česká spořitelna` for `…/0800`) or `null` when the account is not Czech (`bankCodeOf` of `czech-account.ts`, the account's shape only, no modulo 11, since it only names) or its code is not listed; `CZECH_BANK_CODES` holds the `source` (the ČNB list "Kódy bank ČR", `kody_bank_CR.csv`), the day it was fetched (`fetchedOn`, 2026-11-06, the file last changed at the ČNB on 2026-09-25) and the names, shortened by hand without the legal form (`a.s.`, `pobočka`, `S.A.` …; the spec refuses one). These hand-made names are preferred and need no request; to refresh them: download the CSV again, add or drop codes, shorten new names the same way and set `fetchedOn`. `shortBankName(name)` shortens a name as the ČNB writes it (cut at the first comma or a branch marker such as `pobočka`, `organizační složka`, `Filiale`, `Prague Branch`, `v likvidaci`, then trailing legal forms such as `a.s.`, `s.r.o.`, `S.A.`, `SA`, `AG`, `GmbH`, `Co.`, `Ltd.`, `spořitelní družstvo`, case-sensitive; `testing/cnb-bank-names.ts` holds the 47 names of the list fetched 2026-11-06 and the spec checks that none keeps a legal form; the name as written when nothing would be left). `bank-names.ts` (`BankNames`, root): `nameOf(account)` gives the hand-made name, else the server's name of the code shortened, else `null`; `require(account)` asks `GET /api/donations/bank-codes` (`bank-codes-api.ts`) only for a Czech account whose code has no hand-made name and only while `Connectivity` does not know the app is offline, so the usual accounts cause no request; at most one request is in flight, a list once held is kept for the page load, and a failed answer or an empty list (a server that has not downloaded it yet) leaves only the hand-made names until the next `require` (a page or a list that shows such an account again) asks once more. The answer's shape is checked and each name shortened once when it arrives. Only the browser shortens and shows bank names, so there is no shared case (the server keeps the ČNB's names as written, contract "Bank codes"; the e-mails and the connector show the number alone). Logos (contract "Bank logos"): `require(account)` of a Czech account also asks once per page load for `GET /api/donations/bank-logos` (`bank-logos-api.ts`, `BankLogosApi`, public, without the access token, `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN`), never while `Connectivity` knows the app is offline (the next `require` online asks); the answer is kept whole, an empty list included; a failure without an answer (status 0, a lost connection) is asked again at the next `require`, which `app-bank-logo`'s effect repeats when `Connectivity` is online again, while a 429 or another refusal is not asked again until the next page load, so the accounts keep their monograms; `forgetLogos()`, which the administration's Loga bank calls after each saved, downloaded or removed logo, drops the list (an answer still on its way is ignored) so the next `require` asks again; only items with a four-digit code and a UUID image id count. `logoOf(account)` gives `/api/donations/bank-logos/images/<imageId>` (`bankLogoImageUrl`) or `null`. `bank-monogram.ts` (`bankMonogram(shortName)`): an abbreviation of two to four capitals (digits and `&` allowed) leading the name stays whole (`ČSOB`, `PPF`, `J&T`), else the first letters of the first two words (`ČS`, `KB`, `FB` for Fio banka, `MM` for MONETA Money Bank), else the first two letters of a single word (`RA`, `MB`), all in capitals. `bank-logo/`: `<app-bank-mark [src] [name] [size]>` (sizes 24 and 40, `aria-hidden`) draws a round mark: the image (`loading="lazy"`, `width`/`height`, `alt=""`, on `--color-paper`, which is the same in both appearances, since a site icon may be transparent, laid only once the image's `load` came, so a logo still loading shows the mark's own ground, #1728), on its `error` the monogram until another `src` comes, the monogram when there is no image (drawn by CSS from `data-monogram`, so it is not part of the text or the accessible name; sized by the theme's `--text-bank-monogram` and `--text-bank-monogram-long` in container units of the mark, so it never leaves the circle) and a plain bank sign (inline SVG) without a name and at 24 px, where letters would be too small to read and the bank's name follows the number; `<app-bank-logo [account] [size]>` feeds it from `BankNames` (its host is not hidden, only the mark inside it). Specs get `testing/bank-logo-fixtures.ts` (`FakeBankLogosApi`, an empty list unless a spec sets `items`) from `src/test-providers.ts`, so no spec asks for the list unless it provides the real `BankLogosApi`. `bank-account/` (`<app-bank-account [account]>`, `BankNames` through an effect on the input): a Czech account starts with its 24 px `app-bank-logo`, then the account in `.ui-figures` and, when its bank is named, `· <name>` after it in `.bank` (muted, regular weight, may wrap onto the next line); a Czech number is one run of text that breaks only at a `<wbr>` before its bank code (`splitBeforeBankCode`), never inside its digits, so VoiceOver reads it as one item, which separate inline blocks would split; the logo and the number stand in `.number` with `white-space: nowrap`, so no line breaks between the logo and the first line of the number while the number itself still breaks where its own `.ui-figures` allows, and a longer number never leaves the logo alone on a line above it (#1556, `e2e/admin-payment-tables.spec.ts`); a parent styles the element by its own class. It stands in the Odesílatel column and both sheets of `/sprava/platby`, the Bankovní účty table of the donor card and the Účet column of `/sprava/starsi-symboly`; the donor's `bank-accounts-card` starts the row of each Czech account with a 40 px `app-bank-logo` (a foreign account leads with the neutral 40 px `app-bank-mark`, its bank sign, so every row leads in the same 40 px column (`--list-lead-size` set to `--space-10` on the list) and the numbers line up; the mark centres on the number line and the lines under it (bank, ověřeno, gifts without a symbol), while the hint, the confirmed symbols, the offer and the action start under the number in the row's `.ui-list__extra` (`.account-body`); each row is the size container `account`, and below `phone` of it the state badge stands on a line of its own under the number in every row (#1749) and the hint, the offer and the action start on the row's edge under the mark (#1761, #1774), and below `tabs` of it, as with enlarged text on a phone, the mark stands on a line of its own above the number and the row's inline padding narrows from the card's `--space-6` to `--space-4`, so the number keeps its line (`e2e/figures.spec.ts`); the number breaks only before its bank code (`splitBeforeBankCode` with a `<wbr>`), `e2e/largest-text.spec.ts`) and names the bank as the first item of the line under the number instead (`.account-bank`, allowed to wrap, through `BankNames` for every listed account), so the status label keeps its place beside the number. Account numbers inside sentences (the overview's question, the add-account dialog's titles, the status lines after an action), the gift lines of the donor card's Dary (the card's Bankovní účty names the bank) and the shortened `…5399/0800` of a gift's tag stay without the bank.
- `spayd.ts`: `spayd(details, { amountMinor, variableSymbol?, purpose? })` → `{ spayd }` or `{ refused: { fields } }` (`amountMinor`, `variableSymbol`, `details`, in that order): the SPAYD text of a payment (`SPD*1.0*ACC*AM*CC*PT:IP[*X-VS][*RN]*MSG`, the message of the purpose; `PT:IP` asks the payer's bank for an instant payment, Jakub 2026-11-06), refused for an amount outside 1 heller to 9 999 999,99 Kč or not whole hellers, a symbol that is not 1 to 10 digits (or none for `ACCOUNT_VERIFICATION`) and details that are not SPAYD-ready (contract, "`GET /api/donations/payment-details`": among them an IBAN that is not the IBAN of the shown account number, checked by `czech-account.ts`, `ibanOfAccountNumber` and `accountMatchesIban`, on the cases of `shared-cases/payment/account.json`); the cases are `shared-cases/payment/spayd.json` (`spayd.spec.ts` runs each through `spayd()` and then `encodeText` at level H against its namesake in `qr/payment.json`).
- `payment-panel/` (`app-payment-panel`, inputs `variableSymbol`, `idPrefix`, `donate` by default, so the home page keeps `#donate-custom-amount`, and `layout`, `stacked` by default; `split` puts `.pay-start` (the content projected with `beforeAmount`, the amount choice, its projected content and the content projected with `afterPayStart`) and `.pay-details` (the content projected with `beforeBankDetails`, the bank details and everything after them, ending with the content projected with `afterPayment`) side by side above `tablet` and taller than `short`, there `.pay-details` is the size container, and where the layout is not compact nor short the QR stands inside the bank details card under its rows, the card shown with the QR alone while the details load or fail, so the QR never moves; a size container `payment-panel` that stacks its bank rows and sets the QR above its caption as on a phone also when the panel itself is at most 480 px of the root text size wide, as in the two-column home section on a tablet upright or with enlarged text; below `pair` (260 px of the root text size, so in the account sheet at 320 px already at normal text, and on Přispět too) each copy button goes under its value, the figures may wrap (an account number at the `<wbr>` after its slash), and the bank details and the standing order box narrow their side padding and reach into the padding around them, so a copy button at the largest text still fits): the amount choice, then its projected content, then the bank details, then the content projected with the attribute `afterBankDetails` (the signed-out receipt note of the home page, the lead and the standing order guide of the card "Poslat dar"), and the QR with "Uložit QR kód" of the home donate section (2) and (4); on phones and tablets (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, slice 2.23c, Q13) the QR's text block gives way, after the projected content, to the primary "Otevřít v aplikaci banky", which on a device whose `navigator.canShare` takes both the payment file and an image (iOS, `sharesPaymentFiles` and `sharesImages` in `bank-handoff.ts`) shares the PNG of the drawn QR (drawn ahead as below and waited for with the button disabled; shared as "QR platba Nová naděje 500 Kč.png" with the drawn amount, because iOS heads its share sheet with the file's name and ignores the share's `title` and `text`, #387, while a saved or downloaded image keeps `qr-platba-nova-nadeje.png`), because no iOS bank app takes the payment file from the share sheet (Jakub's iPhone test of 5 November 2026; Alza's `pay-via-bank-app` shares the image on iOS too), and when that share is dismissed or refused the notice says to choose "Uložit obrázek" and read the payment from Photos in the bank app; there the second button is "Poslat jako soubor platby", which shares `platba.spayd` (the SPAYD text of the drawn amount, `application/x-shortpaymentdescriptor`) for a bank app that may take it, with its own notice pointing back to the image; a drawing that failed ahead makes the primary share `platba.spayd` instead; elsewhere the primary hands `platba.spayd` to the share sheet where `navigator.canShare` takes the file and otherwise, when the service worker controls the page and reserves the payment (`local-payment-route.ts`, `LocalPaymentRoute.reserve`: a message `nfnn-payment-file` over a `MessageChannel`, answered within 2 s with a random id), opens `/platba.spayd?id=<id>`, which the worker answers on the device (Android, follow-up of 2.23c: `frontend/service-worker/payment-route.js`; the notice then says what to do if no bank opened), and otherwise downloads it (a browser without the worker, a worker of an older release or the safety worker, which do not answer the message); when another amount was picked while the worker was asked, neither the earlier payment opens nor its file downloads, then (where the primary does not share the image) "Poslat QR jako obrázek", which shares the PNG of the drawn QR (`QrCode.pngBlob`, 720 px, drawn ahead after each redraw and waited for with the button disabled, so `navigator.share` is called within the tap before anything is awaited and keeps its user activation on iOS; a drawing that failed ahead leaves the button to save the PNG instead) or, where images cannot be shared or the share is refused, downloads it and is then labelled "Uložit QR kód"; while a share is open both buttons stay focusable with `aria-disabled` and a second tap does nothing, and the answer to a share that comes after another amount was picked says nothing; one always-rendered `role="status"` line after the first button says, while a hand-over runs, what happens ("Otevíráme nabídku sdílení…" while the share sheet is open, so the dimmed buttons in the second or so iOS takes to answer a closed sheet are explained, "Otevíráme platbu v aplikaci banky…" while the worker reserves the payment, "Připravujeme obrázek…" while the image is drawn to be saved; #387), and then what to do when the bank did not open (a dismissed or refused share, or the downloaded file), is cleared by another amount, and says nothing after a share that went through, since the page cannot know whether a bank app took it; the QR itself stays rendered (so its image can be made) in a `role="region"` folded with `hidden` under the disclosure button "Platíte z jiného zařízení?" (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`; the region is named by the button's constant first line), 260 px wide when unfolded (`max-width: 100%`, never a percentage the frame would size itself by: such a width sizes the frame by the SVG's default 300 px and leaves the QR at its left side) with a caption naming the amount (read out too, since the image's label names no amount), and its failure callout stands outside the fold before the buttons, which wait disabled while no payment is drawn; there "Zkusit znovu" moves the focus to the block of the buttons (`tabindex="-1"`, `aria-busy`) while it waits and to "Otevřít v aplikaci banky" once the payment is drawn; on computers the panel is as before, shared since Jakub's decision of 29 October 2026 by the home page and the card "Poslat dar" of the donor overview; the chosen amount (a preset, or a valid "Jiná částka" with its typed value) is kept in memory by the root `ChosenAmountStore` (`chosen-amount.ts`, nothing stored in the browser, 500 Kč after a reload) and shared by both places, so moving from the home page to the overview or back keeps it (testers of 2026-10-30); it exposes `accountState` (`loading`, `ready` with the account, `failed`) and `amount` (the chosen whole crowns, `null` while none is typed) so the standing order guide in the card shows the account of the same answer and the chosen amount, `retry()` and `focusBankDetails()`. Under the bank rows a `role="note"` says "Údaje účtu z 3. 11. 2026 · nemusí být aktuální" ("Údaje účtu nemusí být aktuální" without a known day) while the details are the kept ones or the app is offline (2.7). When the details fail, the bank card keeps the height it had while loading and holds only the `role="alert"` "Platební údaje se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu. Když to nepomůže, napište na info@nfnn.cz." and "Zkusit znovu" (`.bank-failure-retry`, "Načítáme…" while it loads), which keeps the accent text on `--color-surface` with the quiet outline `--color-border`, as the buttons inside `.ui-confirm`, so the only way forward keeps a visible edge on the accent card (#1427). The QR's failure is a callout of two kinds, inside an always-rendered `role="status"` region when it comes with the page and a `role="alert"` once the visitor's own retry fails again: `unavailable` (the details did not load: the error text) and `refused` (the details loaded but `spayd()` or `encodeText` refused them: "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Zaplaťte prosím podle bankovního spojení."); "Zkusit znovu" calls `PaymentDetailsStore.reload()`, moves the focus on computers to the QR box (`tabindex="-1"`) while it waits (on phones and tablets to the block of the buttons, above) and back to the button when it fails again. "Jiná částka" clears the ready line; an amount is announced once, not again when it is picked again, but again after the placeholder or an invalid amount came between. "Jiná částka" renders its field and focuses it within the tap itself (`ChangeDetectorRef.detectChanges()` before `focus()`), also when it is pressed again, because iOS opens the keyboard only for a focus inside the tap's handler (#381); the field is `inputmode="numeric"` with `enterkeyhint="done"`, and Enter lets go of it.
- `gift-arrival/` (Jakub 2026-11-06): a signed-in donor who pays from the payment panel (Přispět or Poslat dar) learns there that the gift arrived. `GiftArrivalWatch`, provided by `PaymentPanel` and enabled only while the panel has a variable symbol and a donor is signed in, starts watching on an intent to pay, never on display alone, because Poslat dar stands on every visit to Můj přehled: a copy of a bank detail (`CopyButton`'s `copied`), a hand-off (Otevřít v aplikaci banky, Poslat jako soubor platby or QR jako obrázek, Uložit QR kód), unfolding Platíte z jiného zařízení?, the QR code at least half in view for 5 s with the tab visible (`IntersectionObserver`; a QR scanned with a phone leaves no other trace), or the tab hidden while any part of the panel is in view and no thanks stand (the donor left for the bank app). The baseline, the gifts there before, is a fresh read at the start of the watch (`GiftFeed.requestGifts()`, read again at the next intent or check when it failed), never the device copy as it was last synced, which may lack a gift imported meanwhile; until it lands nothing counts and no check asks the bank (a check waiting for it asks once it lands). While it watches it follows the `donations` feed (and the resync after a reconnect) and asks the bank through the on-demand check 30, 60, 120, 240 and 480 s after each intent and at once when the tab becomes visible again, never sooner than 30 s after its own previous ask (the server's cooldown would make it a no-op), never while the tab is hidden or `Connectivity` is offline (once when it is back), one check at a time and at most 12 per watch; it ends 10 minutes after the last intent or return (paused while hidden, so a donor who stays long in the bank app is still thanked), on the first arrival, when the panel goes or its page is left for another (a kept page of a tab, `whenLeft`), the symbol goes or another donor signs in. A gift counts as arrived when it belongs to the donor the watch started for, its id is new, it is `RECEIVED` and it is neither an account verification nor an answer about an older symbol (`CONFIRMED_SYMBOL`); a gift paired by the sender account counts, and the client clock plays no part. The gifts come from a `GiftFeed` (`GIFT_FEED`): outside the donor area `RepositoryGiftFeed` (`DonorDonationsRepository.fresh('sync')` for the baseline and on each hint, `checkBankUntilDone()` for the check and, without a live stream, one read after it), in the donor area `DonorDonations` itself (`donor-area` provides it as `GIFT_FEED`), so Můj přehled asks the bank through its own check (a check of the watch leaves the outcome line of the donor's own check alone unless it finds a new gift or the line said the check failed or was unfinished) and follows the gifts through its own reading, with its Kontrolujeme nové platby v bance… and Zobrazit 1 nový dar; only the baseline is a read of its own. The panel shows the thanks right after the content projected with `afterBankDetails`, so above Otevřít v aplikaci banky on a phone and above the QR on a computer, where the donor returns or looks, without scrolling or moving focus: `.ui-notice--status` with an always rendered `role="status"` paragraph, "Váš dar 500 Kč dorazil. Děkujeme!" ("Dorazily 2 dary, celkem 1 500 Kč. Děkujeme!", "Dorazilo 5 darů, …"), the link Zobrazit v přehledu outside Můj přehled, and Zavřít, which returns the focus it held to Otevřít v aplikaci banky or the QR. The thanks stay until closed, the panel goes, the panel loses its variable symbol or another donor (or nobody) is signed in (no timeout, WCAG 2.2.1). Anonymous visitors are never watched.
- `donations-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/me` and `/me/summary` (the API's `year` filter has no caller); `requestBankCheck()` (POST) and `bankCheck()` (GET) on `/me/bank-check`; `checkBankUntilDone()` posts and, while `checking`, reads the check again as soon as `LiveUpdates` hints `bank-import`, else after 2 s without a stream and 15 s with one, for at most 90 s, emitting every answer; `downloadedFile(response, fallbackFileName)` turns a blob answer into `DownloadedFile` (`blob`, `fileName` from `Content-Disposition`: `filename*=UTF-8''` first, then `filename=`, else the fallback) for the administration's downloads; the donor's receipts are drawn in the browser (`receipt-pdf/`, plan 1.9), so the donor area no longer calls the PDF endpoints.
- `bank-accounts-api.ts`: `GET /api/donations/me/bank-accounts` (`ACTIVE`, `AWAITING_PAYMENT`, `DISCONNECTED`, `shared`, `pairingExcluded`, `verificationPaymentOn`, `symbolOffer` with `variableSymbol`, `paymentCount`, `firstMonth`/`lastMonth` as `YYYY-MM`, and `confirmedSymbols`), `POST …/{id}/disconnect|reconnect` (204), `confirmSymbol` / `declineSymbol` on `POST …/{id}/symbol-claim/confirm` (`{ pairedCount, changedYearlyReceipts }`) and `…/decline` (204) with `{ variableSymbol }`; `DonationItem.pairedBy` (`VARIABLE_SYMBOL` | `SENDER_ACCOUNT` | `CONFIRMED_SYMBOL` | null), `senderAccount`, `confirmedSymbol` and `receiptAvailable`.
- `receipt-format.ts`: what a receipt shows, one place for the verification page and the receipt drawn in the browser: `formatReceiptAmount(minor)` (`formatMinorUnits` + `\u00a0Kč`), `formatReceiptDate` (`5. 3. 2026` with ordinary spaces, its own format apart from the no-break `formatDonationDate`, Jakub 2026-11-06), the English values of a bilingual receipt `formatReceiptAmountEn` (`CZK 1,000.50`) and `formatReceiptDateEn` (`15 March 2026`), `fillReceiptText(text, values)` (the frozen texts' `{name}` placeholders with MessageFormat's apostrophe quoting; a missing value throws), `formatReceiptDonor` (name, IČO for a company, `street, postal code city`), `receiptDisplay(receipt)` for a `SignedReceipt`; the donor and amount cases of `shared-cases/receipt/` run in its spec.
- `receipt-pdf/` (plan 1.9): the donor's receipt drawn in the browser from its frozen data, so the server renders no PDF (since 1.10 it has no drawing at all). `receipts-api.ts`, the feature service of both sides: `issue(subject)` → `POST /api/donations/me/receipts` (`{donationId}` or `{year}`, `IssuedReceipt` of `frozen-receipt.ts`), `image(sha256)` → `GET /api/donations/me/receipt-images/{sha256}` as bytes with their media type, and the two `ReceiptSource`s a drawing reads from: `donorSource(subject)` (that issue and those images) and `administrationSource(receiptId)` (1.19: `GET /api/admin/donations/receipts/{id}`, which issues nothing, and `GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-images/{sha256}`). `receipt-document.ts`: `receiptDocument(receipt, issuedAt, captions)` puts every line together from the frozen texts and values (the English line beside the Czech one on a bilingual receipt, English table values in brackets, the footer from `receipt.footer`); the caption beside the verification QR and the images' alternative texts also come from the frozen texts (`receipt.verification.*`, `receipt.signature.alt`, `receipt.portrait.alt`, frozen since 1.10, the alternative texts in Czech), and `captions` (`receipt-captions.ts`, the same words) only fills in for a receipt frozen without them, which only the shared cases still hold and names the file `potvrzeni-o-daru-<number>.pdf` / `potvrzeni-o-darech-<year>.pdf`, on an English receipt `donation-receipt-<number>.pdf` / `donation-summary-<year>.pdf`. `verification-qr.ts`: `verificationSymbol(qrText)` encodes the proof address as a byte segment and the proof digits as a numeric one, level H at the smallest version that holds it, so the foundation's star fits in its middle, else L at the smallest version, without the star (above about 220 gifts; `smallestVersion` of `shared/ui/qr-code/qr-encoder.ts`); `qrPlacement(symbol)` puts a symbol up to version 15 beside the signature as a 46.2 mm square (modules of at least 0.6 mm, Jakub 2026-10-31) and a larger one in an area of its own with modules of 0.6–0.8 mm (100 mm at most). `receipt-art.ts`: the signature and portrait images, PNG, JPEG or the vector JSON of `vector-art.ts` (strict: exact keys, a positive view box, 1–2 000 paths of SVG path commands starting with `M`, fills `#rrggbb`, opacity 0–1, 256 KB at most), each refused unless its SHA-256 is the digest in the frozen data; a raster signature is drawn multiplied onto the signature card (`ReceiptPdfFile.multiplyNextDrawing`, an `ExtGState` with `BM /Multiply`), so the white of a scan or of the person's one signature (Jakub 2026-11-12) does not show as a box, because pdfkit's browser build cannot embed a PNG with an alpha channel. `receipt-style.ts`: `resolveReceiptStyle(element)` reads the theme's `--receipt-*` tokens as text, with no canvas: colours only as literal `#rrggbb`, sizes in pt, margins in mm, leading as a number and the three font files by their content-named file name (`body.<16 hex>.ttf`, `body-bold.…`, `heading.…`); anything else is refused with `ReceiptStyleRefused`, and so is a text colour (`text`, `muted`, `english`) or the QR colour (`code`) below 4.5:1 on the white page (`checkedReceiptStyle`, which `drawReceipt` runs again in the worker). `receipt-layout.ts`, the only module that imports pdfkit (`eslint-confinement.js`), draws an A4 tagged PDF (PDF/UA subset, `lang`, Document, H1, H2, P, Table with TR/TH/TD, Figure with alt text, Link): the vector logo, the replaced-receipt notice, both parties, the gift line or a table whose header repeats as an artifact on every page, the totals, the closing kept on one page (declaration, then the signature block in the layout "3B dopis" of plan 1.16: place and date on the left, on the right a card with the round portrait, the signature, a rule, the name and role), the verification QR in the look of the site's payment QR (Jakub 2026-10-31: dots in `--receipt-color-code`, rounded finder and alignment marks in `--receipt-color-code-mark`, at level H a badge in the middle with the foundation's star in `--receipt-color-code-star` on `--receipt-color-code-star-fill`, 12 % of the symbol's width; the geometry is `brandedQrLayout` of `shared/ui/qr-code/qr-branded-layout.ts`, which the site's QR component draws from too) inside a link to its address with a caption (`receipt-captions.ts`, "Ověření potvrzení" and "Načtěte kód telefonem a uvidíte, co fond podepsal." (Jakub 2026-10-31), on a bilingual receipt with their English ids `…English`; beside the signature the caption stands under the card, and the signature row counts the code in the left column and the card with the caption in the right, so a long caption moves the closing to the next page instead of running into the footer, and a QR in its own area keeps its four-module quiet zone above the bottom margin), and the footer with the page numbers on every page; vector art is clipped to its box (a signature to 60 × 18 mm), and Title, Author and Subject are XML-escaped for the XMP packet only (pdfkit 0.20.2 writes them there raw; the document information dictionary keeps them as written); it is deterministic (the creation date is the issue time, the font subsets are named from the font id). Its specs run in the Node environment (`// @vitest-environment node`, so pdfkit's XMP is the bytes a worker writes) and draw every frozen case in both themes with each theme's own `--receipt-*` values and the captions the site prints: exact page counts (v2's, apart from the listed differences: the gift rows are 1.2 mm lower than v2's so a year of 12 or 13 gifts stays on one page with the larger code (Jakub 2026-10-31), which also brings the 366-gift year from 12 pages to 10, and a bilingual year of 2 or 3 gifts takes a second page for its closing in the default theme), the page estimate of the `phase` events (`estimatedReceiptPages(gifts, bilingual)`, fitted to the default theme) equal to the drawn count, each caption once, no text below the bottom margin, the quiet zone clear, the XMP well-formed with `pdfuaid:part` 1, `dc:title` and `xmp:CreateDate`. `receipt-pdf.worker.ts` is a module worker (its own chunk `worker-<hash>.js`, compiled with `tsconfig.worker.json`) that fetches the fonts the style names from `/receipt-fonts/` (copied from `src/themes/<theme>/receipt/` by `angular.json`; the hash in the name makes them immutable, as the worker chunk is), says `ready`, draws the request it gets, posts `page` per page and `done` with the transferred bytes, or `failed` with a reason and the closed `name` of what it caught (`errorNameOf`, `core/errors/error-vocabulary.ts`): `tools-missing` when a font answers 404 (`receipt-fonts.ts`, `loadReceiptFonts`: a tab older than a deploy that changed the receipt fonts, which then takes the same one reload as a lost worker chunk), `fonts` for any other font failure, `drawing` for a throw in the layout. `receipt-pdf.ts`: `ReceiptPdf.draw(source)` emits the phases `data` (the receipt from its source and the images), `tools` (the worker loading) and `drawing` (with the page), then `done` with the `DownloadedFile`; it fails with `ReceiptPdfFailure` `tools-missing` (the worker failed before it said ready, as when a deploy removed its chunk, or could not be started), `fonts`, `image`, `unprintable` (the frozen data holds a character outside the printable set, found before any worker starts), `style` (the theme's receipt tokens were refused) or `drawing` (any other throw, on the main thread too), or after 120 s without an event (`drawing`, named `TimeoutError`); each failure carries the closed `errorName` of its cause, and `ReceiptPdf` reports it through `ErrorReporter` (place `global`): `pdf-load` for `tools-missing` and `fonts`, `pdf-render` for `drawing`, `image` and `style`, nothing for `unprintable` (the data, not the browser) or a refusal of the server; unsubscribing terminates the worker; specs replace the worker and the style through `RECEIPT_PDF_ENVIRONMENT` (`testing/fake-receipt-worker.ts`). `pdf-delivery.ts`: `PdfDelivery.needsSecondTap(file)` is true on an iPhone or iPad (iPadOS's desktop agent with touch too) whose `navigator.canShare` takes the file, because Safari drops the tap's user activation while the PDF is drawn and would refuse both the download and the share sheet; `save(file)` goes through `saveBlob`, `share(file)` answers `shared`, `dismissed` (the sheet closed) or `failed`. `receipt-restart.ts`: `ReceiptRestart.tryRestart(download)` reloads the page once per tab (`sessionStorage` `nfnn.receipt-restart`) with `?stahnout=rok-<year>` or `dar-<id>` and answers `reloading`, `reloaded-already` (this tab already reloaded for that download; the marker is cleared) or `unavailable` (no session storage, so no reload); `finished()` clears the marker, `abandoned(download)` only when it names that download.
  Bulk (slice 2.29): `receipt-layout.ts` splits into `ReceiptPdfFile` (the document, its fonts, the `Document` structure root and an image cache that opens each raster once with `doc.openImage`) and `ReceiptLayout` (one receipt in its own structure parent, footer pages numbered within that receipt), so `drawReceipt` draws exactly as before (the 14 frozen cases with a portrait give the same bytes) and `drawReceiptBundle(items, {info, duplex}, …)` puts each receipt in its own `Sect` on a new page, with a blank page before it when `duplex` needs it to start on an odd page, and flushes the pages after each receipt to keep the worker's memory flat. Its types sit in `receipt-bundle-options.ts`, outside the pdfkit confinement. The worker takes a `bundle` request beside the single one (`receipt-worker-messages.ts`) and reports `{kind: 'receipt', index}` after each receipt; `ReceiptPdf.drawBundle` fetches each image digest once (`ReceiptsApi.administrationImage`), sets the PDF's creation date to the latest `issuedAt` and emits `images`, `tools`, `drawing {receipt, receipts}` and `done`. `receipt-bundle.spec.ts` proves that each receipt's pages in a bundle print exactly what its single PDF prints, footer numbers included.
- `verification/` (the page `/overit`, public shell, plan 1.8, D32): `receipt-proof.ts` reads the proof of `shared-cases/verification/payload.json` strictly (digits back to bytes with `BigInt`, the body as `ProofBytesReader`/`ReceiptProofBody` read it on the backend, every rejection reason in the backend's order, only a key whose status is `active` verifies, `statusDigest` the SHA-256 of the signed message and the signature); `ed25519.ts` verifies with WebCrypto `Ed25519` and, when the browser lacks it, with `noble-ed25519.ts` (`@noble/curves` 2.4.0, `zip215: false`), the only file `eslint-confinement.js` lets import it and only by `import()`, so the library is a lazy chunk the WebCrypto path never loads; `receipt-keys.ts` (`trustedKeys(list, hostname)`) checks each entry of a key list and drops a bad one, counts a key id listed twice as revoked, and trusts the RFC 8032 test keys only on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` and `[::1]`; `receipt-keys-api.ts` (`GET /api/donations/receipt-keys` with the request header `Cache-Control: no-cache`, the app's HTTP backend being XHR) and `receipt-status-api.ts` (`POST /api/donations/receipts/status` with `{ digest }`) both send `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN`; `receipt-key-store.ts` keeps the last trusted list in `localStorage` `nfnn.receipt-keys` (every access in try/catch, replaced whole); `receipt-verifier.ts` (`ReceiptVerifier.verify(fragment, progress)`) reads with the kept keys, fetches the list when the browser says it is online (5 s), keeps it, reads again, reports `asking-status` and asks the status (5 s) only for a valid signature; a key list failure is offline only when no response came at all (status 0) or the browser is offline, anything else (429, 5xx, timeout) is `no-answer`, and a list that is not a list is `keys-refused`; once the list came, any status failure or unknown answer is `unanswered`, never offline; outcomes `empty`, `broken`, `newer` (also a reserved flag), `no-connection`, `no-answer` (no keys), `keys-refused`, `failed` (an exception while verifying, reported as `verify-signature`/`global`; a code that is broken, invalid or revoked is no browser failure and reports nothing), `invalid`, `revoked`, `unknown-key` (`reach` `online`, `offline` or `no-answer`) and `signed` with the status `active`, `replaced`, `withdrawn`, `outdated`, `unknown`, `offline`, `no-answer` (key list failed, kept keys) or `unanswered` (status asked, no answer). `verification-page` drops the fragment with `Location.replaceState('/overit')`, sets `robots` `noindex` while shown (nginx also sends `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` for `/overit`, mirrored by `scripts/serve-dist.mjs`), says "Ověřujeme potvrzení…" with a `role="status"` line that changes to "Podpis sedí, ptáme se fondu na stav potvrzení…" once the signature has passed, and focuses the verdict of the result, and on "Zkusit znovu" that of the checking state: the page keeps one `h1`, "Ověření potvrzení o daru", in every state, and each verdict or the checking state is an `h2` (`#verify-title`, `tabindex="-1"`) in its card (J-O1, Jakub 2026-11-14); `verification-result` shows the result: green (✓) only for a valid signature the foundation keeps as `active` or one checked offline (with the sentence that a replacement cannot be seen), a caution (!) for `replaced`, `outdated` (1.26, Jakub 2026-11-05: "Potvrzení platilo ke dni vystavení" and that the donor's gifts changed since, so only a newer receipt counts; without one, write to info@nfnn.cz with the receipt's number), `unknown` (Jakub 2026-10-30: never green), no usable answer from the foundation ("Podpis sedí, ale fond teď neodpověděl, zda potvrzení vede", with "Zkusit znovu", a primary button across the card on a phone (`ui-button--block` while `CompactLayout.isCompact()`) and sized by its text from `nav` up, #1639), an unknown key offline or without an answer, no keys, a refused key list ("Neověřeno: seznam klíčů fondu teď nejde použít", no retry, write to info@nfnn.cz), a broken or newer code (a broken one says to scan it again only when it was read by the camera or from a photo, `ProofInput`'s `found` carrying `{ fragment, scanned }`; from the address, a pasted link or a PDF it says to open the link from the PDF again or read its QR code) and a failure, red (×) for `withdrawn`, `invalid`, `revoked` (write to info@nfnn.cz with the receipt number; no new receipt is promised, since the web cannot issue one under a new key yet) and a key the foundation does not publish; the signed data as a `ui-data-list` (number, issue day, the receipt it replaces, donor, IČO, address, gift day and amount or year and total; the amount or total `.verify-total` closes the list on a row of its own above a separator line, its figure `--text-2xl` bold, the strongest figure of the result, #1640) and, for a yearly receipt, the table "Jednotlivé dary" with each day as a row header and amounts on one line; under every result that checked a signature a line saying what left the device (the digest after the foundation answered, the digest without an answer after an unanswered status call, nothing otherwise), and always the hint that the foundation verifies only at nfnn.cz/overit. Without a code the page offers `proof-input` (Jakub 2026-10-31, from the plan's admin scanner 2.30 brought to the public page): under three visually hidden `h2`s "Načíst fotoaparátem" (only when `navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia` exists), "Nahrát soubor" and "Vložit odkaz". "Naskenovat QR kód" opens the camera through `openScanCamera` (`scan-device.ts`, plan v3 2.30): the camera last used to scan on this device (its `deviceId` in `localStorage` `nfnn.scan-camera`, kept when a camera opens unless it faces the user, every access in try/catch, forgotten when it is gone, `NotFoundError` or `OverconstrainedError`, not when it is busy), else the rear camera, else any camera, each asked for 1920 × 1080, and a refused permission (`NotAllowedError`, `SecurityError`) ends the chain at once; an opened camera is asked for continuous focus where its track offers it, with the same size, waited for at most a second, and a camera that fails while it is set up is turned off; the reads alternate between a square from the centre of the frame at its native resolution (at most 1 600 px) and the whole frame scaled to at most 1 280 px, so a dense code held close (version 40, `e2e/pwa/camera-largest.spec.ts`) is read without scaling it down; it shows a muted inline `<video>` named "Náhled z fotoaparátu" with "Zastavit skenování" (which takes the focus when the scan button it replaces had it; stopping turns off every track and focuses the scan button again), and reads a frame 250 ms after the previous read ended, until a timer of its own ends the scan after two minutes with what to try; a preview that cannot play, a frame that cannot be read or a camera that ends by itself stops the scan with "Fotoaparát se nepodařilo spustit…", a hidden tab turns the camera off (the scan button takes the focus if it was lost), and a code handed over by any way turns it off too; when the scan ends by itself the scan button takes the focus only if it was lost; "Nahrát PDF nebo fotku" (`app-file-button`, `application/pdf,image/*`, at most 20 MB) reads a PDF with `pdf-proof-link.ts` (the first `/URI` string of the file, literal or hex, a hex string in UTF-16 included, that is a proof link; the PDF is not rendered, so it finds the link the browser-drawn receipt carries on its QR code and nothing in a scanned or re-saved PDF without link annotations) and an image through `scan-device.ts` (the size from its PNG, JPEG or WebP header by `picture-size.ts`, more than 50 megapixels refused as "Obrázek je příliš velký…" and any other format, GIF included, as unreadable, then `createImageBitmap`, upright by its EXIF orientation, scaled to at most 2 400 px into a canvas); `pdf-proof-link.ts` looks at no more than 64 `/URI` strings and reads each over at most 64 KiB, so a crafted PDF cannot hold the page; "Odkaz z QR kódu" is a template-driven field with `appProofLink` (`proof-link-validator.ts`). Every way accepts only the exact prefix `https://nfnn.cz/overit#` followed by digits (`proof-link.ts`, at most 7 089 digits; a code of another sender says "Tento QR kód nepatří k potvrzení fondu Nová naděje…" and scanning goes on), never navigates anywhere and hands the digits to the page, which verifies them in place without touching the address or the history, so the code stays out of the URL; after any result "Ověřit další potvrzení" returns to the empty page and focuses its `h1`. The page looks exactly like a donor section (Jakub 2026-11-14, J-O3: "Ověřit má vypadat úplně stejně jako dárcovská sekce nebo sekce správa"), with their blocks and nothing of its own: route data `head: 'light'`; the host carries `ui-grouped` and paints `--color-surface-grouped` from under the header, the donor area's column (960 px with `--space-8`/`--space-6` padding at wide widths, `--space-2`/`--space-4` below `nav` through `CompactLayout`, centred, since the page has no side panel), the donor sections' `app-page-title` and the shortened intro in the look of Potvrzení o daru's intro (J-O2, i18n id `donor.verify.empty.intro`); every block is a donor card (#850, as Potvrzení o daru, which links here): the scan ("Načíst fotoaparátem") and the file ("Nahrát soubor") are each a `.ui-card` with its `h2` as `.ui-card__title` in its `.ui-card__bar` over one action row of a plain `.ui-list` (`.ui-list__row--action`, `.ui-list__glyph`), the preview a row of the scan's card, the file `app-file-button` with `look="row"`, the file's hint a `.ui-card__footer`, the status lines plain lines under the row and the problems `.ui-callout--danger`; the link is a `.ui-card--padded` with its `.ui-card__title`, the form field and a primary button, as the profile card; a verdict or the check under way is a `.ui-card--padded` with an `.ui-list__tile` of its tone beside its `.ui-card__title` `h2`; the data are a `.ui-data-list` in a `.ui-card--padded` titled "Údaje v kódu", as Údaje shows the donor's data, and the gifts list rows of a card titled "Jednotlivé dary (n)" (`h3`) in its `.ui-card__bar`, whose amount drops under its day in a narrow card; the line on what left the device is a `.ui-footnote` under the cards, "Ověřit další potvrzení" an action row of a card of its own, and the domain hint a `.ui-footnote`: without a code right under the intro, before any way to read a code, so the visitor sees it before handing over a file (Jakub 2026-11-16), and after a code at the end, on the inset (`--space-6`) of the line above it, so both notes under a result start on one edge (#1638). From `bp.up(tablet)`, when `proof-input` (its own size container `proof-input`) is wider than `tablet` in the root text size, its three cards stand side by side in three equal columns, each a flex column only as tall as its own content (`align-items: start`, so the camera card, which has no hint, keeps its action row right under its title, #1637; the file's hint under its title, the bar's line dropped and one above each action row), so the choice fits above the tab bar at 1440 × 900; narrower, with the side panel of `/sprava` or at enlarged text they stay one under another. The page keeps the public chrome (the tab bar, and the site footer on wide screens). `qr-reader.ts` (`QrReader`) asks the browser's `BarcodeDetector` for `qr_code` where it has one and otherwise, or when it finds nothing, the module worker `qr-decoder.worker.ts` (its own `worker-*` chunk, one per page, answers matched to their read by id, ended when the page is left or after a 10 s timeout, which gives every waiting read nothing; a worker that fails before its first answer, or cannot be started, is reported as `qr-encode`/`global` (the closest kind the closed list has for the QR code worker), and after three such starts in a row the reader starts no more and rejects every read with `QrReaderOutdated`, which `proof-input` shows as "Web se mezitím aktualizoval. Obnovte prosím stránku a načtěte kód znovu."), which alone loads `qr-decoding.ts`, the only importer of `jsqr` 1.4.0 (`eslint-confinement.js`), so jsQR is never in the page's bundles. Two `role="status"` lines, always rendered (an empty one is taken out of the layout with `position: absolute`, never `display: none`, so it stays in the accessibility tree), say under the camera "Spouštíme fotoaparát…" or "Namiřte fotoaparát na QR kód z potvrzení." and under the file "Čteme kód ze souboru…", and a `role="alert"` callout under the way that caused it names a refused or missing camera, a failed camera or a scan that gave up, a PDF without a verification link, an image without a code, an unreadable or too large file or picture. The gifts of a yearly receipt are list rows (date as the title, amount as the value), as Moje dary lists them. The tone of a verdict is the shared `.ui-list__tile` (`--success` green for ✓, `--warning` amber for !, `--danger` red for ×).
- `donation-month.ts`: `formatMonth('2025-01')` → `leden 2025` (names `@@donor.month.january` … `december`), `formatMonthRange(first, last)` → `říjen 2026`, `březen–říjen 2026` or `leden 2025 – říjen 2026`, with no-break spaces and no locale data.
- `donation-date.ts`: `YYYY-MM-DD` → `15. 3. 2026` without locale data (no leading zeros in day and month, ČSN 01 6910, Jakub 2026-11-06), a no-break space between day and month and an ordinary one before the year; `formatBankTime` → Prague `5. 1. 2026 8:05` (the date as `formatDonationDate` joins it, an ordinary space before the time) (hour without a leading zero, minutes two digits, as the backend's e-mails), `formatPragueClock` → its time alone (`8:05`). `donation-amount.ts`: whole crowns without decimals, hellers always two (`1 000,50`). `testing/` fixtures.

## operations

- `setup/setup-api.ts` (`SetupApi`, `providedIn: 'root'`): `status()` → `GET /api/operations/setup` (`SetupStatusResponse`, sent with `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN`: the endpoint is public, and a session token the restarted backend no longer accepts would make every poll fail), `unlock(code)`, `sendTestEmail(code, request)` → `POST /api/operations/setup/test-email` (`SetupTestEmailRequest`: `MailServerRequest` plus `sender` and `recipient`; `TestEmailResponse` with `outcome: TestEmailOutcome`, `sent`, `recipient`), `complete(request)` (`SetupRequest`, `SettingValueRequest`, `SettingsSavedResponse`), and `watchRestart()`, which polls the status every `RESTART_POLL_MS` (3 s) and answers `'restarted'` after a failed poll followed by an answer (or after ten answers in a row, when the restart was faster than a poll) and `'slow'` after 100 polls; before answering `'restarted'` it refreshes an open session once (`AuthStore.refresh()`, a refusal ignored), because the access token's audience is `PUBLIC_URL` + `/api` and a changed site address makes the restarted backend refuse the old token with `auth/unauthenticated`, which the interceptor does not refresh; `SettingName` lists the setting names of the contract. `setting-labels.ts`: `SETTING_LABELS` (Czech label per setting, `@@operations.setting.<NAME>`), `settingLabel(name)` and `settingErrors(failure)`, which turns the `errors[]` of a 400 `operations/invalid-settings` into "Label: message" lines; shared with the administration's settings page. `test-email.ts`: `testEmailNotice(result, { host, sender })` turns a `TestEmailResponse` into the `Notice` both pages show (`@@operations.testEmail.<outcome>`): `SENT` "Zkušební e-mail odešel na {recipient}. Podívejte se prosím do schránky, i do nevyžádané pošty. Když do pár minut nedorazí, zkontrolujte adresu odesílatele a ověření její domény v Brevo." as a status, and as alerts: cannot connect to {host} (check the server name and port), {host} did not answer within 10 seconds (check the port, Brevo uses 587), no STARTTLS so the password was not sent, the sign-in refused (check the name and the SMTP key), the sender {sender} refused (verify the sender and its domain in Brevo), the recipient refused, or a general failure.
- `setup/first-setup` (`/prvni-nastaveni`, public shell): asks the status ("Zjišťujeme, jestli je web nastavený…"; a failure says to wait a minute with "Zkusit znovu"). Already set up: "Web už je nastavený" with a link to `/sprava/nastaveni`. With `codeRequired`: `h1` "První nastavení webu", a lead saying where the code is (ZimaOS → the app → terminal and logs → container `nfnn-backend`) and that the home network needs none, the field "Kód z logu" ("Opište prosím kód z logu." when empty) and "Pokračovat" ("Ověřujeme kód…"); a 403 says the code changes with every start. Otherwise, or once unlocked, the form in five numbered sections: 1. Web ("Adresa webu", filled with the page's origin when it is `https://`, else `https://nfnn.cz`; the field checks `https?://` without `?`/`#`), 2. Správce ("E-mail prvního správce", required; before the e-mails since the test e-mail goes there), 3. E-maily (server `smtp-relay.brevo.com`, port 587, name and password in `<app-password-reveal>`, both required while "Server vyžaduje přihlášení" is ticked, the checkboxes for sign-in and STARTTLS folded in `<details>` "Pokročilé nastavení serveru", sender and contact recipient `info@nfnn.cz`; at its end the hint "Zkušební e-mail přijde na e-mail prvního správce z kroku 2. …" and "Poslat zkušební e-mail" ("Posíláme zkušební e-mail…"; Jakub 2026-10-24), which first marks the involved fields that are invalid, says "Než zkušební e-mail pošleme, doplňte prosím označená pole." and focuses the first of them, and otherwise sends the typed server, sender and first administrator and shows `testEmailNotice` beside the button), 4. Banka ("Token Fio API (jen pro čtení, nepovinné)"), 5. Klíč k zálohám (Jakub 2026-10-28: the backups are encrypted and the setup does not go on until the key is saved): a lead saying the backups are encrypted with it and cannot be restored without it, the key from `SetupApi.backupKey(code)` (`GET /api/operations/setup/backup-key`, asked whenever the form opens and the key is not loaded; "Načítáme klíč k zálohám…", a failure with "Načíst znovu", 409 `backup-key-unavailable` a warning that the key appears later in Zálohy, and then the setup goes on without it) shown by `features/operations/backup-key/backup-key-panel` (`app-backup-key-panel`: "Klíč k zálohám je těchto 24 slov", an ordered list in a monospace grid (two columns on a phone) computed in the browser by `backup-key-words.ts` (with its own `bech32.ts` and synchronous `sha256.ts`, and the list `czech-wordlist.ts`); the `AGE-SECRET-KEY-1…` text itself is not shown (Jakub 2026-10-29: one form only); "Kopírovat", which copies the 24 words separated by spaces; "Stáhnout jako soubor", which saves `nfnn-klic-k-zaloham.txt`, an age identity file whose `#` lines explain how to restore and carry the 24 numbered words, so `age -d -i` takes it as it is and a printout holds both forms; a hint to keep the words in a password manager or on paper, four letters a word being enough, never only on the server), then "Mám uloženo, ověřit": the words disappear and three required fields "N. slovo klíče" ask for the positions the API drew, so they are typed from the saved copy (`backup-key-word-check.ts`: `appBackupKeyWord` compares the typed word read as the backend reads it, "Slovo nesedí s klíčem …" otherwise; autocomplete, autocorrect, autocapitalization and spellcheck off; focus on the first field), with "Ukázat slova znovu" to go back; "Uložit a spustit" before that step says at the button to save and check the words first and focuses it; the words are sent as `backupKeyWords`; a note that the web restarts, and "Uložit a spustit" ("Ukládáme…"); under the lead a "Budete potřebovat:" list (Brevo SMTP name and key, a verified sender domain, the administrator's e-mail, optionally the Fio token). Unless a test e-mail was sent with exactly the server and sender about to be saved, "Uložit a spustit" first shows the `role="alert"` warning "Zkušební e-mail s těmito údaji zatím neodešel. Bez funkčních e-mailů se správce nepřihlásí." with "Poslat zkušební e-mail" (focus to the test button, then the test) and "Uložit i tak". A 400 puts each refused value at its field (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and lists the rest under the form by label; success shows "Web se restartuje" with a spinner while `watchRestart()` runs, then "Web je nastavený" with the four next steps as a numbered list (register with the administrator's address, open the link, turn on the second factor in Údaje a zabezpečení, sign in again with the code) and "Zaregistrovat se" (opens the sign-in dialog in registration mode with that address filled in), or after 100 polls an alert pointing at the container log with "Zkusit znovu". Above the form a box offers "Obnovit ze zálohy" for a site moved or recovered from another server: the panel (a form) "Obnovit web ze zálohy" asks for the `.dump.age` file ("Soubor zálohy", `accept=".age,.dump"`; an older `.dump` is accepted too, another ending is named at once under the field, "Tohle není soubor zálohy …") and, for a `.dump.age`, the field "Klíč k zálohám" in `<app-password-reveal>` (required, the key or its 24 words; `appBackupKey` checks it with `parseBackupKey` and the canonical `AGE-SECRET-KEY-1…` form is sent); "Nahrát a obnovit" (disabled until a backup file is chosen) streams it with `SetupApi.uploadBackup(code, file, key)` (`PUT /api/operations/setup/restore`, the code in `X-Nfnn-Setup-Code`, the key in `X-Nfnn-Backup-Key`) showing "Nahráváme zálohu… N %", "Zpět k nastavení" returns. Then "Obnovujeme web ze zálohy" waits with `watchRestart(RESTORE_MAX_POLLS)` and asks the status: setup no longer required → "Web je obnovený ze zálohy" (sign in with the old administrator account; the backup brought the SMTP password and the Fio token with the backup key, and only a backup from an earlier version needs them entered again in Nastavení) with "Přihlásit se" (sign-in dialog returning to `/sprava/nastaveni`); still required and `lastRestore` of this request OK → back to the form with "Záloha je obnovená. Pochází z doby před prvním nastavením …"; failed → the restore panel with "Obnova se nepovedla: {error}.
