Web zůstal prázdný …"; not taken → "Kontejner záloh si obnovu do pěti minut nevzal …"; a code that changed with the restart returns to the code step first. `setup-redirect.ts` (`SetupRedirect.checkOnce()`, called by the public shell): asks the status once per load; while the setup is required it sends every other page back to `/prvni-nastaveni` and asks again after each navigation away from it, and stops once the status says it is done. Specs `first-setup.spec.ts`, `setup-api.spec.ts` and `setup-redirect.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/first-setup.spec.ts` (home network: the home page leads to the setup, saves everything and waits for the restart, the registration gets the administrator's address; the backup key shown, downloaded and three of its words sent; saving without a test e-mail warns first; the test e-mail goes with the typed values to the first administrator; leaving for the home page comes back to the setup; from elsewhere: the code unlocks the form; a backup uploaded and restored ending at the sign-in; the form at 390 px) and the axe case for the code step, the form with its errors and the backup key, and the restore panel with a refused file and an encrypted file without its key.

## donors

- `login-dialog/`: sign-in and registration through `AuthStore`, a `[tall]` `[grouped]` sheet below `nav` (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`) and a centred grouped dialog above it, in the look of the account sheet (#911, Jakub 2026-11-14): the logo at the top left on the row of the round ×, the switches (Přihlášení / Registrace, Fyzická osoba / Firma, Heslem / Přístupovým klíčem) as the iOS segmented control `.segmented` (the chosen part a raised `--color-segment-thumb` with `--shadow-segment-thumb` on the `--color-fill` track, wrapping at large text; the first "Posuvník výběru": one `aria-hidden` `.segment-thumb` under the buttons, placed by `SegmentThumb` (`segment-thumb.ts`, the pattern of the tab bar's `pill-at-rest.ts`), takes the chosen segment's width at once and glides only by `translate` in percent of its own width; it jumps without gliding at its first placement, on a resize that moves it in percent of its width and under reduced motion (a resize that keeps its percentage, as the dialog widening for Registrace on a computer, lets a running glide finish), and while the segments wrap onto two rows, or before it is placed, the chosen button draws the raised look itself; `appRadioGroup` and `aria-pressed` stay as they were), the logo hidden on short screens (`bp.height-down(short)`, a phone on its side) so the field fits above the keyboard, with the first row keeping room for the ×, every step after the first change of step moving in with `motion.appear` and the form's alert and `role="status"` lines with its opacity only, the fields in `.ui-field-group` cards (PSČ and Město side by side with a hairline between them, stacked below `phone`), each submit a `.ui-button--primary.ui-button--grouped` capsule and every other full-width button (the passkey, Poslat znovu, Zpět na přihlášení) a `.ui-button--grouped` row in the accent, and the recovery details a card of rows; not in the initial bundle (#998): `App` asks `LOGIN_DIALOG_LOADER` (`src/app/login-dialog-loader.ts`, an `import()`) for the component the first time `LoginDialogService.isOpen()` turns true and shows it through `NgComponentOutlet` once it arrives (from the service worker's cache when it has prefetched the build, else from the network), loads it once per page load, and when the code cannot be loaded closes the dialog's state and hands the error to `ChunkLoadRecovery.handleImportFailure`, whose bar offers "Načíst znovu"; the next opening asks for the code again; while `Connectivity` says offline, the two starting tabs give way to `.offline-entry` (`h3` "Jste offline" focused, "Přihlásit se ani zaregistrovat se bez připojení nejde. Jakmile budete připojeni, přihlášení se tu ukáže samo.", an always rendered `role="status"` line and "Zkusit znovu", which asks `Connectivity.tryAgain()` and says "Pořád se nedaří připojit." when it reaches nothing), and the form comes back by itself when the app is online again (Jakub 2026-11-12, offline sign-in, scenario E); a later step (the code, the recovery) stays as it is; under the password a row `.session-choice` (plan v3 2.10, Jakub 2026-11-05 Q7, left out in the installed app): the checkbox "Zapamatovat si mě" (unticked, `REMEMBERED` when ticked, else `BROWSER`) and the small button "Cizí počítač?", which swaps the row for the ticked checkbox "Cizí počítač, nic neukládat" (`PUBLIC`) with the hint "Po hodině bez používání vás odhlásíme a po zavření prohlížeče tu nic nezůstane." (`aria-describedby`) and "Zpět" (unticking it or "Zpět" brings the first row back); each swap moves the focus to the row's checkbox, and the chosen kind goes with the password, the passkey and the second-factor step (without a choice, such as a confirmation link's sign-in, `BROWSER`); `core/auth/public-session-idle.ts` (`PublicSessionIdle`, started at app start) signs a `PUBLIC` session out after `PUBLIC_IDLE_MS` (1 hour) without a pointer, key, touch or wheel input in any of its tabs (each tab tells the others over the session channel at most once a minute, `public-activity` in `session-tabs.ts`, and keeps the last input in `sessionStorage` `nfnn.public-activity`, so a reload continues the hour instead of starting it again), rechecked on every input and when the tab is shown again, then goes home with the signed-out notice of reason `idle`, because the page's own refreshes keep the server's idle limit from ever running out; the problem's title and detail on failure, "Příliš mnoho pokusů z této adresy. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu." on 429; a sign-in answered `auth/second-factor-required` swaps the form for the code step. Every step after the two starting tabs has the same shape: "← Zpět na …" on top, a visible `h3`, then the content, and no tab switch; every change of step moves the focus to the step's `[autofocus]` element (the first field, or the heading of a sent panel). The code step: "← Zpět na heslo", `h3` "Ověření kódem z aplikace", "Přihlášení dokončíte kódem z ověřovací aplikace.", field "Kód z aplikace" with `inputmode="numeric"`, `autocomplete="one-time-code"` and the six-digit pattern, which posts the challenge and the code through `AuthStore.completeSecondFactor`; a wrong code keeps the step with the problem in the alert line, `auth/invalid-second-factor-challenge` returns to the password form with the problem's sentence, 429 the same sentence as the sign-in; under the code form "Nemáte telefon s aplikací? Obnovit přístup platbou" posts the challenge through `AuthApi.startSecondFactorRecovery` and replaces the step with the instructions ("← Zpět na kód" back to the focused code field, `h3` "Obnova přístupu platbou" focused, a lead asking for 1 Kč "z bankovního účtu vedeného na vaše jméno (u firmy na firmu), ze kterého jste nám už poslali dar" and saying a payment from another person's account unlocks nothing, so that donor writes to us (the payer's name must be the donor's, #1146), never pointing to the overview a signed-out donor cannot open, a warning "Platbu pošlete nejdříve …" before the details while `usableFrom` is set, with a second line in the same box "Nemůžete tak dlouho čekat? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz a kód z aplikace vám vypneme." (mailto link), a warning "Tuto obnovu už jste zahájili … Pošlete platbu s tímto kódem." when the answer is `resumed` (Jakub 2026-10-24), account number and "OBNOVA …" message with copy buttons, "1 Kč", then `<app-qr-code>` with the answer's `spayd` encoded in the browser at level H (an alert "QR kód se nepodařilo vykreslit. Zaplaťte prosím podle uvedených údajů." if it cannot be) in a framed box below the details (so the details come first on a phone too), "Zpráva pro příjemce platí do …" in Prague time, and a note that the dialog may be closed because starting again while the message is valid shows the same details); 409 `donors/second-factor-recovery-unavailable` stays on the code step with the problem in the alert line; a registration answered 202 replaces the form with "Zkontrolujte si e-mail" (focused; the address in a `role="status"` line, a hint about the spam folder, "Poslat odkaz znovu" posting `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` with its own `role="status"` line (the registration, this request and the sign-in tab's "Poslat ověřovací e-mail znovu" send `returnTo: '/#prispet'` when the dialog was opened from the "Přispět" section, `LoginDialogOptions.confirmationReturnTo`, kept also after switching tabs), "Opravit e-mail" back to the kept form with the e-mail focused, "Zpět na přihlášení"); a 400 with field errors puts them at their fields (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and keeps only the title and detail in the alert line; a sign-in answered `auth/email-not-verified` keeps the problem in the alert line and adds "Poslat ověřovací e-mail znovu", which posts `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` and confirms in a `role="status"` line (its own sentence on 429); "Zapomněli jste heslo?" right under the password field opens the reset step ("← Zpět na přihlášení", `h3` "Obnovení hesla"), which posts the trimmed address through `AuthApi.requestPasswordReset` ("Zkontrolujte si e-mail" focused on 202 with the address, the hour of validity, the spam hint and "Poslat znovu"; its own sentence on 429, `describeProblem` otherwise). An address typed on one tab follows to the other and to the reset. When the browser supports passkeys, the login tab adds under the form a "nebo" divider (with `--space-6` above and `--space-5` below, so the passkey button is not hit instead of "Přihlásit se") and "Přihlásit se přístupovým klíčem" (`AuthStore.signInWithPasskey`, both buttons disabled while it runs, the form's fields made untouched so an empty e-mail shows no error; a dismissed device prompt says "Přihlášení přístupovým klíčem neproběhlo. Zkuste to prosím znovu, nebo se přihlaste heslem." and a refusal the problem, both in a `role="alert"` line under the passkey button). The registration opens with a lead saying the name (or company name, IČO and seat) goes on the receipt, no field carries an example placeholder (they read as filled-in values; labels and hints stay, as in the code field of the code step), the visible label "Registruji se jako" over "Fyzická osoba" / "Firma", PSČ before Město, name, street and city checked with `appPrintableOnReceipt` as in the profile card, IČO checked by its mod-11 checksum (`company-identifier.ts`, `appCompanyIdentifier`: "Toto IČO neexistuje, zkontrolujte prosím číslice."); a company types the IČO first (it takes the `[autofocus]`), and once it holds 8 digits with a valid check digit the dialog asks `CompanyLookupApi.lookUp` (`features/donors/company-lookup-api.ts`, `GET /api/donors/company-lookup`, 204 read as `null`; Jakub 2026-10-24) and fills the name, street, PSČ and city that are empty or still hold an earlier lookup's value, never what the donor typed, then says "Údaje jsme doplnili z obchodního rejstříku (ARES). Zkontrolujte je." in an always rendered `role="status"` region (`.company-lookup`); a 204, any error or a lookup still running when the registration is sent, the type switches to a person or the dialog closes changes nothing and shows nothing (no spinner, no alert, the registration never waits for it), the consent above "Vytvořit účet" ("Vytváříme účet…"). When the browser supports passkeys, it asks "Jak se budete přihlašovat" with an always visible hint explaining a passkey (radio group "Heslem" / "Přístupovým klíčem"); with the key the password field gives way to a hint and the request sends `password: null`. `donor-field-messages.ts` holds the name, street, city and postal code messages shared with the profile card.
- `email-verification/` (`/potvrzeni-emailu#token=…`): reads the token once and removes it with `Location.replaceState`; posts `AuthStore.confirmEmail` at once ("Potvrzujeme váš e-mail" with a spinner and a `role="status"` line); on 200 the donor is signed in, "E-mail je potvrzený" takes focus and "Přejít do přehledu" navigates to `/darce/prehled`; a link with `returnTo=%2F%23prispet` (checked by `core/auth/confirmation-return.ts`, anything else ignored) says the QR code now carries the symbol and offers "Pokračovat k příspěvku" to `/#prispet` instead, with a second, soft link "Přejít na můj přehled" (`a.overview-link` to `/darce/prehled`) under it, and its new-link form and "Přihlásit se" keep that destination; `donors/invalid-email-verification-token` and a missing token show a form asking for the address that posts `AuthApi.requestEmailVerification` ("E-mail odeslán" on 202 with the spam hint and "Na úvodní stránku", the reset's 429 sentence, `describeProblem` otherwise); the invalid link adds "Už jste e-mail potvrdili? Pak se stačí přihlásit." with "Přihlásit se" (the dialog returning to `/darce/prehled`); another failure shows "E-mail se nepodařilo potvrdit" with the reason in `role="alert"` and "Zkusit znovu"; each panel's `<h1 id="email-verification-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch. For an account without a password the confirmed panel asks "Teď si vytvořte přístupový klíč…" with "Vytvořit přístupový klíč" (options with `{}`, the device, `addPasskey` named by `deviceName`; then "Přístupový klíč je uložený…" and "Přejít do přehledu"), a refusal in `role="alert"` saying it can be done later, and "Přejít do přehledu bez klíče". The link of an e-mail change carries `&change=email` (`changesEmail`): the page posts it with `AuthApi.confirmEmailChange` to `/api/donors/auth/email-change/confirm` (204, no session), signs out a session the browser still holds (the change ended it on the server), and its confirmed panel says "E-mail je změněný", "Nová adresa je potvrzená. Přihlaste se jí, heslem nebo přístupovým klíčem jako dosud. Kvůli bezpečnosti jsme vás odhlásili na všech zařízeních." and offers only "Přihlásit se" (the dialog returning to `/darce/prehled`), never the passkey step.
- `connect-app/` (`/pripojeni?client_id=…&redirect_uri=…&code_challenge=…&scope=…&resource=…&state=…`, the consent page of the MCP connector, public shell): reads the OAuth request from the query (`oauth-scopes.ts` names the known scopes "Čtení: …" and "Změny: …" for the connected applications of the account card and lists, in `SCOPE_ABILITIES`, what each lets the application do in plain words for the consent); without `client_id`, `redirect_uri` or `code_challenge` it says the request is incomplete; a visitor sees "Aplikace žádá o přístup ke správě" with "Přihlásit se", which opens the dialog (staying on the page) and the page reacts to `AuthStore.status()`; a signed-in donor without `ADMIN` sees "Připojit aplikaci může jen správce" with "Přihlásit se jiným účtem" (signs out, opens the dialog, and the page goes on to the consent after the sign-in) and "Odmítnout a vrátit se do aplikace"; an administrator sees "Ověřujeme aplikaci" while `AuthApi.describeClient` runs, then the consent ("Povolit přístup ke správě aplikaci claude.ai?", who the application says it is, the administrator's name, what reading allows as a list, the changes (`admin:write`, when asked for) as the checkbox "Povolit i změny" unticked by default with their list and the hint that the extent changes only by connecting again, a paragraph "Nikdy nebude smět …" naming the web-only actions, where to disconnect (Údaje a zabezpečení → Připojené aplikace); for a loopback redirect a note that the application runs on this computer) with a fresh confirmation: for an administrator with the code from the app the field "Kód z aplikace" (`#connect-app-code`, six digits, "Povolení potvrďte kódem z ověřovací aplikace.", the hint asks to wait for the next code) and "Povolit", plus under the field "Nebo bez kódu: Povolit přístupovým klíčem" (a link button) when the browser supports passkeys; an administrator with the code who also has a passkey (`AuthStore.hasPasskeys()`, asked before the consent shows) gets the passkey first instead, with "Povolit přístupovým klíčem" as the main button and "Nebo bez přístupového klíče: Zadat kód z aplikace" (Jakub 2026-10-29); for one without the code only "Povolit přístupovým klíčem" (disabled with an explanation in a browser without passkeys), which runs `AuthStore.confirmWith(null)`; and "Odmítnout", all posting `AuthApi.authorize` (an approval carries only the granted scopes and `secondFactorCode` or `passkey`; a refused code or key, or a cancelled device, shows a `role="alert"` line in the consent and focuses the code with its text selected, so the next code replaces it) and leaving through `ExternalNavigation.assign(redirectTo)` ("Vracíme vás do aplikace…"); a refused client or a failed request shows the problem with "Zkusit znovu"; each panel's `<h1 id="connect-app-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch.
- `password-reset/` (`/obnova-hesla#token=…`): reads the token once and removes it with `Location.replaceState`; asks for the new password once, in `<app-password-reveal>`; posts `AuthApi.confirmPasswordReset`; on 204 calls `AuthStore.logout()` if a session is held and offers the sign-in dialog; `donors/invalid-password-reset-token` and a missing token offer the dialog's reset tab (`LoginDialogService.open('reset')`); other failures show `describeProblem` in the form's alert line; each panel's `<h1 id="password-reset-title" tabindex="-1">` takes focus after a switch. With passkey support the page is "Obnovte si přístup" with a radio group "Nové heslo" / "Nový přístupový klíč"; the key form has "Název klíče" (from `deviceName`) and, after `donors/second-factor-code-required`, "Kód z aplikace" (focused, the server's sentence in the alert line); "Vytvořit klíč" posts `AuthApi.passkeyRecoveryOptions`, lets the device create the key and posts `recoverWithPasskey`, then "Přístupový klíč je uložený" offers the sign-in dialog; an administrator's refusal shows the backend's title and detail.
- `dashboard/`: `symbol-card/` (variable symbol with a copy button, "Přispět" to `/#prispet`, where the home page puts the symbol into the QR; "Přispět znovu" once the input `hasDonated` is true). `profile-card/` ("Údaje na potvrzení o daru": name, IČO for a company, address (the street and then the postal code with the town as two parts that each stay whole when they fit, the postal code's groups joined by a no-break space, #1758), receipt language; "Upravit údaje" turns it into a form (street, then PSČ and Město in the order of the registration) saved with "Uložit údaje" sending name, address and `preferredLocale` through `AuthStore.updateProfile` (`PATCH /api/donors/me`); e-mail and IČO read-only; focus to the first field on edit and back to the button after save or cancel; the hint says a new language reaches only receipts downloaded for the first time; name, street and city carry `appPrintableOnReceipt` (`features/donors/printable-on-receipt.ts`, `printedText` of `receipt-characters.ts`, whose spec runs the shared cases of `receipts/printable.json`), so a character no receipt prints is refused at its field before anything is sent ("Obsahuje znak, který na potvrzení o daru nevytiskneme, například emoji nebo čínské písmo. Napište prosím tento údaj latinkou, cyrilicí nebo řeckým písmem."), and a 400 with field errors puts them at their fields (`placeServerErrors`, focus to the first) and keeps only the title and detail, with any error no field owns, in the form's alert line; a `role="status"` line confirms the save; `sessionEnded` is answered like a failed load). `account-card/` ("Přihlášení a zabezpečení", Jakub 2026-10-24): one summary list (`dl.account-data`, one `.account-row` per item: label, state, one action; while the card itself is at most `cards` (640 px) wide, a size container on its own box (`account-card`), the state goes under the label and the action, so the card in the account sheet's form sheet on an iPad stacks as on a phone and an e-mail is never split inside its domain; the e-mail breaks first after its `@` and its dots (`<wbr>`, #1757), and in a card at most `pager` wide the gap before the action narrows to `--space-1`, so "Dvoufaktorové ověření" keeps one line beside "Zapnout" at 375 pt (#1775); the switch is instant, and in both layouts the labels keep one left axis and the buttons one right edge, `e2e/donor-dashboard.spec.ts` at 900, 940 and 1024 px) with the rows E-mail (the address; while `pendingEmailChange` is set "Čeká na potvrzení: <adresa>" in amber, "Potvrzovací odkaz platí do …" and "Poslat znovu" (`AuthStore.resendEmailChange`, "Posíláme…") and the quiet "Zrušit" (`AuthStore.cancelEmailChange`, "Rušíme…", focus back to "Změnit e-mail"), their outcome in the card's status line and a refusal as an alert under them; action "Změnit e-mail"), Heslo ("Nastavené", "Bez hesla" or "Bez hesla, jen přístupové klíče"; "Změnit heslo" or "Nastavit heslo", plus the quiet "Odebrat heslo" for a donor with a password and a key), Dvoufaktorové ověření ("Zapnuté, kód z ověřovací aplikace" / "Vypnuté", with a passkey and the code off the note "Přihlášení přístupovým klíčem je chráněné i bez kódu.", for an administrator without it the amber hint "Správa se vám otevře, až se přihlásíte přístupovým klíčem nebo zapnete kód z aplikace a přihlásíte se s ním."; "Zapnout/Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření") and Přístupové klíče ("Žádný", "1 klíč", "2 klíče", "5 klíčů"; "Spravovat přístupové klíče"). Each action is a disclosure (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` to the row's `dd.account-panel`, which spans the row): opening one closes any other, the focus moves to the form's first field (to the passkeys panel for keys), and cancel or success returns it to the row's button; every visible action word carries the rest of its name as hidden text. The forms are the earlier ones: "Zapnout kód z aplikace" asks the current password (`AuthStore.setupSecondFactor`) after saying what a lost phone means, since there are no backup codes (Jakub 2026-10-29): "Když telefon nebo tablet s aplikací ztratíte, přístup obnovíte platbou 1 Kč z bankovního účtu na vaše jméno, který u nás máte ověřený aspoň týden. Dřív to nejde; do té doby nám napište a kód z aplikace vám vypneme." (the 7-day rule of `SecondFactorRecoveryPolicy`, the administrator's reset otherwise), then shows the QR code (`app-qr-code`, plain, 200 px, encoded in the browser from the enrolment's `otpauthUri` at level M; a URI the encoder refuses, such as one with a character outside ISO-8859-1, shows instead the alert "QR kód se nepodařilo vykreslit. Opište prosím klíč níže do ověřovací aplikace." and the key and the code field stay), the link "Přidat do ověřovací aplikace v tomto telefonu nebo tabletu" to the enrolment's `otpauthUri` (under the QR, above it up to 480 px), the key in groups of four with a copy button and the code field posting `AuthStore.confirmSecondFactor`, which adopts the new session, and the status line says the factor is on and the other sign-ins ended; "Vypnout kód z aplikace" asks the password and a code (`AuthStore.disableSecondFactor`); the e-mail form sends the new address and the current password through `AuthStore.requestEmailChange`, after 202 the status line says the link went to the new address and the current one stays until it is opened, and the store reads the profile again so the pending change shows (a failed reload changes nothing); the password form posts `AuthStore.changePassword`, which adopts the new session; refusals are form-level alerts and `sessionEnded` is answered like the profile card. For an account without a password and without a passkey (`passkeyCount` 0) the rows offer no action but "Spravovat přístupové klíče", and the amber callout "Účet zatím nemá heslo ani přístupový klíč. Nejdřív si vytvořte přístupový klíč, …" with the primary "Vytvořit přístupový klíč" opens the passkeys row with its form (no button where the browser cannot create passkeys). With a key the password form replaces every password field with "Po odeslání vás zařízení požádá o potvrzení jedním z vašich přístupových klíčů." (`AuthStore.confirmWith`). `passkeys-panel/` (`app-passkeys-panel`, the Přístupové klíče row's panel, a region named by the row label through `labelledBy`, `focusTarget()` and `collapse()` for the card; it reports the count through `countChanged`): an intro opening with the benefit ("Přihlaste se otiskem prstu, obličejem nebo zámkem obrazovky místo hesla."), naming Apple, Google and Windows Hello and saying a passkey sign-in is protected without the code from the app; the keys from `AuthApi.passkeys` with "přidáno … · naposledy použito …" or "zatím nepoužito", "Přejmenovat" opening an inline "Nový název klíče" field (prefilled, focused) with "Uložit název" / "Zrušit" posting `AuthApi.renamePasskey`; "Odebrat" with the inline "Odebrat klíč <název>? Přihlásit se jím pak nepůjde." and "Ano, odebrat" / "Ponechat"; "Přidat přístupový klíč" opens a form with "Název klíče" (prefilled from the user agent) and the current password, "Vytvořit klíč" ("Čekáme na zařízení…") asks for the options, lets the device create the key and posts it; outcomes in a `role="status"` line, refusals and the device's own answers as a form alert; after a removal the focus goes to the next key's "Přejmenovat", else the one above, or to the panel's region when no key is left (#1223); without browser support a sentence replaces the button; for an account without a password the form asks no password, the only key shows "Jediný způsob přihlášení…" instead of "Odebrat", one key brings the advice to add a second device and none an amber warning; the first key of a donor with a password also says the password can now be removed at the Heslo row. `connected-apps-card/` ("Připojené aplikace", its own card after the account card, only for an administrator): `AuthApi.connections`, one row per connection with the host, "připojeno … · naposledy použito …", the permissions as a list and "Odpojit" (`AuthApi.disconnect`, "Odpojujeme…", then a `role="status"` line that the application is disconnected and cannot act any more, and the focus on the card's heading, never on another application's "Odpojit", which disconnects at once without a question, #1223), "Žádná aplikace není připojená. …" without one, a failed load in `role="alert"` with "Zkusit znovu"; `sessionEnded` like the other cards. the card "Když web nefunguje" (`.diagnostics-card`, after "Data v tomto zařízení", for administrators too) holds `<app-diagnostics-download source="account-page">`, and the alerts of "Načíst data znovu" for `mismatch` and `no-copy` ask to attach the file from it; every receipt PDF failure the drawing reports (not `unprintable`, and `tools-missing` only once the reload did not help) sets `ReceiptError.offersDiagnostics`, and each place that shows the donor's receipt error (Přehled, Moje dary above the years, at a year and at a gift, both Potvrzení o daru cards) shows `<app-diagnostics-download source="receipt-pdf">` under it. `device-data-card/` ("Data v tomto zařízení", after the account card, plan v3 2.14): one state line (a semibold `.ui-card__intro`, "Uloženo v zařízení · aktualizováno <datum> <čas>" in Prague time, the date and the time each kept whole but free to wrap apart, "Data se ukládají do zařízení…" during the first sync, "Kopie v tomto zařízení je zamčená. …" when `DeviceVault.locked()`, which also drops "Načíst data znovu", "V tomto zařízení zatím nic uloženo není.", or in memory-only mode "V tomto prohlížeči se nic neukládá. …", which also drops the sentence about the encrypted copy and the delete button), the sentence (a `.ui-card__intro`, the one intro style of the cards on Údaje a zabezpečení, as the explanation of "Když web nefunguje" and the paragraphs of "Smazání účtu", #1222 Q21, `e2e/donor-details-intros.spec.ts`) that the copy of gifts, receipts and bank accounts is encrypted with a key only this device knows, that the server does not change, and when sign-out keeps it (password: always; passkey: only when the key gives PRF), "Načíst data znovu" (`DonorCopySync.reload`, every chunk again, "Načítáme data…", disabled while running; `appNeedsConnection`, so offline it is held with the hint "Potřebuje připojení" and no request; the outcome in `app-live-notice`, `failed` blaming the connection, `mismatch` saying the server's data could not be verified ("Data ze serveru se nepodařilo ověřit. …") and `no-copy` naming info@nfnn.cz) and "Smazat data z tohoto zařízení" (danger outline), which opens `delete-device-data-dialog` ("Smazat data z tohoto zařízení?", the lead that the copy goes from this device, the donor is signed out here, the server keeps everything and the copy comes back at the next sign-in, "Smazat data a odhlásit" / "Mažeme data…" and "Zrušit", which takes the focus, both in the action row `.ui-dialog__actions`, so on a phone the sheet keeps them at its bottom edge; `role="alertdialog"` through `[alert]` and described by its lead through `describedBy`, #1248; `app-dialog [dismissible]="false"` while deleting, so Esc, × and the backdrop do nothing): it calls `DeviceVault.forget`, then `AuthStore.logout`, goes home and shows the signed-out notice with the reason `device-data-deleted` ("Data z tohoto zařízení jsme smazali a odhlásili jsme vás.", Q6); a failed deletion keeps the dialog open with an alert and signs nobody out. Every busy label speaks as "we" ("Ukládáme…", "Odebíráme…", "Ověřujeme kód…").
- `bank-accounts-card/` ("Moje bankovní účty", on the section Bankovní účty, one `.ui-card` of the donor cards like Potvrzení o daru and Údaje a zabezpečení, #815): the title is the card's `.ui-card__title` in its `.ui-card__bar`, followed there by the `.ui-card__aside` text button "Jak přiřazujeme platby?" with a round "?" icon (`aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`) that expands a `role="region"` panel (`pairing-help/`, inputs `variableSymbol` and `open`, `closeRequested` on Escape), named by the button itself (`aria-labelledby="accounts-help-toggle"`) and without a heading of its own, so its words do not repeat (#1772), collapsed by default, answering short questions, each in at most two sentences (how a gift arrives, a bank without a symbol field (write the symbol into the message for the recipient), a payment without the symbol, a standing order with an old symbol, the answer to the question, the shared account, how to stop pairing, the exceptions: another donor's symbol, symbol 0, collection accounts; then the e-mail) in true-to-backend Czech with the donor's symbol; Esc collapses and refocuses; the text is capped at 520 px, in its accent box inside the card. The accounts are default `.ui-list` rows edge to edge under the bar's separator (a hairline above them too while the help is open), one row per account (its number in `--text-account-number`, the large size capped at 7vw, with a `<wbr>` before the bank code from `splitBeforeBankCode`, so the bank code never splits) with its state as a `.ui-badge` (`--success`, `--warning`, `--accent`, `--neutral`), hint, the bank's short name first in the line under the number (`.account-bank`, `BankNames`, wrapping), "ověřeno …", "vypnuto od …" (the day after `disconnectedOn`) and the gifts without a symbol with their total as one item (`.account-gifts`, the count and the total each led by a separator of their own in one flex item, so the two wrap together, a separator never stands on a line without its text and one at a line's start falls into the list's hidden strip without clipping the text, #1763; `donationCount` counts only donations paired by account), plus a line per `confirmedSymbols` entry; "Přidat účet" is the last row of the list (`.ui-list__row--action` with a plus glyph, disabled and muted without a symbol, the focus return target). A row with `symbolOffer` shows `symbol-claim/` (`app-symbol-claim`: inputs `accountId`, `offer`, `donorName`, `busy`; output `answer`): a `role="group"` named by the question "Z tohoto účtu k nám chodí i platby s variabilním symbolem … (14 plateb, leden 2025 – říjen 2026). Jsou to dary od vás (Jana Nováková)?" (never an amount) in an accent box with "Ano, jsou moje" (`.ui-button--primary`, described by the note that the answer cannot be changed) and "Ne, nejsou moje" (`.ui-button`); while saving both are `aria-disabled` and the pressed one `aria-busy`; the answered offer is dropped at once. "Ano" posts `confirmSymbol`, emits `donationsChanged` when `pairedCount` is above 0 (the area refetches summary and list) and announces "Přiřadili jsme vám … plateb se symbolem … z účtu …" plus "Potvrzení za rok … se změnilo, stáhněte si nové." for `changedYearlyReceipts`; "Ne" posts `declineSymbol`. While no account is verified (`ACTIVE`) and none carries a question, a short nudge in the card's `.ui-card__footer` under the list says a 1 Kč from the standing order's account brings the older gifts too; an empty card holds only "Přidat účet" in its list and says in its footer that the account appears by itself once a gift with the symbol comes from it, and that a gift sent without the symbol, with another one or before the account existed needs the account added through "Přidat účet" (earlier gifts from it usually follow), then, in two sentences, that a standing order is best switched to the donor's symbol, else a 1 Kč through "Přidat účet". The public `focusTitle()` focuses the card heading; the public `openAddDialog()` (used by the section when the address carries `?pridat=ucet`, in the `read` phase of `afterNextRender` so it runs after the page title took the focus) focuses "Přidat účet" first, so closing returns there, and opens the dialog unless the donor has no symbol. A shared account's hint is an amber warning box (`account-hint--warning`, `--color-warning*`, hidden "Upozornění:") that never names the other donor, in the list and in the add dialog alike. The buttons and chips avoid the word "párování": a `pairingExcluded` account keeps its chip ("Ověřený" / "Nepřiřazujeme"); its live row says only payments with the symbol pair now; an active one offers no "Nepřiřazovat platby z tohoto účtu", an `AWAITING_PAYMENT` one reads "Jen s variabilním symbolem" and keeps that button, a disconnected one offers no "Znovu přiřazovat platby z účtu" and says why on its newest row. "Nepřiřazovat platby z tohoto účtu" (set apart by `--space-2-5` more while the row asks a question) asks an inline confirmation "Ano, nepřiřazovat" / "Ponechat" (focus on "Ponechat"; with confirmed symbols it says those stop pairing too); "Znovu přiřazovat platby z účtu" shows only on the newest row of its account number; both reload the list, announce in `role="status"` and focus the card heading; problems go through `describeProblem` in `role="alert"`, 404/409 reload. "Přidat účet" opens an `app-dialog` with one short paragraph (send 1 Kč by the details below; the account then appears and pays without the symbol), right under it the watch box, then while nothing new is verified one line naming the accounts already verified (`ACTIVE`, not shared, not symbol-only) that need no 1 Kč ("Účet … už máte ověřený, 1 Kč z něj posílat nemusíte." / "Účty … a … už máte ověřené, …"; the dialog cannot know which account the donor means, testers of 2026-10-30), then the branded QR (`app-qr-code`, 200 px) that `spayd()` writes from `PaymentDetailsStore` with `accountVerificationAmountMinor`, the donor's symbol and `ACCOUNT_VERIFICATION` and `encodeText` draws at level H (when the details did not load the error with "Zkusit znovu", when they loaded but are refused "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Zkuste to prosím znovu za chvíli.", both retrying through `PaymentDetailsStore.reload()`; when the donor's own variable symbol is what `spayd()` refuses, "QR kód teď nejde vytvořit. Napište nám prosím na info@nfnn.cz." with no retry, which could not help), and account, amount, message "Ověření účtu Nadační fond Nová naděje" and symbol, each but the amount with a copy button, the buttons in one column at the right edge and every value as far under its label (each value starts at the top of its row, its first line centred on the height of `--button-small-min-height`, and the copy button stands at the top beside it, so a two-line message starts as far under its label as the others, #1759; up to 480 px the details come before the QR, from there up the QR starts level with them, #1891), under them the line that a bank without a field for the variable symbol takes it in the message ("Ověření účtu Nadační fond Nová naděje <symbol>", pairing rule 9), and a closed `<details>` "Kdy to funguje a co se stane s dřívějšími dary" (Czech accounts only, no collection account, earlier gifts for the first verifier, a standing order with an older symbol needs exactly 1 Kč with that message, the timing); it watches in the `role="status"` box ("Čekáme na vaši platbu…" with a spinning ring that after `--duration-calm` fades into a still clock, `motion.wait-settles` and `motion.wait-calm`): 30 s after opening and 30 s after each check ended (the backend's global pause) it runs `checkBankUntilDone()` and reloads the list, emitting `bankChecked`; an account `ACTIVE` now that was not when the dialog opened turns the box into a drawn tick "Účet … je ověřený" (plus "Přiřadili jsme vám také N dřívější dary z tohoto účtu, celkem … Kč." from the row's `donationCount` and `totalAmountMinor` when it brought any) and ends the watch, and so does a changed `verificationPaymentOn` ("Platba z účtu … dorazila"); the result then replaces the intro, the QR, the payment details and the `<details>`, takes the focus (`tabindex="-1"`) and the close button reads "Hotovo"; with a `symbolOffer` the dialog asks the question itself (`app-symbol-claim` under the box, hidden from that account's row meanwhile, so it stands once) and announces the answer in its own `role="status"` line, which takes the focus (a refused answer shows its `role="alert"` in the dialog); a hidden tab pauses and checks at once when visible; while the dialog is open a `bank-accounts` hint reads the list at once, so a verification the hourly import or another check brought shows without waiting for the next 30 s, and a tick ends the watch; after 20 checks "Platba zatím nedorazila" with "Kontrolovat dál"; under `prefers-reduced-motion` the clock stands from the start and the tick only fades. A payment paired by `SENDER_ACCOUNT` carries the tag "bez symbolu · účet …3333/2010" in its purpose cell and one paired by `CONFIRMED_SYMBOL` "symbol 6123456789 · účet …5399/0800" (the full sentence as hidden text, no `title`).
- Donor area (`donor-area`, 2.23): the app header and the tab bar of the public pages (Layout), the header's top padding as room above the content and `--tab-bar-clearance` below it; on wide screens the side panel "Můj účet" with the sections Můj přehled, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, Bankovní účty and Údaje a zabezpečení, and for an administrator the group "Pro správce" with Správa, around the routed section; on phones (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, the `side-nav` with `[panel]="false"`, so one `router-outlet` serves both widths and a page keeps its state when an iPad turns) no panel, the account sheet leads between the sections and the header's "Zpět" leads home; on phones (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`) a section chosen in the account sheet opens inside it as the next step of a stack, after the Music app (Jakub 2026-11-11, option a): `AccountSection` renders the section's page from `DONOR_SECTION_PAGES` of `donors.routes.ts` with its own `DonorDonations` and `PAGE_IN_SHEET`, so `PageTitle` leaves out its `h1`, the sheet's head (`shared/ui/sheet-head`, the same head as the administration's sheet, #989) shows a round Zpět on the left, the section's title in the middle, which takes the focus, and a round Zavřít on the right in place of the sheet's ×; Zavřít closes the whole sheet and asks "Zahodit rozepsané údaje?" first while a form of the section is dirty, with the dialog's own lead "Co jste do formuláře napsali, se neuloží." (a swipe down, Esc and a tap on the backdrop do not ask yet, #1071); Zpět, the edge swipe and the browser's Back return to the list with the focus on the row the donor came from, and the next Back closes the sheet; the address of the page under the sheet does not change and the browser gains no entry; a link inside the section to another section opens that one in the sheet, any other link closes the sheet and opens its page; a step back while a form of the section is dirty (`form.ng-dirty`) keeps the section and asks "Zahodit rozepsané údaje?" (Pokračovat v psaní, Zahodit); dragging the sheet down or Esc closes it whole; Správa, wide screens and a `/darce/…` address opened directly stay pages; the push animation of the stack waits for the shared back transition; the routed section and the help line "Máte dotaz k platbě? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz" (Jakub 2026-10-30 kept it without a second link to the contact form, because two colour-only links in one line would need underlining). It provides `donor-donations.ts` (`DonorDonations`, one per visit of the area, so switching sections loads nothing again and a new sign-in starts empty; only opening Přehled or Moje dary more than 30 s after the last bank check ended (the backend's pause, measured from the device's clock) starts the same silent check as opening the area, through `checkBankIfStale()`, and nothing while one runs): the bank accounts from `BankAccountsApi.list()` once on open (a failure leaves none; the bank accounts card replaces them with every list it shows or answer it records, through its output `accountsChanged`), whose accounts with a `symbolOffer` are the overview's question callouts and the badge of "Bankovní účty" in the side panel and, through `AccountBadges`, on the account button and the sheet's row (Jakub 2026-10-24: the count, read as "1 otázka čeká na odpověď", "2 otázky čekají na odpověď", "5 otázek čeká na odpověď" through `pluralCategory`); summary and list from `DonationsApi` on open (`state` loading/ready/error with retry, shown by `donations-pending/`, whose alert names its page through the input `page`: "Přehled darů se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to znovu." on Přehled, "Dary se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to znovu." on Moje dary, "Potvrzení o daru se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to znovu." on Potvrzení o daru, each with "Zkusit znovu"; a 401 `auth/unauthenticated` or a session lost by a failed refresh sends the donor home with the sign-in dialog, returning to the same section), the bank check (on open and on "Zkontrolovat nové platby" it posts `requestBankCheck`, reads it again while `checking` as `checkBankUntilDone()` does, refetches summary and list whenever a check ends, keeps `checkingBank` until they arrived, ignores an answer overtaken by a later fetch, and counts `bankDataArrived` when `bankDataAsOf` changed or the donor asked, so the bank accounts section reloads its list; after "Zkontrolovat nové platby" `bankCheckOutcome` says how many `RECEIVED` gifts are new, none, that the bank is still busy after the last poll, or that the check failed). `bank-check/` (`app-bank-check`, on the overview's bank line and in Moje dary) shows "Kontrolujeme nové platby v bance…" or the outcome ("Přibyl 1 nový dar." / "Přibyly 3 nové dary." / "Přibylo 5 nových darů.", "Žádný nový dar zatím nepřišel. Banky se ptáme nejvýš jednou za 30 sekund, zkuste to proto za chvíli znovu." (the backend's `on-demand-cooldown`), "Banka zatím neodpověděla. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu.") before "Údaje z banky k …" in one always rendered `role="status"` line, the button "Zkontrolovat nové platby" (`appBusy` while checking: `aria-disabled`, still focusable and with its name, the look of a disabled button, a press ignored, #1222) and, for a failed check, the `role="alert"` "Nové platby se nepodařilo zkontrolovat. Zkuste to prosím za chvíli znovu."; leaving the section clears the outcome. From the device copy (2.15): `DonorDonations` reads summary and gifts only through `DonorDonationsRepository` (`features/donations/device-copy/donor-donations-repository.ts`, root): `held()` is the open copy's view while it agrees with its summary (a copy that disagrees counts as absent, so the page reads the API instead of a list that contradicts its totals), and `fresh('catch-up' | 'sync')` answers from the copy after that catch-up or sync stored it and from the API (`/api/donations/me/summary` and `/api/donations/me`) when there is no open copy, when it is still empty (its catch-up then runs beside) or when the sync did not store it, so a sync that fails, times out or finds the session gone never leaves the page without data. A copy that holds the gifts is shown at once and caught up, and what the catch-up brings waits behind the "Zobrazit N nový dar" button like a live hint; the reload that ends an automatic bank check (on opening the area) offers its gifts the same way while the page shows the copy, so a catch-up's button is not replaced by a list that moves; every other reload (the end of a bank check the donor asked for, a receipt whose row is gone) syncs and shows its answer directly; a copy that opens after the page asked the API is shown as soon as it opens (also after the API failed), unless the API's answer on the page is newer than the copy; a copy that opens for another donor than the one the page showed (a session that changed donor in place; a read from the API names the donor signed in when it was asked) replaces the list at once instead of offering its gifts as new ones; a failed reload keeps a list shown from the copy instead of the error. A receipt is drawn through `copyBackedReceiptSource`, so the copy's active receipt and its kept images are drawn again when the server cannot be reached (status 0, or a gateway status 502–504 or 520–524 without a problem body). The bank accounts still come from the API (2.15 kept the Bankovní účty page on it). `first-sync-notice/` (`app-first-sync-notice`, in the donor shell, an always rendered `aria-live="polite"` region without `role="status"`, as `chunk-load-notice`): when the first sync of an empty copy takes longer than a second, a bar fixed at the bottom of the screen (`ui-notice--floating` in a neutral look, the page kept clear of it through `keepRoomForFloatingNotice`, so nothing on the page moves) says "Ukládáme přehled vašich darů do tohoto zařízení…", and when it stored the copy, in the status look, "Přehled vašich darů je uložený v tomto zařízení." for 4 s; when it ended with nothing stored (`holdsData()` false) while the copy is still open, in the warning look, "Přehled vašich darů se do tohoto zařízení teď nepodařilo uložit. Online vše funguje dál." with "Uložit znovu" (`appNeedsConnection`, runs `sync()`), which stays until a later sync ends it (#1002); a sync that starts while it shows brings the saving bar back at once. Nothing for a quick sync (stored or failed), a sync that ended because the copy closed (signing out), a copy that already held the gifts or a device that keeps nothing (memory only, "Cizí počítač"). It promises nothing offline: until the copy opens without a connection (after 2.7), the offline-unlock PR adds that sentence under a new id. Live hints (2.16): on `donations` `DonorDonations` reads summary and list (with a copy: syncs it) in the background and shows them at once unless they bring gifts the page does not show yet, which wait behind a button at the end of the same status line, "Zobrazit 1 nový dar" / "Zobrazit 3 nové dary" / "Zobrazit 5 nových darů" (`incomingCount`, `showIncoming()`, the focus then on "Zkontrolovat nové platby"), so the list never moves under the donor's finger; while a bank check runs the button is hidden (its end reloads everything anyway, and the focus would land on the busy check button); a check the donor starts, or any full reload, shows everything at once and drops the button. On `bank-accounts` it reads the bank accounts again (the badge and the questions), on `bank-import` the time of the bank data (`GET` of the bank check, unless a check runs), and after a reopened stream all three. `missing-gift/` (`app-missing-gift`, in the empty states of Přehled (in "Jak poslat první dar" under its three steps with `--space-6` below it before the bank line, #1222), Moje dary and Potvrzení o daru): the `h3` "Poslali jste dar a nevidíte ho?", two sentences (a payment without the symbol, with another one or from before the account is not paired yet; verifying the account it came from with 1 Kč usually brings the earlier gifts) and "Přidat účet, ze kterého jste platili" to `/darce/ucty?pridat=ucet`, which opens the add dialog and drops the query (`replaceUrl`). `standing-order/` (`app-standing-order`, input `variableSymbol`): the text button "Jak nastavit trvalý příkaz" (`aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`) opens a `role="region"` named by it, scrolls the button with the opened guide into view (`scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest' })` after render, so the opening never happens below the screen), with the account number (input `account` from the card's `app-payment-panel`, otherwise, as in Moje dary, from `PaymentDetailsStore`; "Načítáme číslo účtu…", on failure a `role="alert"` "Číslo účtu se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." with "Zkusit znovu", which loads again or, with `account`, emits `retryRequested` for the card), the donor's symbol, both with copy buttons, "<amount> Kč, nebo jiná, kterou chcete posílat" (input `amount` from the card's panel) or, without an amount, "Jakákoli, kterou chcete posílat", "Každý měsíc, nebo jak často chcete", that every payment then appears by itself and counts in the yearly receipt, that the amount and the day are changed only in the donor's bank and the site needs no setting, and for an older symbol the advice to switch it or verify the account under "Bankovní účty" (link); Esc closes and refocuses the button., the receipt is drawn in the browser by `ReceiptPdf` (plan 1.9; a running one marks its button `aria-disabled="true"` and `aria-busy` (the button keeps the keyboard focus, so a drawing of several seconds never drops it to the page, and a press meanwhile starts nothing); `yearlyButton(year)` / `singleButton(item)` give the state of its button (`idle`, `running`, `ready`, `failed`) to `<app-pdf-button-face>` inside it, and `yearlyProgress(year)` / `singleProgress(item)` hand the line's state to `<app-pdf-progress class="receipt-saved">` (in the table row, the year heading of Moje dary, the year row of Potvrzení o daru and the card of Přehled; design P1, Jakub 2026-11-15, #823): the button's download icon turns into an indeterminate ring while the PDF is made, with no percentage, and the line names the real phase, "Připravujeme údaje potvrzení…", "Načítáme nástroje pro PDF…", "Vytváříme PDF… · strana N" (N from the worker's `page` messages), each only once it has lasted 300 ms, then `PdfDelivery.save` and "Potvrzení <file> je stažené. Najdete ho ve stažených souborech." (seen as a check and "PDF je stažené"), or on an iPhone or iPad that shares files "Potvrzení <file> je připravené. Otevřete ho tlačítkem Otevřít potvrzení." (under a payment's row "… tlačítkem Otevřít PDF."; seen as a check and "PDF je připravené") while the same button, now with a share icon, reads "Otevřít potvrzení za rok <year> (PDF)" (in the payments table "Otevřít PDF") and takes the focus unless the donor moved it elsewhere while the PDF was drawn (after a save, a failure, a cancel and on ready the button gets the focus back only when it fell to the page or still stands where it stood when the download started); a ready file is dropped, with the finished lines, as soon as the signed-in donor's name or address changes (`AuthStore.donor`), because the next issue replaces that receipt, while a change of the language keeps it; tapping it hands the file to the share sheet and issues nothing again ("Potvrzení <file> jste předali z nabídky sdílení."; "… je stažené …" when the browser saved it instead or sharing failed; a closed sheet keeps it ready); "Zrušit" from 8 s on ends it ("Přípravu potvrzení jste zrušili.", the `cancelled` state in the neutral colour) and returns the focus to the download button; every download keeps its own line (`receiptTasks`, one per year or gift), and a new download clears only the finished lines of others; `tools-missing` reloads the page once through `ReceiptRestart`, and after its first load the section resumes the download named by `?stahnout=` only when this tab's `sessionStorage` marker names that same download (`ReceiptRestart.resumes`), so a link with the parameter issues nothing; it removes the parameter either way; every other end of that download (saved, failed for another reason, cancelled) clears the marker (`ReceiptRestart.finished`, `abandoned(download)`), so a later link cannot issue it without a click; a miss after that one reload (`tryRestart` answers `reloaded-already`) says "Nástroje pro PDF se nepodařilo načíst ani po obnovení stránky. Zkontrolujte prosím připojení nebo blokování skriptů, zkuste jiný prohlížeč, nebo nám napište na info@nfnn.cz.", and a browser without session storage (`unavailable`, no reload) "Web se mezitím aktualizoval. Obnovte prosím stránku a stáhněte potvrzení znovu.", an image that fails its digest "Podpis na potvrzení se nepodařilo ověřit, a tak jsme PDF nevytvořili…", a drawing failure "PDF se v prohlížeči nepodařilo vytvořit…") and their failures (`receiptError` with its scope: the problem title for `donations/year-not-yet-completed`, `donations/yearly-receipt-not-available` and `donations/receipt-before-first-year`, the title of a 404 or `donations/receipt-before-first-year` on a single receipt after which summary and list are fetched again, the title and detail of `donations/receipt-unprintable`, of `donations/receipt-signing-paused` (slice 1.13), of `donations/receipt-signing-key-unpublished` (slice 1.22) and of `donations/receipt-signatory-missing`, `donations/receipt-issue-limit` and `donations/receipt-too-long` (slice 1.21: each says why a retry cannot help now and what to do instead), else "Potvrzení se nepodařilo stáhnout. Zkuste to prosím znovu."). Every section starts with `page-title/` (`<h1 tabindex="-1">`, focused on open). `overview/` (Přehled), the same order on phones and desktops (Jakub 2026-10-24): first the notices, a card "Potvrzení o darech za rok … je připravené" with "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" when the donor gave in `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` and that year is not before `receiptsAvailableFrom`, and an amber callout per bank account with a `symbolOffer` ("Máme na vás otázku k platbám z účtu …" with the account number free to wrap, so the sentence stays inside the callout at the largest text (`e2e/largest-text.spec.ts`), and "Odpovědět" to `/darce/ucty`, from the area's bank accounts); then for a donor with gifts (an account verification alone is none, Jakub 2026-10-29), in this order in the DOM as on the screen (#1222), one row of stats as a `dl` without icons (Celkem darováno, Počet darů (the summary's count without the `RECEIVED` account verifications, which a smaller "a ověření účtu" names under it), "Darováno v roce …" for the year after `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough`, 0 Kč without a gift that year; three columns, up to 640 px three lines of label and value), the card "Poslední dar" (the donor cards again since #818, like Potvrzení and Údaje: the `h2` as the `.ui-card__title` inside the padded card, the amount, date and state of the newest item that is not an account verification, then `app-bank-check` with the note on the hourly search and bank delays), the card "Poslat dar" (`send-gift/`, Jakub 2026-10-29; `id="poslat-dar"`, the target of the link "Poslat dar" on an empty Moje dary; a padded `.ui-card` of the donor cards again since #816, like Potvrzení and Údaje: the `h2` as the `.ui-card__title` inside the card, `app-payment-panel` with the donor's symbol and its public look, then, projected right after the bank details and before the QR (testers of 2026-10-30 missed it under the QR), the lead that the symbol is already in the details and the QR and the standing order guide fed by the panel (`app-standing-order` with its underlined link and box, as on Moje dary); one instance outside the state branches, so loading the donations never asks for the QR again; it replaced the symbol card and every link to `/#prispet`) and "Poslední dary" (a card with the `h2.ui-card__title` and "Všechny dary" to `/darce/dary` in its bar, over the newest five through `donations-table/` without the receipt column); for a donor without a gift "Jak poslat první dar" takes the place of the last gift and the stats (a padded card with three steps that name the card „Poslat dar“ in quotes, without "níže" (#1222), and the donor's symbol, then the missing gift block, then the bank line; the symbol and "Přidat účet, ze kterého jste platili" wrap at the largest text, `e2e/largest-text.spec.ts`), followed by "Poslat dar"; while loading the overview keeps one visually hidden `role="status"` "Načítáme vaše dary…" and, through `loadingView` (#1222 Q17), two `app-skeleton` cards (`aria-hidden`) where the stats and the last gift come, their place (`.overview-skeletons`) held empty and invisible from the start of loading and faded in after `--delay-loading`, so "Poslat dar" moves at most once (D5, `e2e/donor-overview-loading.spec.ts`), held for `--duration-loading-min` once shown, after which those cards, the receipt card and "Poslední dary" appear together with `.ui-skeleton-reveal` and nothing is announced; a failure after a shown skeleton also waits for `--duration-loading-min` and is revealed with `.ui-skeleton-reveal`; an answer before the delay and an offline device still show at once, and data already in memory shows no skeleton; a failure's alert stands above "Poslat dar". Last comes `install-card/` (`app-install-card`, plan v3 2.4, only while `AppInstall.offered()`): the `region` "Nová naděje jako aplikace" with a lead, for `ios-share` and `mac-dock` the steps in Safari and that the app asks to sign in once more (an app added to the Home Screen keeps its own storage), for `prompt` "Nainstalovat aplikaci", which opens the browser's install question, and "Teď ne", which hides the offer for 90 days; it never takes the focus as it appears (it can come at any moment, when the browser fires `beforeinstallprompt`), and after either button the card is replaced by its closing line ("Aplikace se instaluje…" or "Dobře, nabídku aplikace teď skryjeme…"), which takes the focus, so it is not lost with the pressed button. It is offered only to a signed-in donor (plan 2.1), never on the public site. `gifts/` (Moje dary, donor cards again since #817): one card "Všechny dary" (its `h2.ui-card__title` with `app-bank-check` beside it in the card's bar) with, under the title, the sentence that single receipts come at once, the yearly one from 1 January of the next year and only one kind goes into the tax return (the yearly one holds every gift of the year), then the gifts grouped by year, newest first (Jakub 2026-10-24): each year a `section` named by its `h3.ui-card__subtitle` (the year) with "3 dary · 1 500 Kč" beside it from the summary's `byYear` (through `gift-count/` (`app-gift-count`, also in the year rows of Potvrzení o daru; `receivedVerifications(items, year)` counts the `RECEIVED` account verifications, which are named apart: "1 dar a ověření účtu · 2 001 Kč", "Ověření účtu · 1 Kč" alone, their amount staying in the sum) and, by `yearReceipt(year, summary)` (`dashboard/yearly-receipt.ts`, also used by Potvrzení o daru, from `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` and `receiptsAvailableFrom`, never the client clock), "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" (the primary button with the download icon, as on Potvrzení o daru, its saved line under it) for a year with its receipt, "Roční potvrzení za rok … stáhnete po skončení roku, od 1. ledna …." for a later year and nothing before the first receipt year; the year's gifts through `donations-table/` named by the year heading, with "Stáhnout PDF" where `receiptAvailable` (a gift on the active yearly receipt of its year says "Je na ročním potvrzení za rok …" instead, #467: `DonorDonations.onYearlyReceipt`, from the yearly receipts the device copy holds, matched to the gifts by day and amount in `giftsOnActiveYearlyReceipts` (`dashboard/yearly-receipt.ts`), or after the server refused that gift with 409 `donations/donation-on-yearly-receipt`, whose title and detail the row's alert shows while the payments load again; a `REFUNDED` row says "Vrácený dar potvrzení nemá", an account verification "Započítá se do ročního potvrzení", one before the first receipt year "Potvrzení vystavujeme od roku …", and a line under the years says from when receipts are issued, verifications aside), and in the card's footer the note on the hourly search and bank delays and the standing order guide (`app-standing-order`, an underlined link opening a tinted box); without gifts "Zatím tu není žádný dar." (#1222, as on Potvrzení o daru), "Poslat dar" (a `ui-button--primary` link to `/darce/prehled#poslat-dar`, the card on Přehled) and the missing gift block; a failed download shows its alert where it was started, a yearly one under that year's heading (the diagnostic file after it) and a single one in the gift's receipt cell (`app-donations-table` input `errorsAtRows`, through `DonorDonations.yearlyError` and `singleError`, the error carrying the download key; Jakub 2026-10-31), so the page does not move; only when that year or gift is gone after the payments load again does the alert stand above the years and scroll into view. `receipts/` (Potvrzení o daru): an intro under the heading (single receipts at once, the yearly one from 1 January of the next year, and for the tax return either the single ones or the yearly one, not both, since the yearly one holds every gift of the year; after a change of name or address a newly downloaded receipt gets a new number and replaces the earlier one), then "Roční potvrzení o darech" as one row per summary year (year, count through `app-gift-count`, total; the columns are year, count, total and action, the count never narrower than its text and the action column `--yearly-column-action` wide where the card allows, so the totals end at one place whether the list has one, two or many years and a long note wraps inside its column, #1584, #1593) with "Stáhnout potvrzení za rok … (PDF)" (download icon `aria-hidden`) up to `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough`, "Ke stažení od 1. ledna …" (the next year) after it and "Za tento rok potvrzení na webu nevystavujeme. Potřebujete ho? Napište nám na info@nfnn.cz." before `receiptsAvailableFrom` (plus the line from which year receipts are issued; without such a year the line "Potvrzení tu vystavujeme za dary od roku …. Za dřívější dary nám napište na info@nfnn.cz." instead, `.yearly-earlier`), no calendar in the frontend, and without gifts a line that a gift with the symbol appears here plus the missing gift block; the card "Potvrzení k jednotlivému daru" lists the gifts with `receiptAvailable` of every year whose yearly receipt is not out yet (`yearReceipt` is `after-year-end`, so the running year) through `donations-table/` named by its heading, with "Stáhnout PDF" and its saved line per row and a failed single download as its alert (Jakub 2026-10-30: testers looked for these receipts on this page first; Moje dary keeps them too), and says older gifts' receipts are under Moje dary; without such a gift it only says single receipts are under Moje dary. Last, always shown (also while the donations load), the card "Ověřit potvrzení" (Jakub 2026-10-31: one entry in the receipts section, not a link per receipt) says the donor can verify their own receipt or one someone showed them and links to `/overit` with a `ui-button`; the three cards carry the one `.ui-card__title` size of every card (#851). `bank-accounts/` (Bankovní účty): an intro and the bank accounts card wired to the area's state. `details/` (Údaje a zabezpečení): the profile card, the account card (with the passkeys panel inside), the notifications card (`notifications-card/`, "Upozornění", donor push slice 4, #802: `DonorPushApi` of `features/operations/donor-push-api.ts` reads `GET /api/operations/push` on open and acts on this device only; with a subscription it shows the switches "Dar dorazil" and "Roční potvrzení" (`role="switch"`, each with its description, a change saves both switches and says so), "Naposledy doručeno …" or "Zatím nic nedoručeno" and "Vypnout na tomto zařízení"; without one it offers "Zapnout na tomto zařízení" only where the session outlives the browser (never `PUBLIC` or `BROWSER`, which say why) and `pushAvailability` is `available`, and otherwise explains the home-screen app on iPhone and iPad, a blocked site, a browser without push or a sign-in from before devices; the permission is asked only inside the tap, then the subscription is put, focus goes to the card's heading, and one confirmation (`POST /api/operations/push/test`) is sent, whose answer the status line reports ("předali k doručení", none delivered, or the refusal) before the card reads itself again quietly (a failed quiet read keeps the card as it is); turning on, turning off and a switch save block one another, and a save the server answers 404 (the device turned off meanwhile) reads the card again; when the server lists this device but the browser no longer holds a subscription (`browserSubscription()`), the card says so in a `ui-callout--warning` and offers "Zapnout znovu"; its notes (loading, the last delivery, the offer) are muted `.ui-card__intro` lines and so are its state lines (`.notifications-state`, semibold in the same size); a failed load says so with "Zkusit znovu", and while the browser has not started the service worker the card says so with "Zkusit znovu", which reads the card again and with it `pushAvailability`, so the sentence no longer asks to reload the page (#1222); and its status and alert line is `app-live-notice look="footer"`, the card's last part (#851); it hands the subscribed device ids to the root signal `DonorNotifiedDevices`, which the devices card reads to say "dostává upozornění" under a device in words; the browser keeps one push subscription for the site, which the administration alerts share, so turning off lets it go (`unsubscribe()`) only when `BrowserPushHolders.administrationAlertsHere()` (`features/operations/browser-push-holders.ts`, through `DonorPushApi` and an `AdminApi` loaded by `import()` only for an administrator, so the donor's chunk does not carry the administration's API) says no administration alert of this device needs it, and the alerts page and the alerts offer ask `donorNotificationsHere()` the same way before letting it go; either question failing keeps the subscription), the devices card (`devices-card/`, "Přihlášená zařízení", 2.9: `AuthApi.devices()` once on open, each row a `ui-list` row with the platform's outline (phone, tablet, computer) as its lead, named by `deviceName()` of `device-labels.ts` from the closed platform and browser lists ("iPhone · Safari", "Neznámé zařízení" when both are other), or "iPhone · aplikace" for a device whose `session` is `APP`, the installed app (Jakub 2026-11-11), with the last use and, below it, the sign-in date and the kind of the other sessions ("zapamatované přihlášení", "do zavření prohlížeče", "cizí počítač"); `groupDevices()` of `device-groups.ts` puts this device first and the others by their last use, newest first, and folds the ones last used more than 30 days (`OLDER_AFTER_DAYS`) before the newest use in the list under a row "Starší (N)" with `aria-expanded`, collapsed at open (the newest use is the server's clock, never the device's), and a narrow card (`bp.container-down(devices, pager)`, large text) puts the row's action under its text; "Toto zařízení" as a badge on the current one and "Odhlásit" (aria-label "Odhlásit {name}") on every other, "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení" while there is another, "Jinde přihlášení nejste." when there is none; each sign-out's status line advises changing the password when the donor did not sign in there, and for an administrator the line after "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení" says the connected applications stay connected (Q12); a device the server no longer finds (`donors/device-not-found`, signed out elsewhere) leaves the list like a signed-out one; "Odhlásit" at another device and "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení" first ask in the card, after the pattern of the bank accounts card's confirmation (#1243: "Odhlásit {name}?" under the row, or "Odhlásit všechna ostatní zařízení?" in place of the button, with a danger "Odhlásit" and "Zrušit"; focus moves to "Zrušit", back to the pressed button after cancelling or a failure, and after the sign-out to the neighbouring row (the next one's "Odhlásit" or "Starší", else the one above) or, when no other device is left, to the heading "Přihlášená zařízení", while the status line says what happened (#1223)); this device has no sign-out there; a failure is an alert with "Zkusit znovu" for the list, and a 401 `auth/session-ended` ends the session through its output), for an administrator the connected-apps card, and "Smazání účtu" (a donor cannot delete the account: an e-mail to info@nfnn.cz with a prefilled subject, what is erased and what stays for accounting, and to download the receipts first). `donations-table/`: input `labelledBy` names the table by its card heading; on a computer every year's table starts from one column grid (a `<colgroup>` of the `--donations-column-*` tokens in the browser's automatic table layout, so a column with a token is never narrower than it and grows only when a cell of that table needs more, and Účel, the one column without a token, takes the rest; the last column aligned to the card's right edge, the first and last cells `--card-inset` from it), and it becomes cards in a container up to `ledger`, with or without the receipt column, its rows `--card-inset` from the card's edge; in the table Částka grows past `--donations-column-amount` to hold „1 250 000 Kč“ inside its padding (#1585) and Potvrzení past `--donations-column-receipt` (as wide as "Stáhnout PDF", R48b) to hold a note in place of the PDF ("Potvrzení vystavujeme od roku …", kept on one line) (#1592); so that Moje dary's years keep one grid, every year of `.payments-card` takes `--donations-column-amount-long` for Částka as soon as one of its amounts is longer than seven characters (`.amount--long`, a width for „1 234 567,89 Kč“) and `--donations-column-receipt-note` for Potvrzení as soon as one of its rows shows a note wider than the button (`.receipt-note--long`: on the yearly receipt, the account verification, before the first year; the refund note fits the button's width); a page with neither keeps Účel at 1280 px wide enough for the purpose and its pairing tag on one line beside the PDF button, and a page with one of them may wrap the tag; the account tag (its account end kept whole, `.account-end`, „účet“ kept with it and „symbol“ with its number, each pair in an inline block `.account-ref`, which takes a line of its own when it does not fit after the rest and breaks before the number only when it is wider than the line, #1675) wraps whole under the purpose, and inside itself only when it is longer than the column; the gift's receipt PDF line and "Zrušit" follow the button in the DOM and stand under it, taking no space while `app-pdf-progress` is `pdf-progress--idle`, and the cells align to the top, so the button keeps its place while the row opens; a yearly button (Moje dary's year heading, Potvrzení's year row) has one `app-pdf-progress` after it in the DOM at every width, so turning an iPad keeps its `role="status"` and Tab goes from the button to "Zrušit" (#1671, replacing the line before the button of Q7 and team decision L2): from `nav` the action is a column at the right edge and the line stands under the button, aligned like it and as wide as it, and the row aligns its items on their first baseline, so the year stands on the line of the button's text; the yearly `app-pdf-progress` carries `reservesCancel` at every width, an `aria-hidden`, `visibility: hidden` zero-width span styled as a small button (`.pdf-progress__strut`) that keeps the line as tall as "Zrušit" also while it is empty, so the button and the line keep their place and the row keeps its height from rest through the preparation and "Zrušit" to the saved PDF; below `nav` the line follows the button with `flex: 1 1 var(--pdf-status-reserve)`, so it takes the free width of its line and "Připravujeme údaje potvrzení…" stays on one line beside "Zrušit" on a 390 pt phone; where a yearly row wraps (Moje dary's year heading in a container up to `tablet`, Potvrzení's stacked years) the action takes a line of its own and keeps the button at the right edge with the line under it; a stacked year of Potvrzení is a wrapping flex row (year and total on one line where they fit, the total at the right edge on a line of its own where they do not, as at the largest text on a 390 pt phone, then the count and the action), so nothing of it reaches past the screen; the Přehled card is the exception (R48): its single `app-pdf-progress` follows the button in the DOM at every width; from `nav` the action is `display: contents`, so the line stands as a line of its own under the row of title and button, aligned like the button, and below `nav` it stands beside the button in its reserve as #1384 has it; so the button never moves when a download starts and Tab goes from the button to "Zrušit"; from `nav` title and button share one row in a card of at least `tablet` (the card is the container `receipt-ready`) and the action takes a line of its own at the card's right edge in a narrower one; the line sits in the wrapper `.receipt-status`, which opens by `motion.expand($closes-by: class)` and is closed (`receipt-status--closing`) while `app-pdf-progress` shows nothing (its public `idle()`, also its host class `pdf-progress--idle`, on until a phase is named after `PHASE_SHOWN_AFTER_MS` or an end or "Zrušit" shows); the closed wrapper keeps `visibility: visible`, so the `role="status"` inside stays in the accessibility tree, and the card and the action keep no row gap, the space above the line being the line's own padding inside what opens, so only the card's padding stands under the button at rest, on a phone too (#1337), and the line opens together with its text; as cards, a gift's right column stacks the amount, Stav and Potvrzení from the top, aligned to the card's right edge, beside the purpose; on phones the line stays under the button and after it in the DOM (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`), and while it is `pdf-progress--idle` its `.receipt-status` stays closed by `motion.expand` and opens and closes with its text (#1514), with no row gap in the action, so a gift row ends at its button as the account verification's row ends at its note (#1537); on phones a gift row reads title, meta, value and state with spaces between them; at a large text size (container up to `pair`) the gift rows (Přehled, Moje dary and Potvrzení alike) stack the date, the amount, the purpose with the pairing tag and the refund badge each on the row's full width, and the tag wraps only between words, so no date, symbol or account end splits (`e2e/largest-text.spec.ts`); every payment cell carries `data-label`, so up to 640 px each row is a card (date and amount without their labels); amounts right-aligned with `--numeric-figures`, bold in the text colour (Jakub 2026-10-29: amounts in the donor overview, Moje dary, Potvrzení o daru, the administration's payments and the donor card are bold `--color-text`, the accent stays for links and buttons); a 1 Kč account verification (`accountVerification`) shows its amount exactly like a gift's and carries the neutral `ui-badge` „Ověření účtu“ (`@@donor.dashboard.payments.verificationTag`) instead of the purpose text: in rows the badge stands in `ui-list__meta`, in the table under the date with the purpose cell holding only a pairing tag (hidden in cards when empty); either way it is read before the amount and is never `aria-hidden` (#1536); each "Stáhnout PDF" carries the gift's date and amount as hidden text.
- `donors/notifications-offer/` (`app-notifications-offer`, donor push slice 5, #831, Jakub 2026-11-12 „Ano, jednou“): the sheet "Chcete vědět, až dar dorazí?" of the installed app. `App` imports it lazily (`NgComponentOutlet`) only once `SESSION_JAR.installed` and a donor is signed in, so the initial bundle carries none of it. It checks when the donor is signed in with a session other than `PUBLIC`, after the first navigation and online: once per start when `DonorDonationsRepository.held()` lists a gift, and on `visibilitychange` to visible within 2 h after `PaymentHandOffs` (root) noted "Otevřít v aplikaci banky" of the payment panel (the note is cleared on that return, and by the panel when the share offer is closed or refused); never on `/sprava` or `/darce/udaje` (the notifications card is there), while `nfnn.notifications-offer.hidden-until` lies ahead (30 days; storage that throws counts as hidden) or when `pushAvailability` is other than `available`. It reads `DonorPushApi.view()` and opens only when the session names a device and `device` is `null`, and only if the same donor is still signed in on such a page when the answer comes. A change of donor or a sign-out closes the sheet and forgets the start's offer and the hand-off. The steps follow the administrators' offer (`presentation="sheet"`, title focused at each step): "Zapnout" (`appNeedsConnection`) asks the browser inside the tap, `PUT …/push/device` (both kinds on) and `POST …/push/test`, then hides the offer and shows "Upozornění jsou zapnutá" with "Hotovo" and "Upravit upozornění" (`/darce/udaje`); a refused permission or subscription shows "Upozornění jsou vypnutá" and hides the offer; a refusal of the server releases the browser's subscription unless `BrowserPushHolders` says administration alerts hold it and offers "Zkusit znovu" (409 `operations/push-subscription-limit` with its own sentence); "Teď ne" or dismissing the first step hides it. Both offers share the global block `ui-offer-sheet` (`src/styles/ui/_offer-sheet.scss`).
- Kept donor answers (Jakub 2026-11-12, smoothness: a section opened in the account sheet loaded again every time): every section opened in the account sheet is a new visit with its own `DonorDonations` and cards, so each opening showed "Načítáme…" again and posted a bank check, and the cards of Bankovní účty and Údaje a zabezpečení did so on every return at every width. `KeptDonorAnswers` (`features/donors/dashboard/kept-donor-answers.ts`, root, typed by `KeptDonorAnswerTypes`, on the general `KeptAnswers` of `core/kept/kept-answers.ts`, which keeps typed answers in memory for one owner) keeps in memory the last answer of the donor's gifts (the read `DonorDonations` showed), bank accounts, signed-in devices, passkeys and connected apps, and the end of the last bank check with the time of the bank data (also when the live stream moves that time). `DonorDonations` and each card show the kept answer at once when they open, without their loading line, and ask the server behind it (`read`: the kept answer, then the fresh one; a failed refresh keeps what is shown, except a 401, which reaches the page as before).
