A list's answer that was asked for before the donor changed that list (signed out a device or the others, added, renamed or removed a passkey, disconnected an app, acted on a bank account) is set aside when it arrives, and once no change is running the list is asked for again (`ListChanges` of `core/kept/list-changes.ts`: every load of a card counts the changes it started after and sets aside an answer older than a change or begun while one ran, before the answer is kept, every change counts itself while it runs, and a set-aside answer asks again only when no load begun after the change is on its way or that load ended without an answer), so a signed-out device or a removed passkey never comes back, not even with an answer already on its way, and a list kept from an earlier visit never stays for the rest of the visit (a device that signed in since then still shows); that asking again is quiet, so its failure keeps the list shown and only a 401 reaches the page; the bank accounts `DonorDonations` asked for when it opened give way to any newer list the card hands over; a retry and the reloads a change starts go to the server first, as before. Gifts from the device copy still come first; kept gifts stand in only without a copy (Cizí počítač, a copy not open yet), and a failed refresh behind them keeps them instead of the error line. A `DonorDonations` opened within 30 s of the end of the last bank check starts none (the pause of `checkBankIfStale`) and shows that check's time of the bank data. The answers belong to the donor who asked for them: an answer is kept only while the donor signed in when it was asked is still signed in (a read of the gifts also by its `owner`), a sign-out or another donor's sign-in forgets them all (an effect on `AuthStore.donor`, and every read and keep compares the donor again), nothing is stored and nothing outlives the tab. `AccountSection` remembers a section's page component once loaded (a `WeakMap` keyed by its loader), so a section opened again renders in the same frame instead of after an empty one.
- Kept administration answers (Jakub 2026-11-12, smoothness, #680: Správci, Upozornění, Loga bank and the cards Kdo podepisuje potvrzení and Podpisový klíč potvrzení in Nastavení read the server on every visit and showed their loading line each time): `KeptAdministrationAnswers` (`features/admin/kept-administration-answers.ts`, root, on `KeptAnswers` of `core/kept/`) keeps in memory the last list of administrators, the people of the foundation (their portraits in that list), the alerts page's answer (its devices, which hold no endpoint or key, and the open alerts), the bank logos, the signature block the signatory card shows and the receipt signing keys, for the signed-in account only while it holds the administrator role, so a sign-out, another account or the loss of the role forgets them all. These pages show the kept answer at once and refresh behind it without their loading line (Loga bank keeps its rows, and a draft typed in them, when the refresh brings the same logos; a kept alerts answer is only shown, never handed to `OpenAlerts`, so the badge follows the fresh one, and "Co se hlídá" names the open alerts `OpenAlerts` holds whenever it holds any, so the page and the badge never disagree); a failed refresh keeps what is shown, except a 401 or 403, which reaches the page as before (a role taken away still leads to the overview), and a refresh that failed offline is asked for again when the connection comes back (`reloadWhenOnlineAgain`); the two Nastavení cards opened while offline show nothing kept and say, as before, that they show once online, so a key or a signatory that may have changed meanwhile never stands as current. Choosing who signs in Lidé fondu and uploading or removing a receipt signature forget the kept signature block (`forget`), so Nastavení never shows the earlier choice from memory. Every change made there (granting or removing the role, turning a second factor off, withdrawing a key, the key a restart brings, a device's alerts turned on, off or switched, a device removed, a bank logo saved, downloaded again or removed) goes through `ListChanges`, so an answer asked for before it is set aside: the list is asked for again once no change runs, unless a load begun after the change or the change's own answer (`answeredByChange`, the withdrawal and the restart) already brings the fresh list (the changes of Upozornění and Loga bank show their one row in place and keep the list so changed, and the list is asked for again only when an answer was set aside; turning alerts on reads the list again itself), so a withdrawn key never shows as valid again for a moment. The administration's home, Zálohy, Historie and Logy keep their loading line for now.

## admin

- `core/auth/admin-guard.ts` on `/sprava/**`: restores the session first; an administrator passes, a signed-in donor without the role lands on `/darce/prehled`, and the guard tells `core/auth/admin-access-refusal.ts` (`AdminAccessRefusal`, a root signal) why, so the overview opens with a warning callout in an always-rendered `role="status"`: "Tento účet nemá přístup do správy. Pokud ho potřebujete, požádejte jiného správce, aby vám ho přidělil.", or, for an administrator signed in without the second factor (`secondFactor.neededForAdministration`), "Správa se vám otevře, až se přihlásíte přístupovým klíčem nebo kódem z aplikace. Kód z aplikace si zapnete v části Údaje a zabezpečení." with a link to `/darce/udaje`; leaving the overview clears it; a visitor goes to `/` with the sign-in dialog open and returns to the asked page after signing in (like `donor-guard.ts`); the guard opens it with `forAdministration: true`, so the login tab's lead says "Pro vstup do správy se přihlaste." instead of "Přístup do vašeho přehledu příspěvků".
- `shell/admin-shell` is the parent route of every `/sprava` page (`admin.routes.ts`, plan v3 2.24, the approved mockup `ucet-a-sprava-mobil-po-revizi-2026-10-31`): the app header and the site's tab bar of the public pages and the donor area (one app, Jakub 2026-10-31; the route `sprava` has `head: 'light'`, `parent: '/'` and `backOnWide: false`), `DIALOG_PRESENTATION` `sheet`, so every `presentation="auto"` dialog of the administration is a sheet below `nav`, and between them `<app-side-nav navId="admin-nav" label="Správa" [panel]="!compact()">` around the `router-outlet`, the panel only above `nav`. `shell/admin-sections.ts` holds `ADMIN_SECTIONS`, the panel: "Přehled" (`/sprava`, exact), then groups by how often an administrator needs them (Jakub 2026-10-24): `ADMIN_DAILY` Každý den ("Platby z banky", "Dárci", "Aktuality", "Pomáháme"), `ADMIN_OCCASIONAL` Občas ("Hromadná potvrzení", once a year and so first there, slice 2.29, "Ověřit potvrzení" (`/sprava/overit`, slice 2.30), "Fotka v sekci O nás", "Lidé fondu", "Správci", "Měsíční e-mail", "Zálohy", "Upozornění", "Historie změn"), `ADMIN_TECHNICAL` Pro technika ("Nastavení", "Logy a diagnostika", "Odpovědi o starších symbolech", "Loga bank"); a new section adds its entry there (texts `admin.nav.*`) and its route a `parent`. Every child route names its `parent` for the header's back button: the daily sections `/sprava`, a donor `/sprava/darci`, an editor its list, the occasional and technical pages `/sprava/vice`, so every section is two taps from the overview (from a daily section to an occasional one it is three: Back, the row, the section) and Back retraces them. From `nav` up the side navigation lists those sections itself and never `/sprava/vice` (#1187): the route's `computerLeavesOutMore` (`more/`) sends a computer to Přehled with `replaceUrl`, and `AdminMore` does the same when the window widens past `nav`, so the address keeps working and a phone keeps the page. `shell/administration-lock.ts`: the token `ADMINISTRATION_LOCK` (`{ locked, panel, keepsPage, firstDownload }`, unlocked with no panel by default; `admin.routes.ts` provides it on the shell route from `lock/administration-lock-service.ts`, slice 2.19): while `locked()` the shell renders `panel()` (a signal, `NgComponentOutlet`) instead of the page, with the header and the tabs kept; locking removes the `router-outlet`, except when `keepsPage()` (a form was half-typed, `DirtyForms.any()`, at the moment it locked): then the page stays rendered, `hidden` and `inert`, under the panel and is there again, text and all, after the unlock. The panel enters with Objevení (`motion.appear`, a short fade under reduced motion, #1195), while the page under it is `hidden` and `inert` from the same render, so nothing of it lingers during the entrance, and the panel's `h1` takes the focus. The kept page sits in `[appSuspendsDialogs]` (`shared/ui/dialog/dialog-suspension.ts`, the token `DIALOG_SUSPENSION`, `false` by default): a dialog of the page closes its native `<dialog>` while locked, without being dismissed, and opens again at the unlock, so a hidden modal never blocks the panel; a navigation while locked asks `unsavedChangesGuard`, which lets a page that is not rendered go (`null`) and lets a kept editor's question wait behind the panel. The panel's heading takes the focus when it appears; at the unlock the shell gives the focus back to the field of the kept page that had it, else to the page's `h1`. While unlocked, `first-download-notice/` (`app-first-download-notice`, in the region `.admin-first-download`) shows "Ukládáme správu do zařízení" with a progress bar and "n z m" during the first download of the administration's copy (`firstDownload()`), and how it ended (`firstDownloadEnd()`, set by `AdministrationCopyDownload` only for a download whose bar showed, cleared when the next one starts or the copy changes; #1002): stored (also unchanged), when the bar stood for at least a second, "Správa je uložená v tomto zařízení." for 4 s; failed, refused as too many requests or a mismatch, in the warning look, "Správu se do tohoto zařízení teď nepodařilo uložit. Online vše funguje dál." with "Uložit znovu" (`appNeedsConnection`, `retryFirstDownload()` = `refresh()`), until the next download; the notice is a size container (`first-download`), and below `pair` it puts "Uložit znovu" under its text, at its start, and below `nav` it keeps the cards' gap (`--space-5`) above the summary card at every text size (#1410); the notice stands in `app-fold` (`shared/ui/fold`) and folds away whenever it leaves, keeping its last text while it folds, and it is created open when it first shows, so it never folds in; folded, it is `display: none`, so the summary card ends where it stands on the page without the notice (on a phone `--sheet-header-gap` under the sheet head, on a computer the cards' gap); after "Uložit znovu" the failure stays up to 1 s while the retry runs, its button busy (`appBusy`: `aria-disabled`, the focus stays on it and a second press does nothing), and stored within it, the failure folds away, the shell focuses the page heading at once (`releasesFocus` → `focusPage`) and the live line says "Správa je uložená v tomto zařízení." once, while a longer retry brings the bar back and the focus goes to the heading as the button leaves (#1426); no longer an administrator, another account or a closed copy end without a word. Its live region is a visually hidden line of its own, always rendered, that says "Ukládáme správu do zařízení." once when the download starts and then the stored or failed sentence, while the visible title and sentences (`aria-hidden`), the count and the bar stay outside it, so a screen reader hears the start and the end and not every stored part (#1003; browse mode still reads the count, and the "Uložit znovu" button stays reachable). `whenAdministrationCopied` and `openUnlockedAdministrationPage` (`e2e/donor-session.ts`) wait for the stored line to go too. `lock/`: `administration-lock-service.ts` (`locked` = an administrator and `AdministrationAccess` not unlocked, `passkeyUnlocks()`, `unlockWithPasskey()`, `accountPasskeyUnlocks()` (the account holds a passkey, `GET /api/donors/me/passkeys`), `unlockWithAccountPasskey()` (a passkey ceremony with PRF over a sign-in challenge, `POST …/unlock` with `{passkey}`, then the access unlocks and the vault's `unlockAdministrationWithPrf` replaces a copy no passkey opens with a fresh one under that passkey, each only while the same administrator is still signed in; a cancelled ceremony sends nothing and the PRF output is zeroed whatever happens), `unlockWithPassword(password, code)`: the server checks both, then the access unlocks and the vault opens the copy with the password, each only while the same administrator is still signed in; `signOut()` keeps `locked` through `logout()` and the navigation home, so a kept page never shows on the way out) and `administration-lock-panel` ("Správa je zamčená": "Odemknout přístupovým klíčem" first when the copy has a PRF wrap, never asked for before the press, and otherwise, when the account has a passkey, the same button as the main way through the server (`appNeedsConnection`, disabled offline, Jakub 2026-11-06), "Použít heslo" beside it or as the main button, disabled offline with "Jste offline…", the password view "Odemknout heslem" with `#zamek-heslo`, the hidden user name and `#zamek-kod`, "Odhlásit se z tohoto zařízení", and only signing out when neither way can unlock; both views' `h1` is the card's `.ui-card__title`, kept at `--text-3xl` by the panel's own styles as the page's heading, #851). `device-copy/`: `administration-sync-api.ts` (`/api/admin/donations/sync`, `/api/admin/donors/sync`, `/api/admin/content/sync` and `/api/admin/operations/sync` (2.20c) with their `/chunks`, `POST …/devices/current/administration-copy` and `/unlock`) and `administration-copy-download.ts` (`AdministrationCopyDownload`, loaded by `device-copies.ts` for an administrator: when the vault opens the administration copy it records a first copy stored on the device before downloading it, downloads the four manifests in that order and their chunks as `donations/<id>`, `donors/<id>`, `content/<id>` and `operations/<id>` (a 429 of any part, `donations/`, `donors/`, `content/` or `operations/rate-limited`, ends the refresh as `rate-limited`; another account's subject in any part stores nothing; a copy stored before 2.20c holds no `content/` or `operations/` chunk and simply downloads them at its next refresh, with no change to the stored records), checks every digest twice at most, reports `device-copy-mismatch`, clears the copy on `administrator: false`, keeps it on a 403, and shows `firstDownload` only for a copy that opened empty) and `administration-copy.ts` (`AdministrationCopy`, root: what the pages read from the open administration copy; `rows(part, kind)` gives one signal per part and kind, the same signal at every call, with the rows of every chunk `<part>/<kind>` or `<part>/<kind>:…` in the order of the chunk ids and each chunk's rows as the server listed them, `null` while no copy is open and loaded (locked, downloading for the first time, empty or damaged) and `[]` for a loaded copy without that kind, so a page can choose between the copy and the API; `summary` the donations summary `{ today, receiptsAvailableFrom, byYear }` and `storedAt` the time of the last download, both `null` while nothing is loaded). Slice 2.20a reads the pages from it: `administration-copy-download.ts` also offers `refresh(startingNow)` (`stored`, `unchanged`, `failed`, `mismatch`, `revoked`, `another-account`, `unrecorded` or `no-copy`; a call while one runs for the same copy joins it, and one with `startingNow` waits for the running one and then starts a new one, which every later such call joins until it starts, so a refresh asked for after a write reads the manifests after it), which downloads the four manifests and only the chunks whose digest changed or that are new, keeps the others, drops those the server no longer lists and stores nothing when nothing changed, and `checkedAt`, the last time the server confirmed the copy; the first download after an unlock goes the same incremental way. `administration-views.ts` computes what the server answers from the copy's rows, specified by the shared cases `admin-payments/list.json`, `admin-donors/record-search.json`, `admin-donors/account-search.json` and `admin-donors/donor-card.json` (`docs/system-overview.md`, Shared cases): `paymentList` (unpaired or paired, the text, amount, date, donor and year filters, paging), `donorRecordSearch`, `accountSearch` and `donorCard` (the card without its bank accounts, or `null` for a donor the copy does not hold). Names are in Czech alphabetical order as the server orders them (`docs/system-overview.md`, "Text order and the database locale"): `Intl.Collator('cs')`, the same ICU Czech collation as `cs-x-icu`, and two names it finds equal by their code points, as PostgreSQL breaks the tie, then the donor id or the e-mail by code points. `administration-reads.ts` (`AdministrationReads`, root) is what the pages call instead of `AdminApi` for these reads: with no copy open it asks the server as before; with a copy open and the device online it refreshes the copy at most once in `REFRESH_AFTER_MS` (a minute), and after every change (each successful administration write, which `core/admin-writes/administration-writes.ts` counts in `administrationWritesInterceptor`: every non-GET under `/api/admin/` but the device's own `devices/current/…`; and every `changed()`, from a live `payments` hint or a refresh the page asks for; a refresh confirms only the changes made before it was asked for, through `refresh(true)`, and one that ends after another copy opened confirms nothing), then computes the answer from the copy; when a refresh fails online it asks the server and waits a minute before the next refresh; offline it answers from the copy without asking, and a new copy (another unlock or account) starts the minute again and counts as fresh only for the changes made before its running or last download started (`AdministrationCopyDownload.writesAtStart`). Since 2.20b a forced refresh starts at most once in `FORCED_REFRESH_GAP_MS` (30 s) after the last one started; a change within that gap is read from the server instead, so a burst of pairings costs one download. A 429 of the sync (`donations/rate-limited`, 60 per 15 minutes per administrator, answered without `Retry-After`) gives the outcome `'rate-limited'` and pauses refreshes for `RATE_LIMITED_PAUSE_MS` (five minutes), with the reads going to the server meanwhile. The tabs of one device share these checks over the `BroadcastChannel` `nfnn.administration-checks` (`{copyId, storedAt, kind: 'checked' | 'rate-limited', at}`, validated, another copy's ignored, times capped at now and at now plus the pause): a refresh that found the copy unchanged starts the other tabs' minute, but only in a tab that holds the same contents (`storedAt`), a 429 their pause, and a stored copy reaches them anyway through the vault's channel; the 30 s gap after a change is each tab's own. `overview(year)` (2.20b) computes the home's year overview from the copy (`administration-overview.ts`, the shared cases of `admin-overview/year-overview.json`): `everyOfferedYear` over the copy's payments, gifts and donor records for the summary's `today` must give the summary's `byYear` (compared by `canonicalRows` over the fields the browser computes, so a field the server adds later does not turn the copy off), and then the asked year's entry answers; when they differ it asks the server and sends a `device-copy-mismatch` report at most once per copy contents and once an hour, as the donor's view limits its reports. The paired payments and the Prague days of the registrations are prepared once per contents (registrations sorted and counted by binary search), not once per year. `importState()` asks the server online (the history import's run lives only there, so `<app-history-import>` shows only with the server's answer) and answers from the copy's `import-state` row offline or when the server is unreachable, with `lastFailure` as kind and time only (`message: null`), so the home names the failure without its text; when the device is back online the home reads the import state again from the server. A newer overview read (another year chosen) cancels an older one. The home's unpaired count reads `paymentList('unpaired')`. Two reads stay with the server while online, because the copy leaves an erased donor's receipts and the payer of their gifts out (2.17): a paired list that would hold a bank gift of an erased donor (on its page, or, when a text is searched, among all the payments its other filters keep), and an erased donor's card. `donorCard` gives the card from the copy at once with `bankAccounts: 'pending'` and then the server's card whole (`'server'`); offline, or when the server is unreachable, it keeps the copy's card with `'offline'` ("Bankovní účty dárce ukážeme, až budete online.", read again once the device is back online), and on another failure `'failed'` with "Zkusit znovu", because the copy holds the links but whether a link pairs is decided by the server's settings; a copy stored before 2.20a (no `receiptsAvailableFrom`) reads the card from the server. Every answer from the copy records the copy's time (the later of `checkedAt` and `storedAt`) in `ShownAnswers` as `device-copy:administration`, so the offline pill says "Offline · údaje z <time>" of the oldest data shown. Every write still goes to the server and is disabled offline (`appNeedsConnection`). Slice 2.20c reads the content, settings, history and backups pages through `administration-page-reads.ts` (`AdministrationPageReads`, root), built on `AdministrationReads.copyFirst(local, server)`, the same refresh and freshness rules: each chunk row is exactly what the page's GET answers, so nothing is computed but the history. The content part holds `news` and `projects` (the admin lists in their order, absent when empty), `project-order` (`{order}`), `about` and `donate-band` (always one row `{photo}`) and `people` (the GET answer with `noteVersion` and `footnoteVersion`), and the operations part `settings` (secrets only as set or not) and `backups`; a copy without the `content/people` row, or without `operations/settings`, holds no such part yet and the pages ask the server. `newsList()`, `newsArticle(id)` (the list item with that id, the server when the copy has none), `projectList()`, `project(id)`, `settings()` and `backupsWithoutRunErrors()` (the administration home and the backup history) answer the row, a chunk the copy lacks sends the read to the server; `projectOrder()`, `about()`, `donateBand()` and `people()` read the server first and the copy only offline or on an unreachable failure (`serverFirst`, #993), because their writes name no version (`docs/api-contract.md`, "Versioned records and writes") and a form opened from a copy up to a minute old would save over a connection's change without a 409; `report()` builds `{settings, sentReports, nextReport, lastEndedMonth}` (with `heldAt`, the copy's time, when it came from the copy) from the donations `report-settings` row, which carries `nextReport` and `lastEndedMonth` as the server computed them (no device clock; a row without them, from an older copy, sends the read to the server), and `changeReportSettings` lets `sentReports`, `nextReport` and `lastEndedMonth` of the refusal's record differ; `backups()` (Zálohy) and `adminActions(after, limit)` (Historie změn) read the server first while `Connectivity` says online, and the copy only offline or on an unreachable failure (`AdministrationReads.serverFirst`, as `importState()` of 2.20b), because the copy holds neither a run's `error` nor an entry's `details`; from the copy `adminActions` lists the donors `audit:<month>` rows of every month newest first, ties by the larger id (the server's `performed_at desc, id desc`; instants compared to the nanosecond), pages by the same `before`/`beforeId` cursor by comparison (the entries strictly older than the cursor's time and id, so a cursor from a server page the copy lacks still pages on), names the administrator from the `accounts:` rows (`adminName`, `adminEmail`; the server actor and an administrator without an account stay without), keeps `donorId`, gives `details: ''`, a missing `summary` an empty one (the row then shows only the action's name), and `fromCopy: true`, so the history page shows "Podrobnosti ukážeme, až budete online." (or "…, až server zase odpoví." while `Connectivity.cause()` is `server` or the device is online) inside Technické podrobnosti of those entries (`details` stays server-only, D37). The pages that read through it: Aktuality (list and editor, its signers from `people()`), Pomáháme (list, its order card and the editor), Fotka v sekci O nás, Fotka v pruhu Přispět, Lidé fondu, Nastavení, Měsíční e-mail, Historie změn, Zálohy and its history; offline each opens from the copy and the offline pill names the copy's time, every write control already carries `appNeedsConnection`, Zálohy is view-only (the running job's poll and the restore watch stay with the server; `backups()` answers with `fromCopy`, and as the copy's runs carry `error: null`, a failed run read from it shows its outcome and time with the plain muted line "Podrobnosti chyby ukážeme, až budete online." or "…, až server zase odpoví." instead of a disclosure), Měsíční e-mail read offline from a copy whose `nextReport.dueOn` has passed by the device clock (display only) says "Podle údajů z <čas kopie> měl přehled za <měsíc> odejít <den> v <hodina>; jestli odešel, ukážeme, až budete online." (or "…, až server zase odpoví."), and "Načíst starší" in the history works offline (`WORKS_WITHOUT_CONNECTION`), paging the copy. A reload after a changed-meanwhile refusal calls `changed()` first, so it reads the server or a fresh copy, and drafts (2.20e) compare their version with the copy's row, which equals the server's. What the copy does not hold says it needs the server instead of failing the page (`reloadWhenOnlineAgain` in `device-copy/online-again.ts`: a failed read while `Connectivity.offline()` sets the part's `offline` state, read again once the device is back online; a failure while online keeps the `error` state with its "Zkusit znovu"; the offline line sits in an always rendered `role="status"` element, worded by `Connectivity.cause()`): the receipt signing key ("Klíč ukážeme, až budete online." / "…, až server zase odpoví.") and who signs the receipts ("Kdo potvrzení podepisuje, ukážeme, až budete online." / "…, až server zase odpoví.") on Nastavení and on a person's card in Lidé fondu, and the order card of an older copy ("Řazení ukážeme, až budete online." / "…, až server zase odpoví."); the logs and a sent report's page stay server-only. `verification/` (`/sprava/overit`, "Ověřit potvrzení", plan v3 2.30, Q10): `admin-verification.ts` shows the public `VerificationPage` under the token `VERIFICATION_CONTEXT` (`features/donations/verification/verification-context.ts`: its own address for `replaceState`, `audience` `administration`, and `registerLine`, the component `VerificationResult` renders through `NgComponentOutlet` right under the verdict of a valid signature with its `receipt` and `status`; the public page keeps `PUBLIC_VERIFICATION`, `audience` `public` and no line), so the reading, the camera, the checks and the asking of the status are the public page's. For the administration the page keeps the donor cards: its `app-page-title` "Ověřit potvrzení" and its own intro (read the code here, not with the phone's camera app, which opens the public page), the host marked `verify-embedded`, which drops the public page's own header offset, height and background inside the shell, and no note about nfnn.cz/overit, and the verdicts that send a visitor to info@nfnn.cz say instead where to look (the donor card) or to send the number to the technician, and offline, or when the fund gave no answer, the verdict points to the register line. `register-line.ts` (`app-register-line`, inputs `receipt` and `status`) adds, between the verdict card and "Údaje v kódu", the card "V evidenci fondu" (a `.ui-card--padded` with its `h2` as `.ui-card__title`, as "Údaje v kódu") from `AdministrationCopy.rows('donations', 'receipts')` as of the last download ("Podle údajů správy stažených do tohoto zařízení … v …"), its state in an always rendered `role="status"`: `receipt-register.ts` (`registerEntry`) finds the row of the receipt's number (`active`, `replaced` with the newer receipt of the same donor and kind issued later, the same year for a yearly receipt or the same gift for a single one, `withdrawn`, `outdated`, `differs` when the kind, the issue day, the number of gifts, the total or the year are not the copy's, `not-held`), and `registerView` sets it against the fund's answer: not asked or no answer (offline), the copy's state with its next step ("Platné", "Nahrazené", "Zastaralé", "Zrušené", "Nesouhlasí", "Nenalezeno"); the fund answered as the copy holds it, the copy's state, confirmed, adding only what the answer lacks (the day, the newer number); the fund answered otherwise, "Starší údaje", or, when the copy holds the number with other data, a red "Nesouhlasí" for the technician (a frozen receipt never changes), or, when the copy lacks the number, "Nenalezeno" with its likely reasons (the answer counts, the copy is never shown as valid then); the fund does not know a receipt the copy holds with these data, "Nesouhlasí" (red, never valid); no copy loaded, that the data are not here yet. Every state with a row links "Otevřít kartu dárce". Nothing more leaves the device than on the public page. `shell/section-rows/` (`app-admin-section-rows`, inputs `label` and `groups` of `SectionRowGroup { id, label, rows: SectionRow { path, label, icon?, badge? } }`): a `nav` of donor cards (Jakub 2026-11-14: the administration shares the donor cards), one `section.ui-card` per group named by its `h2.ui-card__title` in the card's `.ui-card__bar`, its rows a plain `ui-list` edge to edge with an optional inline icon (`payments`, `donors`, `news`, `projects`, `more`), the title, an amber count pill `.ui-list__count--warning` (`aria-hidden`, its description visually hidden, only above 0) and the chevron. `more/` (`/sprava/vice`, "Občas a pro technika", `h1` focused on open): the same rows, `nav` "Další sekce správy", for `ADMIN_OCCASIONAL` and `ADMIN_TECHNICAL`. On a phone (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`) the shell stands as a sheet over the page (#638, Jakub 2026-11-13, variant A; host class `admin-shell--sheet`): instead of `<app-header>` it draws an `aria-hidden` backdrop `.admin-sheet-backdrop` (`--color-backdrop`) and puts the side nav and its `router-outlet` into `.admin-sheet`, which is the next screen of the account sheet (#989): its top edge (`sheet.$top`, the top safe area plus `--sheet-top-gap`), width, corners, shadow and colours come from `styles/_sheet.scss`, the same partial as the account sheet's tall panel, and its head is the account section's `app-sheet-head` (`role="banner"`): Zpět (`BackTarget.back(parent)`, as the header's button did), the page's title in the middle and Zavřít. The title is the text of the page's `h1`, followed by a `MutationObserver`, so a donor's name shows once it arrives; it is `aria-hidden`, since the `h1` stays in the page for the focus and screen readers, visually hidden by the global block `.ui-sheet-page` (`styles/ui/_sheet-page.scss`), which also starts the page `--sheet-header-gap` under the head and adds what the sheet's inline insets with the safe areas exceed the pages' own `--space-4` by, so the page's cards and the notices above them stand where a section's body does. Zavřít in a section asks first only on its own press; a swipe down, Esc or a tap on the backdrop still close without asking (#1071). The window scrolls, not the sheet, so scroll restoration, large titles and reveals work unchanged. The shell records the history depth of its first page (`nfnnAppDepth` in `history.state`, read at once or, when the router creates the shell during its own navigation, at that navigation's end) and keeps it as `nfnnAdminEntry` in the state of every administration entry, so a reload, a return from outside the administration and a new shell still know it. Zavřít steps back `depth now − entry + 1` steps through `BackTarget.backBy`, which counts as a press of a button, so the page it returns to takes the focus on its `h1`. It goes to the page the administration was opened from, or, when the administration was its first page (a notification, `/aplikace`, a typed address), replaces with `/`. The head sticks at the sheet's top edge and keeps its rounded corners while the page scrolls under it, and a fixed `aria-hidden` strip `.admin-sheet-cover` (as tall as the top edge plus `--radius-sheet`) hides what scrolls above it, on the same opaque ground as `.admin-sheet-backdrop` (the backdrop colour over `--color-surface`, one rule for both, so a sheet narrower than the window shows no seam beside it), so the status bar strip looks as above an account section; the sheet's shadow above the head is drawn by the head's `::before`, clipped at the head's bottom. Before #989 the head pulled itself under the status bar with a negative top margin, which collapsed through `.admin-sheet` and lifted the whole sheet by `--safe-area-top`: in the home-screen app (where that is the inset plus `--home-screen-top-clearance`) the administration started at the screen's top while a section started under the status bar. Its leading button keeps `--window-controls-inset-inline` beside an iPad's window controls, and the backdrop carries the sheet backdrop's blur, so Safari does not tint its bars from it. A route with `data.adminFrame: 'full'` (the news and story editors) keeps the full-screen frame with the app header. The `router-outlet` keeps its parent across both frames, so a page keeps its state when the frame changes. Wide screens are unchanged. The pages render no "← Správa" link; the crumbs of a donor and of the news and project editors show only above `nav`, where no back button stands; their `.container` is left-aligned beside the panel.
- `admin-api.ts`: `importState()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/import-state` (`ImportStateResponse` with `historyImport`, `defaultHistoryFrom` and `balance` (`AccountBalance`), `ImportFailure`, `ImportFailureKind` as in the contract); `requestHistoryImport(from)` → `POST /api/admin/donations/import/history` (`HistoryImportResponse`, `HistoryImportStatus`, slice 2.33); `payments(pairing, year)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/payments` (`AdminPaymentListResponse`, `AdminPaymentResponse`, `PairedDonationResponse`, `PairingMethod`), `searchDonors(query)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/donors` (`AdminDonorSearchResponse`, `AdminDonorResponse`), `pairPayment(paymentId, donorId)` and `unpairPayment(paymentId)` → the two `POST`s (`PairPaymentResponse`, `UnpairPaymentResponse`); `administrators()` → `GET /api/admin/donors/administrators` (`AdministratorListResponse`, `AdministratorResponse`), `grantAdministration(email)` → `POST` there, `revokeAdministration(donorId)` → `DELETE .../administrators/{id}`, `resetSecondFactor(donorId)` → `POST /api/admin/donors/{id}/second-factor/reset`; `symbolClaims()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/symbol-claims` (`SymbolClaimListResponse`, `SymbolClaimResponse`, `SymbolClaimAnswer`), `reverseSymbolClaim(id)` → `POST .../symbol-claims/{id}/reverse`, `deleteSymbolClaim(id)` → `DELETE .../symbol-claims/{id}`; `adminActions(before?, limit = 50)` → `GET /api/admin/donors/actions` (`AdminActionListResponse`, `AdminActionResponse`); `donors(query)` → `GET /api/admin/donors` (`DonorAccountListResponse`, `DonorAccountResponse`, `AddressResponse`), `donor(donorId)` → `GET /api/admin/donors/{id}`, `donorDonations(donorId)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/donors/{id}` (`AdminDonorDetailResponse`, `AdminDonorRecordResponse`, `AdminReceiptResponse`, `ReceiptKind`; the accounts and donations reuse `BankAccountItem` and `DonationListResponse` of the donor features), `disconnectDonorAccount`, `reconnectDonorAccount` and `removeDonorAccount(donorId, linkId)` → the two `POST`s and the `DELETE` under `.../bank-accounts/{linkId}`; `report()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/report` (`AdminReportResponse`, `ReportSettingsResponse`, `ReportSectionsResponse`, `SentReportResponse`, `NextReportResponse`), `changeReportSettings(request)` → `PUT .../report/settings` (`ReportSettingsRequest`), `sendReport(month)` → `POST .../report/send` (`SendReportResponse`), `overview(year)` → `GET /api/admin/donations/overview` (`AdminOverviewResponse`, `MonthOverviewResponse`, `TallyResponse`), `backups()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/backups` (`AdminBackupsResponse`, `BackupFileResponse`, `BackupRunResponse`, `RestoreFileResponse`, `BackupKind`, `RunOutcome`, `RestoreFileState`, `BackupProblem`), `requestBackup(kind)` → `POST .../backups/requests` (`BackupRequestResponse`), `restoreBackup(fileName)` → `POST .../backups/{fileName}/restore` (`RestoreRequestResponse`), `downloadBackup(fileName)` → `GET .../backups/{fileName}` as a blob turned into a `DownloadedFile` by `downloadedFile` from `donations-api.ts`; `logFiles()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/logs` (`LogFilesResponse`, `LogFileResponse`), `logWindow(name, from, bytes)` → `GET .../logs/{name}` (`LogWindowResponse`), `searchLog(name, pattern, before, limit)` → `GET .../logs/{name}/search` (`LogSearchResponse`, `LogMatchResponse`), `logLevels()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/loggers` (`LogLevelsResponse`, `LoggerResponse`, `LogOverrideResponse`, `LogLevel`), `setLogLevel(logger, level, minutes)` → `PUT .../loggers/{logger}`, `resetLogLevel(logger)` → `DELETE` there, `diagnostics()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/diagnostics` (`DiagnosticsResponse`, `JvmDiagnosticsResponse`, `DatabaseDiagnosticsResponse`, `DiskSpaceResponse`), `threadDump()` → `GET .../diagnostics/threads` as text, `push()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/push` (`AdminPushResponse`, `PushSubscriptionResponse`, `OpenAlertResponse`, `PushAlertSwitches`, `PushAlertArea`, `PushService`), `subscribePush(request)` → `POST .../push/subscriptions` (`PushSubscriptionRequest`), `changePushAlerts(id, alerts)` → `PUT .../push/subscriptions/{id}/alerts`, `removePushSubscription(id)` → `DELETE .../push/subscriptions/{id}`, `testPush()` → `POST .../push/test` (`PushTestResponse`), `settings()` → `GET /api/admin/operations/settings` (`AdminSettingsResponse`, `AdminSettingResponse`, `SettingGroup`), `changeSettings(changes)` → `PUT` there (`SettingsSavedResponse`), `sendTestEmail(request)` → `POST .../settings/test-email` (`AdminTestEmailRequest`: `MailServerRequest` plus `sender`; `TestEmailResponse`), `receiptSignatory()` and `chooseReceiptSignatory({personId, receiptRole})` → `GET` and `PUT /api/admin/donations/receipt-signatory` (`ReceiptSignatoryResponse` with `ChosenReceiptSignatoryResponse`; `MAX_RECEIPT_ROLE_LENGTH` 120; the person's signature is set at the people of the foundation, `PeopleApi.setSignature`); `receiptSigningKeys()` → `GET /api/admin/donations/receipt-signing-keys` (`ReceiptSigningKeysResponse`, `ReceiptSigningKeyResponse`, `ReceiptSigningKeyStatus`); fixtures in `testing/admin-fixtures.ts` (`importState`, `unpairedPayment`, `pairedDonation`, `paymentList`, `foundDonor`, `administrator`, `symbolClaim`, `adminAction`, `donorAccount`, `adminBankAccount`, `adminDonation`, `adminReceipt`, `donorDetail`, `reportView`, `monthOverview`, `overview`, `backupFile`, `backupRun`, `backupsView`, `logFile`, `logWindow`, `logOverride`, `loggerEntry`, `diagnostics`, `setting`, `settingsView`, `receiptSignature`, `noReceiptSignature`).
- `payments/` (`/sprava/platby`, "Platby z banky"): `h1` focused on open, an intro, and a toolbar with the two `aria-pressed` buttons "Čekají na přiřazení" (default; "Čekají na přiřazení (N)" once the unpaired list has answered, N its `total`, one more after each unpairing) and "Přiřazené" (the count only from an unfiltered unpaired list), then a `role="search"` bar with one text field "Hledat" (hint "Jméno odesílatele nebo dárce, účet, variabilní symbol, zpráva nebo částka v korunách.", asked 300 ms after the typing pauses) and the native date fields "Od" and "Do" (asked on change, each bounding the other with `min`/`max`; on a computer, under a chosen day, "Vybraný den: {datum}." through `formatDonationDate`, its `aria-describedby`, because a desktop browser draws a native date field in its own language and order, so an English one shows month first even on this Czech page; not while `CompactLayout.isCompact()`, where the phone already says the date in its own words, #1207). The state lives in the address (Jakub 2026-10-24): `stav=prirazene` for the paired tab, `q`, `od`, `do` and `darce` (a donor id, from the donor card's link "Platby tohoto dárce"; switching to the unpaired tab drops it), written with `replaceUrl` so a filter change adds no history entry, while Back from a donor card and a shared link open the same view; the page follows `queryParamMap`, so the panel link resets it. Each view asks for 50 (`limit=50`, `switchMap` drops a stale answer); while fewer are shown than `total`, the line "Zobrazeno 50 z 312." and "Načíst další" ("Načítáme další…") under the table ask for the next 50 with `offset` and append them, the focus moves to the first new row (`tabindex="-1"`), a failure is a `role="alert"` "Další platby se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." and the button stays. With any filter the always-rendered `role="status"` line `.payments-count` says "Nalezena 1 platba." / "Nalezeny 3 platby." / "Nalezeno 12 plateb." / "Nenalezena žádná platba.", and when a live `payments` hint (2.16) brings payments the view does not show yet, the same line ends with the button "Zobrazit 1 novou platbu" / "Zobrazit 2 nové platby" / "Zobrazit 5 nových plateb", which shows the first page again and focuses the card's title, while the tab count follows at once (only payments that come before the last row shown count as new, so a payment that moves up from below when one leaves a longer list simply joins the rows; a hint that brings nothing new updates the shown rows in place; while the pairing dialog or an unpairing confirmation is open, or the next page loads, the page waits and reads after it closes or arrives), an empty result says "Žádná platba neodpovídá hledání." with "Zrušit filtr" (clears text, period and donor and focuses the search field), and a donor filter adds "Jen platby dárce <jméno>." (the name from the first listed donation; "Jen platby jednoho dárce." without one) with "Zobrazit platby všech dárců". The paired view lists every year newest first (no year choice any more). The card lists the payments as a table (cards with `data-label` up to 640 px; `.ui-table--compact` at a computer width, as Starší symboly): date (with the amber tag "jen ručně" on a line of its own under the date and the visible note "Správce zrušil přiřazení, web ji proto sám nepřiřadí." when `unpairedByAdminAt` is set), amount (`.ui-amount`, with the badge "Ověření účtu" on a line of its own under it when `accountVerification`, so the 1 Kč of a verification ends where every other amount ends, #1563), sender (name and account), symbol and message ("bez VS" without one; in the five columns of the waiting payments the message keeps at least `--table-text-column`, so a long message reads in a few lines while the sender keeps the rest, #1564; the paired view's six columns have no room for that minimum at 1280 px and keep what the table gives them; in the folded cards the right column takes at most half the row (`fit-content(50%)`), so a long message no longer narrows the sender beside it), for paired payments the donor as a link to `/sprava/darci/{donorId}` ("dárce bez záznamu" when the record is gone) and the tag "vráceno" for a refunded donation, the method in words ("podle variabilního symbolu", "podle účtu", "podle potvrzeného staršího symbolu", "ručně správcem") and the tag "potvrzení vystaveno"; the last column holds "Přiřadit dárci" (unpaired) or "Zrušit přiřazení" (paired; replaced by "Nelze zrušit, k daru je vystavené potvrzení." when `receiptIssued`), which turns into the inline confirmation "Dar 500 Kč zmizí z přehledu dárce <jméno> a platba se vrátí mezi čekající na přiřazení; web ji sám znovu nepřiřadí. Zrušit přiřazení?" (a group named "Zrušit přiřazení platby <platba>?") with "Zrušit přiřazení" ("Rušíme…") / "Ponechat" before the `POST`; focus moves to "Ponechat" when it opens, back to the opener after "Ponechat" or a refusal, and to the page's notice after success. The card titles are "Platby čekající na přiřazení" and "Přiřazené platby". Empty lists say "Všechny platby z banky jsou přiřazené…" or "Zatím není přiřazena žádná platba."; loading is a `role="status"` line, a failed load a `role="alert"` line with "Zkusit znovu"; 401 ends the session and opens the sign-in dialog, 403 goes to `/darce/prehled`. "Přiřadit dárci" opens an `<app-dialog>` naming the payment (amount and day, sender, symbol and message; for an account verification the badge "Ověření účtu" and a warning callout that pairing it by hand counts 1 Kč for the donor but verifies no account, which a payment with the donor's symbol does) with a search field prefilled with the first word of the sender's name (two letters or more), searched at once and focused with its text selected (Jakub 2026-11-12, smoothness, #709: the dialog grew while it slid in as the donors found and their e-mails arrived, so it shows once the first search has answered with both, or failed, at most 300 ms after the press, `PAIRING_WAIT_MS`; a press of another payment meanwhile replaces it, a second press of the same one is ignored, a change of tab, filter or search drops the wait, and a live change waits for the dialog as while it is open); a typed query goes to `searchDonors` 300 ms after the typing pauses, `switchMap` drops stale answers and a failed search does not end the stream listing the donors as radio buttons ("Jana Nováková · VS 4821937560 · jana@example.cz · IČO …": the e-mail comes from `donors(query, 100)`, `GET /api/admin/donors` with the same query, asked beside the search and matched by `donorId`, so two donors of one name can be told apart; the results show at once and the e-mails follow, and a failed lookup only leaves them out; "Žádný dárce neodpovídá." as `role="status"`, a failed search as `role="alert"`); "Přiřadit" becomes "Přiřadit dárci <jméno>" once a donor is chosen and shows "Přiřazujeme…" while posting; a refusal stays in the dialog as a `role="alert"` line (`describeProblem`), success closes it, reloads the list and puts a `role="status"` line on the page ("Platba 500 Kč z 10. 10. 2026 je přiřazena dárci Jana Nováková."), extended by "Dárce si už stáhl roční potvrzení za 2026; při dalším stažení dostane nové. Dejte mu prosím vědět." when `changedYearlyReceipts` is not empty; unpairing reports the same way ("… už není přiřazena. Web ji sám znovu nepřiřadí; najdete ji mezi platbami čekajícími na přiřazení."), extended, when it withdrew the yearly receipt that listed the gift (1.26), by "Roční potvrzení za 2026, na kterém dar byl, je teď zrušené; ověření ukáže, že platilo ke dni vystavení. Zbyly-li dárci za ten rok další dary, může si stáhnout nové. Dejte mu prosím vědět."; after either, the link "Otevřít kartu dárce <jméno>" sits under the notice. After any step back from a donor card (‹, the swipe, the system step), Platby focuses the row of that donor's payment (on a wide table its donor link), else its heading, and scrolls it wholly into view (`block: 'nearest'`). Specs in `admin-payments.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-payments.spec.ts` (pair through the dialog with the receipt note and the paired view, unpair with the confirmation and the held tag, a refused pairing staying in the dialog and Escape returning focus to the opener, a search with the next page and Back from a donor card restoring it) and the axe case for the page and the open dialog.
- `administrators/` (`/sprava/spravci`, "Správci"): `h1` focused on open (outline hidden, like the other script-focused headings), an intro saying who can become an administrator. The card "Kdo web spravuje" lists the administrators in the look of the people of the foundation (Jakub 2026-10-31: the shared block `.ui-person-list` / `.ui-person-row` of `styles/ui/_person-list.scss`): each row has a small round portrait (`app-avatar`, the initials without one), the name as a link to the donor card, the e-mail under it and the badges "To jste vy" (the signed-in one, found by `AuthStore.donor().id`) and "Dvoufaktorové ověření zapnuté" or "Dvoufaktorové ověření vypnuté" for `secondFactorEnabled` (the code only; the API does not say whether passkeys exist) with the line "Správa se mu otevře jen přístupovým klíčem." under the latter. The portrait is the one of the person of the foundation linked to the administrator: the page also loads `PeopleApi.load()` once and joins its members to the administrators by e-mail, taking the first member in the order of the site with `linkedToAccount` whose personal or fund e-mail equals the administrator's (the same person `GET /api/admin/content/people/me` gives that administrator for the account button; both e-mails come already normalised by the backend, so the join compares them as they are); when the people cannot be loaded the rows keep their initials and nothing else changes. The actions sit at the right of the row, and under it, as on a phone, while the list (the size container `administrators` on `.administrators-list`) is narrower than `tablet` in the root text size, so at 900 px or with 200 % text the text column keeps its width and no badge reaches under a button (#1577, `e2e/admin-page-widths.spec.ts`): for another administrator "Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření" (only with the factor on; inline confirmation across the row "Ověřili jste, že žádá majitel účtu (například odpověděl z registrovaného e-mailu)? Správce se pak přihlásí jen heslem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí." with "Ano, vypnout" ("Vypínáme…") / "Ponechat") and "Odebrat roli správce" (danger outline; "<jméno> přestane být správcem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí. Jeho dárcovský účet zůstane. Odebrat roli správce?" with "Ano, odebrat" ("Odebíráme…") / "Ponechat"); focus moves to "Ponechat" on open, back to the opener after it or a refusal, and to the notice after success; the signed-in administrator has no buttons but the line "Svůj účet spravujete v Údajích a zabezpečení." (a link to `/darce/udaje`). Outcomes are a `role="status"` line on the page ("Petr Svoboda už není správce. Všechna jeho přihlášení jsme ukončili.", "Dvoufaktorové ověření u Petr Svoboda je vypnuté … Poslali jsme mu e-mail; …") or a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail; the list reloads after each. The card "Přidat správce" is a form with the field "E-mail dárce" (`required`, `email`; "Vyplňte prosím e-mail registrovaného dárce." / "Zadejte prosím platnou e-mailovou adresu.") and "Přidat správce" ("Přidáváme…" while posting); success clears the field and says "<jméno> je teď správce. Správa se mu otevře, až se přihlásí s kódem z ověřovací aplikace nebo přístupovým klíčem." (or "… po dalším přihlášení." when the factor is already on); a refusal (404 not registered, 409 already) stays at the form as a `role="alert"` line and keeps the value. Loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the payments page. Specs in `admin-administrators.spec.ts` (the portrait of the linked person, the first one when two share the e-mail, and the initials when the people cannot be loaded); Playwright `e2e/admin-administrators.spec.ts` (the portrait, add by e-mail, the refusal at the form, reset and removal with their confirmations) and the axe case for the page with an open confirmation and the empty-form error.
- `symbol-claims/` (`/sprava/starsi-symboly`, "Odpovědi o starších symbolech"): `h1` focused on open, an intro paragraph explaining the older-symbol question followed by a two-item list of what each correction does. The card "Odpovědi dárců" lists every answer as a table (cards up to 640 px; `.ui-table--compact` at a computer width): donor as a link to the donor card ("dárce bez záznamu" when the record is gone), account and symbol in the mono font, in the unfolded table the donor's name keeping up to `--table-text-column` (only as much as the name needs, so a long name takes at most two lines and a short one leaves the rest of the room to the other columns; here the token is the cap, and a name of one word longer than it breaks inside the word, `overflow-wrap: anywhere`, instead of running into „Bankovní účet“, #1925) and the opening action button at least `--table-action-column-min`, so „Vrátit přiřazení darů“ and „Zeptat se dárce znovu“ take at most two lines (#1569; at 1280 px an account number without a prefix keeps one line beside a name of an ordinary length, while beside a long name that needs both its lines it may put its bank code on the next line, since 870 px of table cannot hold all three), the answer as a tag ("Ano, jsou moje" green, "Ne, nejsou moje" grey) with the Prague time under it, the donations paired through it ("1 dar", "3 dary", "5 darů"), and the action: "Vrátit přiřazení darů" (danger outline) for a yes (an inline confirmation naming the donations that will be deleted, the payments going back to waiting and the answer turning to Ne, with "Vrátit přiřazení" / "Ponechat" before the `POST`) or "Zeptat se dárce znovu" for a no (with "Zeptat se znovu" / "Ponechat" before the `DELETE`); focus moves as on the administrators page. Outcomes are a `role="status"` line ("Přiřazení darů u Jana Nováková (VS 6123456789) jsme vrátili: 3 dary jsme smazali a platby s tímto symbolem zase čekají na přiřazení. Odpověď je teď Ne, dárce se už neptáme." / "Odpověď Ne u … jsme smazali. Pokud otázka pořád platí, dárce ji uvidí znovu ve svém přehledu.") or a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail (the receipt refusal); the list reloads after each. The empty list says "Zatím nikdo neodpověděl na otázku o starším variabilním symbolu."; loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts` (reverse and delete with their confirmations, the refusal by a receipt) and the axe case for the page with an open confirmation.
- `bank-logos/` (`/sprava/loga-bank`, "Loga bank", contract "Bank logos"): `h1` focused on open, two intro paragraphs (the server downloads the icon from the address, makes it a small square and serves it itself, so the donor's browser never contacts the bank or Apple; only App Store icons from is1-ssl.mzstatic.com to is5-ssl.mzstatic.com (in the intro each host's `isN-ssl.` stays whole in a `.ui-word` and breaks only after its dot, `<wbr>`, #1862) or a picture from the sites of the big banks, named in the text, https without a query, PNG or JPEG; a weekly download again that keeps the old logo on a failure). The card "Banky s logem" lists every bank as a `ui-person-row`: `app-bank-mark` (centred on the code and the state line, the failure and address notes under them) of the stored image (or the monogram of the ČNB name shortened), the code and the short name ("Banka, kterou seznam ČNB nezná" without one; `#bank-title-<code>`, which the row's three buttons name in `aria-describedby`, while the address field is named "Adresa loga <code> · <bank>"), "Staženo …" with "naposledy zkontrolováno …" when the last check is later, or "Zatím nestaženo.", the last failure in Czech words of a closed map (`FAILURE_WORDS`, an unknown word "důvod neznáme"), and for `sourceAllowed: false` "Adresa už není povolená…" without "Stáhnout znovu"; under it, on the same edge as the name (`.bank-form` in the text column, #1576, `e2e/admin-bank-logos.spec.ts`), a form with the field "Adresa loga" (`type="url"`, `enterkeyhint="send"`, required, `https://` without spaces, at most 500 characters), "Uložit a stáhnout" (a secondary button, since every row has one and only "Přidat a stáhnout" of the card below is primary; `PUT` with `If-Match`; the status says "z nové adresy" only when the address changed), "Stáhnout znovu" (`POST …/refresh`; the status says whether the picture changed) and "Odebrat", which opens `bank-logo-remove-dialog` ("Odebrat logo banky …?", `DELETE` with `If-Match`; a refusal stays in the dialog as an alert). The card "Logo další banky" adds a code ("Kód banky", four digits) with its address (`PUT` with `If-None-Match: *`); a code already listed is refused locally at the field "Kód banky". A refused address (`bank-logo-source-invalid`) is shown at its field, a failed download (502), a 429 and the rest under the row's form or the add form as an alert (`describeProblem`), a 404 (removed meanwhile, also for a removal, which closes its dialog) as the page's alert followed by a fresh read. A 409 opens `ChangedMeanwhileDialog` (subject `content`): with `version` it keeps the address in the form and offers to save it over the newer version; without one (the logo was removed and created again meanwhile, or a removal found another version) it only reads the list again. Success lines go to the page's `app-live-notice`; every control that calls the server carries `appNeedsConnection`; each read numbers the rows anew, so the forms start clean after it; only the first read shows the loading line, a read again after the sheet or a 404 keeps the rows until the answer and then focuses the row's address field, or the notice when the row is gone; each saved, downloaded or removed logo calls `BankNames.forgetLogos()`, so the other pages ask for the logos again.
- `yearly-receipts/` (`/sprava/hromadna-potvrzeni`, "Hromadná potvrzení", slice 2.29; contract "Bulk yearly receipts"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what is kept, issued and replaced, and that a single receipt to send by e-mail comes from the donor card. The card "Vystavit a vytisknout" asks `yearlyReceiptCandidates(null)` first (the last completed year) and offers the years from `receiptsAvailableFrom` through `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` in a select, which keeps its place and focus while another year loads (an always rendered status line "Načítáme dárce za rok …" instead of the counts, the button and the table; "Zkusit znovu" after a failure asks for the year chosen); a 400 `donations/year-not-yet-completed` or `donations/receipt-before-first-year` there says that bulk receipts come after the first year the foundation issues them for. Four labelled counts (donors with gifts, receipts already issued, to be issued, erased donors skipped), the checkbox "Tisknu oboustranně" (on by default, because two donors' receipts on one sheet cost more than a blank page, UX review of 2.29; its label says it adds blank pages and to turn it off for one-sided printing; never disabled, so it keeps focus; changing it while drawing or after the PDF is ready draws it again without issuing), "Vystavit a připravit PDF k tisku" (`aria-disabled` while running, so focus stays on it; it says "Vystavujeme potvrzení…" and then "Připravujeme PDF…"; soft once a PDF is ready; after a failure it goes on as "Zkusit znovu" does) and "Nechte prosím stránku otevřenou…", which sends the year's donors in batches of 50 one after another (`issueYearlyReceipts`) with "Vystavujeme potvrzení N z M…", then draws every returned receipt into one PDF through `drawBundle` with "Kreslíme potvrzení…" (announced once; the counter "k z n" is the progress bar's visible detail and `aria-valuetext`). A failure shows a `role="alert"` line with "Zkusit znovu", which sends only the donors not yet answered, or after a drawing failure only draws again; the run can be cancelled, which moves focus to that line. While it runs `screen-wake-lock.ts` (`ScreenWakeLock` behind `SCREEN_WAKE_LOCKS`) keeps the screen on, takes the lock again when the tab comes back and lets it go on done, failure, cancel or leaving. When done: "PDF k tisku je připravené: obsahuje N potvrzení.", "Stáhnout PDF" ("Stáhnout znovu" from memory, offline too, for the life of the page) and "Otevřít PDF" through `PdfDelivery` (`potvrzeni-o-darech-<year>-hromadne.pdf`, titled "Roční potvrzení o darech za rok <year>"), "Tiskněte ve skutečné velikosti (100 %)…", the card "Výsledek" (in place of the counts from before the run) with the four outcomes and the replaced donors (with the sentence that the old receipts stop being valid, so the donors get the new ones) and the skipped donors, each linking to the donor card with a Czech reason per problem code, and the table "Dárci za rok <year>" (donor, gifts, total, receipt number or "zatím ne"; sorted with `Intl.Collator('cs')`; cards up to 640 px) updated to the new numbers. Tests: `admin-yearly-receipts.spec.ts`, `screen-wake-lock.spec.ts`, `receipt-pdf-bundle.spec.ts`, `e2e/admin-yearly-receipts.spec.ts` (a duplex run read back page by page and downloaded again with no request; a failed batch retried with 50, 10 and 10 donors), the axe, theme and device steps, and `e2e/bulk-receipts-load.spec.ts`, the nightly 300 receipts at 4× CPU (`BULK_RECEIPTS_LOAD=1`).
- `actions/` (`/sprava/historie`, "Historie změn"): `h1` focused on open, an intro. The card "Co správci udělali" lists the `admin_action` trail newest first as a table in a `.ui-table-frame` (cards at the card's `ledger` width; above it Podrobnosti asks for 40 % of the table and Zásah for 20 %, so the server's sentence has room while „Otevřít kartu dárce“ stays on one line, #1567): the Prague time, the administrator (name and e-mail; "správce bez účtu" when the account is gone, its id then inside the details; "přes aplikaci claude.ai" under them when the change came through a connected application, from the item's `client`), the action in Czech (`ACTION_NAMES` maps `PAIR_PAYMENT`, `UNPAIR_PAYMENT`, `GRANT_ADMINISTRATION`, `REVOKE_ADMINISTRATION`, `RESET_SECOND_FACTOR`, `REMOVE_PASSKEYS`, `REVERSE_SYMBOL_CLAIM`, `RESET_SYMBOL_CLAIM`, `DISCONNECT_SENDER_ACCOUNT`, `RECONNECT_SENDER_ACCOUNT`, `REMOVE_SENDER_ACCOUNT_LINK`, `ERASE_DONOR`, `CHANGE_REPORT_SETTINGS`, `SEND_MONTHLY_REPORT`, `CHANGE_RECEIPT_SIGNATURE`, `REMOVE_RECEIPT_SIGNATURE`, `ISSUE_RECEIPT` "Vystavení potvrzení o daru", `WITHDRAW_RECEIPT` "Zrušení potvrzení o daru" (1.26), `CREATE_NEWS`, `UPDATE_NEWS`, `PUBLISH_NEWS`, `UNPUBLISH_NEWS`, `DELETE_NEWS`, `CREATE_PROJECT`, `UPDATE_PROJECT`, `PUBLISH_PROJECT`, `UNPUBLISH_PROJECT`, `DELETE_PROJECT`, `CHANGE_PROJECT_ORDER`, `CHANGE_ABOUT_PHOTO`, `REMOVE_ABOUT_PHOTO`, `REQUEST_BACKUP`, `REQUEST_TEST_RESTORE`, `DOWNLOAD_BACKUP`, `RESTORE_BACKUP`, `BACKUP_RESTORED`, `BACKUP_RESTORE_FAILED`, `CHANGE_LOG_LEVEL`, `RESET_LOG_LEVEL`, `CHANGE_SETTINGS`, `SUBSCRIBE_PUSH` "Zapnutí upozornění přes push na zařízení", `CHANGE_PUSH_ALERTS` "Změna upozornění přes push na zařízení", `REMOVE_PUSH_SUBSCRIPTION` "Vypnutí upozornění přes push na zařízení" (2.27), `CHANGE_BACKUP_SCHEDULE`, `REGISTER_RECEIPT_SIGNING_KEY` "Registrace klíče k ověřovacím QR kódům", `ADOPT_RECEIPT_SIGNING_KEY` "Převzetí klíče k ověřovacím QR kódům ze zálohy"; an unknown code is shown as it is; a row by the server itself, `adminId` the nil UUID, names "server" as the administrator and no id in the details), the link "Otevřít kartu dárce" to `/sprava/darci/{donorId}` whenever the server names the donor the change concerned (`donorId`: donor actions, payment pairings, symbol answers, bank accounts), and in the details column the server's sentence `summary` (Jakub 2026-10-24: human Czech written by the backend, e.g. "Platba 500 Kč ze dne 10. 10. 2026 ručně přiřazena dárci Jana Nováková.") above the stored details inside a closed `<details>` "Technické podrobnosti" (with the ids; English for rows before 2026-10-24). The first page asks for 50; while `hasMore`, "Načíst starší" under the table asks for the next 50 after the last entry listed (`adminActions(lastListed)`: `before` its `performedAt` and `beforeId` its `id`, so entries recorded at one moment are not skipped) and appends them ("Načítáme starší…" while loading, a `role="alert"` "Starší záznamy se nepodařilo načíst. Zkuste to prosím znovu." on failure, the button stays). The empty trail says "Zatím žádný správce nic nezměnil."; loading, a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-actions.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-actions.spec.ts` (the first page and the appended older page, the empty trail) and the axe case for the page with its paging button.
- `donors/` (`/sprava/darci`, "Dárci"): `h1` focused on open, an intro saying what the card offers. The card "Hledat dárce" has the field "Jméno, e-mail nebo variabilní symbol" (`type="search"`, hint "Hledáme část jména nebo e-mailu a začátek variabilního symbolu; nabídneme nejvýše 20 dárců."); typing is debounced 300 ms, trimmed and deduplicated, each query goes to `donors(query)` and lands in the address as `?q=` (`replaceUrl`), so the page reopens with the same search and the browser's back button returns to the result list. States as `role="status"` or `role="alert"` lines: "Začněte psát a dárci se objeví tady." (empty query), "Hledáme dárce…", "Hledání se nepodařilo." with the button "Zkusit znovu" (#1800; asks the same query again: during the retry the failure stays and "Hledáme dárce…" does not show, and a new query cancels the retry), "Žádný dárce neodpovídá.", a visible "Zobrazujeme prvních N dárců. Upřesněte hledání…" when `hasMore`, and a visually hidden "Nalezení dárci: N" above the table (a table up to the single `ledger` fold, so at 1280 px it stays a table in the card: the name wraps, the account badges stack and the header "Variabilní symbol" may take two lines; an e-mail may break only right after its "@" and after each of its dots, a `<wbr>` between its segments (`emailSegments`), in the table, the folded cards and the rows alike, so "jana.novakova@example.cz" reads "jana." / "novakova@" / "example." / "cz" at most and the text and its copy stay whole; a segment takes `overflow-wrap: break-word`, so it breaks inside only when it alone is wider than its line; in the table the e-mail is a block of `max-content` capped by `--donor-email-max-inline-size` (16ch, R103c: the longest test address made the 870 px card at 1280 px 114 px too narrow, and 16ch leaves about 30 px to spare there), so a long segment without a dot wraps inside its cell instead of widening the table, and in the folded cards and the rows `overflow-wrap: anywhere` is the last rescue for a segment wider than the card; never a sideways scroll (WCAG 1.4.10); checked at 1280 and 1920 px in `e2e/tables-in-card.spec.ts` with a long dotted address and one without dots, #1565): the name as a link to `/sprava/darci/{id}` with "Osoba" or "Firma · IČO …" under it, e-mail, variable symbol, registration (the Prague date only) and the tags "Správce", "Dvoufaktorové ověření", "E-mail neověřen" (or "běžný účet"); an erased donor (`erasedAt`) shows "vymazán" instead of the placeholder e-mail and only the tag "Údaje vymazány". Every row leads with an `app-avatar` (40 px): the portrait of a person of the foundation linked to the account by e-mail (`people/portraits-by-email.ts`, the same match as `administrators/`, from `PeopleApi.load()`; none for an erased donor), else the initials; a failed load leaves the initials and shows nothing. While the query is empty, the page lists "Naposledy přispěli" (an `h3`) instead of the idle line, once the administration copy holds rows: `recent-donors.ts` takes the copy's `donations/donations` and `donors/accounts` rows (`AdministrationCopy.rows`), keeps `RECEIVED` gifts that are no account verification, takes each donor's latest (gift day, then `recordedAt`, then id), sorts newest first and keeps the first 10 whose account the copy holds (the two parts are of two moments); each row is the search row with the last gift (amount and day; in the table the column "Poslední dar" replaces "Registrace"). It is computed in the browser and asks the server nothing (local first); without a loaded copy the idle line stays. The first `/sprava` page that reads the copy. 401 and 403 as on the other admin pages. Specs in `admin-donors.spec.ts` and `recent-donors.spec.ts`.
- `donor-detail/` (`/sprava/darci/:id`, "Dárce"): the link "← Dárci", `h1` with the donor's name (focused on open; "Dárce" until it loads or when there is none). Once `donor(id)` answers, a summary strip (`section.donor-summary`, "Shrnutí dárce") shows the variable symbol with `app-copy-button` ("Kopírovat variabilní symbol"), the e-mail with "ověřen" / "neověřen", the received gifts ("2 dary za 800 Kč", once `donorDonations(id)` answers), "Dvoufaktorové ověření: zapnuté" for a donor who has it on (the words of "Přihlášení", no "2FA" badge, #1188) and the badge "Správce". Every label–value pair of the card (the summary, "Údaje", "Přihlášení") is a `dl.ui-data-list`, so from `nav` the labels share the portal's one style, each above its value, and the summary's values share one baseline (the copy button takes a negative `margin-block`); below `nav` the labels keep the phone's look of before, the summary's label beside its value in rows (#1181), on the first line of the value (`first baseline`); the summary card is a size container `donor-summary` (in `rem`, so it also switches at large text) with three layouts and no transition between them (#1356): wider than `narrow` the values share one column (a grid whose rows are subgrids), between `pair` and `narrow` each row keeps its own width, and at most `pair` wide each label stands above its value on one left axis; the DOM order of `dt` and `dd` stays in all three; only the e-mail value breaks anywhere, every other value only at `break-word`, „E-mail“ never breaks after its hyphen (`nowrap` on that label), the VS row wraps „Kopírovat“ whole under the number, and the copy button already holds the width of „Zkopírováno“, so the row does not wrap again when copying is confirmed; and the card's grids fall to one column at `min(100%, 12.5rem)`, so a large root text gets one column instead of overflowing. Under it the `nav` "Části karty dárce" lists in-page links "Údaje · Přihlášení · Bankovní účty · Dary · Potvrzení" (only to the cards shown; `href` `/sprava/darci/{id}#udaje` and so on): a click keeps the address, and focuses the card (`id` without diacritics, `tabindex="-1"`, no ring), which scrolls it into view below the top safe area thanks to the global `scroll-padding-top`. The cards, the first two from `donor(id)`, the rest from `donorDonations(id)`, each loading on its own: "Údaje" is a `dl` (type, e-mail (its "ověřen" / "neověřen" tag only where the summary is not shown above, a part opened on its own, so it is said once, #1188), address, variable symbol, language ("čeština" / "angličtina", the same words as the receipts' language, #1188), registration, role "správce" (a link to `/sprava/spravci`) / "dárce"). "Přihlášení" (once the account is loaded) is a `dl` (two-factor authentication "zapnuté" / "vypnuté", passkeys with their count or "žádné") with "Vypnout dvoufaktorové ověření" (danger outline, as every action that weakens the donor's security, #1184; from `nav` each hint of the card stands under its button, #1182) for a donor who has it on (inline confirmation "Ověřili jste, že žádá majitel účtu (například odpověděl z registrovaného e-mailu)? Dárce se pak přihlásí jen heslem a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí." with "Ano, vypnout" / "Ponechat"; success "Dvoufaktorové ověření u <jméno> je vypnuté a všechna přihlášení jsme ukončili. Dárce dostal e-mail; …") and "Odebrat přístupové klíče" (danger outline) with the hint "Pro dárce, který přišel o zařízení s klíčem." for a donor with keys (confirmation "Dárce se jimi pak nepřihlásí a všechna jeho přihlášení skončí. Odebrat?", `removePasskeys(id)` is a DELETE, success "Přístupové klíče u <jméno> jsme odebrali a všechna přihlášení ukončili. …"); after either the account is fetched again without the loading state, so the card and its result stay. A 404 leaves a single card "Účet dárce" saying "Takový dárce není. Vraťte se na seznam dárců a vyhledejte ho znovu." with the link "Zpět na hledání dárců" and hides everything else. Every inline confirmation answers "Ponechat"; focus moves to "Ponechat" when it opens, back to the opener (ids `donor-reset-open`, `donor-passkeys-open`, `account-<kind>-<linkId>`) after it or a refusal, and to the result line after success. The result lines sit in the card the action belongs to: one `app-live-notice` each in "Přihlášení", "Bankovní účty" and the erasure card (`cardNotice` names the card). The last card, "Výmaz osobních údajů (GDPR)", offers "Vymazat osobní údaje" (danger outline, id `donor-erase-open`) with the hint "Pro dárce, který požádal o výmaz podle GDPR."; for an administrator the hint "Správci nejde vymazat osobní údaje. Nejdřív mu odeberte roli správce na stránce Správci." replaces the button, and an erased donor reads "Osobní údaje tohoto dárce jsou vymazané.". The button opens an `<app-dialog>` "Vymazat osobní údaje: <jméno>" saying the step cannot be undone, listing what is deleted (name, address, e-mail and password replaced by placeholders, the bank accounts, the older-symbol answers) and what stays (donations and receipts for the accounting, the receipts unchanged on the server with the donor's name and address; the bank payments as the statement gives them, with the account number and payer name, and the entries in Historie změn; A6), that the donor gets an e-mail, and a checkbox "Dárce o výmaz požádal a ověřil(a) jsem, že jde o majitele účtu"; "Zrušit" has the focus when it opens, the danger button "Vymazat osobní údaje" stays disabled until the box is ticked and shows "Mažeme…" while `eraseDonor(id)` (a DELETE) runs; a refusal stays in the dialog as a `role="alert"` line, success closes it, puts "Osobní údaje dárce jsme vymazali a na jeho dosavadní e-mail poslali potvrzení. …" in the erasure card with the focus, and both halves reload. An erased donor (`erasedAt` on either half) gets a `role="note"` box under the `h1` "Osobní údaje tohoto dárce jsme vymazali <čas>. …", "vymazán" / "vymazána" instead of the placeholder e-mail and address, no actions, and "Bankovní účty" says "Bankovní účty jsme s osobními údaji dárce smazali; další platby se mu už nepřiřadí." "Bankovní účty" says that its column "Dary přiřazené podle účtu" (formerly "Dary přes účet", which Hana read as an error for a company giving with its symbol from a verified account, 30. 10. 2026) counts only payments without the symbol that were paired by the account, the gifts with the symbol being under Dary, explains when to switch pairing off and when to remove the account, and lists the links (account with the tag "sdílený", the status "Páruje platby" / "Čeká na platbu se symbolem" / "Párování vypnuté" with the verification, connection and disconnection dates, the donations paired by the account with their total and the last date; the dates and the total with the last date are `.ui-separated` lists, so a „·“ stays on the line of the item after it and is hidden where that item starts a line, as in `app-bank-account`, #1566) with "Vypnout párování" (live link), "Zapnout párování" (disconnected, not `pairingExcluded`) and "Odebrat účet" (danger outline; from `nav` the two stand side by side at one height, #1182; "Vypnout párování", "Zapnout párování" and "Odebrat účet" end with a visually hidden "účtu <číslo>, <banka>" / "<číslo>, <banka>", so each name says which account while the visible text starts it, #1200; confirmation "Smažeme N darů přiřazené přes tento účet i odpovědi o starších symbolech; platby zůstanou nespárované. Odebrat?"), replaced by "Nejde odebrat: k darům z účtu je vystavené potvrzení." when the link is the only one of that account and one of its donations has a receipt number; the outcomes are `role="status"` lines naming the account, a refusal (409 by a receipt) a `role="alert"` line with the backend's title and detail plus, for `donations/bank-account-has-receipt`, the next step (switch pairing off), and the three cards reload after each. "Dary" says "Celkem N darů za X Kč." (counting only received donations, plus "Vrácené dary (n) se nezapočítávají." when some were refunded) and lists every donation (received, amount, purpose, how it was paired with the account or symbol, the receipt number or "zatím žádné"); "Vydaná potvrzení" lists every receipt (number, "Dar z <datum>" or "Rok YYYY", issued at with CZ/EN, "platné" / "nahrazeno novějším" / "zrušeno, dary se změnily" (1.26, `withdrawalReason` `DONATIONS_CHANGED`), and at each valid receipt, also of an erased donor, "Stáhnout PDF" (1.19, A7; its name ends with the hidden "potvrzení <číslo>", so it holds the visible text in every state): `admin-receipt-downloads.ts` (`AdminReceiptDownloads`, provided by the card) draws it with `ReceiptPdf.draw` from `ReceiptsApi.administrationSource(id)`, so it issues nothing, and refuses a receipt the read no longer answers as `ACTIVE` (replaced since the card loaded: the card says so in its alert and lists the receipts again), shows the donor portal's button face (`AdminReceiptDownloads.state(id)`) and steps through `app-pdf-progress` at the row (from `nav` the status stands before the button on one axis in an 11rem reserve that also holds "Zrušit", so the row keeps its height and the button stays put, Tab reaches "Zrušit" first and the row's cells are centred on that axis; the PDF cell takes only what the button and the reserve need, and the table's inner cell padding is `--space-3` there (the outer edges keep `--space-5`), so the dates beside it keep their lines; on phones it stays under the button; the portal's pattern Q8, #1179; the step and saved texts of `receipt-pdf/receipt-progress.ts`, which both use under their `donor.dashboard.receipt.*` ids, so rewording them changes both pages; "Zrušit" returns focus to the row's button; a new download clears the finished lines of the others), saves it through `PdfDelivery` or, on an iPhone that shares files, turns the button into "Otevřít PDF" for the share sheet, and puts any failure as a `role="alert"` line at the row: the problem of the read, or for a drawing failure an administrator's sentence (tools not loaded: reload the page; drawing; signature or portrait not verified; unprintable character; theme refused); a lost session goes to the sign-in as the card's other calls do; under the button of a receipt whose `keyId` the published key list names `revoked` an amber note "QR na tomto potvrzení podepsal zneplatněný klíč. Stránka /overit ho ukáže jako zneplatněné." (Jakub 2026-11-05, Phase 1 handover card 6: the PDF is still offered, warned), the card reading `GET /api/donations/receipt-keys` without the token (`ReceiptKeysApi`) once it lists a valid receipt, and only once per visit, through `trustedKeys`, as `/overit` does, so it warns exactly when `/overit` says "Neověřeno: fond tento klíč zneplatnil"; the note describes the button (`aria-describedby`); a list that cannot be read, and a key the list does not name, warn of nothing; empty: "Dárci zatím nikdo žádné potvrzení nevystavil.") and, unless the donor is erased, offers "Vystavit potvrzení" (1.7): a select "Potvrzení k vystavení" (its hint says the same data return the receipt that already holds) with "Roční potvrzení za rok YYYY" for each year from `receiptsAvailableFrom` to `yearlyReceiptsAvailableThrough` (both from the detail, so no client clock) in which a received donation lies, newest first, then "Dar z <datum>, X Kč" for each received donation with `receiptAvailable`; without any it says "Dárce nemá žádný dar, za který by šlo potvrzení vystavit.".
