the same unsaved-changes guard) and add the year and the category: the list has the columns Rok and Kategorie (Děti, Dospělí, Zvířata, Ostatní from `projectCategoryLabel` in `features/content/project-category.ts`, ids `content.project.category.*`) after the title and its intro points to the card Řazení na webu under the list (`project-order-card`, its own `GET`/`PUT /api/admin/content/project-order`; Jakub 2026-10-30: every choice says how it orders): three radio buttons from `PROJECT_ORDERS`, each named by its label and described (`aria-describedby`) by a sentence from `project-order.ts` (`projectOrderLabel`, `projectOrderDescription`, ids `admin.projects.order.*`), the button Uložit řazení (Ukládáme… while it runs), the saved line or the refusal in a `LiveNotice`, its own loading line and retry; a saved order makes the page ask for the list again without its loading line, so the table shows the new order; the choices use the global block `.ui-choice` (`styles/ui/_form.scss`, `__title`, `__detail`, `.is-selected`), which the pairing dialog's donor results use too; the form has the choice Kategorie after Rok (a `select` over `PROJECT_CATEGORIES`, Děti for a new project, the hint says whom the project helped and that the order is chosen in the list); the form has the number field Rok after the title (required, 2005–2100, this year for a new project; messages "Vyplňte prosím rok příběhu.", "Rok nemůže být před založením fondu, tedy před 2005.", "Zadejte prosím rok nejvýše 2100."), the tag hint names a project area ("Kompenzační pomůcky"), the perex hint the project card, the address hint `nfnn.cz/projekty/<slug>`, and the date hint says the projects on the same place of the order come from the one published last; the preview shows the public project card (with the year) and project page under "Karta na úvodní stránce a v Pomáháme" and "Stránka příběhu". Texts under `admin.projects.*` and `admin.projectEditor.*`. Specs `admin-projects.spec.ts`, `admin-project-editor.spec.ts`, `features/content/projects-api.spec.ts` (fixtures in `features/content/testing/project-fixtures.ts`); Playwright `e2e/admin-projects.spec.ts` (the same five journeys with the year in the table and the requests, and choosing the order by category, with the description of the choice and the list asked again; `project-order-card.spec.ts` for the card) and the axe case with a year before 2005 among the errors.
- `settings/` (`/sprava/nastaveni`, "Nastavení"): `h1` focused on open, an intro (what the page holds, the technical values at the bottom, that secrets are stored encrypted and never shown, that saving restarts the site). The page is ordered by how often a part is needed (Jakub 2026-10-24), not by the backend's groups: the cards "Potvrzení o daru" (`RECEIPT_FIRST_YEAR`), "Kdo potvrzení podepisuje" (below), "Kontaktní formulář" (`CONTACT_RECIPIENT`), "Odesílatel e-mailů" (`MAIL_FROM`) and "Přihlášení" (`SESSION_REMEMBER_DAYS`, plan v3 2.10, `nastaveni-prihlaseni`), each with a one-line intro, then the card "Technické nastavení (pro správce serveru)" with the warning callout "Chybná hodnota tady může zastavit odesílání e-mailů nebo stahování plateb z banky. Než změny e-mailů uložíte, pošlete si zkušební e-mail." and a `<details id="technicke-nastaveni">` (summary "Adresa webu, server pro odesílání e-mailů a napojení na banku", folded unless a secret is unreadable, a refused value is technical or the address points into it) holding the parts "Adresa webu" (`PUBLIC_URL`), "Server pro odesílání e-mailů" (`MAIL_HOST` … `MAIL_STARTTLS`) and "Banka" (`FIO_*`, `PAIRING_EXCLUDED_ACCOUNTS`) as `h3`. The sections are `EVERYDAY` and `TECHNICAL` in `admin-settings.ts`; every field keeps the labels of `setting-labels.ts`, the fields render from one `ng-template`, and the changes are sent in the backend's order. Headings take `tabindex="-1"` with Czech ids `nastaveni-<part>` (`nastaveni-potvrzeni`, `-kontaktni-formular`, `-odesilatel`, `-technicke`, `-adresa-webu`, `-odesilani-emailu`, `-banka`); `FRAGMENT_ALIASES` keeps the older addresses, so `/sprava/nastaveni#settings-MAIL` (the link from the report page) opens the technical settings and focuses "Server pro odesílání e-mailů". Fields: `MAIL_AUTH` and `MAIL_STARTTLS` as checkboxes, `FIO_HISTORY_FROM` as a date, a secret as a `role="group"` labelled by its state line "<label>: Nastaveno / Nenastaveno" (amber "Uložená hodnota nejde přečíst, zadejte ji prosím znovu." when `unreadable`) with the field named after the secret ("Nové heslo nebo klíč SMTP", "Nový token Fio API", else "Nová hodnota: {label}") in `<app-password-reveal>` and, when a row is stored, the checkbox "Odebrat uloženou hodnotu", which once ticked shows what the removal stops (`REMOVAL_EFFECTS`: no e-mail at all without the SMTP password, no bank import without the Fio token); every other setting a text field with its value. The mail server part ends with the hint "Pošle e-mail na vaši adresu {own e-mail} s údaji z formuláře, i když ještě nejsou uložené, …" and "Poslat zkušební e-mail na moji adresu" ("Posíláme zkušební e-mail…"): the form's server, port, user, sign-in, STARTTLS and sender (a blank password means the stored one, which the backend uses only for the running server), the result from `testEmailNotice` beside it. The card "Kdo potvrzení podepisuje" (`settings/receipt-signatory/`, `<app-receipt-signatory>`, slice 1.16, inside the form after "Potvrzení o daru" but with its own requests and no control of the form) reads `receiptSignatory()` and, when the signatory has a portrait or a signature, the people (`PeopleApi.load()`) for both images: "Načítáme, kdo potvrzení podepisuje…", a failed load with "Zkusit znovu", then the preview of the 3B signature block as one piece of paper in both appearances (#1826): the token `--color-paper-muted` without a border, its caption, lines and rule in the receipt's ink (`--receipt-color-text`, `--receipt-color-muted`, `--receipt-color-rule`), across the whole text column of the card like the warning under it (#1881), as a `figure` named by its caption "Náhled podpisu na potvrzení" (the round portrait `?w=240`, or no portrait at all, as the receipt drops it, "Za Nová naděje - nadační fond:", the person's one signature (`?w=480`, `alt` "Podpis na potvrzení", at most 220 px wide, blended into the paper by the token `--blend-ink-on-paper` (`multiply`), so the white of the uploaded image does not show as a box, as the receipt prints it without its white), over a rule spanning the text column in the token `--receipt-color-rule` (`#b8bec6` as in 3B, about 1.7:1 on the paper; decorative, not text), the name and the receipt role; `forced-color-adjust: none`, so a dark forced-colours mode keeps the paper) and a warning callout naming what is missing (portrait, signature or both) or, with `portraitPrintable` false, that the portrait cannot be prepared for print and receipts go out without it ("Portrét této osoby nejde připravit pro tisk…"), together with the missing signature in one sentence when that is missing too; with nobody chosen a static danger callout (no live role, since it shows on every visit) "Potvrzení o daru teď nikdo nepodepisuje, a tak je web dárcům nevystaví. …"; always the link "Otevřít lidi fondu" to `/sprava/lide-fondu`, where the signatory, the receipt role and the signature are changed. Texts under `admin.signatory.*`, spec `receipt-signatory.spec.ts`. After it the card "Klíč k ověřovacím QR kódům" (`settings/receipt-signing-key/`, `<app-receipt-signing-key>`, its `h2` `#klic-k-overovacim-qr` with `tabindex="-1"`, the fragment the overview's red row opens): an intro in the present tense since receipts are signed (1.6: "Server podepisuje ověřovací QR kódy na potvrzeních o daru svým klíčem.", worded so it still reads true while that key is withdrawn; the site publishes its public part so that receipts can be verified, if it may have leaked, withdraw it and the server makes a new one), "Načítáme klíč…", then a `dl` of "Klíč serveru" (the key id), "Zaregistrovaný" (`formatBankTime`) and "Stav" ("Zneplatněný", "Zveřejněný" or "Nezveřejněný"), the line "Zveřejněné jsou i dřívější klíče: <id> (zaregistrovaný <time>)." for the published keys of earlier servers (", zneplatněný" inside the brackets for a withdrawn one) (when the server's own row is missing, the `dl` shows its id and "Nezveřejněný" only), and in one `role="alert"` region a red callout per warning: the overview's sentence for every key whose seal fails, "Klíč serveru <id> je zneplatněný a server zatím nemá nový, …" while the server's own key is withdrawn and no restart is under way, and one sentence per audit mismatch (`settings/receipt-signing-key/key-warnings.ts`: "Klíč <id> byl ve správě zneplatněný, ale jeho záznam v databázi je znovu aktivní …" for `reverted`, "Klíč <id> je v historii správy, ale jeho záznam v databázi chybí …" for `missing`), and one per unreadable entry (`unreadableEntrySentence`: "Záznam „Zneplatnění klíče“ z <Prague time> v Historii změn neodpovídá žádnému známému znění, …", with "Registrace klíče" or "Převzetí klíče ze zálohy" for the other two actions; an answer kept from before the field came reads it as empty, so the field is optional in `ReceiptSigningKeysResponse`). Below, a small danger-outline button per key that can be withdrawn (published and `active`, or `revoked` only by the trail while its row is `reverted`): "Zneplatnit klíč serveru…" first, then "Zneplatnit klíč <id>…" for earlier keys, "Zneplatnit klíč <id> znovu…" for a reverted one. It asks `AuthStore.hasPasskeys()` first (the button stays focusable, `aria-disabled` meanwhile, so the dialog returns focus to it) and opens `<app-key-withdrawal-dialog>` (`key-withdrawal-dialog.ts`, on `<app-dialog>`, labelled "Zneplatnit klíč <id>?" with the id in `ui-mono`): a lead (withdraw only when it may have leaked; it cannot be undone), points (the site keeps publishing it as withdrawn, so its QR codes are not to be trusted, earlier receipts included and not re-signed; for the server's key the site restarts at once and makes a new key, about a minute without receipts; for an earlier key nothing restarts; what to answer donors who write about a receipt under that key (the verification page no longer verifies it and the web cannot issue a new one yet, so the administrator confirms its data by its number; slice 1.21); it is recorded in the history and e-mailed to every administrator), then the fresh second factor exactly as the backup key card asks it (the code field with `autofocus`, "Nebo bez kódu: Potvrdit přístupovým klíčem" only when the administrator has a passkey, or the passkey first with "Zadat kód z aplikace"), the refusal in a `role="alert"` line, "Zneplatnit klíč" (`ui-button--danger`, "Zneplatňujeme klíč…" while sending) and "Zrušit" (`autofocus` when no code field is shown). The answer replaces the card's keys; the card focuses its `h2` and an `<app-live-notice>` says "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný." or, with `restarting`, "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný. Web se restartuje a vytvoří si nový klíč, obvykle do minuty." while `watchKeyRenewal` (`settings/receipt-signing-key/key-renewal.ts`) asks for the keys every 3 s (`RESTART_POLL_MS`), at most 100 times (`KEY_RENEWAL_MAX_POLLS`, five minutes, room for a slow mail run before the backend restarts, review 1.13 r2). It decides only once the server went down and answered again, or the answer names another `currentKeyId`; an answer that still names the withdrawn key from a server that never went down (the notices are still being sent) claims nothing and keeps waiting. Only an unreachable web counts as down (status 0, 502, 503 or 504, what the frontend nginx answers while the backend restarts, review 1.13 r3); any other failure (a 500, a 429, a 401 the refresh could not mend, a 403) proves no restart and does not end the wait, so a single blip before the restart can no longer turn the withdrawn key that follows into the "no new key" alert. Then "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný a web po restartu podepisuje novým klíčem." when the new key is published (`currentKeyPublished`) and not withdrawn; the alert "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný a web po restartu má nový klíč <new-id>, který ale zatím není zveřejněný." when it is not published; and when the web came back with the same key the alert "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný, ale web po restartu nový klíč nemá, takže se potvrzení o daru nestáhnou; …" (the server signs nothing while its key is withdrawn), which replaces the card's own warning about it. When the polls run out, the restarting line goes and only an alert stays, with "Zkusit znovu", which watches again: "Klíč je zneplatněný, ale web se po restartu zatím nespustil. …" when the web went down and has not answered since, and "Klíč <id> je zneplatněný, ale web se zatím nerestartoval a nový klíč nemá, takže se potvrzení o daru nestáhnou. Počkejte pár minut a zkuste to znovu; když se nic nezmění, obraťte se prosím na technika serveru." (`admin.signingKey.notRestarted`) when it never went down in the whole wait (the request for a new seed could not be written, or the notices never finished), so the card never claims a restart that did not happen. a failed load says "Klíč se nepodařilo načíst." with "Zkusit znovu". Texts under `admin.signingKey.*`, spec `receipt-signing-key.spec.ts`. Without a settings key a `role="alert"` box says in plain words that secrets cannot be saved and the server administrator helps with the key (the missing `secrets/settings-key` under "Technické podrobnosti"), and their fields are disabled; with a key, unreadable secrets are named in a `role="alert"` box at the top ("Tyto tajné údaje jsou uložené jiným klíčem, než má tento server, třeba po obnově ze zálohy na novém serveru: {labels}. Dokud je znovu nezadáte a neuložíte, nebudou fungovat."), which is how a restore of an older backup on a new server asks for the SMTP password and the Fio token again (Jakub 2026-10-23; since 2026-10-29 a backup carries them under the backup key). From `nav` up, while the form holds changes, the save row becomes a `.ui-action-bar` (`appActionBar` publishes its height, so the page's `scroll-padding-bottom` keeps a focused field clear of it) sticking to the bottom of the content column; it enters with `motion.appear` and leaves with `motion.leave` once the changes are undone only while it is stuck to the bottom of the window (one `IntersectionObserver` on the marker `.save-row-end` after the row says whether the row's own place lies below the window; disconnected with the page), while in its own place the class changes without motion, and as a sticky box it keeps its room in the flow, so nothing jumps and at the end of the page it sits below the last field (#1189). "Uložit nastavení" computes the changes (trimmed; a secret only when typed or removed); nothing changed says "Nic jste nezměnili." right under the button (`.ui-form-status`); otherwise the `role="group"` "Uložit nastavení?" beside the button lists "Mění se: {labels}." (a removed secret as "{label} (odebrat)"), a danger callout per removed secret with its effect, a warning when the mail server settings or the sender changed without a sent test e-mail for exactly those values ("Zkušební e-mail jste s novými údaji neposlali. …"), and asks "Uložením se web restartuje a asi minutu nebude odpovídat. Uložit?" with "Uložit a restartovat" ("Ukládáme…") and "Zrušit" (focused; cancelling returns the focus to "Uložit nastavení"). A 400 lists the refused values by label under the form; success shows the restart line with a spinner (`motion.spin`) while `watchRestart()` runs, which after 5 s (`RESTART_CALM_AFTER_MS`) gives way to a still clock icon beside the same words (D11 of Motion, #1197), reloads the settings and says "Nastavení je uložené a web po restartu běží s ním.", or after 100 polls an alert saying the site has not come back yet, to wait and then ask the server administrator (the container log under "Technické podrobnosti"), with "Zkusit znovu". Loading, a failed load, 401 and 403 behave as on the other admin pages. Texts under `admin.settings.*`. Specs `admin-settings.spec.ts`; Playwright `e2e/admin-settings.spec.ts` (a value and a secret changed, only the changes sent, the restart waited for; the order of the parts and a test e-mail with a new sender before saving; the report page's link opening the mail server part; the signatory: the preview of the signature block, the warning when nobody signs, the block in a dark forced-colours mode) and the axe case for the page with its confirmation and a refused test e-mail.

- `changed-meanwhile/` (plan v3 2.18, contract "Stale writes"): `ChangedMeanwhileDialog`, an `<app-dialog>` sheet "Mezitím se to změnilo" shown when a versioned write answers 409 `…/changed-meanwhile` (`isChangedMeanwhile`, `versionInRefusal`, `savedAtInRefusal` and `ifMatch` in `core/version/row-version.ts`; every versioned call of `admin-api.ts`, `news-api.ts` and `projects-api.ts` takes the `version` of the record it was read with and sends it as `If-Match`). It has three forms. On a page of actions (payments, older-symbol answers, the donor card's bank accounts), and in an editor whose refused action had no unsaved edits, it says the change was not made and offers "Načíst aktuální stav", which reads the page again (closing it does the same); its lead names what changed (`subject`: a payment, a donor's account link, an older-symbol answer, an article or project). In a form whose save was refused (the monthly report settings, the news and project editors; `keepsDraft` with `canOverwrite`) it says the edits stayed in the form and offers "Uložit svou verzi místo novější", which sends the form again with the version from the refusal (a refused publish keeps its intent and publishes after saving), and "Zahodit své úpravy a načíst novější verzi", which reads the record again; no button has the focus at opening, so Enter only closes the sheet, and closing it keeps the form as typed. When a publish, withdrawal or deletion is refused while the editor holds unsaved edits (`keepsDraft` without `canOverwrite`), it says the change was not made and the edits stay, tells how to go on (save them first or discard them), and offers "Nechat úpravy ve formuláři" and the discard button; after a refused deletion focus returns to "Smazat aktualitu" or "Smazat příběh". Where the refusal's record carries it, the sheet says when the newer version was saved. Its buttons wrap and stack below the phone breakpoint (the device steps open it in every pose, `admin-changed-meanwhile`), and its lead is tied to the dialog by `describedBy` (an input of `<app-dialog>` bound to `aria-describedby`). After a reload each page announces it in its status line once the data has arrived and moves focus there (the editors and Přehledy to their heading); the report form is pristine again (`DirtyForms`), and a repeated refusal while saving over is announced through `DialogAnnouncer`. Since 2.20c the settings page (`/sprava/nastaveni`, where "Uložit svou verzi" opens the restart confirmation again instead of saving at once, since a save restarts the site) and Lidé fondu (the founders' note and the line under the board, each with its own version; the order's answer moves a text's version forward only while its form is untouched) use the same sheet, and the donor's profile card shows the refusal inline instead (`.profile-changed`): the form takes at once every field the donor did not touch from the refusal's `current` and keeps the touched ones, the callout names a field both sides changed, and it offers "Uložit své úpravy" (the merged form with the refusal's version; the form's own save button is hidden meanwhile) or "Zahodit své úpravy a načíst uložené údaje"; "Zrušit" after a refusal reloads the stored profile when online. An edit keeps the id of the donor it started for and closes unsaved when the tab switches account, so one account's typed data never go to another; an edit started from the device copy, whose profile has no version and a placeholder language, first reads the stored profile and merges onto it the same way, so it never sends an empty `If-Match`. A version is not stable across releases, so `core/version/held-record-retry.ts` (`heldRecordRetryInterceptor`, before `authInterceptor`) resends a refused write once with the refusal's version when the request carries the record the page held (`ifMatch(version, held)`, an `HttpContext` value) and the two records are equal key for key (key order ignored; a key on one side only counts as a difference unless it is `version`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt` or named by the request as allowed to differ, as `sentReports` of the report settings; at least one compared key besides `id`). The held records follow the backend's `current` shapes (`storedSymbolClaimOf`, `storedPageTextOf`, `storedProfileOf`, `storedNewsArticleOf`, `storedProjectOf`): `core/version/stored-records.json` lists their keys, `scripts/stored-records.test.mjs` (in `npm run lint`) checks the list against the Java records and `held-records.spec.ts` each caller's held keys against the list, so a new backend column fails first: the record only looks changed because its hash changed with a deploy. A page passes a held record only while the version it sends is the one it last received with that record, never for a draft started from an older version or for "Uložit svou verzi", so the retry never bypasses a real conflict; the payments, the account links and the settings pass none, because what they hold is not the refusal's row shape.

## Theme, fonts, assets

- The theme is a folder chosen at build time: `src/themes/nova-nadeje/_theme.scss` declares the `@font-face` rules and every token on `:root` (inventory in "Frontend conventions in detail", UI, below), `styles.scss` loads it as `@use 'theme'` through `stylePreprocessorOptions.includePaths` (`src/styles` and the theme folder), and the configuration `control-theme` swaps in `src/themes/control/` (a deliberately different look with system fonts, built and checked by `e2e/theme.spec.ts` in CI only; `scripts/check-themes.mjs`, part of `npm run lint:styles`, keeps the token sets equal). The files of a theme's look live in its `assets/` folder and are served at the site root (each build configuration maps its theme's `assets/` with `output: "/"`, plan v3 2.22): `favicon.svg`, `favicon.ico`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `app-icon.svg` and `images/logo.svg` and `images/logo-on-dark.svg`, at the addresses they had in `public/`; the control theme carries copies of the same files for now. `check-themes.mjs` fails when a theme lacks a file of `nova-nadeje` or has one more, when a theme file is also in `public/` (the build would serve one of them unseen), and when a build serves no `assets/` of its own theme or another theme's. The receipt PDF still draws the logo from `receipt-logo.ts` (`receipt-logo.spec.ts` compares it with the `nova-nadeje` logo), so a theme with another logo also changes that file. The theme also carries the tokens of the app layout (2.22, used from 2.23): the floating tab bar of decision 2.1 in the one chrome material of the design plan (slice 1, "Glass and no glow", Jakub 2026-11-06, plan sections 2.2 and 2.6; until then navy glass at 66 % in both appearances): in the light appearance white glass, `--color-tab-bar` white at 94 % with the rim `--color-tab-bar-rim` `#0f172a` at 10 %, labels `--color-on-tab-bar` in the text colour, the active pill `--color-tab-pill` the accent `#1d5fc9` at 10 % with `--color-tab-active` and `--color-tab-focus-ring` that accent, `--shadow-tab-bar` `0 4px 12px` at 12 % (its reach, offset plus blur, at most `--tab-bar-gap-browser`, so Safari does not cut it off at the screen's edge, #1251); in the dark appearance, and in every appearance over a photo or a dark ground (the `-on-dark` twins of each token, which the block `.ui-glass--on-dark` puts in place of the light ones), dark glass `#1b2130` at 90 % with a white rim at 12 %, labels `#e6e8ee`, a white pill at 8 % with `#93c5fd`, a white focus ring and `--shadow-tab-bar-on-dark` `0 4px 12px` black at 40 %; `--blur-bar` 24 px with `--bar-saturate` 180 % (the plan's 72 % white and 82 % dark glass and its 14 % pill left the active label under 4.5:1 over a black page under the light glass and over a white page under the dark one, so the bar is denser and the dark active label is the accent on dark grounds, `--color-accent-on-inverse`); `--tab-icon-size` in `rem` (1.5rem since the filled glyphs of #565), so the icons follow the text size, `--tab-bar-height` 62 px as a minimum that grows with the text, `--tab-bar-gap` 20 px to the sides and also the installed app's bottom offset (`max(var(--tab-bar-gap), env(safe-area-inset-bottom) - 13px)`, iOS expert 2026-10-31, so 2.23 adds no token for it), `--tab-bar-gap-browser` 16 px above Safari's row, `--tab-pill-inset` 4 px (5 px until the pill was drawn without a rim, Jakub 2026-11-06, then 2 px, and 4 px since #1252 for the HIG's 4 to 6 px, the bar keeping its height), the bar's inner padding at the sides `--tab-bar-padding-inline` (at least the pill inset, `0.25rem` as the text grows) and the gap between the tabs `--tab-bar-item-gap` (`0.25rem`), both in `rem` so they grow with the text (#1335), and the labels' size formula subtracts them, `--color-tab-pill-rim` transparent (the control theme keeps its bar's rim on the pill), `--shadow-tab-bar`; solid under `prefers-reduced-transparency`: the light bar `#ffffff`, the dark one and the one over photos `#1a2232` (the dark elevated surface), with no blur or saturation, each pill a tint of its label colour as in the glass, the control theme its glass over white `#5c6662` with a pill darkened to `#0b110e`, because its plain bar colour gave 2.99:1; even the decided glass leaves its pill under 3:1 against the bar, so 2.23 marks the active tab by a shape or weight too, not by its colour alone), the sheet (`--sheet-max-width`, `--radius-sheet`, `--shadow-sheet`, `--duration-sheet`, `--color-sheet-grabber`, `--sheet-grabber-width`/`-height`), the list rows (`--list-row-min-height`, `--list-group-title-top`) and the dialog values that were raw in `dialog.scss` (`--dialog-max-width`, `-wide`, `--dialog-close-inset`, `--dialog-close-size`, `--blur-backdrop`). There are no top bar tokens: Jakub chose variant E on 2026-11-05 07:43 (no fixed bar at the top; the logo only in the corner of the landing photo and the sign-in button or avatar at the top right, both scrolling away), and what the app shell still needs there (the back button one level deeper) waits for its mockups in 2.23. `check-themes.mjs` requires the tab bar colours as literal `#rrggbb` (the glass colours also as `color-mix(in srgb, #rrggbb N%, transparent)`) and fails when, over a white or a black page, `--color-on-tab-bar` on the bar or `--color-tab-active` on the pill over the bar falls below 4.5:1, or the focus ring on the bar or the pill below 3:1, for the light glass and for its `-on-dark` twin alike, in the top-level `:root` and in every media query that sets one of them. The same glass (`.ui-glass` of `src/styles/ui/_glass.scss`: rim, background, `backdrop-filter: blur(var(--blur-bar)) saturate(var(--bar-saturate))`, shadow and label colour, with `.ui-glass--on-dark` mapping the `-on-dark` twins over the tokens) carries the tab bar, the header's back and account circles (the account button's initials in `--color-tab-active`) and its "Přihlásit se" pill (ios-designer's review of #544), those three with `--on-dark` from the route's `head` (`photo` and `dark`, the same choice that picks the white logo), the tab bar, which floats over the page's body, from the route's `darkThroughout` (`RouteChromeData`, inherited by child routes): set on the pages that are a photo or dark all the way down (the landing page, `/overit` and the panel pages `/obnova-hesla` and `/potvrzeni-emailu`; the 404 has a light head since #609), so a story or project under its dark head keeps the light glass over its white body (ios-designer's review of #534); the sheet's round close button of #483 takes it through its tokens (`--color-sheet-close` the bar's glass, `--shadow-sheet-close` its rim and shadow), and a share circle, which the site does not have yet, takes the block when it comes (plan slice 9). Icons follow the text in `rem`: `--tab-icon-size`, `--list-chevron-size` 1rem for the chevrons of `.ui-list`, `--sheet-chevron-size` 1.25rem in the sheet's rows, and the back and the sheet's close icons (`--back-icon-size`, `--sheet-close-icon-size`) up to a share of their fixed circle, so they never outgrow it at a large text size. No coloured glow anywhere (plan 2.6): `--shadow-button`, `-button-sm`, `-cta`, `-cta-nav` and `-tab` are `none`, `--shadow-cta-hero` under the call to action on the photo is neutral black at 25 %, and `--shadow-qr` a neutral lift instead of the accent glow. Fonts: Playfair Display and Inter as self-hosted variable WOFF2 (latin and latin-ext from Fontsource, OFL licences beside them in `src/themes/nova-nadeje/fonts/`); the build hashes them into `/media/`, which nginx caches as immutable. Type roles (design plan slice 2, Jakub 2026-11-06): `--font-sans` puts the system face first (`-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter'`, so SF Pro on Apple devices and Inter elsewhere; deliberately not `system-ui`, which would give Segoe or Roboto), `--font-display` (the serif) carries the hero, each page's `h1`, public article and section titles and the O nás numbers, `--font-title` (the sans, set semibold) the card, dialog and panel titles and the titles of the cards and dialogs of `/darce` and `/sprava`, `--font-figures` every amount, account number, IBAN, variable symbol and receipt number through the block `.ui-figures` (`_figures`: `--numeric-figures` plus `--figure-features` `'tnum' 1`, never wrapping inside a number; `.ui-figures--groups` wraps only between its child spans: the IBAN's groups of four in O nás, its halves in Přispět, a list of symbols), and `--font-mono` (`ui-monospace, 'SF Mono', Menlo`) only logs, key ids, codes and file names. Labels: `.ui-eyebrow` only on public marketing sections; labels in cards and sheets sentence case at `--text-md` in `--color-text-soft`; table headers sentence case at `--text-sm` semibold in `--color-text-secondary`; the side panel's group titles sentence case; the inset-list section headers of `/sprava/vice` small uppercase (`--text-xs`, `--tracking-slight`). Long-form text (`shared/ui/markdown`) is `--color-text-secondary`. Type sizes, weights, line heights, letter spacing, the spacing scale (`--space-unit` × n) and transition durations are tokens there too; `src/styles/_breakpoints.scss` holds the named widths and the `bp.down`/`bp.up` mixins (on the include path, so components write `@use 'breakpoints' as bp;`), and the named heights with `bp.height-down` (`short` 500 px, a phone on its side, and `hero-lead` 680 px, an iPhone SE upright, where the landing hero gives way); `core/layout/breakpoints.ts` (`BREAKPOINTS`, `downQuery(name)`, `HEIGHTS`, `heightDownQuery(name)`) holds the same widths and heights for code, kept equal by `scripts/breakpoints-parity.test.mjs` (run by `npm run lint`), and `core/layout/soft-keyboard.ts` (`SoftKeyboard`, root, started by the first component that asks: the tab bar, a dialog or a form field) counts the on-screen keyboard open while a text field has the focus and `visualViewport` is at least 150 px shorter than the window (`isOpen()`, `visibleArea()`), and then marks the root `keyboard-open` with `--keyboard-covered-height` (iOS Safari ignores `interactive-widget`, measured on iOS 27.2); `core/layout/compact-layout.ts` (`CompactLayout.isCompact()`, root) follows `matchMedia(downQuery('nav'))`, the width of `bp.down(nav)`, false where the browser has no `matchMedia`, and keeps its answer while the window is 1 px wide or less (Chromium reports a 1 × 1 viewport for a moment while it captures a page beyond its viewport, and under load the screen spec's full-page shots caught the phone layout it set for that frame), so a page renders the compact list or the table with `@if`, never both hidden by CSS (2.22; used from 2.25). `core/layout/short-viewport.ts` (`ShortViewport.isShort()`, root) follows `matchMedia(heightDownQuery('short'))` the same way, for what a phone on its side cannot fit (the landing hero's actions). `src/styles/ui/` holds the shared building blocks (global `ui-` classes for page head, card, notice and load state, badge, button, table, form errors and segmented choices, the title, lead and points of a confirmation dialog (`_dialog`: the restore, unsaved changes and key withdrawal dialogs), the list of people with a round portrait, name, a line under it, badges and actions (`_person-list`: the people of the foundation and the administrators), the list rows of the app layout (`_list`, 2.22, unused until 2.25, which also settles the rest of the block: a group is `section > h3.ui-list__group-title + ul.ui-list` (a title inside an `li` does not stick), a second action sits beside its row as `__item` with `__action`, rows take grid areas so a row without a lead or value keeps no empty gap, the value moves under the title at large text, and rows get `scroll-margin-top` so focus is not hidden under a sticky title), the standalone panel pages, and the public site's section width, eyebrow, page intro band and calls to action, list in "Frontend conventions in detail", UI, below), used by every page of the administration, the standalone pages (password reset, e-mail verification, connected application, first setup), the sign-in dialog, the cards of `/darce` and the public pages (home sections, news and project detail, privacy policy, not found, footer); their SCSS is linted with the components. `npm run lint:styles` fails on a raw colour, `font-family`, font size, weight, line height, letter spacing, `border-radius`, shadow, `px` spacing (except 1 px), transition duration or `width` media query outside the theme.
- Light and dark appearance (#347; decided 2026-11-06 under Jakub's instruction of that day that the app behave like a native iOS app): the site follows the device's Light / Dark setting. `index.html` declares `<meta name="color-scheme" content="light dark">`, so the browser paints its canvas dark before the styles load, and each theme ends with a block `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root { color-scheme: dark; … } }` that only overrides colour tokens (and `--filter-ink`, `--blend-ink`), so the light look is the top-level `:root` unchanged and native controls (date fields, selects, checkboxes, scroll bars) follow through `color-scheme`. `nova-nadeje` dark, as an iOS designer and an accessibility expert agreed: the page and the cells `#121826` (a navy-tinted near-black, not pure black: less halation for astigmatic readers and the brand's navy stays visible; `#10141f` until the design slice s3), `--color-surface-alt` `#080b12` for quiet grounds inside them, the navy bands `#13213f` and `--color-surface-inverse-strong` `#05070c` (the former footer's), text `#e6e8ee` (not white, less glare) to soft `#9aa3b2`, links and focus ring `#6ea8ff`, softened danger, warning and success colours, a darker backdrop, and its own block for `prefers-contrast: more` in the dark (placed after the light one, which would otherwise win). A colour used both as text and as a fill splits in two: `--color-accent` (text, links, borders) becomes the light blue, while `--color-accent-fill` (the filled buttons, the selected amount, the stats band, badges, and the glow shadows) stays `#2563eb` with white text (5.17:1), since no single blue reads both on the dark page and under white text; `--color-danger-fill` likewise for danger buttons, and `--color-surface-quote` for the quote bubble that used `--color-text` as its ground. Each new token equals the old value in the light look. What must stay light stays light: the QR tokens (dark modules on white, so banks scan them) and the logo tokens that paint its star, every `--receipt-*` token (the PDF never depends on the device), the manifest colours (checked against the top-level `:root` only) and the receipt-signature previews of `/sprava` on `--color-paper` and `--color-paper-muted`, which show the ink as it prints. Photos are not dimmed; the header and the sign-in dialog show `logo-on-dark.svg` whenever `Appearance.dark()` holds (the header only where its page head is light; a dark head always takes it), so the logo follows a chosen appearance as well as the device's; every signature on the page's own background takes the block `.ui-ink` (`src/styles/ui/_ink.scss`: `filter: var(--filter-ink)` and `mix-blend-mode: var(--blend-ink)`), under the founders' note in O nás and in the administration's Lidé fondu (the row's "Podpis" and the editor's preview), because the server flattens every upload onto white (`invert(1) hue-rotate(180deg)` and `screen` in the dark, so blue ink turns light blue and a white upload background disappears; both off under `inverted-colors: inverted`, where Smart Invert turns the page light again). The status bar needs nothing: the site sets no `theme-color` (above), and the untinted bar follows the page. The static launch screen follows `--color-surface`, so it is dark in the dark, because it shows on every load in a browser tab too; the `apple-touch-startup-image` PNGs of an app opened from the Home Screen stay light (drawn from the manifest's `background_color`), so a cold start of the installed app in the dark still begins white for a moment: dark startup images wait until a device shows whether iOS honours `prefers-color-scheme` in their `media`. `scripts/check-themes.mjs` (`appearanceProblems`) fails a theme without a dark block and, in the light and the dark appearance, each with and without `prefers-contrast: more` (blocks applied in source order as the cascade does), any text token below 4.5:1 on the grounds it is used on (text, secondary, muted and accent on the eight light-family grounds, the grouped ground and the elevated surface among them; soft, subtle and danger on the page, the quiet ground, the muted ground, the grouped ground and the elevated surface; the accent on its own 14 % tint; white on the fills, the text of the filled warning badge on it, the glyph of each icon tile at 3:1 on it, the navy bands and the quote; each status colour on its own ground), the focus ring below 3:1 on the page, the quiet ground, the grouped ground and the navy band, and the field border below 3:1 on the page and grouped ground everywhere but the light default (Jakub kept that border soft); `var()` references are followed and a translucent mix is laid over the page first. `e2e/dark-appearance.spec.ts` checks that a light device still gets the white page and dark logo, that a dark one gets the dark page, light text, the light logo and `color-scheme: dark`, that the payment QR keeps dark modules on a light ground, and runs axe in the dark on O nás, Přispět (also on a phone with the QR unfolded), the privacy policy, the news and project lists and pages, the sign-in dialog and the donor's overview; `e2e/theme.spec.ts` opens the public pages and the sign-in dialog of the control theme in the dark too. The `visual` job shoots the light look only.
- Appearance choice (#637; Jakub 2026-11-06, the three choices 2026-11-12, the look 2026-11-13): the account sheet's head carries one round button beside the close button that cycles Automaticky (monitor) → Světlý (sun) → Tmavý (moon), named `Vzhled: <choice>`; on a wide screen it is plain like the dialog's ×, beside it. `core/appearance/appearance.ts` (`Appearance`, started by `appearanceAtStart`) keeps the choice only on the device, in `localStorage` under `nfnn.appearance` (removed for Automaticky; every read and write in try/catch, so a refused storage keeps the choice for the visit), and applies it as `data-appearance="light"|"dark"` on `<html>` (none for Automaticky) and as the content of `<meta name="color-scheme">` (`light`, `dark`, or `light dark`). `public/appearance.js`, a classic script in the `<head>` of `index.html` beside `boot-guard.js` (the CSP refuses an inline one; the service worker keeps it with the shell), applies the kept choice before the body is parsed, so neither the launch screen nor the page paints the other appearance first, even while the app's code is still on its way (`scripts/appearance-script.test.mjs`, and an e2e that holds `main-*.js` back). Signing out (`AuthStore.forget` while a donor was signed in: the sign-out button, another tab's sign-out, a refused session) sets Automaticky again, since only a signed-in donor reaches the switch and the next visitor of the device could not undo it (Jakub 2026-11-14). A press also says the new choice through `DialogAnnouncer`, since a changed `aria-label` of the focused button is not reliably read out. The Smart Invert block `@media (inverted-colors: inverted)` names `:root:not([data-appearance='light'])`, so it still outranks the dark blocks, and `check-themes.mjs` fails a theme whose inverted-colors values lose in any appearance. A `storage` event carries a choice made in another tab of the site to this one without a reload, and `Appearance.dark()` (the choice, or the device under Automaticky) drives the logos. Each theme writes its dark tokens once, in the mixins `dark-appearance` and `dark-appearance-more-contrast`, and includes them under `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root:not([data-appearance='light']) }` and under `:root[data-appearance='dark']` (the more-contrast one under the matching `prefers-contrast: more` queries), while `:root[data-appearance='light']` only sets `color-scheme: light`; `check-themes.mjs` expands the mixins and checks the forced dark appearance on a light device and the forced light one on a dark device too, and `make-startup-images.mjs` reads the dark surface from the device block. Switching needs no server (`WORKS_WITHOUT_CONNECTION`). `e2e/dark-appearance.spec.ts` switches from the account sheet on a light and a dark device, checks the page, the logo and `color-scheme`, the choice after a reload, another open tab following and the return to Automaticky, and runs axe in the forced dark and the forced light. Switching the appearance puts `appearance-switching` on `<html>` for one drawn frame, which turns every transition off, so all colours change together; a choice made in another tab waits until no page transition (`.page-transition`) and no leaving dialog (`dialog--leaving`) runs; the icon of the appearance button cross-fades (`motion.state(opacity)`, exempt from the pause) and changes at once under reduced motion (#1277).
- Surfaces, dark levels and accent (design slice s3 of the designers' joint plan, sections 2.1, 2.5 and 2.6; Jakub's decision J6, 2026-11-06): the app areas `/darce` and `/sprava` stand on `--color-surface-grouped` (`#f2f3f7`, dark `#05070c`): `DonorShell` carries the block class `ui-grouped`, its pages paint that ground, and `html:has(.ui-grouped) body` takes it too, so Safari's overscroll and the strip under its toolbar match. Inside `.ui-grouped`, and inside a `[grouped]` dialog (the account sheet, where its sections open on a phone), a `.ui-card` is a flat cell (`--radius-group` 24 px, as the account sheet's groups, `--shadow-group` `none`, no border, in the dark too) filled with `--color-group-cell` (`--color-surface`, in the dark the raised `--color-surface-elevated`; the grouped dialog points it to the raised `--color-sheet-group` and its `--color-surface-alt` to the translucent `--color-fill`, so a section in the sheet stands on the sheet's levels and a table head or box inside a cell rises from it in the dark and sinks a little in the light, as iOS fills do; any other dialog points it back to `--color-surface`); a danger card keeps its red border, and a card inside an ordinary dialog keeps the public look (one language for the administration, the donor sections and the account sheet, Jakub 2026-11-12, `requirements.md`); inside `.ui-grouped` the quiet ground `--color-surface-alt` points to `--color-grouped-alt` (`--color-surface-muted` `#f1f3f5` in the light, the translucent `--color-fill` in the dark, where a cell is the raised `--color-surface-elevated` and `#1b2130` would vanish on it) and `--color-divider` to `--color-group-divider` (`--color-divider` in the light, white at 12 % in the dark), so the boxes, table heads, totals and hairlines inside a cell stay apart from it; elsewhere a card is a public card with `--shadow-card` (`0 1px 2px` at 4 % and `0 4px 16px` at 5 % of the navy) and a `--color-border` hairline. Three levels in the dark: the grouped ground `#05070c`, cells and pages `--color-surface` `#121826`, and `--color-surface-elevated` `#1a2232` for dialogs and sheets (`#ffffff` in the light). A card's hairlines (under its bar, over its footer, between its groups) and the hairlines inside the donor sections' cards are `--color-divider`, the one kind of line inside a card; table rows and the list of people are `--color-separator` (navy at 10 %, white at 8 % in the dark); the rows of `.ui-list` keep `--color-divider`, except an `--inset` list, whose separators are `--color-separator` and start at the title. `--color-fill` (the iOS tertiary fill) and `--color-badge-warning` with `--color-on-badge-warning` are tokens for the later control slices; `--color-badge-accent-bg` fills `.ui-badge--accent` and the gifts table's `.pairing-tag` (`--color-accent-bg` in the light, `--color-accent-soft` in the dark, where `--color-accent-bg` vanished on a raised cell); `--color-backdrop` is `--color-backdrop-tint` at `--backdrop-strength`, so `styles.scss` can paint the opaque colour of a page under it; the icon tiles `--color-tile-accent`, `-success`, `-warning`, `-neutral` with `--color-on-tile` colour `.ui-list__tile`. `--color-media-rim` frames the photos of Přispět and O nás with a 1 px inner outline, white at 10 % in the dark and transparent in the light. The light `--color-accent` is `#1d5fc9` (logo-family blue, 5.94:1 on white, 4.84:1 on its 14 % tint) for text, links, eyebrows, outlines and tints, with `--color-accent-soft` `#edf2fb`, `--color-accent-bg` and `--color-surface-accent` `#f1f5fc` and `--color-border-accent` `#c0d2f0` mixed from it, while `--color-accent-fill` stays `#2563eb` for filled buttons and selected states (white text 5.17:1; the plan's dark `#3b82f6` would give white text only 3.68:1, so the fill stays `#2563eb` in the dark too) and the dark accent stays `#6ea8ff`; the manifest's `theme_color` follows the accent. The e-mail templates take the same values (`EmailTemplateColoursTest`): links `#1d5fc9`, filled buttons `#2563eb`, the tinted note `#edf2fb` with `#c0d2f0`, grey text `#676f7d`. The light soft and subtle text darkens to `#676f7d`, the lightest grey that keeps 4.5:1 on the grouped ground. The control theme mirrors every token in its own way (square groups with a 1 px rim, a warm grey ground, a teal accent `#0e7068` for text beside its fill `#0f766e`).
- Motion (Jakub 2026-11-16, "Střídmější, méně nápadné jako iOS, ale promyšlené a dokonalé … vše je animované"; agreed by both polish teams, mobile and web, in phase 0): every change of state moves, briefly, with iOS curves, never for its own sake and never holding anything up.
  - Rules: motion explains a change of state; the result never waits for the motion (focus, `close`, `aria-expanded`, a `role="status"` text and the navigation happen at its start, and what leaves is `inert` from its first millisecond), with one named exception: a modal `<dialog>` that leaves, since without `overlay` and `allow-discrete` (which Safari lacks) a closed dialog leaves the top layer at once, so it stays open through its leaving transitions with its panel `inert` and the focus on the dialog itself from the first millisecond, and `close()`, `dismissed` and the return of the focus to the opener come at their end, at the latest their duration plus 0.3 s (R38 of #1331, decision (a), both teams 12/12); what can turn back half way (hover, press, dialog, disclosure, switch) is a `transition` or `@starting-style`, never keyframes; an interaction lasts at most `--duration-base`, a move across the screen at most `--duration-page` (a sheet `--duration-sheet`); longer only the hero, a section moving in, a count-up and a spring settling. Only `transform`, `translate`, `scale`, `rotate`, `opacity` and colours move (plus the press's `filter` and the `box-shadow` of small controls); never `all`, a size (except the one grid row through which Rozbalení and a leaving row open and close, D9), a position, `background` (only `background-color`), the radius of `backdrop-filter`, `outline` or a focus shadow, and no lasting `will-change`; the focus ring never animates. What leaves without a finger takes the next shorter duration; opacity is full at about 60 % of a move; text never reflows during a change (a swapped label reserves the width of its longer wording, a screen reader hears only the final value, once). A busy button is locked at once (`disabled` or `aria-disabled`) and carries `aria-busy="true"` until the work ends; its visible label ("Ukládáme…") changes only after `--delay-loading` and turns back at once when the work ends, so an answer faster than the delay never changes the label, and a new start waits the whole delay again (#1250, replacing D1 variant a′). The accessible name stays the resting label while the work runs, the busy label being `aria-hidden` (explicit exception from D1/#1250, Label in Name, R66: a locked button cannot be operated by voice, and VoiceOver and NVDA do not read a renamed focused button again); after the work the name equals the visible label again. The start is said instead by the card's existing `role="status"` line, after the same delay and only if the work still runs, with the busy label's own text (same i18n id, no new sentence), visually hidden so the card does not move, and the result replaces it; both come from one signal (`showsBusy` of `shared/ui/busy-label`, read through the card's `BusyAnnouncement`), so the line and the label change in the same frame, with no second timer. `shared/ui/busy-label` keeps both labels in one grid cell, so the button holds the width of the longer one where its row has room (where it has none, the unseen label gives way and the button stays within its row) and the label is never shortened, centres them in it, lets them wrap between words within the button at enlarged text, fades the coming label in by a `transition` (`motion.state(opacity)`; `--duration-reduced` under reduced motion) while the leaving one is gone at once (no transition, so no frame shows it broken inside a word, R65), and announces nothing itself: no `aria-live` on the button, no second live region and no focus move. Where no `BusyAnnouncement` is provided (no status line says the start: the administration until #1380, the contact form, the sign-in, `/pripojit`), the label falls back to naming the button by what it shows (R69): after `--delay-loading` the busy label is the accessible name, the resting one `aria-hidden`, and the button gets no `aria-busy`, so a screen reader still hears the start.
  - Tokens (every theme; `control` keeps them plain): curves `--ease-standard` (colours, opacity), `--ease-out` (arriving, hover), `--ease-in` (leaving without a finger), `--ease-spring` (moving in space; `--ease-sheet` and `--ease-page` point at it) and `--ease-spring-bouncy`, a `linear()` spring that peaks at 1.038, drawn for `--duration-done` and used only with it (`check-themes.mjs` refuses a rule that names it without it) and only for the icon of an action's response, never text or a surface; durations `--duration-fast`/`-base`/`-slow`/`-page`/`-sheet`/`-entrance` and `--duration-reveal`, `--duration-stagger` (at most six steps), `--duration-reduced`, `--duration-spin`, `--duration-pulse`, `--duration-calm` (how long a wait spins before it settles), `--delay-loading` (how long loading waits before its skeleton shows), `--duration-loading-min` (how long a shown skeleton stands at least), `--duration-done`, `--duration-done-hold`, `--duration-count`; distances in px, since `rem` grows with Dynamic Type: `--motion-rise`, `--motion-rise-reveal`, `--motion-lift`; scales `--motion-scale-enter`, `--motion-scale-done`, `--pressed-scale` and `--pressed-scale-surface` (a surface the component lays out wide). Each theme's `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` block on `:root` sets every distance to 0, every scale to 1, the stagger to 0s, the entrance to `--duration-reduced` and the bouncy spring to `--ease-standard` (`check-themes.mjs`).
  - Vocabulary (`src/styles/_motion.scss`, `@use 'motion'`; keyframes and the moving-in classes in `_motion-base.scss`): Stisk `pressed`; Změna stavu `motion.state(…)` (colours and opacity in `--duration-base`, refuses a moving or forbidden property); Hover `motion.hover(…) { … }` (only `(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`, never on `[aria-disabled='true']`, in `--duration-fast` with `--ease-out`, back in `--duration-base`; a control changes colour or tint, only a card lifts by `--motion-lift`); Posun `motion.move(…, $duration, $ease)` (every `transform`, `translate`, `scale` and `rotate`; with `spring-bouncy` only over `done`, behind `@supports` with `--ease-out` as the fallback); Objevení `motion.appear` (opacity and `--motion-rise`, or opacity only with `$rises: false` for a line in the flow, through the keyframes `motion-fade-in` rather than a zero `--motion-rise`, which would be inherited and still a block appearing inside it (D3, both teams 12/12; `scripts/motion-appear.test.mjs`), `--duration-slow`, only when it replaces a loading or empty state that showed, or on a first insertion); Zmizení `motion.leave`; Odezva akce `motion.done` (the icon grows from `--motion-scale-done` on the bouncy spring); Načítání `motion.spin` and `motion.pulse` (the pulse stops after three breaths, WCAG 2.2.2; the skeleton of `shared/ui/skeleton` breathes through it, never under reduced motion; a wait that can last longer than `--duration-calm` settles instead of spinning on, Q28 and WCAG 2.2.2: the spinning part carries `motion.wait-settles` and fades out after it, a still icon beside it carries `motion.wait-calm` and fades in, and under reduced motion only the still icon shows; first user the waiting ring of "Přidat účet", which becomes a clock); Nájezd sekce `appReveal` (below); Přechod stránky nad `nav` (below). Posuvník výběru (a thumb under a segmented choice: its width at once, `motion.move(translate, slow, spring)` between segments, a jump on first placement, a resize that changes its percentage, wrap and reduced motion; first user the sign-in dialog's `SegmentThumb`); Dialog `motion.present($panel, $leaving, $from-opacity, $from-scale)` (first user `<app-dialog>`, #1245): a `transition` with `@starting-style`, never keyframes, so a close half way turns back from where it got; the opacity only on the dialog with its backdrop (`--duration-slow`, `--ease-out`), the panel only `scale` from `--motion-scale-enter` (`--duration-slow`, `--ease-spring`); the leaving class takes both back over `--duration-base` with `--ease-in`; no `overlay` or `allow-discrete`, so the dialog stays open while it leaves and closes when its transitions end; under reduced motion only the fade, in `--duration-reduced`, and no leaving phase at all. `<app-dialog>` passes its own `--dialog-enter-opacity` and `--dialog-enter-scale`: a dialog that fills the screen (`full` below `nav`, the centered one below `phone`, the viewer) only fades, and a sheet below `nav` arrives opaque by `translate` (`motion.move(translate, sheet, sheet)` with `@starting-style`) while its veil dims by `background-color` over the same duration and curve, fading only under reduced motion. Rozbalení `motion.expand` (T7, D9 and Q28, #1223: a disclosure's content, an in-card confirmation or an inline group opens and closes by its height, its one grid row going between `0fr` and `1fr` in `--duration-base` on `--ease-standard` while it fades in within `--duration-fast` (D9 named `--ease-spring`, but a spring overshoots, and a height that grows past its content and back means nothing, so the height keeps the standard curve), so the content below slides instead of jumping; the element carrying it holds one unpadded child, which clips while it moves, and that child holds the visible box, so padding, border and margins sit inside the clip; it opens from `@starting-style`, so it works for an element inserted by `@if` and for one whose `[hidden]` is lifted, and closes when `[hidden]` returns, leaving the layout at the end through `display` with `allow-discrete`, so a closed panel adds no gap, or, for one an `@if` removes, through `animate.leave` with its own `<block>--closing` class, which `motion.expand($closes-by: class)` emits instead of the `[hidden]` state, so each disclosure carries one closed state only; content an `@if` removes inside a closing disclosure stays and fades through `motion.expand-content-leave` while the height closes, and goes at once when other content replaced it, so the account card's panels switch with one closing while the next opens; `aria-expanded` and the focus change at once, and so does its visibility on opening, so the focus lands in the content from its first frame (a starting `visibility: hidden` that faded would refuse it), while on closing it is `inert` in the same update that turns `aria-expanded` false, through `appDisclosure` (`shared/ui/disclosure/`), and takes no pointer (`pointer-events: none` in its closed states), so neither Tab, a click nor a screen reader reaches the vanishing content, and it stays visible until the height has closed (`visibility` with `step-end`, only in the closed state, `[hidden]` or `--closing`), and the content stays clipped while the height opens (`overflow: clip` with `step-end` over `--duration-base`; `clip`, not `hidden`, because a `hidden` box is a scroll container that a focus or a scroll into view inside the opening fold would scroll, so its content jumped (#1661); and `min-inline-size: 0` on the clipped child, which `hidden` gave implicitly, so a grid item does not grow to its content's width), so focus rings show at rest; a fold that brings itself into view once open does so on `appDisclosure`'s `unfolded`, which it emits after opening once its `grid-template-rows` transition has finished (at once when none runs, never when it closed again first), because a scroll into view in the opening render measured the fold at no height and left it unfolding below the screen (the standing order guide on a phone, #1488); under reduced motion the height changes at once and only opacity fades in `--duration-reduced`; a chevron turns through `motion.move(transform)`, which `.ui-list__chevron` carries in every list); Řádek přidán a odebrán (T8, #1223; a row enters by a class the CSS animates as it is inserted, a row leaves through Angular's `animate.leave`): `motion.row-enter` (in a list only opacity, `motion-fade-in` in `--duration-base` as `motion.appear($rises: false)`, plus a short tint: the row's background starts at `--color-accent-soft` and fades out over `--duration-slow`, this reading of Q28's "short tint" awaiting a vote of both teams; only the fade, without the tint, under reduced motion) only on a row the visitor's own action added (a `[class.…--entering]` binding true only for that row as it is inserted, so a list drawn or refreshed never animates; not `animate.enter`, whose runtime would join the first bundle, which nothing before the donor portal needs), `motion.row-leave` (the row's height closes with its fade in `--duration-fast`, `motion-row-leave` over its one grid row, so the rows below slide up instead of jumping; the row is an unpadded `li` around one unpadded child that holds the visible row, as for T7; only the fade, in `--duration-reduced`, under reduced motion; no pointer reaches the row, and `markRowsLeaving` (`shared/ui/leaving-row/`) makes it `inert` from its first moment, so neither Tab nor a screen reader's cursor reaches its controls; a leave runs only for a row inside the component, so a page left never waits on it) and `motion.after-rows-left` for the empty state, bound only after a row left, which stays transparent and out of the flow until the row's `--duration-fast` (or `--duration-reduced`) has passed and then fades in, so it never shows beside the leaving row and the card moves once; the focus moves at once to the neighbouring row's control, else the one above, else the card's heading, never `body`, through `neighbourAfterRowLeaves` (`shared/ui/leaving-row/`, given the leaving row and the list's controls before the change; a control that acts at once without a question, such as a connected application's "Odpojit", is never a focus target, so its caller passes none and the focus goes to the heading, while one that asks first or opens a form, "Odhlásit" since #1294 or "Přejmenovat", is; first users the devices, passkeys and connected apps of the portal, `scripts/motion-rows.test.mjs`, `e2e/donor-motion.spec.ts`); Odezva akce's first portal user is the verified account's disc and tick in "Přidat účet".
  - Reduced motion: the global rule in `styles.scss` still cuts every animation and transition to 0.01 ms; a `motion` mixin wins over it with `!important` on its class and keeps only a fade in `--duration-reduced` without a delay, while the tokens have already stilled every distance and scale; push and pop, the swipe, the count-up and the tab pill change at once.
  - Přechod stránky nad `nav` (T12, desktop team Q27, the whole site: public pages, the donor's account and the administration): only the content fades through, the rest stands still. `core/navigation/page-fade.ts` (`PageFade`, beside the phone's `PageTransitions`, which still alone does push, pop and the swipe below `nav`) takes a still copy of the page layer at `ResolveEnd` (`stillCopyOf`, its `main` turned into a `div.page-fade__main` without an `id`, so the document never holds a second `main` and the skip link and `getElementById` never reach the copy; the copy keeps `with-tab-bar`, which the public shell and the contact form select by class, so it is as long as the page it copies), with how far the layer starts above the window (not `scrollY`, which counts the landing stage's room above the layer), its ground (the first opaque background from the layer up to `html`, as Kontakt in view darkens `body` under the light `app-root`) and the router's scroll held from then on; until the new page arrives the old one stays live and readable, however long its data or code take. At `NavigationEnd`, in the same task as the swap of the pages, so no frame shows the swap, it puts the copy over the screen (`.page-fade`, `aria-hidden` and `inert`, under the header and the tab bar, without pointer events), scrolled to where the old page stood, with only the old `main` visible, and hides the new `main#obsah` from the eye only (`page-fade-hidden` on `<html>`), so the shell focuses its `h1` at once. Two frames later the copy fades out in half of `--duration-base` with `--ease-standard`; in the frame it is gone, the cover goes, the scroll the router and `TabMemory` asked for runs (`ScrollHold`: `SmoothAnchorScroller` and `TabMemory` scroll through it, and it holds only during a fade), and the new `main` fades in over the other half (`page-fade-arriving`), so the two texts are never readable at once and the page moves to its top while the content is invisible. The header, the side navigation (sticky, live, so its selection switches at once) and the tab bar never move; when the shell changes (a public page and the account, the account and the administration) the header swaps at once and the cover keeps the old page's ground under the fading copy, so the new side navigation and ground show only once the old content is gone; it keeps that ground too whenever the new page changes the ground under it (a tab to the home page, which darkens `body`). Nothing fades on the first page, below `nav`, under reduced motion (the pages swap at once), with a dialog open (one sliding away does not count), on the browser's back or forward (`popstate`) or a fragment it changes, on Zpět (with a step behind it it is `popstate`, without one `BACK_NAVIGATION`, the edge swipe of the home-screen app included), when only the query or the fragment changes (filters, tabs and sections of a page) or the address stays, or from one page of the administration to another while it is locked (`AdministrationAccess.unlocked()` is false), where the lock panel stays as it is and a fade would be motion without a change of state; a pointer or key press, a turn of the wheel or a resize (a rotation, an iPadOS window crossing `nav`) during a fade ends it and shows the new page at once, so a click into the navigation reaches its target; after a pointer press the held scroll waits until the press has ended, so the page does not move under the pointer between its press and its click; a navigation cancelled or failed before its arrival never covered or hid anything. Rules: `pageFadeFor` in `page-transition-kind.ts`; styles `src/styles/_page-fade.scss`; tests `page-fade.spec.ts` and `e2e/page-fade.spec.ts` (1280 px with animations slowed through CDP, an iPad on its side at 1180 × 820 with touch and upright at 820).
  - Nájezd sekce: `shared/ui/reveal/` (`[appReveal]="order"`) only on the public pages; today on Pomáháme (each year's head, grid rows, strip and link to every year, the Přispět band), Aktuality (on page 1 the heading "Starší aktuality" and each card, at most six steps apart, and the pager; a later page's cards arrive as one list) and O nás (the figures by rows, "Kdo fond vede" with its people staggered, the Přispět band), never on an article's text, payment details or a QR, and never on an anchor's target (the contact intro is `#kontakt`, which an anchor glides to, D12) (desktop team P31; `e2e/motion-states.spec.ts`). A block that starts below the screen waits hidden (`motion-reveal`: transparent, `--motion-rise-reveal` lower) and is watched with `threshold: 0` and `rootMargin: 0px 0px -10% 0px`; scrolled in over that line it moves in once (`motion-reveal--shown`, `--duration-reveal`, opacity in `--duration-slow`, delayed by its order, at most six, times `--duration-stagger`) and, once its shift has ended, drops the classes, so its own transitions (a card's hover) are its again; landed above 70 % of the screen by a jump (an anchor, find in page, a restored scroll, End or Page Down, a kept tab page coming back) it shows at once; a block that stops in the last tenth of the screen, where the page ends, moves in after `--duration-reveal` (a second observer on the screen itself); focus entering it or the page being printed shows it at once. While the page fade above `nav` holds the scroll (`ScrollHold`), the directive decides whether a block starts on the screen only once `ScrollHold.whenReleased` resolves, so the new page is measured where it will be seen and the figures of O nás count up only once its content shows (#1210). The directive emits `revealed` once the block shows and rests (at once when nothing hid it, after its shift when it moved in), so what moves inside it, as the figures' count, starts only then. Nothing hides a block on the screen from the start, under `prefers-reduced-motion`, without `IntersectionObserver` or in the app on the home screen of an iPhone or iPad (`navigator.standalone` with touch points, as `SystemTextSize`; the Mac's Dock app keeps it), the hiding is only opacity and a shift (never `visibility`), a still copy of a page in a page transition always shows its blocks, and print shows them.
  - Testing a height that folds in WebKit (R106 to R106d, #1515): WebKit starts a pending transition only with a painted frame and back-dates it. It also updates the computed `grid-template-rows` only when it paints (opacity changes between frames too), and Playwright WebKit paints the administration sheet's 40 px shadow and its blurs in software, so on a CI runner no frame may be painted during the whole 200 ms fold (475 ms between paints on `9c48af6`), and neither an intermediate height nor a progress between 0 and 1 can be sampled there. So the first-download notice's e2e (`e2e/device-copy.spec.ts`) checks the fold in the `iphone` project by one and the same `CSSTransition` on `grid-template-rows` of `app-fold`, pending or running, sampled by a timer independent of frames: not `allow-discrete`, keyframes from `1fr` to `0fr`, the page's `--duration-base` and the easing of `motion.expand`, and it is the transition that finishes with the `transitionend` the test waits for, at least 80 % of the duration after the click (by `performance.now()`, never the back-dated `startTime`), while the notice stays connected and rendered until then. The interpolation itself is proved by `chromium` frame by frame and, on a real iPhone, by `safari.yml` and the device checklist. This holds only for a painted height in WebKit: a test where waiting on the page's state is enough keeps waiting on it.
- `public/images/`: hero in 1024/1536 px with `srcset` (the source photo is 1536 px wide, so wider screens scale it up), `sizes="max(100vw, 150vh, 900px)"` and `fetchpriority="high"`; about and donate in 720/1440 px; project and news cards at 600 px; every photo below the hero has `loading="lazy"` written before `src`/`srcset` (Angular sets attributes in template order) plus `width`/`height`, so photos hidden on phones are never downloaded there. Convert a new photo with Pillow or `cwebp` at quality about 78; no file is kept that no template names.
- Icons: `favicon.svg` is the white star of the logo (its traced outline, PR #228) in a flat circle of the logo blue `#005dab` (viewBox 1024 wide, Jakub 2026-10-30); the face (`g.face`) stays at every size, also at 16 px (Jakub 2026-10-30; `e2e/home-assets.spec.ts`). `favicon.ico` holds 16, 32 and 48 px renders of it. The app icon is Jakub's white tile with the blue star outline of the logo (2026-10-30, the PWA icon of `nfnn-podklady/logo/ikona-pwa/` in project files): `app-icon.svg` is the tile with rounded corners (`rx` 230 of 1024) for the ZimaOS app list (ZimaOS shows SVG, Jakub 2026-10-30, so there is no PNG of it); `apple-touch-icon.png` is 180 px on opaque RGB, the white square without rounded corners because iOS rounds them itself (iOS takes no SVG). The cloud ImageMagick has no SVG renderer: render with Playwright's Chromium (`omitBackground`) and pack with `convert 16.png 32.png 48.png favicon.ico`.
- Launch screen (#420; the white tile on the dark surface fixed 2026-11-06 at Jakub's report): each theme carries `assets/launch-icon.svg`, the star of `logo-on-dark.svg` (its white body and blue outline with the face) placed as in `app-icon.svg` but without the tile, so it stands on the light and on the dark surface alike. Before the app's code runs, `src/index.html` shows it beside `<app-root>` (`.launch-screen`, `src/styles/_launch-screen.scss`: the SVG at `--launch-icon-size` in the middle of `--color-surface`, which follows the device's appearance, above the app's own layers; `e2e/install-app.spec.ts`), and it stays until the first page is drawn (Jakub 2026-11-12, smoothness: inside `<app-root>` Angular replaced it as soon as the root component started, and the empty surface showed for 0.3–1 s while the shell, the page's chunks and the session restore were still on their way, then the page jumped in): `core/layout/launch-screen.ts` (`LaunchScreen`, `launchScreenAtStart` in `app.config.ts`) removes it after the render that follows the first navigation that ends, fails, is skipped or is turned down by a guard (a guard's redirect, such as `/aplikace` to the donor's start page, and a new navigation that takes the first one's place keep it until their page is drawn), and at the latest 10 s after the first navigation starts, so the first painted frame without it holds the page; a first page whose chunk fails to load then shows its notice, and the boot guard's panel stands above it. The iPhone and iPad home-screen app first shows the `apple-touch-startup-image` iOS picks by media query, and iOS takes only a raster there, so the `postbuild` step `scripts/make-startup-images.mjs` draws one PNG per screen of `STARTUP_SCREENS`, orientation and appearance into `startup/` of the build: the theme's `launch-icon.svg`, drawn by `scripts/svg-raster.mjs` (filled paths, `matrix` transforms and the `nonzero` and `evenodd` rules only; anything else fails the build and `check-themes.mjs`, which also fails a launch icon that paints its corners) at `--launch-icon-size` times the screen's scale, on the manifest's `background_color` for `startup/<w>x<h>.png` and on the dark `--color-surface` of the built stylesheet for `startup/<w>x<h>-dark.png`, whose links add `and (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` and follow every light one. iOS chooses one when the app is added to the home screen (the iOS 27.2 simulator added in the dark appearance fetched only the dark image, 2026-11-06), so a later change of the appearance may keep the image of that moment until the app is added again; the launch screen of `index.html` follows the appearance at every start. No raster of the launch is kept in the repository.
- Web app manifest (plan v3 2.4): each theme carries `assets/manifest.webmanifest`, linked from `index.html` (`<link rel="manifest">`), with `id` and `start_url` `/aplikace`, `scope` `/`, `display` `standalone`, `lang` `cs`, the name "Nová naděje – nadační fond" and the short name "Nová naděje" under the icon (#386: at most the 12 characters an iPhone shows there, and equal to `apple-mobile-web-app-title` in `src/index.html`, which names the app in Add to Home Screen), `theme_color` the theme's `--color-accent` and `background_color` its `--color-surface`, and the icons `icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png` (`any`) and `icon-maskable-512.png` (`maskable`, the star inside the safe circle), Jakub's PWA icon of `nfnn-podklady/logo/ikona-pwa/` (the control theme carries copies, since every theme carries the same file names). `scripts/check-themes.mjs` checks each theme's manifest: those fields, both colours equal to the tokens of the top-level `:root`, and every icon a PNG in `assets/` whose real size is the one its `sizes` names, with a 192 and a 512 px `any` icon and a 512 px `maskable` one, and the short name of at most 12 characters equal to the `apple-mobile-web-app-title` of `src/index.html`.

## Tests

Specs beside the code; `src/test-setup.ts` gives jsdom `showModal()`/`close()`, puts jsdom's own `window.scrollTo`, `scrollX`, `scrollY`, `innerWidth`, `innerHeight`, `matchMedia` and `visualViewport` back before every test (a spec that deleted one, as `landing-stage.spec.ts` did, broke every later spec of its worker, #964); the setup runs again for each spec file while the window stays the worker's, so `src/testing/window-properties.ts` keeps the first capture of each window on `globalThis`, taken when the setup first loads, and clears `localStorage` and resets the TestBed before every test (specs share a worker, and an `afterEach` that throws, an open request at `http.verify()`, skips Angular's own reset and the storage clear after it, so every later spec of the worker would fail with it), `src/test-providers.ts` the `cs` locale, `FakeLiveUpdates` in place of `LiveUpdates` and no tab locks or session channel (`TAB_LOCKS` null, `SESSION_CHANNEL` a factory of null), so no spec opens a real `BroadcastChannel`; specs of the tabs override them with `FakeTabLocks` and `FakeSessionChannel` from `core/auth/testing/fake-session-tabs.ts`, and `e2e/session-tabs.spec.ts` (Chromium only, because WebKit's request interception shows no `Cookie` header) runs two tabs over one rotating refresh cookie and a refresh whose answer never arrives. Shared cases (`docs/system-overview.md`, Regression against v2 and shared cases, Shared cases): the Vitest global setup `src/testing/shared-cases-setup.ts` (`vitest.config.mts`) reads `../shared-cases` in Node and `src/testing/shared-cases.ts` hands specs frozen, schema-checked cases (`sharedCases(file)`); `donation-amount.spec.ts` runs the Czech receipt amounts through `formatMinorUnits`. `core/i18n/plural-category.ts`: `pluralCategory(count)` → `one` | `few` | `other` for text built in TypeScript. Every e2e spec imports `test` from `e2e/fixtures.ts`, whose automatic fixture answers an unmocked `/api` request with a 404 problem. `e2e/devices/` opens the main pages, dialogs and menus in the poses of `poses.ts` (iPhones upright, on their side and with large text, iPads, a Pixel), checks each layout from its boxes and keeps a screen of each in `test-results/device-report/` (`.claude/rules/testing-frontend.md`, Phones and tablets); what it finds today is listed in `known-findings.ts`. Playwright in `e2e/` by journey (`chunk-load-recovery.spec.ts`, through `LostChunks` in `lost-chunks.ts`, answers 404 for every chunk the first page did not load, until a given document request or for good: the deploy path where the automatic load shows the page, the page of its own when that load fails too, an address with a fragment, a throwing `sessionStorage` (no automatic load, the bar), `context.setOffline` (no automatic load), chunks whose requests are cut off while `navigator.onLine` stays true (`cutOffEveryChunkNotLoadedYet`: the probe fails, so no automatic load and the bar) and a dirty news editor left for a lost chunk (one question in the page, no `beforeunload` prompt, the list loads from the server); `accessibility.spec.ts` runs axe over the bar and the page of its own, `accessibility-keyboard.spec.ts` checks at 320, 390 and 1280 px that the bar hides neither the focused control nor the end of the page, and `theme.spec.ts` covers both states; `site-metadata.spec.ts` reads `robots.txt` and the sitemap and checks the canonical link, `og:url` and the preview image on a page): `donor-session.ts` holds the sample donor and `administrator` (the same donor with the `ADMIN` role), `signIn(page, user?)`, `rememberedDonorSession(page, { installed?, user? })` (the refresh answered and the session flag of the browser's or the app's jar set before the first page), `session(status, user?)`, `openSignInDialog`, `donationItem(overrides)` and `bankAccountItem(overrides)` (the API's full item shapes) and `mockDonorApi` (payment details (from `shared-cases/payment/details.json`), summary and list, bank check, empty bank account list; with `receipts` `receipt-issuing.ts` answers `POST /api/donations/me/receipts` with an issued receipt made from a case of `shared-cases/receipts/v1/frozen.json`, and the synthetic signature and portrait by their digests, so the real worker draws the PDF), which every donor spec registers first so nothing escapes to the dev proxy; `qr-matrices.ts` reads the payment matrices (level H) the browser must draw from the shared cases `shared-cases/qr/payment.json` and the payment details the mocks serve from `payment/details.json`, by case name (found from the file's own folder, `__dirname`, since Playwright loads the specs as CommonJS, where `import.meta` is a syntax error), and `drawnModulesMatch(qr, matrix)` compares the drawn dots with one; `second-factor.spec.ts` decodes the drawn authenticator QR with jsQR back to `otpauthUri` (the backend sends no matrix since 1.4); the automatic fixture of `e2e/fixtures.ts` serves the details and fails any journey that asks for another `/api/donations/payment-*` path, and `donate-qr.spec.ts` checks the same dots for 500 and 1 000 Kč, one request in all, the redraw after typing and the saved PNG;
