`password-reset.spec.ts` covers the dialog's reset tab and the page; `email-verification.spec.ts` the confirmation page, and `donor-account.spec.ts` the registration's "check your e-mail" panel and the resend button; `donor-dashboard.spec.ts` also the password and e-mail change forms; `connect-app.spec.ts` the consent page (sign-in on the page, the consent for claude.ai with the fresh code and the return with the authorization code, a used code refused and a new one accepted, a plain donor declining, an incomplete request, `mockConnectApi` answers the client description and swallows `https://claude.ai/**`) and the connections in the account card (`claudeConnection`, "Odpojit"); `older-symbol.spec.ts` answers the older-symbol question both ways; `admin-administrators.spec.ts` the administrators page, `admin-donors.spec.ts` the donor search and card, `admin-copy-offline.spec.ts` the payments and the donor search read from the administration copy and kept offline with the copy's age, `admin-symbol-claims.spec.ts` the older-symbol answers, `admin-actions.spec.ts` the history, `admin-news.spec.ts` the news, `admin-projects.spec.ts` the projects, `content-pages.spec.ts` the home page with published content, both detail pages and an unknown slug (`mockDonorApi` answers `/api/content/home` with empty lists, so every other journey sees the placeholders); `admin.spec.ts` covers the administration (the link from the overview, the import state and its outage, the retry, a remembered session, a donor without the role sent to the overview, a 403 after the role was taken away, a visitor sent to the sign-in dialog); `passkeys.spec.ts`, `second-factor.spec.ts`, `add-bank-account.spec.ts`, `contact-form.spec.ts` (also copying the IČO and the IBAN from "Údaje pro účetní") and `privacy-policy.spec.ts` cover those journeys, `device-copy.spec.ts` the device copy in Chromium and WebKit (`mockDonorSync` of `donor-session.ts` serves the manifest and chunks; the password wrap made by the Argon2id worker under the production headers, the raw database scanned for the donor's gifts, e-mail and password, the copy kept over a reload and a sign-out, "Načíst data znovu" and the deletion with the database gone), `receipt-pdf.spec.ts` the receipt drawn in the browser (through `receipt-donations.ts`: the progress line while the data is held and the saved PDF read back with `pdf-reader.ts`, a worker chunk gone after a deploy with the one reload that resumes the download, still gone after it, an emulated iPhone with `canShare` and the second tap, the focus kept on the pressed button through the drawing and the save, and the 120-gift year whose median warm drawing in the worker stays within 1 s at CPU 1×, with the cold and warm times and their ×4 values as annotations (`system-overview.md`, receipt PDF)), `verify-receipt.spec.ts` the verification page (through `receipt-verification.ts`, which reads the proofs from `shared-cases/verification/payload.json`, serves or keeps a key list, cuts the network after load with `context.setOffline` and takes WebCrypto's Ed25519 away: the signed result and the only requests that leave, the status answers that are not green, a yearly receipt, a changed amount, a tampered key list, a revoked key, a cut-off link, both signature paths, the kept keys offline, no keys offline and the retry, an unknown key offline, the server unreachable while online), and the other specs are named with their page above; downloads use `Promise.all([page.waitForEvent('download'), click])` and `download.suggestedFilename()`; `accessibility.spec.ts` runs axe on every page and dialog.

## Frontend conventions in detail

The full text of the frontend rules as they stood on 2026-11-06, with every list of files, tokens, blocks and checks, kept here when the rules were cut to their constraints (`.claude/rules/frontend.md`, `.claude/rules/testing-frontend.md`). A change updates the rule's constraint there and the description here.

### Angular 22 (standalone, zoneless, signals)
- Components: flat names `hero.ts/.html/.scss`, class `Hero` (no `Component` suffix), selector `app-*`, no `standalone: true` (default), no NgModules. Change detection is OnPush (Angular 22's default; the existing components still spell it out, a new one may omit it); never `ChangeDetectionStrategy.Eager`/`Default` (lint rejects it). So a template may read only signals, inputs and what its own events change: state that arrives otherwise (the forms API, a subscription) goes through a signal, as `appFormControl` turns the control's events into `showsError()` and `errorKey()`.
- State in `signal`/`computed`/`linkedSignal`; `input()`, `output()`, `model()` instead of decorators; `inject()` instead of constructor parameters; a parameterless constructor holds the start-up work (initial load, `effect`, `afterNextRender`, `DestroyRef.onDestroy`); `host: {}` metadata instead of `@HostListener`/`@HostBinding` (lint: `prefer-host-metadata-property`); no `any`; strict TS and templates stay on.
- Templates: `@if/@for/@switch/@defer`; `[class.x]`/`[style.x]` (no `ngClass`/`ngStyle`); import only the directives and pipes used (never `CommonModule`); logic in the class.
- Forms: template-driven `ngModel` inside `<app-form-field label hint [messages]>` with `appFormControl` (needs `id` + `ngModel`) and `form[appFocusFirstInvalid]`; the field shows its own first error after touch or submit (`aria-invalid`, `aria-describedby`), a field left by a pointer press shows it only when the press ends, so the message cannot move the button away from under the pointer, and a required field the visitor only passed through without typing says nothing until submit (experts' decision for #359: on iOS the tap ends before the focus moves, so the message jumped the form under the finger); every text field names its return key with `enterkeyhint` (`next` before another field, `go` or `send` on the last; a textarea keeps its new line), and with the on-screen keyboard open `next` moves to the next field unless the form is already valid; a form-level alert only for what no field owns (passwords differ, an API refusal). Signal Forms are stable in Angular 22 but not used here: switching means migrating `form-field`, `appFormControl` and every form at once, so only by decision.
- Local first (`backend.md`): compute and render here whatever the data already in the browser allows; the backend only serves it.
- HTTP and data: one `providedIn: 'root'` service per backend resource, `<name>-api.ts` in its feature folder (sign-in and account calls: `core/auth/auth-api.ts`), relative `/api/...` only, every id or slug in a path through `encodeURIComponent`, so an address such as `/aktuality/..%2F..%2Fdonors%2Fme` cannot send the token to another API path, DTO interfaces mirroring `docs/api-contract.md`; a component calls it and `subscribe`s into signals, as every existing page does (no `httpResource`/`rxResource` piecemeal). The donor's gifts are the one read that may come from the device: Moje dary, Přehled and Potvrzení read them only through `DonorDonationsRepository` (`features/donations/device-copy/`, 2.15), which answers from the device copy while it agrees with its summary and from `donations-api.ts` otherwise, and reaches the copy only through `DonorCopyLink`; every write, and every other read, goes to the `-api` service. Every call passes `keptPublicAnswersInterceptor` first (`core/navigation/kept-public-answers.ts`: a GET of the public content, the home answer, the news and project lists and pages, a subset of the service worker's same-for-everyone list that its spec pins, is answered from memory when the tab already had it within 10 minutes, and asked again in the background; any write under `/api/admin/content/` forgets them all; a new kept path stays inside that list), then `authInterceptor` (Bearer header except on the same-for-everyone answers of `service-worker-bypass.ts`, problem+json Blob parsing, one refresh on `auth/expired-token`) and then `versionInterceptor` (`core/version/`: the app's `Nfnn-App` and `Nfnn-App-Api` on every `/api` call, the server's `Nfnn-Revision` and `Nfnn-Api` read from every `no-store` answer into `ServerVersion`, and while the server speaks another API generation every write but the session refresh, sign-out and receipt status answered locally with the problem `app/update-required`, which the server also answers to a write from another generation; a page that keeps its own failure text recognises the problem, as the contact form and the receipt download do); only the API service of a public endpoint may send its request without the token, with the context `WITHOUT_ACCESS_TOKEN` (`core/auth/auth-interceptor.ts`), and ESLint's `no-restricted-imports` in `eslint.config.js` allows that import only in the files listed there (today `payment-details-api.ts`, the receipt verification's `receipt-keys-api.ts` and `receipt-status-api.ts`, `setup-api.ts` for the setup status the restart wait polls, and `core/connectivity/connectivity-probe-api.ts`, whose probe asks the same status): a new public endpoint that should skip the token adds its service to that list; errors become one line through `describeProblem` (`core/auth/api-problem.ts`). The live stream (`core/live/live-api.ts`, `GET /api/live`) is an `HttpClient` call too, a text stream read through download progress, so it carries the token and refreshes like any call; a page listens through `LiveUpdates.changes(feeds)` and keeps working when no stream is open. Three calls bypass it with a raw `fetch`: `core/errors/error-reporter.ts`, whose browser error report goes with `keepalive` and `credentials: 'omit'`, so it never carries a token, survives the page closing and cannot itself fail through the interceptor into another report; `core/reload/chunk-probe.ts`, whose `HEAD` asks the server for a lost chunk (`cache: 'no-store'`, aborted after 4 s) and is no API call; and `receipt-pdf/receipt-fonts.ts` in the receipt PDF worker, which reads the theme's TrueType files from `/receipt-fonts/` (static files of the build, no API call; a worker has no `HttpClient`). Nothing else calls `fetch`. The HTTP backend is XMLHttpRequest (`withXhr()` in `appHttpClient`, `app.config.ts`, for the whole app): fetch, Angular 22's default, reports no upload progress and `reportUploadProgress: true` under it throws NG02824, while the first setup's backup upload shows a progress bar (`reportUploadProgress`; `reportProgress` is deprecated since 22.0).
- Own history (#431, Jakub 2026-11-06): `core/navigation/own-history.ts`, installed in `main.ts` before Angular, turns every `pushState` into a replace of the browser's one entry of the site and keeps the steps itself (`sessionStorage` `nfnn.app-history`), on every device, so the browser's Back and Safari's swipe leave the site and the site steps back only through Zpět, a dialog's ×, `history.back()` and the swipe of the home-screen app (`edge-swipe-back.ts`, on iPhone and iPad only there). Nothing else may add a browser entry: a full page load inside the site goes through `ExternalNavigation.loadFromServer` or `history.replaceState` + `location.reload()`, never `location.assign` or a plain `href` to a page of the site (content links go through `Markdown`'s router click); e2e steps back with `stepBack(page)` of `e2e/app-history.ts`, and `page.goBack()` is the browser leaving the site. A dialog that hands its entry over to a page (`DialogHistory.handOver`) passes `BackTarget.nextDepth()` as `APP_DEPTH_KEY` in the navigation's state, as the account sheet and the paired payment's sheet in Platby do, so the page's step back, its swipe preview and × of the administration count the handed-over entry.
- Stepping back, one rule for the whole site (both polish teams 12/12, 2026-11-17, after Jakub found the step back inconsistent; the code is brought to it group by group, #1358 first): like a native iOS app, no step without the screen moving under the finger and no movement without a step, so the edge swipe either moves a real screen or does nothing and never jumps. While dragged, the top screen follows the finger 1:1 with no transition over the real previous screen, never blank or black, under the existing `--shadow-page-edge` and `--color-page-dim`; on release it takes the finger's speed (`easingLeavingAt`) and finishes or returns within `--duration-page` on `--ease-page`; a swipe that starts during another transition takes the page over where it is (a running T12 fade finishes at once and the drag starts from the finished page) or is ignored, never a jump. The target is where the visitor came from in the same tab's stack, an open sheet on the same section included (a page or the administration opened from the account sheet steps back into that sheet, which already stands under the leaving page and does not enter again); between tabs the parent stays the target (#394), since switching tabs is no step; Zpět, the swipe, the system step and Esc share the target, the scroll of the previous screen is kept, and focus goes to the control the visitor left by, else the target's heading, and the target's name is announced (no new sentence or i18n id; Zpět stays „Zpět“). Over a modal sheet or dialog the edge swipe does nothing (the sheet closes by its drag down or ×, focus to its opener), except the sections inside a sheet (the account sheet's stack), where it does what ‹ does: the sheet's content follows the finger over its list and the sheet stands. From `nav` up (iPad landscape, windows of 899 px and more) only a real push follows the finger (a detail within the section: article, story, donor card, payment, editor): the content area, clipped at the side navigation's edge and without `--radius-page-card`, while the side navigation and the header stand and are not dimmed; choosing another section in the side navigation is no step, so the swipe does nothing there and the T12 fade stays; a step back the site starts itself (‹, a key, `BACK_NAVIGATION`) takes the T12 fade when pressed with a mouse, a trackpad or a key, and the same pop as the swipe when ‹ is tapped by a finger (decided by the pointer's `pointerType`, not the device, so an iPad with a trackpad fades), while `popstate` (the browser's Back, a mouse button, Safari's own gesture with its snapshot) stays without a transition so two transitions never meet; the flag for a site's own step is set before `history.back()` and cleared at `NavigationStart`, focus moves at the start of the step, not after the fade, and after a step between sections it goes to the side navigation's item the visitor left. Every gesture keeps its one-tap control (‹, ×, Esc); with `prefers-reduced-motion` the page does not follow the finger and the step happens on release, at once or with a short fade.
- Routing: lazy per feature (`<feature>.routes.ts`, `loadChildren`/`loadComponent`); only the routes marked `LOADED_AHEAD` (`core/navigation/tab-preloading.ts`: the public shell, the content routes and the four tab pages) are loaded ahead once the first page has opened, so a tab opens without waiting for its code, and every other page stays lazy (the chunk-load journeys rely on that), `title` via `$localize`, addresses and section ids in Czech without diacritics (`/darce/prehled`, `/sprava`, `/prispet`, `/o-nas#kontakt`; only `/api/...` stays English; a page that moves keeps its old address as a redirect, as `oldSectionRedirect` does for the former `/#…` sections), `/darce/**` behind `donorGuard`, `/sprava/**` behind `adminGuard`, public shell with header and tab bar, unknown paths show `not-found`; nginx answers the app's routes with 200 and every other path that is no file with the same `index.html` and 404 (the map `$app_page` in `frontend/nginx.conf`, kept equal to the routes by `app.routes.spec.ts`: a new route or a renamed one changes its line there in the same PR), and an unknown slug of a detail route with 200, where the page sets `noindex`.
- Formatting is Prettier's (`.prettierrc`: 100 columns, single quotes, Angular parser for templates): run `npm run format` before committing; CI fails on a file it would change.
- Lint is ESLint with angular-eslint (`eslint.config.js`: recommended TypeScript and template rules, template accessibility, `prefer-inject`, `prefer-signals`, `prefer-output-readonly`, `prefer-on-push-component-change-detection`, `prefer-control-flow`, `prefer-self-closing-tags`): run `npm run lint` before committing; CI fails on a finding. `eslint-confinement.js` is the frontend twin of `AdapterLibraryRulesTest`: its table `CONFINED_IMPORTS` names each library or module family with the scopes allowed to import it (`SCOPES`: `app`, `testSupport`, `routes` and the adapters; a narrower scope names the one it lies `within`, which then ignores its files, so every file is in exactly one scope whatever the order of the config objects), refuses it elsewhere statically, as a type or by `import()` of a string or template literal, refuses every `import()` of a computed name outside route files, and with `lazyOnly` allows a library in its adapter only through `import()` (types still pass). ESLint runs with `noInlineConfig` and `ng lint` fails on a warning (`maxWarnings: 0`): no `eslint-disable` directive, since it is a comment (`CLAUDE.md`, No comments); an exception is a scoped config object or a named helper in a file the rule ignores (`e2e/fixed-waits.ts`, `testing.md`, Frontend e2e). `npm run lint` also runs `scripts/eslint-confinement.test.mjs`, which lints an example of every entry in every scope and fails on a scope that matches no file; a new library or adapter adds its entry or scope and its examples there. Two scoped exceptions, both for the native `<dialog>`: `autofocus` is allowed in `*dialog*.html` because the dialog moves focus to it on open, and `shared/ui/dialog/dialog.html` closes on a backdrop click without key events because Esc closes it natively.
- Folders: `features/{content,donors,donations,contact,operations}` mirror backend contexts; `features/admin` holds every `/sprava` page whatever context it calls (a new page: `features/admin/<page>/`, calls in `admin-api.ts` unless the context already has a feature service for both sides, as content's `news-api.ts` and `projects-api.ts`, a route in `admin.routes.ts`, an entry in `ADMIN_SECTIONS`); singletons in `core/`; a behaviour-only piece used twice moves to `shared/ui/` (`form-field`, `dialog`, `copy-button`, `share-button` for the Web Share API with the copy button as its fallback, `password-reveal`, `qr-code`, `avatar` for a person's round portrait or initials, `zoom-viewer` for a photo or QR code opened large and zoomable (`app-zoomable`), `markdown` for content bodies, `side-nav` for the side panel of `/sprava` and `/darce`, `account-username` for the hidden user name of a form that asks for the current password).
- Trusted Types are enforced (`security.md`, Browser): no string reaches a markup, script or script-address sink (`innerHTML`, `insertAdjacentHTML`, `DOMParser`, `script.src`, `importScripts`, `eval`); text goes in through bindings or `textContent`, a worker starts only as `new Worker(new URL('./x.worker', import.meta.url), { type: 'module' })`, which the build turns into `/worker-<hash>.js` and the site's `default` policy (`core/security/trusted-types.ts`, installed in `main.ts`) admits, and a new library is checked for sinks under the e2e suite before it lands.
- No CDN, inline script or third-party resource (CSP rule in `security.md`): fonts, images and scripts come from `public/` or the build, and QR codes are inline SVG drawn by `shared/ui/qr-code` from the module matrix (the saved PNG goes through a `data:` image and a canvas), which the browser encodes itself with `qr-encoder.ts` (no library; the authenticator QR since 1.3, the payment QR from 1.4).
- Web as an app (plan v3 2.4, `docs/frontend-overview.md` `core/updates/`): Angular's `ngsw-worker.js`, registered only by `core/updates/angular-service-worker.ts` (`@angular/service-worker` is confined to it in `eslint-confinement.js`; the rest asks `AppUpdates` through `SERVICE_WORKER_UPDATES`). `ngsw-config.json` keeps the shell and, of the API, only answers that are the same for everyone (`security.md`, Browser): a new cached path joins `ServiceWorkerCachedAnswersTest`'s probes and its list of public handlers, which fail otherwise, and the anchored list of `core/updates/service-worker-bypass.ts`, without which the app sends it with `ngsw-bypass` (every other `/api` request does, because Angular matches a data group URL anywhere in the address). A new version loads by itself, with no button, only at a free moment (`AppUpdates`): before the first touch after the start, at the next navigation, or at once for a new API generation (once per generation and build), never while `DirtyForms.any()`, `RunningWork.any()`, a dialog is open or the app is offline; every form registers through `appFocusFirstInvalid` (its host directive `TrackedForm`), and a form without it carries `appTrackedForm`, so no half-typed text is lost to an update. A reload the app starts by itself goes through `ReloadGuard.claim` with its reason. The app's start address is `/aplikace` (`appEntryGuard`), and the manifest is a theme asset checked by `check-themes.mjs`. The built worker begins with the site's own payment route (`frontend/service-worker/payment-route.js`, prepended by the `postbuild` of `npm run build`, so build with `npm run build`, never `ng build` alone, for anything that ships or runs `e2e/pwa/`): it serves only a payment the page reserved by a message (`LocalPaymentRoute`), once, at `/platba.spayd?id=<id>` on the device, and any other code in the worker is a security change (`security.md`, Browser).
- Receipt PDF: drawn in the browser by `features/donations/receipt-pdf/` in a module worker (`receipt-pdf.worker.ts`, its own chunk); pdfkit only in `receipt-layout.ts`, and that file only in the worker and tests (`eslint-confinement.js`), so no other bundle carries it; the layout takes its colours, type sizes, leading and margins from `ReceiptStyle` and its fonts from the theme (it owns only the geometry of its blocks, as a component owns its structure), and every word from the frozen receipt or the captions passed in.
- Receipt verification (`/overit`, `features/donations/verification/`, `docs/frontend-overview.md`): the proof is read and checked in the browser, with WebCrypto `Ed25519` and, only where the browser lacks it, `@noble/curves` from `noble-ed25519.ts` through `import()` (a lazy chunk); the key list kept in `localStorage` is replaced whole by each trusted list, never merged; a code is read from the browser's `BarcodeDetector` or else jsQR, which only `qr-decoding.ts` imports and only the module worker `qr-decoder.worker.ts` loads (`eslint-confinement.js`), so it is in no page bundle; the camera (`Permissions-Policy camera=(self)`) starts only on the visitor's press and stops on a read, on stop, after two minutes and when the tab is hidden or the page is left; only the exact prefix `https://nfnn.cz/overit#` with digits is accepted, and nothing read is ever opened or navigated to.
- Device copies (`core/device/crypto/`, plan v3 2.13, `docs/security-overview.md` "Device copies"): only WebCrypto and the Argon2id worker; every key imported or generated is non-extractable, the root key lives only in an open `OpenCopy` and is zeroed by `close()`; every record and wrap is sealed with AES-GCM and the length-prefixed additional data of `framed`, so a new kind of record or wrap names its own label and fields there; hash-wasm only in `argon2.ts`, which only `argon2.worker.ts` loads (`eslint-confinement.js`), and the app derives a password key only through `WorkerPasswordKeyDerivation` with its parameters stored beside the wrap; no e-mail, name or user handle in the clear outside an open copy (the registry keeps tags); the core stores nothing itself (lint refuses it every browser store, `scripts/device-crypto-storage.test.mjs`); the PRF input is per device, never per copy, so one passkey ceremony opens every copy; a PRF output never leaves the device, so `PasskeyAuthenticator` keeps sending no `clientExtensionResults`. The store (`core/device/store/`, 2.14) is the only code that keeps a copy: idb only in `idb-device-storage.ts` (`eslint-confinement.js`), every change one transaction, the `DeviceVault` the only writer and reached by the session only through `SESSION_DEVICE` (`device-copies.ts` loads it lazily); a new kind of stored value goes through it, and its tests scan the raw storage for a canary (`testing/raw-scan.ts`).
- Browser storage names (IndexedDB databases, `localStorage` and `sessionStorage` keys, Web Lock and `BroadcastChannel` names) start with `nfnn.` (the older `nfnn-refresh` and `nfnn-session` stay as they are) and are never renamed, because a tab of the previous build and the data a device already holds use them.
- Sessions: access token in memory (`AuthStore`; after a refresh posted to the other tabs over the `BroadcastChannel` `nfnn-session`, never stored), refresh cookie set by the browser, `localStorage` flag `nfnn.donor-session` only says a session may exist (a "Cizí počítač" session keeps it in `sessionStorage` and `PublicSessionIdle` signs it out after an hour without input, plan v3 2.10); the installed app has its own lock, channel and flag (`core/auth/session-jar.ts`) and names its own refresh cookie with `Nfnn-Session: APP` on refresh and sign-out; nothing else adds auth headers.

### i18n
- Czech source: every visible string has `i18n="@@<area>.<page>.<key>"`, the first segment one of `home`, `content`, `donor`, `admin`, `operations`, `layout`, `ui` (follow the neighbouring component); attributes `i18n-alt`, `i18n-aria-label`, `i18n-placeholder`; TS text with `$localize\`:@@id:text\``. Ids are stable, never renamed. There is no translation file yet.
- The source locale is `cs` (`angular.json` `i18n.sourceLocale`, so the build carries the Czech locale data and `LOCALE_ID` is `cs`; specs get the same from `src/test-providers.ts`). Format through the cs-CZ helpers, one place per format, not Angular's locale pipes: `formatMinorUnits`/`formatCrowns` (`donation-amount.ts`), `formatDonationDate`/`formatBankTime`/`formatPragueClock` (`donation-date.ts`), `formatMonth`/`formatMonthRange` (`donation-month.ts`), `formatReportMonth` (`admin/report/report-month.ts`); ICU plurals use the Czech categories `one`, `few` and `other` (`=0` only for a sentence of its own); TypeScript picks a form with `pluralCategory(count)` (`core/i18n/plural-category.ts`, over `Intl.PluralRules('cs')`).
- Czech copy: no vocative or declension of names (show the name alone); sentence case; the donate action is "Přispět"; amounts from the API (hellers) go through `formatMinorUnits(minor)` (crowns typed by a visitor through `formatCrowns`), which gives `1 000` or `1 000,50`, followed by `&nbsp;Kč` in a template and `\u00a0Kč` in TS; dates `15. 3. 2026` and times with a date `15. 3. 2026 8:05`, without leading zeros except the minutes (ČSN 01 6910, Jakub 2026-11-06), the receipt PDF too; the day and month of a date stay together and the year may go to the next line (ÚJČ, ČSN 01 6910; Q13b of both design teams, 2026-11-16, after the largest text at 320 pt broke a date with no-break spaces inside its year): `formatDonationDate` and the date of `formatBankTime` join day and month with a no-break space (U+00A0) and put an ordinary space before the year, the time follows after an ordinary space where the line may break, a date in a short item ("ověřen 17. 10. 2026", a date cell of the administration, a date beside a gift or a connection in the donor's account outside the gifts table) carries `.ui-date` on its inline element and stays whole from `nav` up when it fits, and a range of dates keeps a no-break space before its dash, while the receipt (`formatReceiptDate`: the PDF and `/overit`) keeps the ordinary spaces of its frozen text and shared cases; bank times in Prague time; backend titles and details are shown as they come (already localized). A one-letter word (k, s, v, z, o, u, a, i) keeps the next word on its line by a no-break space (U+00A0: `&nbsp;` in a template, `\u00a0` in `$localize`; team decision M1′, #1486, #1512). The no-break space is required when that word has at most N letters, and before a longer word it may stay wherever the group fits the line. N and its reason are in `frontend/scripts/one-letter-word-limit.json` (`longestWordKeptWithAOneLetterWord`, today 6, `reason`: the intro heading of `/o-nas`, an h2 of the privacy policy at 320 pt and the card title of `/sprava` at 320, 900 and 1000 px are the narrowest lines): the largest number of letters at which the widest such group of the site's texts fits the narrowest line at 200 % text (paragraphs, headings, list items, table headers and cells, `data-label`, `ui-badge` and `ui-pill-button` on a phone 320 pt wide and in the administration's tables 900 and 1000 px wide), measured by `e2e/largest-text.spec.ts` ("Given the longest word a one-letter word keeps beside it…"), which fails when the measurement differs from the file. „1 Kč“ and a number with its unit, IČO with its number and a word with the dash after it stay together; a dash after a bold lead-in name (the list of rights in the privacy policy) may start the next line. Never U+2011 in a label or heading. The lint `scripts/portal-no-break-spaces.test.mjs` (`npm run lint`) checks one way only: every template and `$localize` text under `src/app` must have the no-break space before a word of at most N letters, and before a longer word it accepts either space, leaving the e2e at 200 % to keep a no-break space there from overflowing; it also refuses U+00A0 in a text read only by assistive technology (`aria-*`, `title`, `alt` and the `$localize` texts bound to them). It leaves out, each with its reason in `NOT_CHECKED` and `NOT_CHECKED_KINDS`, the browser tab titles and the lines of the downloaded backup key file, and the texts drawn into the receipt PDF (`*.pdf.*`), which change only with a look sheet and get the rule of their own in #1522. At 200 % text on a phone 320 and 390 px wide no group of a one-letter word overflows its line and no word breaks inside (`keptTogetherTooWide` of `e2e/kept-together.ts` in `e2e/accessibility-keyboard.spec.ts`). At the largest accessibility text (AX5, root 53 px) the check covers only the groups a one-letter word keeps by a no-break space: none of them may reach past the line of the element that holds it (`e2e/largest-text.spec.ts`, `oneLetterGroupsPastTheirLine`); every other overflow at AX5 is tracked in #1583. Only there may the safety net, `overflow-wrap: break-word` set on `html` in `styles.scss` and inherited by every text (it acts only where a line overflows and leaves min-content sizes alone) and on both faces of the busy label (`busy-label.scss`), break a group wider than its line, even a short one; it never permits breaking a word at ordinary enlarged text. Specs compare a component's text through `withOrdinarySpaces` (`src/testing/ordinary-spaces.ts`), so only the lint checks the no-break spaces, and a bold reserve filled from `data-label` keeps the label's width (`boldReservesApartFromTheirLabel`, at 390 and 1280 px).
- Names in running text (#1254, #1306): a button, card, section or choice named inside a sentence stands in Czech quotes („Přidat účet“, „Firma“, „Bankovní účty“) with the full stop after the closing quote; a name that is itself a link (a routerLink such as Moje dary) takes none; an iOS or macOS element is named literally as the system has it („Sdílet“, „Přidat na plochu“, „Soubor“, „Přidat do Docku“); the donor's portal says „v kartě“, never „na kartě“. A device's label inside a sentence comes from `deviceNameInSentence` (`devices-card/device-labels.ts`), whose fallback words are in lower case („Odhlásit neznámé zařízení?“), while the list keeps „Neznámé zařízení“ as its title.

### UI (the look)
- The look is a swappable theme, independent of behaviour (Jakub 2026-10-23, plan `/mnt/project-files/nfnn-ux/oddeleni-vzhledu.md`). The theme owns every value of the look; a component owns only its structure (which grid, flex or order, which widths of its own content, which token for which part). Replacing the theme must restyle the whole site without touching a template, a component's SCSS or any TypeScript.
- A theme is a folder `src/themes/<name>/` with `_theme.scss` (its `@font-face` rules and every token on `:root`, plus at most rules that restyle the `ui-` blocks), its fonts, `assets/` (the files of its look, served at the site root by its build configuration with `output: "/"`: the tab icons `favicon.svg` and `favicon.ico`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `app-icon.svg`, `launch-icon.svg` (the star without a tile, from which the launch screen and the iOS launch images are drawn), `images/logo.svg` and `images/logo-on-dark.svg`; every theme carries the same file names and none of them is also in `public/`) and `receipt/` (the receipt PDF's TrueType fonts `body.<hash>.ttf`, `body-bold.<hash>.ttf` and `heading.<hash>.ttf` with their licence, made by `scripts/make-receipt-fonts.sh`, which also writes their names into the theme's `--receipt-font-*` tokens; `angular.json` copies them to `/receipt-fonts/`, the control theme's under its configuration; the `--receipt-color-*` tokens are literal `#rrggbb`); `styles.scss` loads it as `@use 'theme'` from the include path, so the build picks the theme: `nova-nadeje` by default, `control` with the configuration `control-theme` (`ng build --configuration=production,control-theme`, `ng serve --configuration=control-theme`). The control theme is deliberately different (teal, system fonts, square corners, 5 px spacing unit) and exists only to prove the swap: CI builds it and `E2E_THEME=control npx playwright test e2e/theme.spec.ts` opens every public page, the sign-in dialog, every `/darce` section and every `/sprava` page and fails on any colour or font of `nova-nadeje` that the control theme does not share. A new token goes into every theme in the same PR: `npm run lint:styles` runs `scripts/check-themes.mjs`, which fails when a theme misses a token the styles use or `nova-nadeje` sets, when a receipt colour is not a literal `#rrggbb` or its text or QR colour falls below 4.5:1 on white, and when a receipt font token names a file that is missing or whose content does not match the hash in its name, or a font sits in `receipt/` that no token names, or a `--receipt-*` token is set outside the theme's top-level `:root` block (a media query would make the receipt depend on the device), when a tab bar colour is not literal or is set anywhere but the top-level `:root` or a `@media` block of `:root`, its labels fall below 4.5:1 (the focus ring 3:1, against the bar and the pill) over a white or a black page, or a solid pill below 3:1 against a solid bar, and when the themes' `assets/` differ, shadow a file of `public/` or a build serves another theme's. A new page gets a line in `e2e/theme.spec.ts`.
- Tokens live only in the theme (`src/themes/nova-nadeje/_theme.scss`) on `:root`: colour by role (`--color-accent*` (`--color-accent` for text, links, outlines and tints, `--color-accent-fill` for filled buttons and selected states), `-text*`, `-surface*` (`-surface-grouped` the ground of the app areas, `-surface-elevated` dialogs and sheets), `-border*`, `-separator` for hairlines between rows, `-fill`, `-media-rim`, `-focus-ring`, `-danger*`, `-warning*`, `-success*`, `-badge-*`, `-tile-*`, `-on-*`), fonts (`--font-serif` Playfair, `--font-sans` the system face then Inter, the roles `--font-display`, `--font-title`, `--font-figures` and `--font-mono` for logs, key ids and codes typed by hand only, `--numeric-figures`, `--figure-features`, `--numeric-titles` for proportional lining figures in titles: the serif's old-style figures would leave a "2025" looking like running text), type size in `rem` (`--text-2xs` 11 px … `--text-6xl` 44 px at the default 16 px, and fluid roles `--text-heading`, `-section-title`, `-section-title-compact`, `-cta-title`, `-article-title`, `-page-title`, `-hero`), weight (`--weight-regular` … `-extrabold`), line height (`--leading-none`, `-display`, `-heading`, `-snug`, `-normal`, `-relaxed`, `-loose`), letter spacing (`--tracking-tight`, `-slight`, `-wide`, `-widest`), spacing (`--space-<n>` = n × `--space-unit` 4 px, half steps `-0-5`, `-1-5` …, so a theme changes density with one value), shape (`--radius-*`, `--radius-group` for the cells of the app areas, `--radius-qr-ground` the white ground of the payment QR inside its frame, concentric with it: `--radius-card` less the frame's 2 px border and `--space-2-5` padding, 4 px, about one module of the smallest drawn QR and so well inside its 4-module quiet zone), depth (`--shadow-*`, one per role: `--shadow-card` for public cards, `--shadow-group` for the flat cells of the app areas), motion (`--duration-fast`, `-base`, `-slow`, `-entrance`), layout (`--content-max`, `--content-max-wide` (1440 px from `bp.up(wide)`; text measures such as the lead's `40rem`, an article's `70ch` and the titles' `18ch` and `22ch` keep theirs), `--header-height`, `--page-top` (the top of a page's first section), `--floating-notice-height`, `--side-nav-width`, `--dialog-max-width`, `-wide` (in `rem`, so a dialog grows with the text; the form sheet and the administration's lock panel cap theirs at the width less `--space-5` on each side), `--sheet-form-height` (the fixed height of a `[tall]` form sheet from `tablet` to `nav`), `--dialog-close-inset`, `-size`, `--back-button-size`, `--scroll-edge-fade` (the height of the shade at the bottom edge of a dialog that holds more below), `--scroll-shade-amount` (the share of `--color-text` in the shade at the bottom edge of a dialog that holds more below: 14 %, under `prefers-contrast: more` 30 % in `nova-nadeje` and 21 % in `control`, whose muted text stays as it is and would fall below 4.5:1 at more; `check-themes.mjs` measures `--color-text-muted` on the shade's strongest point over `--color-surface-elevated` and `--color-sheet-grouped` in every appearance, at least 4.5:1), `--touch-target-size`, `--list-row-min-height`, `--list-group-title-top`), blur (`--blur-backdrop`, `--blur-bar`, `--bar-saturate`), the app layout of plan v3 2.1 (the floating tab bar: `--text-tab-label` (the labels' size, the smaller of `--text-2xs` and what four labels in one row allow in the bar's width, in `cqi` of its container), `--color-tab-bar`, `-bar-rim`, `-pill`, `-pill-rim`, `-active`, `--color-on-tab-bar`, `--color-tab-focus-ring`, `--shadow-tab-bar`, `--tab-bar-height`, `--tab-bar-rendered-height` (the theme's height, replaced on the root by the bar's measured one), `-gap`, `-gap-browser`, `--tab-pill-inset`, `--tab-icon-size` in `rem`, each colour and `--shadow-tab-bar` with an `-on-dark` twin for the dark glass over photos and dark heads, solid under `prefers-reduced-transparency`; the sheet: `--sheet-max-width`, `--radius-sheet`, `--shadow-sheet`, `--duration-sheet`, `--color-sheet-grabber`, `--sheet-grabber-width`, `-height`; no top bar, Jakub 2026-11-05), the receipt PDF (`--receipt-*`: colours, sizes in `pt`, `--receipt-leading`, margins in `mm`, read by `features/donations/receipt-pdf/receipt-style.ts`, whose list `check-themes.mjs` requires in every theme).
- Breakpoints only through `@use 'breakpoints' as bp;` (`src/styles/_breakpoints.scss`, on the include path): `@include bp.down(phone)` is `max-width`, `bp.up(cards)` is `min-width` one pixel above; names `tabs` 208 (only for size containers, 13 rem: the attention list of `/sprava` and the bank accounts card), `pair` 260 (only for containers: the narrowest that still holds two cards or signatures side by side, so enlarged text gets one column), `pager` 312 (only for containers: the pager's, both labelled steps and the position side by side at the smallest text, with room to spare; and the news card's, above which its tag keeps one line), `tiny` 360, `narrow` 420, `phone` 480 (dialogs full-screen), `amounts` 520 (container width under which Přispět's amounts stand two by two), `figures` 551 (container width under which the hero's figures stand two by two instead of four in a row, never three and one, #1605), `cards` 640, `tablet` 768, `ledger` 864 (the one fold of every table: a `ui-table` in a `.ui-table-frame` and the donor's gift table become cards in a container up to this, `bp.container-down(ui-table, ledger)`, in `rem`, so enlarged text folds them sooner), `nav` 899 (side panel collapses), `laptop` 1100 (end of Přispět's 2fr/3fr columns from `nav`; as the size container `donate-band`, the width under which the Přispět band puts its photo above its text; as the size container `stories`, the width of the strip of the year's stories above which a tile's tag keeps one line), `wide` 1599 (`bp.up(wide)` from 1600 px: the wide content width `--content-max-wide`, the page header's row and its back button, Pomáháme, O nás (its intro, the founders, the Přispět band and the contact section; the intro photo then follows the text's height instead of a square, so a wider photo leaves no hole beside the text), the privacy policy (the text stays `70ch` on the header's left axis), the 404 page (only its left edge, onto the logo) and `/overit` (with the three ways it takes the whole wide width and its intro keeps the measure of the former 960 px column; a result keeps that column's width, a single card that would only stretch, but starts on the header's left axis too, so both states share it; from `nav` below `wide` both states keep that column's width and start on the header's left axis of `--content-max`, as the 404 page and the privacy policy do, #1212); an iPad 13" in landscape, 1366 pt, stays below it; nothing of the tab bar, the sheets or `CompactLayout` asks it, and the width switches without a transition). A new width is a new name there, never a raw `@media`, and the same name and width in `core/layout/breakpoints.ts` (`scripts/breakpoints-parity.test.mjs` fails otherwise). Code that must choose a layout asks `CompactLayout.isCompact()` (`core/layout/compact-layout.ts`, `bp.down(nav)`) and renders one layout with `@if`, never two hidden by CSS; a list that is a table on wide screens asks `CompactLayout.listsAsRows()` instead (also true where the window is narrower than `nav` in the root text size, so a table that would not fit enlarged text becomes rows, plan v3 2.25). Where a layout must give way when the visitor enlarges the text, `bp.container-down(<container>, name)` and `bp.container-up` ask a size container the same width in `rem` of the root text size: the payment panel (`payment-panel`) and the administration's months (`admin-overview`) keep their viewport query and add `container-down(…, phone)`, the donor's table of gifts (`donations-table`) asks its container alone (above), and the yearly receipts of Potvrzení stack in their card (`yearly-receipts`) up to `cards`, so at the default text size nothing changes and at a large one they stack (`e2e/devices/`). A container is a containing block for its fixed descendants, so it goes on a component's own box, never on a shell that holds a fixed bar.
- `npm run lint:styles` (in CI) rejects in components a raw colour, font, font size, weight, line height, letter spacing, radius, shadow, a `px` spacing other than a 1 px hairline (`padding`, `margin`, `gap`), a transition duration and a `width` media query. A missing value is a new token in the theme (pick the nearest existing one first; a value used once is not a reason for a token), never an inline value or a lint exception.
- Shared building blocks are global classes in `src/styles/ui/` (prefix `ui-`, BEM modifiers, loaded by `styles.scss` after the form controls): `_page` (`.ui-page`, `-head`, `-title`, `-intro`, `.ui-crumbs`; from `nav` every `/sprava` page takes one of two widths (#1220): `.ui-page--full` fills the side navigation's column, so it ends on the avatar's axis (Přehled, Platby, Dárci and a donor, Aktuality, Pomáháme, Hromadná potvrzení, Správci, Odpovědi o starších symbolech, Loga bank, Lidé fondu, Historie změn, Všechny zálohy, a log file), and `.ui-page--form` holds its content (`content-box`, the page's padding outside it) to `--page-form-max`, 45 rem, so it grows with the text up to the column, and its wide fields fill their cards while short ones (a time, a day, a month) stay short (Nastavení, Logy and its pages but the file, the editors, the two photos, Měsíční e-mail and a sent report, Zálohy, Upozornění, Ověřit potvrzení, Občas a pro technika); below `nav` both keep the margin of `.ui-page`), `_card` (`.ui-card`, flat with `--radius-group`, `--shadow-group` and `--color-group-cell` inside `.ui-grouped`, the class of `DonorShell` on `/darce` and `/sprava`, where `--color-surface-alt` points to `--color-grouped-alt` and `--color-divider` to `--color-group-divider`, and inside a `[grouped]` dialog, `--danger`, `--padded` for a card whose content sits on its padding (its direct `__title` keeps `--space-3` below it and its last child no bottom margin, both at the lowest specificity, so a component's own rule wins; a direct `__intro` that ends the card also loses the margin the padded card gives an intro, #1324), `__title` (one size on every card, Jakub 2026-11-14, #851), `__subtitle`, `__bar` for a heading strip over a table or list (a trailing control beside the title, the bar laying them out itself), `__aside` for a text button in the accent beside a card's title that reaches the finger size (the disclosure "Jak přiřazujeme platby?" of Bankovní účty), `__body`, `__intro` (at most `--measure-prose`, 65ch, left-aligned, as the portal's page leads `.page-intro` and `.receipts-intro`, `.ui-group-note` and every other paragraph of the donor area, each in its own component, the payment panel's sentences included (#1211, #1221); a paragraph with a tinted box of its own, as `.yearly-empty`, keeps the box and stops its text through its end padding, as the footer does), `__empty`, `__footer` (`--status` and `--alert` colour a line of `app-live-notice look="footer"`; right under `__bar` it has no separator of its own; its text stops at `--measure-prose` through its end padding, so its hairline still spans the card); `.ui-data-list` for a `dl` of label and value), `_notice` (`.ui-notice--status|--alert|--warning`, `--floating` for a bar fixed at the bottom of the screen with `__text` beside its button (the page keeps room for it through `:root.has-floating-notice` and the layout token `--floating-notice-height`, `0rem` in the theme and measured by the component that shows it), `.ui-load-state` `--error`, `--inset`, `.ui-badge--success|--warning|--danger|--accent|--neutral`, `.ui-mono`, `.ui-callout--danger|--warning` for a framed message inside a card; `.ui-notice` and `.ui-callout` take `--radius-lg`; a `.ui-cta` inside a notice keeps `line-height: normal` instead of the notice's, so a link there is as tall as a button (#1624); a floating notice appears through `motion.appear` and leaves through `motion.leave`, its templates naming `animate.leave="ui-notice--leaving"`), `_figures` (`.ui-figures` for every amount, account number, IBAN, variable symbol and receipt number, never wrapping inside; `--groups` wraps only between its child spans; `.ui-date` on the inline element that holds a date in a short item, such as "ověřen 17. 10. 2026" or a date cell of the administration, makes it an inline block from `nav` up, so a date that fits stays whole on one line and one that does not breaks only before its year or time, never past its cell even with Dynamic Type or a larger default text in a wide window (the mobile team's condition, 2026-11-16, instead of `nowrap`, which a `.ui-card` with `overflow: hidden` would cut off; `e2e/largest-text.spec.ts` checks 1180 pt with a root of 34 and 53 px); a range gives each date its own `.ui-date`, so it may break after the dash), `_ink` (`.ui-ink` for an uploaded picture of ink on white, a signature: the theme's `--filter-ink` and `--blend-ink`, so in the dark the ink turns light and the white disappears), `_button` (`.ui-button` soft accent, `--primary`, `--small` (the one small button: `--button-small-min-height`, `--button-small-padding` and `--text-button-small` in the theme, which `.ui-pill-button` shares, so a small tinted button and a pill are equally tall and set in one size; every small button of the donor's portal is one of the two, the PDF button of a gift row too, #1603), `--quiet`, `--outline` (accent text on `--color-surface` with the quiet outline `--color-border`, for the step a visitor is expected to take on a tinted card, as "Zkusit znovu" of the payment card's failure, #1427), `--large` beside a primary, `--danger`, `--danger-soft`, `--danger-outline`, `--block` for a full-width form submit, `--grouped` for a full-width button of a `[grouped]` dialog drawn as a row of its cards (`--color-sheet-group`, accent text, `--sheet-row-height`), with `--primary` the filled capsule of the sheet; `.ui-link-button`; every `.ui-button` and `.ui-link-button` reaches a finger on a touch screen by itself (`touch-target.reaches-finger-size`), every variant has the same 1.5 px border (transparent on the filled ones), so an outlined and a filled button of one size are equally tall, and the filled `.ui-button--primary` and `.ui-cta` hover by colour (`--color-accent-fill-hover`), never by lifting; `.ui-pill-button` with `--done`, the small outlined pill of `copy-button` and `share-button`; one row never mixes the tinted button and the pill, so a copy button in a row of tinted buttons takes `look="tinted"`, as „Kopírovat“ beside „Zkusit znovu“ in the attention list of `/sprava`, #1560, and beside „Stáhnout jako soubor“ of the backup key (`backup-key-panel`); a secondary date line of the portal (the devices, the connected applications) is `--text-sm` at the regular weight, as `.ui-list__meta`, and the device data's state line with „aktualizováno“ keeps the card intro's size at the regular weight, as every card intro does (`e2e/donor-details-intros.spec.ts`), #1603), `_table` (`.ui-table` directly inside a `.ui-table-frame`, the size container `ui-table` (#1547): `.ui-card` is the container `ui-card`, tables measure `ui-table` on their frame, and a card never takes its width from its content (12/12 A, 2026-11-21, in place of #1561's ban), and `scripts/table-frames.test.mjs` in `npm run lint` keeps every table of the app in its frame, the donor's gift table excepted, whose component box is its own container; the table becomes cards in a frame up to `ledger` (`bp.container-down(ui-table, ledger)`, one breakpoint for every table, Dárci and the administration's overview included, whose frame reaches over the padded card's inset so it measures the whole card) and wherever `.ui-table--stacked` asks, as the donor card's parts do in rows, instantly, without a transition; `.ui-table--compact` narrows the cells' inline padding to `--space-3` in the unfolded table, the first and last cell keeping `--card-inset`, for the wide tables of five or six columns at a computer width (Platby and Starší symboly, #1564, #1569); the first and last cell of a row, and a card's row in the folded form, start `--card-inset` from the card's edge, the theme token the card's title, body and bar take too (#1213); a button in a cell may wrap its text and never grows past the cell, so no action is clipped by the card in either form, at 200 % text too (#1548, #1333); a link in a cell has one look, the accent colour, semibold, underlined only under a hovering pointer (#1568); `e2e/tables-in-card.spec.ts` checks at 900, 1000 and 1280 px that every table and every button or link in it lies inside its card and that the folded table still has the roles `table` and `cell`; each card cell shows its column's name from `data-label`, out of the accessibility tree (`/ ''`), and every table element in a template names its role (`role="table"`, `rowgroup`, `row`, `columnheader`, `cell`), because a table laid out as blocks is no table for WebKit, so a screen reader would hear a cell without its column; `scripts/table-roles.test.mjs` in `npm run lint` keeps it), `_form` (`.ui-field-error` under a form, `.ui-segmented` choice of two or three buttons with `.is-active`, `.ui-form-status` for the saved line after a form, `.ui-field-group` for the fields of a `[grouped]` dialog as one inset card of iOS settings: `--color-sheet-group`, `--radius-sheet-group`, each `app-form-field` a row with its label inside in `--color-sheet-secondary` (`--color-danger` while the field is invalid), the control borderless on the card and hairlines in `--color-sheet-separator` that start at the text; a focused field draws no ring of its own, its whole row does, 3 px of `--color-focus-ring` inside the row (`outline-offset: -3px`, `Highlight` in forced colours, never animated) with `--radius-sheet-group` on the first and last row, so the ring no longer crosses the label (a browser without `:has()` keeps the field's own ring); the sign-in dialog), `_action-bar` (`.ui-action-bar`: the sticky bar at the bottom of the viewport that ends a long form, with `appActionBar` from `shared/ui/action-bar` so `scroll-padding-bottom` keeps the focused field above it), `_dialog` (inside `<app-dialog>`: `.ui-dialog__title` for its `h2`, `__lead` for the sentence under it, `__points` for the list of what a confirmation does, `__actions` for its row of buttons, sticky at the bottom of a sheet so they stay in reach, and a field the focus moves to under it is scrolled above it), `_offer-sheet` (`.ui-offer-sheet` on an `<app-dialog>` that offers notifications: centred, `__icon` the bell tile, `--on` its success colours, `__actions` the buttons stacked full width; the administrators' and the donors' offer), `_list` (`.ui-list` of rows `__row`, an `a` or `button`, one target per row with a second action as its own named button beside it, never inside: `__lead`, `__title`, `__meta`, `__value` with tabular figures, `__count` for a count pill beside the title (`aria-hidden`, its description visually hidden; `--warning` amber for what waits on an administrator), `__chevron` with `aria-hidden`, `__group-title` a sticky heading, `--inset` for grouped rows inside a sheet (since #851 only the dialogs and sheets of the administration that match the account sheet: the other device of `/sprava/upozorneni`, the level of a logger and a log entry; every page is on the donor cards, Jakub 2026-11-14: `--color-group-cell`, rows at least `--list-inset-row-min-height` on `--space-2` of block padding with titles and values in `--weight-list-inset-title`, the iOS 2.765 rem and regular weight, variant B (a count pill keeps its weight); a `__lead` at least `--list-lead-size` wide with its icon centred, so titles line up whatever the icon; separators in `--color-separator` (darkened under `prefers-contrast: more`) that start at the title, past `--list-lead-size` when both rows lead with an icon; a list has an icon on every row or on none: `.ui-list__tile` with `--accent`, `--success`, `--warning`, `--danger` (neutral without one) is a coloured square for a state, `.ui-list__glyph` an accent icon for a section, both `--list-lead-size` wide inside `__lead`; a disabled `button` row is muted, `GrayText` in forced colours, and gets no hover tint; an `--inset` group whose rows cannot be `li` marks each row `.ui-list__item` to get the same separators; `__chevron--open` turns a disclosure row's chevron up; `--grouped` for the inset grouped cards of iOS settings on a `[grouped]` dialog's `--color-sheet-grouped` (`--color-sheet-group`, `--radius-sheet-group`, dividers that start at the title, regular titles; the account sheet), with `__row--danger` for a destructive row whose title stands centred in `--color-danger`; `__state` for a badge under `__value`, `__extra` for what spans the row under the title (a receipt download, a summary); a row without `__meta` stands on one line, and rows inside a `.ui-card` take its padding (`--list-row-padding-inline`, `--space-6` set on the card, which a part that lines up with the rows uses too; a list that is a direct child of a card, and a `.ui-card__group` (a titled group of rows inside a card: its `h3.ui-card__subtitle` lined up with the rows over its `.ui-list`, a separator between groups; the sendings of a sent report), is the size container `list-card`, and below `pair` (16.25 rem, so at large text sizes too) the property drops to `--space-4` for its rows, a glyph lead takes only its icon's width and the gaps beside the glyph and the chevron shrink to `--space-2` and a `__value` (not in a row with a `__state` or a switch) moves under the title and its meta, starting where they start and wrapping, so a long value stays inside the row (#848: "Úrovně logování" beside "1 dočasně"), and in a row with both a `__value` and a `__state` the value and then the state each take a line of their own under the title and its meta, starting where they start (each month of `/sprava/platby` is a `.ui-card__group`, so its rows are in a `list-card` container too: before, at 200 % on 320 pt the amount beside the title broke the sender's name inside its words), so a word in a narrow card keeps whole, with room for wider fonts); `.ui-list__row--action` (a next-step link or an action button row) is in the accent outside `--inset` too; `__lead--blank` is an empty lead of `--list-lead-size` for a next step under a row with a tile, so its title lines up under the title above, and the divider before it starts at the title; the lists of `CompactLayout.listsAsRows()` since 2.25), `_group` (grouped rows inside the administration's sheets that match the account sheet; every page moved to the donor cards, Jakub 2026-11-14, #851: `.ui-group-title` the small upper-case heading above a group of rows in a sheet, `.ui-group-note` the footnote under it with `--status` and `--alert`, which `app-live-notice look="note"` uses in a sheet; a `label.ui-list__row` row holding `.ui-switch`, a native `input type="checkbox" role="switch"` drawn as the iOS switch from the `--switch-*` and `--color-switch-*` tokens (its track changes colour through `motion.state(background-color)`, its thumb moves through `motion.move(translate, base, spring)` without an overshoot; under `prefers-reduced-motion` the thumb jumps at once and only the track's colour fades in `--duration-reduced`, decision E1), its description in `__meta` named by `aria-describedby`; `__row--action` takes `--color-accent` in an `--inset` list too), `_person-list` (`.ui-person-list` of `.ui-person-row`: `__avatar` on an `app-avatar`, `__name`, `__meta` for the line under the name, `__badges`, `__note` as a sibling of the text block under it, across the columns beside the portrait, so the portrait centres on the name, the line under it and the badges only, `__actions` at the right of that first row and under the row up to `cards`, `__detail` across the row for an inline confirmation; the people of the foundation and the administrators), `_panel` (a standalone page such as the password reset: `.ui-panel-page` around `.ui-panel`, `__title`, `__lead`; `.ui-status` with `__icon` (`--success` green, `--caution` amber, `--danger` red for a verdict; the older `--warning` is red too, so an amber warning takes `--caution`), `--spinner` for a result or a wait), `_glass` (`.ui-glass`, the one chrome material of the tab bar and the round back, account and close buttons, with `--on-dark` over photos and dark grounds: the circles by the route's `head`, the tab bar by its `darkThroughout`), `_section` (the public site: `.ui-container` for the content width of a section, `.ui-eyebrow` above a section title inside a page (never above a page's `h1`) with `--accent-on-inverse` on a dark background, `.ui-footnote` for a grey note under a group like an iOS group footer, its link in the accent, with `--on-inverse` on a dark band (the privacy notes of Kontakt and Přispět), `.ui-footnote` for a grey note under a group like an iOS group footer, its link in the accent, with `--on-inverse` on a dark band (the privacy notes of Kontakt and Přispět), `.ui-intro` for the header that opens every tab and every page under it (O nás, Aktuality, Pomáháme, Přispět, a news or project page, the privacy policy, the 404): no ground of its own, so the page's `--color-surface` runs from the top in both appearances, its top at `--page-top` up to `tablet` and `--space-10` lower above it, with `__head` (what holds the title and lead, where on short screens they stand side by side; every page but O nás, whose head sits beside its photo), `__meta` (a small line of data above a title, the tag and date of an article or "Chyba 404"), `__title` (`--text-page-title`, 34 px on a phone as an iOS large title, `--article` for a news or project title) and `__lead` (experts' decision with Jakub's mandate, 2026-11-12, `docs/requirements.md`); a page with a side column puts `.ui-intro` on its grid or flex row beside `.ui-container` and keeps only its bottom padding, `.ui-cta` for a large call to action with `--ghost` and, on top of it, `--on-inverse` or `--on-image`; every variant has the same `--cta-border-width` border (transparent on the filled one) and the bold label, so a filled and a ghost CTA side by side are equally tall and their labels share a baseline and a weight, #1606), `_skeleton` (`.ui-skeleton` on a `.ui-card`, or `--inset` inside one, with `__line` bars one `em` high, a line's height apart, in `--color-skeleton`, a translucent `--color-fill` darkened under `prefers-contrast: more` in both appearances, `__line--title`, breathing through `motion.pulse` without it under reduced motion; `.ui-skeleton-reveal` for the content that replaces it), `_pager` (the paging row at the end of Pomáháme and Aktuality, like an iOS toolbar: `.ui-pager` is a three-column grid and a size container `pager`, `__step` a `.ui-button` link at an edge, `--newer` in the first column and `--older` in the last, each with an inline chevron `__icon` (`aria-hidden`) beside its `__label`, and `__position` "Strana N z M" centred in the middle column; a direction that does not exist is left out while its column stays, so the position stays in the middle; below the container width `pager` the steps become 44 px round chevrons with the label visually hidden, so the row never wraps on one side, and the position may balance onto two lines). A page, card, notice, button or table uses them in its template and never copies their declarations; its SCSS keeps only layout and the rare override (a margin, a narrower width). An element keeps its own semantic class beside the `ui-` ones where specs or layout need a hook (`class="ui-button ui-button--primary profile-save"`). A look used in two features becomes a new block there, not a copy. Behaviour with a template stays an Angular component in `shared/ui/`.
- An icon that stands beside a label and should grow with it takes `--icon-beside-text-size` (`min(1.5em, 2rem)`) for its width and height: the `em` resolves on the icon, so the icon carries the text size of its label, capped at `2rem`. Today it sizes the „?“ disc of „Jak přiřazujeme platby?“ in `bank-accounts-card` (the mark inside keeps `--text-xs`), the signed-in devices' icons in `devices-card` and the icon of „Přidat na plochu“ in the account sheet (#1293).
- Values (#1553, #1554, #1595, #1597; both teams 12/12): the same kind of value has one look in the portal and the administration, and `e2e/value-looks.spec.ts` checks each. An amount in a row of a table or a data list (Platby, Dárci, the donor card, the overview and its balance, Hromadná potvrzení, Moje dary) carries `.ui-amount` (`_figures`): the figures of `.ui-figures` at `--text-md`, `--weight-semibold` and `--color-text`, on the element that holds the amount: a `.ui-table` cell that holds only the amount may carry it itself (`.ui-table td.ui-amount` outranks the table's cell colour), otherwise a span inside the cell, because a component's own cell rule outranks a global class (the overview's cells keep their secondary colour only `:not(.ui-amount)`); a headline figure (Celkem darováno, the last gift, a year's total) and a list's `.ui-list__value` keep their own size. A status is one `.ui-badge` with its tone (`_notice`): the administration's own `.tag`, `.held-tag`, `.refunded-tag`, `.receipt-tag` and `.answer-tag` builds became it and keep only their class as a hook for layout and specs; the neutral and warning fills are `--color-badge-neutral-bg` and `--color-badge-warning-bg`, a tint of the badge's own text colour, so the pill stands out from any card in light and dark. A badge wraps (`white-space: normal`), never shrinks in a flex row (`flex-shrink: 0`) and is never wider than its container (`max-inline-size: 100%`); in a table that is not stacked it is as wide as its one-line text (`inline-size: max-content`, `_table.scss`), so a column cannot squeeze it onto two lines (the answers of `/sprava/starsi-symboly` and every tag at a computer width stay one line), while a stacked table resets it to `auto`, because a `max-content` width would also be the badge's narrowest width and hold its grid row wider than the card; so it breaks only when it does not fit even on a line of its own, so a short one stays one line and a long one („jen s variabilním symbolem“, „zrušeno, dary se změnily“) stays within its card at 200 % and the largest text; its corners are `--radius-badge`, which in nova-nadeje is half the height of a one-line badge (`calc(0.5lh + var(--space-0-75))`), so one line is the full pill and two lines become a rounded box instead of a stadium whose ends would cut into the text, and in the control theme `--radius-pill`. `.ui-mono` and `--font-mono` are only for a value read or typed character by character (a key id, a code such as the second-factor recovery message, a logger, a file name, a log line), never a name or a sentence: a connected application's name and the 1 Kč verification message are in the text face.
- Translucency with `color-mix()`, never `rgba()`. Fonts self-hosted variable WOFF2; nothing loads from another origin.
- Touch like a native app (#380, #385; `docs/frontend-overview.md`, Touch): no tap flash, and controls (`a[href]`, `button`, `summary`, `[role="button"]`, `[role="tab"]`) are neither selectable nor draggable and have no long-press callout, while content text, the bank details, fields and the classless links of running text keep selection and the system menu (`src/styles/_controls.scss`). Every enabled control shows a pressed state on `:active` at once, through `@use 'pressed'` (`when-pressed` or `look` with `tints`, `darkens` or `shrinks`, so `forced-colors` and `prefers-reduced-motion` keep holding) and the theme's pressed tokens (`--pressed-tint-amount`, the share of `currentColor` in the tint, `--pressed-brightness`, `--pressed-scale`); `PressedOnTouch` keeps the touch listener Safari needs for it. Hover looks stand only inside `@media (hover: hover)`, which `npm run lint:styles` enforces (`nfnn/hover-only-with-hover`), so a tap leaves no hover behind.
- Tables become cards by the width of their card, at `bp.container-down(ui-table, ledger)` (`data-label` per cell); fluid sizes are `clamp()` tokens; motion follows "Motion" under Theme, fonts, assets, and under `prefers-reduced-motion` movement becomes an instant change or a short fade (the global rule in `styles.scss` plus the `motion` mixins).
- Short screens (a phone on its side, an iPhone SE): heights are named like widths, in `$heights` of `_breakpoints.scss` with `bp.height-down(name)` and in `HEIGHTS` of `core/layout/breakpoints.ts` (`short` 500, `hero-lead` 680; the parity test covers both maps), never a raw `max-height` query; code asks `ShortViewport.isShort()` (`core/layout/short-viewport.ts`) and renders with `@if`. A full-screen block is at least `100svh` (Safari's `100vh` is the viewport with its toolbar retracted, so it scrolls) and grows with its content; its text keeps `max(<space>, env(safe-area-inset-left|right))` beside it as `.ui-container` does, the header's room above and `--tab-bar-clearance` under it. Safari paints the area outside the page (under its floating toolbar) with the `body`'s background, not the `html`'s, so a page that wants that strip dark colours `body`. Text never runs over the face in a photo: the landing hero measures it in `e2e/first-screen.spec.ts`; on short screens `.ui-intro__head` stands its title and lead side by side and the hero shrinks its headline (`--text-hero-short`) and only visually hides its lead.
- Windows of an iPad (#396): in a window smaller than the screen the iPadOS window controls float over the top-leading corner of the home-screen app and WebKit reports no inset for them, so `WindowControls` (`core/layout/window-controls.ts`) marks the root `has-window-controls` and content at that corner keeps `max(<its own space>, var(--window-controls-inset-inline))` beside it (the header, the back button, the skip link) or `var(--window-controls-inset-block)` above it (the sticky side panel); a new fixed or sticky control at the top-leading corner does the same (`e2e/window-controls.spec.ts`).
- Images: everything that can be SVG is SVG (Jakub 2026-10-30: the logo, the tab icon `favicon.svg`, icons, drawings); raster only where no SVG can do (photos, and where a platform takes only raster: `og-image.jpg`, `apple-touch-icon.png`, `favicon.ico` at the default address, unlinked, for services such as claude.ai's connector list, and the iOS launch images, which the build draws from the theme's `launch-icon.svg`, never kept as files). Photos are WebP in `public/images/` (except `og-image.jpg` for link previews); the logo, `logo.svg` and `logo-on-dark.svg` for a dark background, is a theme asset in `src/themes/<name>/assets/images/`, served at `/images/…`, and its star keeps its face, since a filter that whitens the logo would whiten the face too; photos come in sized variants with `srcset`/`sizes`, `width`/`height`, `loading="lazy"` written before `src` below the fold, `fetchpriority="high"` for the hero, decorative photos `alt=""`; icons inline SVG `aria-hidden="true" stroke="currentColor"`; no emoji in UI text.
- Budgets in `angular.json`: the initial bundle warns at 575 kB and fails the build, and so the `frontend` job, at 600 kB (#998; 560 kB once the sign-in dialog became lazy, of which Angular, RxJS and the global styles are about 470 kB), component styles warn at 4 kB. What only one dialog or page needs stays out of the initial bundle: `App` imports the sign-in dialog only when it first opens (below, `login-dialog/`).

### UX (how it behaves)
- Offline (plan v3 2.7, `docs/frontend-overview.md` `core/connectivity/`): whether the app is offline comes only from `Connectivity`, which judges the API's answers and its own probe, and trusts the browser only when it says offline (`navigator.onLine` false, which a phone in airplane mode reports while its requests hang; Jakub 2026-11-06), never when it says online; `browserSaysOffline()` serves the error reporter and the chunk-load recovery. Nothing waits on the network when `Connectivity` says offline: a public page shows the service worker's copy at once and is refreshed behind it (`keptAnswerRefreshInterceptor`), and the session restore gives up at once. Every control that needs the server (every submit button, every button without a `type`, every `ui-button--danger` or `--danger-soft` button and every button whose `(click)` reaches a `subscribe`, `firstValueFrom` or `lastValueFrom` through its component's own methods) carries `appNeedsConnection`, and its component imports `NeedsConnection`: offline it stays visible and focusable, `aria-disabled` with the hint "Potřebuje připojení", and nothing is sent; `scripts/needs-connection.mjs` (run by `npm run lint`) fails on one without it, and a control that works offline goes into its `WORKS_WITHOUT_CONNECTION` (a template, or `template#handler`) with the reason; an entry that names no control fails too. A call the check cannot see (a click that hands the work to another service) still gets the directive by hand. The fixed offline pill keeps room for itself through `html.is-offline` and `--offline-pill-height`, so a new fixed or sticky bar at the bottom rises above it as `.ui-action-bar` does. Public data shown from a copy (the service worker's or the payment details kept in `localStorage` under `nfnn.payment-details`) says how old it is and that it "nemusí být aktuální".
- Every action shows loading, success (`role="status"` line) and failure (`role="alert"` line with the backend title/detail or a Czech sentence saying what to do next); a running request disables its button and names what happens ("Připravujeme PDF…"); a converted button does it with `appBusy` (`aria-disabled`, `shared/ui/busy-button`), so the focus stays on it. Put `role="status"` on an element that is always rendered and the `@if` inside it (`<p role="status">@if (msg()) {<span>{{ msg() }}</span>}</p>`, as in `passkeys-card.html`): a status element inserted together with its text is often not announced. `role="alert"` may be inserted with its text. A page's success-or-failure line is a `Notice` (`{ kind: 'status' | 'alert', text }`) shown by `<app-live-notice [notice]="…" />` (`shared/ui/live-notice`), which keeps both regions rendered; elsewhere wrap the `@if` in `<div role="status">`. A line that appears with a page or with a whole new view (the first loading line, a wizard step that takes focus) may stay inside its `@if`. A notice of the app shell that may appear on any page (`chunk-load-notice`) is an always rendered `aria-live="polite"` region without `role="status"`, so each page keeps exactly one status region. Specs read these regions as `[role="status"]:not(:empty)`, e2e as `getByRole('alert').filter({ hasText: /\S/ })`.
- Forms: visible labels, hint before error, error at the field in its own words, focus to the first invalid field, placeholders never the only label, every password field inside `<app-password-reveal>`.
- Dialogs through `<app-dialog>` on native `<dialog>` (`showModal()`: focus trapped, Esc, backdrop and × dismiss, focus returns to the opener); shared open state in a service (`LoginDialogService.open(mode, options)`); every dialog keeps a step in the site's own history through `DialogHistory`, so a step back (Zpět, the home-screen app's swipe, `history.back()`) closes it (a stack: a dialog over a dialog has its own step, and a step back closes only the top one); the browser's Back leaves the site (Routing) (only a dialog whose opener keeps the history itself sets `[pushesHistory]="false"`: the sign-in dialog and the unsaved-changes dialog of a navigation guard); `presentation` (`auto` from the area's `DIALOG_PRESENTATION`, `centered` today, `sheet`, `full`) chooses the look below `nav`, a sheet keeps its ×. A sheet goes down under the finger as on iOS (#382): by its grabber, and by its content when that is scrolled to its top; a release far enough or a flick closes it exactly like ×, otherwise it springs back; every close of a sheet (the release, ×, Esc, the backdrop, a step back) slides it down from where it is with its veil and blur fading, and only then removes it, except under `prefers-reduced-motion`; a sheet that cannot close now, or whose page sets `[unsavedInput]` for input that took effort and is kept nowhere else, only stretches, and the latter asks "Zahodit rozepsané údaje?" first. The sign-in dialog is a sheet below `nav` like the account sheet and goes down under the finger too (#911, Jakub 2026-11-14, replacing the experts' full-screen decision for #382); it sets no `[unsavedInput]`, because the registration keeps its draft (`keepRegistrationDraft`, all but the password) and the other steps hold an address, a password the password manager fills again, or a code. A control inside a sheet that needs its own vertical drag is one `canPullSheetFrom` (`sheet-drag.ts`) leaves alone. A modal dialog makes the page behind it inert, so something behind it that must be heard while it is open (the chunk-load bar) announces through `DialogAnnouncer` (`shared/ui/dialog`), whose always rendered `role="status"` region sits inside every `<app-dialog>`; e2e therefore reads a dialog's status as `getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: /\S/ })`.
- Accessibility (Jakub 2026-10-23): WCAG 2.2 AA for keyboard, screen readers, zoom and system settings, without changing the default look or behaviour; a visible change made for it needs Jakub's OK (he declined darker field borders and stopping the hero scroll dot: those change only under `prefers-contrast: more` and `prefers-reduced-motion`).
  - Semantics: native elements first; one `main#obsah` per shell behind the skip link "Přeskočit na obsah" (top-left, Jakub 2026-10-18), named `nav` landmarks, one `h1`, headings in order; exclusive choices use `appRadioGroup` (`shared/ui/radio-group`: arrows, Home/End, roving `tabindex`), toggles `aria-pressed`, disclosures `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`; an `aria-label` contains the visible text; live regions `role="status"`/`role="alert"`, and a notice of the app shell an always rendered `aria-live="polite"` region (UX above).
  - Focus: the 3 px `--color-focus-ring` outline on `:focus-visible` for every control, never removed without a replacement of the same colour; after a route change the shell focuses the page `h1`, after an in-page link the target section (`tabindex="-1"`, no ring); dialogs and panels return focus to the opener; an overlay closes when focus leaves it; `scroll-padding-top` keeps focus from under the fixed header and `scroll-padding-bottom: var(--tab-bar-clearance)` from under the floating tab bar (`e2e/accessibility-keyboard.spec.ts` tabs through the About page at 390 and 1280 px); on the dark Přispět band and in the dark part of the contact section the ring takes the light `--color-tab-focus-ring-on-dark`, since the blue one keeps only about 3:1 there. The root scrolls without animation, so the scroll a Tab causes is instant; only a link to a section glides there, through `SmoothAnchorScroller` (`core/navigation/smooth-anchor-scroller.ts`, the router's `ViewportScroller`), and jumps under `prefers-reduced-motion`.
  - Size: type tokens in `rem`, so the browser's text size applies; pages hold at 320 px and with WCAG text spacing (nothing cut off, single grid columns `minmax(0, 1fr)`); targets at least 24 px, and on a touch screen (`pointer: coarse`) a small control reaches the 44 pt of Apple's guidelines without growing visibly: `@include touch-target.reaches-finger-size` (`src/styles/_touch-target.scss`, a `::before` of `--touch-target-size` that also positions the control, or `($positioned: true)` for one positioned already; `e2e/touch-targets.spec.ts`).
  - Zoom (Jakub 2026-10-31 00:56, plan v3 2.23d): the site and the installed app behave like an app, so the whole page never zooms: the viewport meta keeps `maximum-scale=1` (Android honours it), `PagePinchLock` (`core/zoom/`) cancels Safari's `gesture*` events (iOS ignores the meta) and `html` keeps `touch-action: manipulation` (no double-tap zoom).
